### [1] [PFAS-induced modulation of vesicle diffusiophoresis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07075)
*Seungsu Han,Gbassey S. A. Oteme,Prathima Nalam,Sangwoo Shin*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验揭示阴离子型PFAS在磷脂囊泡中的分配会改变囊泡表面电荷并增强其扩散泳（diffusiophoresis）运动，且分配过程伴随显著脂质流失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全氟和多氟烷基物质（PFAS）如何分配进入生物膜并改变膜性质，是阐明其毒性机制的核心问题。

Method: 实验研究阴离子PFAS分配对磷脂囊泡表面电荷及电动驱动囊泡运动（特别是扩散泳）的影响，并结合解吸实验与耗散型石英晶体微天平（QCM-D）进行表征，同时采用Nernst-Planck方程与Langmuir吸附方程组合模型进行解释。

Result: PFAS分配增强囊泡扩散泳，与Nernst-Planck–Langmuir模型预测一致；解吸实验与QCM-D表明PFAS分配过程中发生显著脂质流失。

Conclusion: 脂质流失归因于PFAS氟碳链与脂质烷基链之间的疏脂相互作用（lipophobic interactions），为PFAS破坏生物系统提供了新的机制性认识。

Abstract: Understanding how per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS) partition into biological membranes and alter their properties is central for elucidating PFAS toxicity. Here, we experimentally investigate how the partitioning of anionic PFAS affects the surface charge of phospholipid vesicles and consequently, their electrokinetically driven vesicle motion, in particular diffusiophoresis. We find that PFAS partitioning enhances vesicle diffusiophoresis, consistent with a model combining the Nernst-Planck and Langmuir adsorption equations. Desorption experiments, together with quartz crystal microbalance with dissipation monitoring (QCM-D), further reveal that substantial lipid loss occurs during PFAS partitioning. We attribute this loss to lipophobic interactions between the fluorocarbon chains of PFAS and the alkyl chains of lipids. Our findings provide new mechanistic insights into how PFAS may disrupt biological systems.

</details>


### [2] [Flexoelectric Switching of Lock-and-Key Topological Bits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07119)
*Zhijian Zhou,Claire Doré,Yusheng Cai,Jed-Joan S. Edziah,Brian Edwards,Nader Engheta,Amish J. Patel,Kathleen J. Stebe*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究表明向列相液晶（nematic liquid crystals）中的点缺陷（point defect）可在药丸形（pillbox）孔中被稳定捕获，并表现为沿外电场方向移动的双稳态“拓扑比特（topological bit）”，可用于存储与重写二进制信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑缺陷（topological defects）是向列相液晶中低序参量的高能位点，通常不稳定；研究旨在探索能否通过孔洞几何形状将其稳定并加以主动操控，从而把缺陷位置作为可寻址的信息载体。

Method: 采用药丸形孔（pillbox-shaped pores）约束缺陷，考察其“锁钥式（lock-and-key）”对接与双稳态行为；施加面内电场（in-plane field）驱动缺陷迁移，并测量速度与场强、极性的关系；同时分析缺陷周围的束缚电荷（bound charge）与空间电荷（space charge）分布，以及花生形孔（peanut-shaped pores）中的拮抗曲率效应。

Result: 点缺陷在两个孔瓣（lobe）之一中对接并呈双稳态；面内电场使其从一个孔瓣迁移至对面孔瓣，撤场后保持位置。迁移速度与场强呈线性关系且随极性反向，符合挠电耦合（flexoelectric coupling）。缺陷邻域的向列相具有正的体束缚电荷密度，由流体其余部分与界面上的负空间电荷补偿。缺陷位移增大沿场方向净偶极（net dipole）分量；花生形孔的拮抗曲率排斥缺陷，使孔几何可设定不同开关阈值。

Conclusion: 此类缺陷虽非拓扑必需（not topologically required），但寿命较长，可作为拓扑比特以位置存储信息；拓扑比特阵列（arrays of topological bits）能够以缺陷位置存储和重写二进制信息，为可重构、场可寻址的软材料（reconfigurable, field-addressable soft materials）提供途径。

Abstract: Topological defects in nematic liquid crystals are high-energy sites of reduced order. We show that a point defect can be stabilized in pillbox-shaped pores. The pore shape promotes lock-and-key docking similar to that observed for colloidal particles. The defect docks in one lobe or the other, displaying bistability. Furthermore, the defect moves like a charged object in an external electric field. An in-plane field drives the defect from one lobe to the opposite lobe, where it remains once the field is removed. The velocity is linear in field strength and reverses with polarity, consistent with flexoelectric coupling. Within the pore, the nematic around the defect carries a positive bulk bound charge density, compensated by negative space charge in the rest of the fluid and on the bounding surfaces. The external field breaks the mirror symmetry of the pore and drives the defect motion. Defect displacement increases the component of the net dipole in the domain along the field. Antagonistic curvature in peanut-shaped pores repels the defect, allowing pore geometry to set distinct switching thresholds. The defects, while not topologically required, are long lived, so information can be stored in their position, allowing them to serve as topological bits. Arrays of topological bits store and rewrite binary information in defect positions, offering a route to reconfigurable, field-addressable soft materials.

</details>


### [3] [A High-Dimensional Lab-Frame Model of Markovian Dynamics in Alanine Dipeptide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07200)
*Jesse M. Hall,Marina G. Guenza*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一个显式包含全局平动、转动与内部构象动力学的丙氨酸二肽高维粗粒化模型，并在不拟合粗粒化动力学观测量条件下重现了广泛的原子级自相关与互相关函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有粗粒化（coarse-grained, CG）模型多聚焦内部构象而忽略全局平动与转动，或需依赖动力学观测量拟合；本文旨在构建一个同时保留内部构象与全局运动耦合的紧凑、可解释的动力学表示。

Method: 基于原子级分子动力学（molecular dynamics）轨迹，构建含10个珠子（bead）的马尔可夫朗之万模型（Markovian Langevin model），包含24维内部平均力势（potential of mean force）与完整的位置特异性摩擦系数矩阵；平衡景观用高斯混合模型（Gaussian mixture model）表示，并引入转动熵修正（rotational-entropy correction）刻画内部构象与全局运动的耦合；摩擦系数由基于时间相关函数（time-correlation function）的广义爱因斯坦关系（generalized Einstein relation）获得。

Result: 转动熵修正对获得物理上可容许的摩擦矩阵是必要的，并显著影响推断出的动力学；在未拟合粗粒化动力学观测量的情况下，所建模型重现了广泛的原子级自相关与互相关函数（auto- and cross-correlation functions）。

Conclusion: 该模型在所选取的粗粒化分辨率下给出整个肽的紧凑且可解释的动力学表示，并在包括构象涨落与全局平动、转动扩散之间在内的广泛运动范围中，显式保留了能量耦合与耗散耦合（energetic and dissipative coupling）的区分。

Abstract: We present a high-dimensional coarse-grained model of alanine dipeptide that explicitly incorporates global translation and rotation alongside internal conformational dynamics. Using atomistic molecular dynamics trajectories, we construct a 10-bead Markovian Langevin model with a 24-dimensional internal potential of mean force and a full matrix of site-specific friction coefficients. The equilibrium landscape is represented by a Gaussian mixture model, while a rotational-entropy correction accounts for the coupling between internal conformation and global molecular motion. Friction coefficients are obtained using a generalized Einstein relation based on time-correlation functions. We show that the rotational-entropy correction is essential for obtaining a physically admissible friction matrix and significantly affects the inferred dynamics. Without fitting to coarse-grained dynamical observables, the resulting model reproduces a broad range of atomistic auto- and cross-correlation functions. The result is a compact and interpretable dynamical representation of the entire peptide at the chosen CG resolution, which explicitly preserves distinctions between energetic and dissipative coupling over a wide range of motions, including those between conformational fluctuations and global translational and rotational diffusion.

</details>


### [4] [Splay ostracism on ferroelectric nematic shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08027)
*Epifanio G. Virga*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 论文提出“展曲排斥（splay ostracism）”近似：在弯曲壳面上的铁电向列液晶（ferroelectric nematic liquid crystals）薄膜中禁止展曲畸变（splay distortion），从而把指向矢涡旋（director vortex）及其隔离壁的平衡问题化为纯几何问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雅典等古希腊城邦曾以放逐（ostracism）将影响力被视为危险的公民逐出城邦；论文借此类比，把“将展曲畸变逐出薄膜”作为一种近似假设，用以简化弯曲壳面上铁电向列液晶中指向矢涡旋与壁的平衡分析。

Method: 在展曲排斥条件下，指向矢场线（director field lines）须处处与壳面上的测地线族（systems of geodesics）正交，由此把平衡问题约化为几何问题；并对高斯曲率（Gaussian curvature）为正和为负的壳面分别展开推论。

Result: 所得准静态动力学（quasi static dynamics）同时给出两类情形：壁从壳面逃逸（walls escape from the shell）与壁被束缚在壳面上（trapped on it），且二者对涡旋动力学可能产生不同反映。

Conclusion: 展曲排斥近似是一个有效的几何化框架，可在正负高斯曲率壳面上统一描述指向矢涡旋与壁的准静态行为，并区分壁的逃逸与束缚两种结局。

Abstract: Ostracism was the practice in use in Athens and other ancient Greek cities to send into exile citizens whose influence was deemed to be dangerous. Quite similarly, splay ostracism is here the approximation that bans splay distortion from thin films of ferroelectric nematic liquid crystals on a curved shell in space. This reduces to a mere geometric problem the quest for equilibria of director vortexes and the walls isolating them, as splay ostracism requires the director field lines to be everywhere orthogonal to systems of geodesics on the shell. Consequences of this theorem are explored for shells with Gaussian curvature of either sign. The resulting quasi static dynamics reveals both cases where walls escape from the shell and cases where they are trapped on it, with possibly different reflections on the vortex dynamics.

</details>


### [5] [Nonequilibrium orientational bistability of a magnetically-driven slender particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07538)
*Zhiyuan Zhao,Longfei Li,Tingting Sun,Mingcheng Yang,Masao Doi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文研究了旋转磁场中横向磁化细长颗粒的取向构型，揭示同步与异步两种转动区域均存在非平衡取向双稳态（nonequilibrium orientational bistability），并给出有效自由能近似描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外驱动系统虽处于非平衡态但可达到稳态，然而磁场驱动的细长颗粒在旋转场中的取向双稳态及其统计热力学描述尚不清晰，本文旨在填补这一空白并为实验设计与应用提供依据。

Method: 结合理论计算与基于粒子的模拟（particle-based simulations），考察横向磁化（transversely-magnetized）细长颗粒在旋转磁场（rotating magnetic field）中的取向构型，并采用有效自由能近似（effective free-energy approximations）刻画稳态取向分布。

Result: 在颗粒与磁场同步转动（synchronous）和异步转动（asynchronous）两种区域均发现非平衡取向双稳态；站立（standing）与躺卧（lying）取向的相对布居数在同步区强烈依赖驱动频率，而在异步区主要由磁场强度控制；有效自由能近似可捕捉两种区域稳态取向分布的主要特征。

Conclusion: 研究为磁驱动细长颗粒的取向双稳态提供了统计热力学（statistical-thermodynamic）描述，有助于相关实验设计与先进应用。

Abstract: Externally driven systems are non-equilibrium but accessible to stationary states. By combining theoretical calculations and particle-based simulations, we investigate the orientational configuration of a transversely-magnetized slender particle in a rotating magnetic field. Our results reveal nonequilibrium orientational bistability in both regimes that the particle rotates synchronously and asynchronously with the field. The relative populations of standing and lying orientations depend strongly on the driving frequency in the synchronous regime, whereas they are primarily controlled by the magnetic field strength in the asynchronous regime. Effective free-energy approximations further capture the principal features of the steady-state orientational distributions in both regimes. Our work aims to give a statistical-thermodynamic description of orientational bistability in magnetically-driven slender particles, paving the way for relevant experimental designs and advanced applications.

</details>


### [6] [Reduced Glass Transition Temperature with Preserved Super-Arrhenius Dynamics in Nanoconfined Glass-Forming Nematic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07623)
*Aleksandra Deptuch,Eugeniusz Rajewski,Ewa Juszyńska-Gałązka*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用宽带介电谱研究4CFPB液晶在35与73 nm阳极氧化铝圆柱纳米孔中的分子动力学与成玻璃行为，发现受限使玻璃化转变温度由217 K降至208–210 K，但保留超Arrhenius特征与脆性指数约60，且β弛豫仍可检测、无冷结晶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究几何受限（geometrical confinement）对液晶分子弛豫、成玻璃与结晶动力学的影响，并与此前6、8 nm小孔径结果对照，以厘清孔径依赖的受限效应。

Method: 在35与73 nm直径的阳极氧化铝（anodic aluminum oxide）圆柱纳米孔中，用宽带介电谱（broadband dielectric spectroscopy）测量4-cyano-3-fluorophenyl 4-butylbenzoate（4CFPB）液晶；主α弛豫用Vogel-Fulcher-Tammann（VFT）与MYEGA模型分析，次级β弛豫的可能分子内贡献用密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算考察。

Result: 受限使玻璃化转变温度从块体（bulk）的217 K降至208–210 K，α弛豫仍保持超Arrhenius（super-Arrhenius）特征，脆性指数（fragility index）约为60；与既往6、8 nm孔不同，β弛豫仍可检测；未观察到冷结晶（cold crystallization）。

Conclusion: 结果表明几何受限对分子弛豫、成玻璃与结晶动力学具有独特影响：玻璃化转变温度与动力学随孔径变化，而脆性与超Arrhenius行为基本保持，且较大孔径下β弛豫与结晶行为区别于更小孔径体系。

Abstract: The molecular dynamics and glass-forming behavior of 4-cyano-3-fluorophenyl 4-butylbenzoate (4CFPB) liquid crystal was investigated by broadband dielectric spectroscopy in anodic aluminum oxide cylindrical nanopores of 35 and 73 nm diameter. The primary $α$-relaxation was analyzed using the Vogel-Fulcher-Tammann (VFT) and Mauro-Yue-Ellison-Gupta-Allan (MYEGA) models. Confinement reduces the glass transition temperature from 217 K in bulk to 208-210 K, while preserving the super-Arrhenius character of the $α$-relaxation and a fragility index of approximately 60. Unlike in previously studied 6 and 8 nm pores, the secondary $β$-relaxation remains detectable, with possible intramolecular contributions examined by density functional theory calculations. Next to the pore-size-dependent changes in molecular dynamics and glass transition temperature, cold crystallization is not observed. These findings highlight the distinct effects of geometrical confinement on molecular relaxation, glass formation, and crystallization kinetics.

</details>


### [7] [Closed-form solution for semi-symmetric folding of waterbomb tessellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08050)
*Sascha Stüttgen,Homam Spartali,Alice C. Niemeyer,Rostislav Chudoba,Daniel Robertz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文将水弹折纸单元（waterbomb cell）的对称折叠解析解推广至半对称情形（180°旋转对称），给出整个折叠过程的代数解，并建立设计参数与折纸壳体三维几何之间的函数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性折纸（rigid-origami）壳体在建筑、工程、医学与航空航天领域应用广泛，但现有数值方法算法复杂度高；解析解可降低复杂度并精确确定可行构型范围。

Method: 在既有对称折叠水弹单元解析解基础上，推导半对称折叠（即单元具有180°旋转对称性）情形的代数解，覆盖完整折叠过程。

Result: 获得设计参数与折叠水弹单元三维几何之间的函数关系，可高效研究可达成的壳体几何形态，并阐明单元几何参数与壳体曲率（shell curvature）、有效截面高度等静力相关几何性质之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 该对应关系的系统描述为采用现代水泥基复合材料（cementitious composites，如织物增强混凝土）设计薄壁、高性能、轻质壳体提供了基础。

Abstract: Rigid-origami folding has versatile applications in the fields of architecture, engineering, medicine and aerospace. Given the design parameters of a single waterbomb cell, the global geometry of the origami tessellated shell at an intermediate state of folding is directly determined. Even though existing numerical methods of rigid-origami can be used to describe geometric forms, algebraic solutions of the waterbomb folding kinematics and tessellations reduce the algorithmic complexity and determine the range of admissible configurations exactly. The existing analytical solution for a symmetrically folded waterbomb cell and tessellation is extended to the case of a cell folded semi-symmetrically, i.e. a cell with a 180° rotation symmetry. The algebraic derivation provides the solution for the entire folding process. The resulting functional relationship between the design parameters and the 3D cell geometry of the folded waterbomb cell enables efficient studies of achievable shell geometries. This development facilitates a deeper understanding of the correspondence between the geometric parameters of a single waterbomb cell and the statically relevant geometric properties, i.e. the shell curvature and the effective cross-sectional height. The systematic description of this correspondence provides the basis for innovative design concepts using modern cementitious composites, e.g. textile-reinforced concrete, to produce thin-walled, high-performance, lightweight shells.

</details>


### [8] [First-order condensation and meniscus pinning in finite open wedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08051)
*Jiří Janek,Miriam Magočiová,Alexandr Malijevský*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文借助经典密度泛函理论（classical density functional theory, DFT）研究有限开放楔形（open wedge）几何中的一阶凝聚（first-order condensation），并将微观相行为与宏观理论预测进行定量比较，分别考察对称与非对称楔形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宏观理论对有限楔形中的凝聚形态及相变给出了预测，但缺乏微观层面的验证；本文旨在检验这些宏观预测在微观尺度（尤其润湿条件下）是否成立。

Method: 采用经典密度泛函理论（DFT）在两种几何中计算相行为：由两片相同倾斜壁构成的对称楔形，以及由竖直壁与倾斜壁构成的非对称楔形；分析凝聚形态随楔形尺寸、开口角（opening angle）与壁接触角（contact angle）的依赖，并与宏观预测对比。

Result: DFT 证实了对称楔形的两种凝聚形态（condensed morphology）与非对称楔形的三种形态及其涌现条件。部分润湿（partial-wetting）条件下微观相界与宏观预测几乎定量一致，包括钉扎/去钉扎弯月面（pinned/depinned meniscus）构型间的重入转变（reentrant change）以及非对称楔形中半双钉扎态（semi-double-pinned state）的出现；完全润湿（complete-wetting）条件下相图拓扑保持一致，但单钉扎（single-pinned）区被系统性扩大。

Conclusion: 宏观理论对楔形凝聚形态与相图拓扑的预测在微观 DFT 层面得到验证；完全润湿下单钉扎区的扩大归因于吸附液膜（adsorbed liquid film）减小气相有效宽度、从而提高受限体系的有效长宽比（effective aspect ratio）。

Abstract: We study first-order condensation in finite open wedges using classical density functional theory (DFT) and compare the resulting microscopic phase behaviour with recent macroscopic predictions. Two geometries are considered: a symmetric wedge formed by two identical inclined walls and an asymmetric wedge formed by a vertical and an inclined wall. The DFT calculations confirm the two condensed morphologies predicted for the symmetric wedge and the three morphologies predicted for the asymmetric wedge and their emergence depending on the wedge size, opening angle, and wall contact angle. Under partial-wetting conditions, the microscopic phase boundaries are in nearly perfect quantitative agreement with the macroscopic predictions, including reentrant changes between pinned and depinned meniscus configurations and the appearance of the semi-double-pinned state in the asymmetric wedge. Under complete-wetting conditions, the DFT phase diagrams retain the topology predicted by the macroscopic theory, but the single-pinned region is systematically enlarged. This shift is attributed to adsorbed liquid films, which reduce the effective width available to the gas and thereby increase the effective aspect ratio of the confinement.

</details>

Conclusion: 研究表明，动理学—格子玻尔兹曼框架可作为含流体力学相互作用聚合物动力学的高效替代方案，在保持流变预测能力的同时大幅节省计算资源。

Abstract: We extend the BGK-type kinetic model for polymer dumbbells and its lattice Boltzmann implementation by incorporating hydrodynamic interaction (HI) effects to accurately recover the slow-variable dynamics governed by the Smoluchowski equation. Unlike Brownian dynamics approaches, which demand the computationally expensive evaluation of the square root of the configuration-dependent diffusion tensor at every time step, this kinetic method is straightforward to implement and avoids such overhead entirely. Consequently, it achieves a significant reduction in computational cost. We validate the model in steady and transient shear flows, demonstrating its ability to capture the distinct signatures of hydrodynamic interactions on bulk rheological properties.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作将 AMB+ 建立在可显现逆奥斯特瓦尔德熟化的微观晶格模型之上，明确了粗化行为随 Pe 变化的场论判据，并获得与理论一致的长寿命气泡态证据。

Abstract: Active model B+ (AMB+) explains how motility-induced phase separation can be arrested by reverse Ostwald ripening, but its coefficients have not been derived from a microscopic lattice model that shows it. We provide an exact hydrodynamic description of a six-polarity lattice gas on a hexagonal geometry and employ a full gradient expansion with angular-harmonic elimination to rigorously derive AMB+ with every coefficient fixed by the Péclet number Pe. Near the critical point (3/4, 4), coarsening is ordinary; further away, field theory predicts reverse Ostwald ripening of vapor bubbles above Pe $> 4 \sqrt{3}$, and simulations show long-lived bubbly states.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球磨过程中的碰撞与破碎结果（目标脆性颗粒被弹出或被俘获、破碎或保持完整）受碰撞几何与速度共同影响，需要一个可解析的最小模型来厘清俘获与断裂各自的机制。

Method: 采用离散元方法（discrete element method, DEM）模拟三球体几何的最小模型，并结合Christensen型与Weibull型归一化断裂准则（normalized fracture criteria）评估断裂；同时发展约化三球碰撞描述，俘获区域由几何与摩擦决定，断裂则基于DEM输出的应力型与接触力型归一化准则判定。

Conclusion: 俘获由碰撞几何与摩擦支配，而断裂可在俘获区域内用基于应力与接触力的归一化准则判定，从而将球磨碰撞与破碎过程归约为几何俘获条件加断裂临界条件的组合框架。

Abstract: We numerically investigate the collision and fracture processes of ball milling based on a minimal model of a three-sphere geometry using discrete element method (DEM) simulations combined with Christensen-type and Weibull-type normalized fracture criteria. The outcome of the brittle target particle (ejected versus trapped, fractured versus intact) depends on the impact geometry and velocity. We develop a reduced three-sphere collision description in which trapping is governed by impact geometry and friction, while fracture inside the trapping regime is evaluated from DEM output using stress-based and contact-force-based normalized criteria. The trapping regime follows a closed-form geometric condition, while the critical conditions for fracture vary with impact angle, obliquity angle, impact velocity, and particle size ratio. Head-on collisions with larger size ratios require lower critical impact velocities for fracture.

</details>

Abstract: The micro pixel chamber ($μ$-PIC), a type of micro-pattern gaseous detectors (MPGD), features pixel electrodes structured similarly to a sliced proportional counter. Conventionally, $μ$-PIC electrodes are connected to orthogonal strip electrodes to reduce the number of readout circuits; however, this configuration introduces multi-hit ambiguity when tracing winding tracks or during high-rate operations. A well-known solution to this problem is the adoption of an additional readout axis. To this end, we designed a new electrode structure using Garfield++ simulations and fabricated a triaxial $μ$-PIC based on through-glass-via (TGV) technology. This triaxial $μ$-PIC achieved a high gas gain of $1.1\times 10^4$ and the energy resolution of 22\% (FWHM) at 5.9~keV, Furthermore, the false-hit probability was less than 0.78\% at a 95\% confidence level for two clusters separated by a distance of over 3 mm, consistent with Garfield++ predictions. With this triaxial $μ$-PIC, single avalanche signals are collected from three independent readout strip electrodes (the anode and two cathodes). The charge ratio among these electrodes remains nearly constant at anode:U-cathode:V-cathode=3:2:1. In addition, we successfully achieved an enhanced gain of $3.2\times 10^4$ without increasing the discharge risk by employing an alternative operation mode.

Kawaue,T. Kikawa,B. Kliček,H. Kobayashi,B. Kovač,J. McKean,T. Matsuo,S. Mikado,A. Minamino,Y. Morimoto,Y. Nakamura,T. Nakaya,H. Oaira,N. Onda,H. Oshima,O. Sato,for the NINJA Collaboration*

Conclusion: 结果表明FROST方案可扩展至米级有效面积，并在实际实验条件下保持高探测效率及显著优于读出间距的定位分辨率。

Abstract: A meter-scale Fiber-Readout mOnolithic and Scatterer-embedded scintillator Tracker (FROST) was constructed and operated in the NINJA experiment to enable muon-track matching between the nuclear emulsion detectors and the downstream muon detector. The detector consists of a 1.32 m $\times$ 1.40 m plastic scintillator plate assembled from optically bonded scatterer-embedded plates, with wavelength-shifting fibers arranged at a pitch of 10 mm. During J-PARC neutrino-beam operation, FROST achieved a data acquisition efficiency of 99.59% and a detection efficiency of 99.946$\pm$0.006% for selected neutrino-induced muons well within the active area. A position reconstruction method based on the light-yield distribution was developed for both single- and multi-particle events. In a neutrino-interaction simulation, muon position resolutions of 1.77 mm and 1.61 mm were achieved in the two orthogonal readout directions, assuming correct muon identification. Using a beam-data sample dominated by single-muon events, upper limits on the position resolution of 2.20 mm and 2.03 mm were obtained for track angles below 20°. These results demonstrate that the FROST concept can be scaled up to a meter-scale active area while maintaining high detection efficiency and a position resolution well below the readout pitch under actual experimental conditions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱zeta函数（spectral zeta function）特殊值的迭代积分表达式中，被积函数分母含循环三对角矩阵（cyclic tridiagonal matrices）的行列式，其显式求值是研究此类特殊值模形式性（modularity）等算术性质的核心组成部分，因此需要对其加以明确计算。

Method: 所研究的行列式属于循环三对角矩阵；求解在容斥原理之外，概念上采用基于单体-二聚体系统的组合论证（combinatorial arguments）进行显式求值。

Result: 得到了出现在迭代积分被积函数分母中的循环三对角矩阵行列式的显式求值结果。

Conclusion: 该显式求值可作为研究谱zeta函数特殊值算术性质（如模形式性）的核心组件。

Abstract: We obtain an explicit evaluation of the determinants that appear in the denominators of the integrands for the iterated integrals expressing special values of the spectral zeta function of the noncommutative harmonic oscillator at integers greater than 1. The determinants in question are those of cyclic tridiagonal matrices; their explicit evaluation is carried out, in addition to the inclusion-exclusion principle, conceptionally using combinatorial arguments based on monomer-dimer systems. This explicit evaluation serves as a core component in the study of arithmetic properties such as the modularity of the special values of the spectral zeta function.

</details>

### [15] [The index of mesoscopic Wilson--Dirac operators: I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08266)
*Mikio Furuta,Shinichiroh Matsuo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是系列论文首篇，研究闭黎曼流形上介观 Wilson–Dirac 算子（mesoscopic Wilson–Dirac operator）的指标（index），并通过谱流（spectral flow）定义该指标，证明在小尺度下它与连续 Dirac 算子指标一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该系列旨在将 Dirac 算子的指标离散化。平坦环面上可借助规则格点上的格点规范理论 Wilson–Dirac 算子实现，但一般闭黎曼流形上不存在规则格点，因此需要引入介于连续算子与离散化之间的中间对象。

Method: 在任意闭黎曼流形上定义介观 Wilson–Dirac 算子，其构造方式为对协变差分（covariant differences）在约等于固定长度尺度的距离上进行积分；并借助谱流定义其指标。证明依赖尺度一致的椭圆估计（elliptic estimate）与 Rellich 型紧性定理（Rellich-type compactness theorem）。

Result: 证明介观 Wilson–Dirac 算子的指标在所有充分小尺度下与连续 Dirac 算子的指标一致。

Conclusion: 介观算子是连接连续 Dirac 算子与其离散化的有效中间步骤；后续论文将从介观算子过渡到离散 Wilson–Dirac 算子。

Abstract: This is the first of a series of papers which will be devoted to a study of the index of mesoscopic Wilson--Dirac operators on closed Riemannian manifolds. The aim of the series is to discretise the index of the Dirac operator. On flat tori, this is achieved by the Wilson--Dirac operator of lattice gauge theory on a regular lattice. On a general closed Riemannian manifold, however, no regular lattice is available. As an intermediate step between the continuum Dirac operator and its discretisations, we introduce mesoscopic Wilson--Dirac operators on any closed Riemannian manifold, defined by integrating covariant differences over distances comparable to a fixed length scale. In this paper, we define the index of the mesoscopic Wilson--Dirac operators via spectral flow and prove that it agrees with that of the continuum Dirac operator at all sufficiently small scales. The proof rests on an elliptic estimate uniform in the scale and a Rellich-type compactness theorem. In the second paper, we shall pass from mesoscopic operators to discrete Wilson--Dirac operators.

</details>

### [16] [Exploring the limit of partonic collectivity in small collision systems at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08327)
*ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: ALICE 在小碰撞系统（p-Pb 与 pp）中测量多种强子种类的 $p_{\rm T}$-微分椭圆流（elliptic flow, $v_{2}$），结果显示在 $N_{\rm ch}\geq 25$ 时与部分子集体性（partonic collectivity）一致；在低多重数 $15\leq N_{\rm ch}<25$ 区间，重子-介子 $v_{2}$ 劈裂仍与高多重数相近，但显著性降至约 $2\sigma$（p-Pb）和不足 $1\sigma$（pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前通过高多重数 p-Pb 与 pp 碰撞中介子与重子 $v_{2}$ 的分组与劈裂现象，已推断小系统中存在部分子相；本文旨在进一步确定部分子流（partonic flow）在小碰撞系统中的适用范围与下限。

Method: 基于 ALICE 在 $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.02$ TeV p-Pb 与 $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV pp 碰撞的数据，测量多种已鉴别强子的 $p_{\rm T}$-微分 $v_{2}$，并按多重数区间（p-Pb：$N_{\rm ch}\geq 50$、$25\leq N_{\rm ch}<50$、$15\leq N_{\rm ch}<25$；pp：$25\leq N_{\rm ch}<50$、$15\leq N_{\rm ch}<25$）考察 $v_{2}$ 的质量依赖性与粒子间的分组、劈裂模式。

Result: $N_{\rm ch}\geq 25$ 的 p-Pb 与 pp 结果均与部分子集体性的发展相符；在 $15\leq N_{\rm ch}<25$ 的低多重数区，以重子-介子 $v_{2}$ 比值量化的劈裂幅度大体与高多重数一致，但因实验不确定度较大，其显著性在 p-Pb 中降至约 $2\sigma$、在 pp 中低于 $1\sigma$。

Conclusion: 该测量是确定 LHC 小碰撞系统中部分子流边界的第一步，为界定部分子集体性出现的多重数条件提供了实验依据。

Abstract: The existence of a partonic phase in small collision systems has been investigated previously through measurements of the elliptic flow ($v_{2}$) of mesons ($π^{\pm}$, K$^{\pm}$, and K$_{S}^{0}$) and baryons ($\rm{p}(\overline{\rm{p}})$, $Λ(\overlineΛ)$) in high-multiplicity p-Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 5.02$ TeV and pp collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV recorded by ALICE. The results have revealed a grouping and splitting behavior of $v_{2}$ for baryons and mesons at intermediate transverse momentum ($p_{\rm{T}}$), establishing that partonic collectivity, as observed in heavy-ion collisions, is also present in these systems. In this Letter, the $p_{\rm{T}}$-differential $v_{2}$ of various hadronic species is investigated in p-Pb collisions for the multiplicity intervals $N_{\rm ch} \geq 50$, $25 \leq N_{\rm ch} < 50$, and $15 \leq N_{\rm ch} < 25$, as well as in pp collisions for $25 \leq N_{\rm ch} < 50$ and $15 \leq N_{\rm ch} < 25$. Here, $N_{\rm ch}$ is the efficiency-corrected number of charged particles produced in the kinematic region of $|η|< 0.8$ and $0.2 < p_{\rm T} < 3.0$ GeV/$c$. The characteristic mass dependence of $v_{2}$, as well as the grouping and splitting patterns among identified particles, are examined. The results in both p-Pb and pp collisions with $N_{\rm ch} \geq 25$ are consistent with the development of partonic collectivity. In the low-multiplicity region, $15 \leq N_{\rm ch} < 25$, the baryon-to-meson $v_{2}$ splitting, as quantified by the baryon-to-meson $v_2$ ratios, remains broadly consistent with that at higher multiplicities. However, its significance decreases to $\sim2σ$ in p-Pb collisions and to less than $1σ$ in pp collisions owing to the sizable experimental uncertainties. These measurements constitute the first steps towards identifying the limits of partonic flow in small collision systems at the LHC.

</details>

Conclusion: 多重不变量可分离的配分函数组合受定向配边支配：非挠部分（如 $d=4$ 的符号差）因 $p_1$ 具局部表达式而无法从非普适因子中分离，挠部分则可被多重不变量完全刻画，且局部数据相同的图可对应拓扑上不同的流形并给出不同符号。

Abstract: In a previous work we conjectured that the multi-invariants of the ground state of a gapped $(d-1)+1$-dimensional system, labeled by bipartite $(d+1)$-edge-colored graphs encoding closed oriented $d$-manifolds $M$, compute the partition functions $Z(M)$ of the low energy topological quantum field theory (TQFT), up to non-universal factors determined by the local structure of the triangulation. In this paper we determine which combinations of partition functions can be isolated from the non-universal factors. The answer is governed by oriented bordism. If $M$ is null-bordant, $Z(M)$ is obtained from an explicit, state-independent finite family of multi-invariants, up to a power of $Z(S^d)$ which is fixed to be $Z(S^d)^{χ(M)/2}$ for even $d$ and can be removed for odd $d$. Conversely, the obstruction is measured by a quotient of the group of "residues" of colored graphs which receives a surjective map from the oriented bordism group, with kernel precisely the torsion subgroup as far as smooth manifolds are concerned. In $d=4$ the obstruction is the signature: $Z(M)/Z(S^4)^{χ(M)/2}$ can be extracted if and only if $σ(M)=0$. This is due to the fact that the first Pontryagin class $p_1$ has a local expression and thus $e^{iθσ(M)}=e^{\frac{i}{3}θ\int_M p_1}$ cannot be separated from the non-universal factors. Torsion invariants, in contrast, are not obstructed: for the ground state of the $4+1$-dimensional invertible phase with partition function $(-1)^{\int w_2w_3}$ we compute the multi-invariants exactly, and two graphs with identical local data, encoding the Dold manifold and $\mathbb{CP}^2\times S^1$, give opposite signs.

</details>

Abstract: We revisit the scattering of massless chiral fermions by a minimally charged BPS 't Hooft-Polyakov monopole. Existing proposals emphasize different aspects of this process and their connections are often unclear, giving rise to apparent conflicts concerning the outgoing states, energy conservation, and anomalous scattering amplitudes. Using the functional renormalization group, we study these theories within a quartic-derivative truncation and find no evidence for an interacting fixed point.   <summary>Details</summary>

For example, at $c-1 = 3\times 10^{-3}$ integrality strengthens the rigorous bound from $Δ_{\rm gap} \lesssim 0.5$ to $Δ_{\rm gap} < 0.041$. Abstract: Coupling two-dimensional fundamental Dirac fermions to $SU(N)_k$ Chern-Simons theory produces non-chiral interfaces that in the infrared flow to a compact boson and a $W_N$ minimal model. The $q$-deformation is achieved by coupling a chain of one-dimensional fundamental complex fermions to $SU(N)_k$ Chern-Simons theory, keeping the underlying topological gauge field continuous. The operator algebra is controlled by quantum groups, which provide an algebraic perspective on the usual categorical formulation of anyon chains. The scalar two-point function and the shift symmetry of its source determine the matter Weyl anomaly, which fixes the logarithmic temperature dependence of the quadratic term in the charge expansion.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We consider a dual version of heterotic Kodaira-Spencer theory on a Calabi-Yau three-fold $X$ with background fluxes, related to the type I BCOV theory of Costello-Li. TL;DR: 该工作利用模糊球（fuzzy-sphere）框架在三维 O(2) Wilson–Fisher 共形场论中构造 U(1) 单值性缺陷（monodromy defect）连续族，并通过数值手段检验其共形稳定性，为临界非紧致 Abelian Higgs 模型中分数 Wilson 线（fractional Wilson line）的共形性质提供非微扰证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wilson 线是探测电荷屏蔽（charge screening）与解禁闭（deconfinement）的规范不变探针，可反映量子临界物质对外部电荷的响应，但强耦合临界点处其普适信息难以获取。作者据此研究三维 O(2) Wilson–Fisher 共形场论中 U(1) 单值性缺陷族，以刻画分数 Wilson 线的普适行为。

Method: 基于 O(2) Wilson–Fisher 共形场论与含一个单位电荷标量的临界非紧致 Abelian Higgs 模型之间的对偶（duality），在模糊球（fuzzy-sphere）框架下于球体两极穿入分数 Aharonov–Bohm 磁通，从而构造该连续缺陷族并进行谱学分析。

Result: 观测到携带分数横向自旋（fractional transverse spin）的带电算符出现，作为所施加单值性的特征信号；位移算符（displacement operator）以其受保护维度出现，支持该缺陷线的共形性质；未分辨出任何相关的保对称性缺陷标量，并识别出电荷为一的单极子算符（monopole operator）为相关的对称破缺形变。

Conclusion: 在对偶假设成立的前提下，结果表明临界 Abelian Higgs 模型的分数 Wilson 线是稳定的共形线（conformal line），为该问题提供了非微扰（non-perturbative）证据。

Abstract: Wilson lines are gauge-invariant probes of charge screening and deconfinement, which can detect the response of quantum critical matter to an external charge, but their universal information at strongly-coupled critical points remains hard to access. Here we investigate a continuous family of $U(1)$ monodromy defects of the three-dimensional $O(2)$ Wilson--Fisher conformal field theory, which is dual to fractional Wilson lines in the critical noncompact Abelian Higgs model with one unit-charged scalar. We realize this continuous family of defects within the fuzzy-sphere framework by threading a fractional Aharonov--Bohm flux through the two poles of the sphere. We observe the emergence of charged operators carrying fractional transverse spins, as a characteristic signature of the imposed monodromy. The appearance of displacement operator at its protected dimension supports the conformal nature of this defect line. Crucially, we resolve no relevant symmetry-preserving defect scalar, and identify charge-one monopole operators as relevant symmetry-breaking deformations. Assuming the duality, these results provide non-perturbative evidence that fractional Wilson lines of the critical Abelian Higgs model are stable conformal lines.

</details>

*Aritra Banerjee*

Main category: hep-th

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为非相对论弦动力学建立可积性分析框架，并厘清相对论弦的非相对论极限中哪些孤子构型能够存活。

Method: 对弦牛顿-嘉当背景上的经典非相对论弦发展 Pohlmeyer 型约化；针对二维球面上的弦确定不变约化场，证明其动力学可积并构造 Lax 表示，同时将该内禀表述与相对论弦的非相对论展开相联系，建立非相对论弦解与约化对应解之间的字典。

Result: 约化场动力学被证明可积并具有 Lax 表示；所建立的字典明确判定哪些相对论孤子构型（solitonic configurations）在非相对论极限下存活、哪些退耦（decouple）。

Conclusion: 该构造为将可积性方法（integrability methods）应用于牛顿-嘉当弦动力学提供了框架，并给出了相对论孤子与非相对论约化解之间的系统对应。

Abstract: We develop a Pohlmeyer-type reduction for classical non-relativistic strings on string Newton-Cartan backgrounds. For strings on a two-sphere, we identify the invariant reduced field, show that its dynamics is integrable, and construct a Lax representation. We relate this intrinsic formulation to the non-relativistic expansion of relativistic strings and obtain a dictionary between non-relativistic string solutions and their reduced counterparts. The construction explicitly determines which relativistic solitonic configurations survive the non-relativistic limit and which decouple. Moreover, it provides a framework for applying integrability methods to Newton-Cartan string dynamics.

</details>


### [45] [The three-dimensional Galilean black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08471)
*Luis Avilés,Oscar Fuentealba,Diego Hidalgo,Pablo Rodríguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文报道了三维伽利略反德西特引力（three-dimensional Galilean Anti-de Sitter gravity）的一种新解，该理论可表述为在三维双扩展牛顿-胡克群（doubly extended Newton-Hooke group）下不变的拓扑规范理论（topological gauge theory）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机；仅可推断其关注伽利略反德西特引力框架下新解及其热力学/黑洞性质的构造。

Method: 将理论形式化为受三维双扩展牛顿-胡克群支配的拓扑规范理论，并借助解的欧几里得延拓（Euclidean continuation）拓扑所对应的正则性条件推导温度、角速度等化学势。

Result: 该解具有能量、角动量、自旋与静质量四个守恒荷（conserved charges），其中自旋大小被静质量从上方限制；解呈现定义黑洞构型的热情形，并可导出温度、角速度等化学势。

Conclusion: 在零温极限下，当自旋与静质量之间的界取等号（饱和）时，出现一种极端构型（extreme configuration）。

Abstract: We report a novel solution of three-dimensional Galilean Anti-de Sitter gravity. The theory can be formulated as a topological gauge theory invariant under the doubly extended Newton-Hooke group in three dimensions. The solution possesses four conserved charges: energy, angular momentum, spin and rest mass. Interestingly, the magnitude of the spin turns out to bounded from above by the rest mass. The solution exhibits the thermal properties that define a black hole configuration in terms of its gauge theoretical description. Temperature, angular velocity and other chemical potentials are obtained from suitable regularity conditions associated with the topology of the Euclidean continuation of the solution. At zero temperature, an extreme configuration is shown to emerge provided the bound between the spin and the rest mass saturates.

</details>


### [46] [Defect Anomalies at a Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08476)
*Oleksandr Diatlyk,Zimo Sun,Yifan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究表面缺陷（surface defects）所携带的Weyl异常与引力异常（gravitational anomalies），证明这些通常表现为缺陷上接触项（contact terms）的异常可通过体应力张量关联函数（bulk stress-tensor correlators）的远距离积分行为编码，并据此导出求求和规则与三维CFT边界Weyl异常的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面缺陷的Weyl与引力异常可视为二维共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）中心荷（central charges）的推广，刻画无能隙表面模式（gapless surface modes）对背景几何的异常响应；但这类异常通常以接触项形式出现，不易从远距离可观测数据中提取，因而需要寻找不依赖重整化方案（scheme-independent）的刻画方式。

Method: 将缺陷异常编码于体应力张量关联函数（bulk stress-tensor correlators）的远距离、方案无关的积分量中，并利用缺陷算符数据（defect operator data）推导相应的求和规则（sum rules）。

Result: 得到了以缺陷算符数据表示的异常求和规则，并建立了三维共形场论中边界Weyl异常（boundary Weyl anomaly）的下界；结果通过若干具体例子加以说明。

Conclusion: 表面缺陷异常不仅可由缺陷上的接触项描述，也可由远距离体关联函数的积分数据刻画，这为约束三维CFT的边界Weyl异常提供了新途径。

Abstract: Surface defects carry Weyl and gravitational anomalies that generalize the central charges of two-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs) and characterize the anomalous response of gapless surface modes to background geometry. These anomalies usually appear as contact terms on the defect. We explain how they are encoded at a distance, in scheme-independent integrated bulk stress-tensor correlators. We derive sum rules for them in terms of defect operator data, establish a lower bound on the boundary Weyl anomaly in three-dimensional CFTs, and illustrate our results with explicit examples.

</details>

### [47] [Spectral symmetry fingerprints in equivariant representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07410)
*Jiacheng Cen,Anyi Li,Ning Lin,Zihe Wang,Ji-Rong Wen,Wenbing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出谱对称指纹（spectral symmetry fingerprints, SymFP），将等变表示的奇异值谱（singular-value spectra）作为结构对称性的统一指纹，通过秩模板匹配识别离散点群、并通过目标秩之外的谱能量量化连续对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何深度学习（geometric deep learning）中，对称性对等变表示施加秩约束，通常被视为限制等变图神经网络（equivariant graph neural networks, GNNs）表达能力的不利因素；本文转而论证这些约束可被加以利用。

Method: SymFP 基于等变表示的奇异值谱，采用秩模板匹配（rank-template matching）实现离散点群（point group）识别，并以超出目标秩约束的谱能量进行连续对称性分析；该方法具有取向不变性（orientation invariant）与架构无关性（architecture agnostic），可实现无参数（parameter-free）的 GNN 实现，其谱可跨多个目标群复用，且泄漏分数（leakage scores）满足子群单调性（subgroup monotonicity）。

Result: 在合成结构、分子、配位化合物及大型生物组装体上，SymFP 能准确识别点群、追踪受控对称性破缺，并揭示易被全局几何量掩盖的局域与按度分辨率（degree-resolved）的对称性变化。

Conclusion: 结果表明，等变表示的谱结构可作为跨物理系统刻画对称性的通用框架（general framework）。

Abstract: Symmetry governs the organization of physical structures, while symmetry breaking reveals their transformations. In geometric deep learning, symmetry imposes rank constraints on equivariant representations, limiting the expressive capacity of equivariant graph neural networks (GNNs). Here we show that these constraints can instead be exploited: the singular-value spectra of equivariant representations provide a unified fingerprint of structural symmetry. We introduce spectral symmetry fingerprints (SymFP), which enable discrete point-group identification through rank-template matching and continuous symmetry analysis by quantifying spectral energy beyond target rank constraints. SymFP is orientation invariant and architecture agnostic, enabling a parameter-free GNN implementation. Its spectra can be reused across multiple target groups, and the resulting leakage scores satisfy subgroup monotonicity. Across synthetic structures, molecules, coordination complexes and large biological assemblies, SymFP accurately identifies point groups, tracks controlled symmetry breaking and reveals local and degree-resolved symmetry changes that can be obscured by global geometric measures. These results establish the spectral structure of equivariant representations as a general framework for characterizing symmetry across physical systems.

</details>


### [48] [SteelAgent: LLM-Orchestrated, Physics-Informed Steel Property Prediction with Audited Generalization and Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06953)
*Aleksandr Volkov,Roman Sultimov,Mikhail Kuzin,Yury Maximov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于 SteelAgent/SteelBench 框架，考察留出单元（held-out unit）的选择如何改变钢材抗拉强度（tensile strength）评估结论，发现随机划分与按钢种族（steel family）留出之间的性能与校准差异巨大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢材性能模型在随机测试划分（random test split）上表现良好，但对陌生合金的外推（extrapolation）能力可能很差，因而需要审视评估协议本身对结论的影响。

Method: 将冶金描述符（metallurgical descriptors）与机器学习模型结合，并通过语言模型接口实现性能预测、不确定性报告与逆向设计（inverse design）；在 SteelBench 上比较随机划分、钢种族留出、留出来源及分等级实验室分析等不同评估设置。

Result: 梯度提升（gradient boosting）的合并折外 R² 从随机划分的 0.859 降至钢种族留出的 0.113；名义 90% 预测区间的经验覆盖率（empirical coverage）由 91.0% 降至 37.6%。物理导出特征（physics-derived features）的影响随评估设置而变：在 17 族留出分析中使 R² 提高 0.2534，而在四牌号实验室分析中降低 0.358。

Conclusion: 报告了组级误差（group-level errors）与一个合成回归实验（synthetic regression experiment），说明留出组上的校准（calibration）可能恶化；据此主张在给出性能估计或合规评分（compliance score）时同时报告评估总体（evaluation population）。

Abstract: Steel property models can perform well on random test splits even when they extrapolate poorly to unfamiliar alloys. SteelAgent couples metallurgical descriptors and machine-learning models with a language-model interface for property prediction, uncertainty reporting and inverse design. We examine how the choice of held-out unit changes the conclusions drawn from SteelBench tensile-strength evaluations. For gradient boosting, pooled out-of-fold $R^2$ fell from 0.859 on a random split to 0.113 when steel families were held out. Empirical coverage of nominal 90% prediction intervals ranged from 91.0% under random splitting to 37.6% on held-out sources. The effect of physics-derived features depended on the evaluation. They increased gradient-boosting $R^2$ by 0.2534 in a 17-family holdout analysis and decreased it by 0.358 in a separate four-grade laboratory analysis. We report group-level errors and a synthetic regression experiment illustrating how calibration can deteriorate on held-out groups. These results motivate reporting the evaluation population alongside a property estimate or compliance score.

</details>


### [49] [Data-Driven Modeling of Molten Chloride Corrosion Using Static Test Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07125)
*Md Mahmudul Hasan Pathik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究汇编291个文献熔融氯化物腐蚀实验数据，构建可解释机器学习框架，并引入氯盐制备指数（Chloride Salt Preparation Index, CSPI）量化盐处理条件，实现腐蚀速率预测（交叉验证R²=0.85）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔融氯化物腐蚀（molten chloride corrosion）研究已开展数十年，但跨合金、盐体系与测试条件的可靠预测仍十分困难。

Method: 将文献中291个静态熔融氯化物腐蚀实验整理为结构化数据集；提出CSPI以统一表征盐处理方法、气氛控制与预暴露杂质状态；采用混合树基集成模型（blended tree-based ensemble）进行腐蚀速率预测，并结合全局敏感性分析、SHAP分析与保形预测（conformal prediction）。

Result: 混合树基集成模型预测性能最为稳定，交叉验证R²为0.85、MAE为0.41 mg cm⁻² day⁻¹；分级CSPI较简单的纯化/未纯化二分类保留更多信息并成为主导描述符；暴露时间、温度与合金化学成分为重要贡献因素，其趋势与已知腐蚀行为一致；保形预测区间校准良好。

Conclusion: 显式表征实验情境时，历史腐蚀数据可支持定量、可解释且含不确定性感知的预测；同时指出现有数据在报告一致性与针对性实验方面的不足，需改进以提升未来熔盐腐蚀模型。

Abstract: Molten chloride corrosion has been studied for decades, yet reliable prediction across different alloys, salts, and test conditions remains difficult. In this work, 291 static molten chloride corrosion experiments reported in the literature were compiled into a structured dataset and used to develop an interpretable machine-learning framework for corrosion-rate predictions. To better represent the differences in salt condition across studies, a Chloride Salt Preparation Index (CSPI) was introduced to bring the reported salt-treatment method, atmosphere control, and pre-exposure impurity condition on a common numerical scale. A blended tree-based ensemble provided the most consistent predictive performance, achieving a cross-validated \(R^2\) of 0.85 and an MAE of 0.41 mg cm\(^{-2}\) day\(^{-1}\). The graded CSPI retained more useful information than a simple purified/unpurified classification and emerged as the dominant model descriptor. Global sensitivity and SHAP analyses further identified exposure time, temperature, and alloy chemistry as important contributors to the model predictions, with several observed trends consistent with the established molten-chloride corrosion behavior. The conformal prediction intervals were well calibrated, providing an estimate of prediction uncertainty in addition to the point prediction. Overall, the framework demonstrates that historical corrosion data can support quantitative, interpretable, and uncertainty-aware prediction when experimental context is represented explicitly, while also highlighting where more consistent reporting and targeted experiments are needed to improve future molten-salt corrosion models.

</details>


### [50] [Equivariant Deep Learning in Direct-Product Space for Electronic-Structure Hamiltonian Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07695)
*Shi Yin,Zujian Dai,Xinyang Pan,Lixin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DP-Learner 提出在直积空间（direct-product space）中直接对类哈密顿量算符块执行消息传递（message passing）的等变学习框架，避免显式 Clebsch–Gordan 分解，并在精度与效率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变架构在不可约球张量（irreducible spherical tensors）的直和空间（direct-sum space）中传播特征，而目标哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）本质上是局部轨道与自旋基上的直积表示（direct-product representation）算符块，二者存在表示失配（representation mismatch），导致连续哈密顿块被割裂、难以直接操作轨道耦合，且角动量分量不规则、变换频繁，不利于硬件执行与内存管理。

Method: 理论上提出基于李生成元（Lie-generator）的方向嵌入，以及不变加权左右乘积与收缩（invariantly weighted left–right products and contractions），在无需显式 Clebsch–Gordan 分解或逆重构的情况下保持对 SO(3)、SU(2) 及空间反演的等变性；方法上实现为 Transformer 架构，类哈密顿特征矩阵/张量在直积空间中经等变算符演化，并由非线性不变标量路由控制矩阵单元的结构化门控、方向传播、注意力与环境上下文交互。

Result: 构建了 MP-HAM-110K 大规模哈密顿数据集，覆盖 68 种元素、逾 110,000 个材料结构，同时包含哈密顿量与波函数数据；在 MP-HAM-110K 与 Materials-HAM-SOC 上的实验表明，相较最先进方法（state-of-the-art），DP-Learner 大幅提升哈密顿量预测精度与推理效率。

Conclusion: 通过在直积空间中直接进行等变消息传递，可同时缓解表示失配与计算瓶颈，为哈密顿量预测提供精度与效率兼优的建模范式。

Abstract: Most existing equivariant architectures for Hamiltonian prediction propagate features in direct-sum spaces of irreducible spherical tensors, whereas the target Hamiltonian is naturally an operator block in a direct-product representation over local orbital and spin bases. This representation mismatch fragments contiguous Hamiltonian blocks, obscures direct manipulation of orbital couplings, and organizes features into irregular angular-momentum components that require numerous transformations and are poorly aligned with efficient hardware execution and memory management. To address both the representation mismatch and computational bottleneck, we propose DP-Learner, an equivariant learning framework that performs message passing directly on Hamiltonian-like operator blocks in the direct-product space. Theoretically, we propose Lie-generator-based directional embedding and invariantly weighted left--right products and contractions that preserve equivariance to SO(3), SU(2), and spatial inversions, without explicit Clebsch--Gordan decomposition or inverse reconstruction. Methodologically, we instantiate this principle as a Transformer architecture, in which Hamiltonian-like feature matrices or tensors evolve through equivariant operators in the direct-product space, while non-linear invariant scalar routing controls structured gating over matrix units, directional propagation, attention, and environment-embedded context interactions. We further construct MP-HAM-110K, a large-scale Hamiltonian dataset comprising more than 110,000 material structures covering 68 elements with both Hamiltonian and wavefunction data. Experiments on MP-HAM-110K and Materials-HAM-SOC demonstrate that DP-Learner dramatically improves Hamiltonian prediction accuracy and inference efficiency over the state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [51] [Towards Multiscale Molecular Representation Learning through Geometry-Based Fragmentation for Polymer Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07998)
*Makoto Takamoto,Chaitanya Sampara,Viktor Zaverkin,Daniel Oñoro-Rubio,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将分子碎片化（molecular fragmentation）建模为受半径约束的 k-中心（k-center）式优化问题，提出不依赖预设化学规则、基于几何信息的碎片划分方法，并构建多尺度碎片注意力机制与多模态学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子子结构对理化性质贡献不均，碎片化表征可引入有化学意义的归纳偏置；但现有基于碎片的方法多依赖预设化学规则，对未见结构可能产生次优碎片化，且忽视多尺度分子表征的重要性。

Method: 将碎片化表述为受半径约束的优化问题（受 k-中心形式启发，簇数由半径决定而非预先固定），提出基于几何的碎片化方法，可对任意分子或材料结构进行划分并灵活控制碎片尺度；在此基础上构建融合多尺度结构信息的几何碎片注意力机制（geometric fragment-based attention），并整合 p-SMILES 表征与三维构象信息构建多模态机器学习模型。

Result: 在基准聚合物数据集（polymer dataset）上的实验表明，引入三维构象信息后，多个性质的预测精度相较仅使用 p-SMILES 表征的模型有所提升；可解释人工智能（explainable AI）分析进一步揭示了所学表征的物理相关性。

Conclusion: 基于几何的半径约束碎片化为摆脱预设化学规则、实现多尺度与尺度自适应建模提供了可行途径，且多模态融合三维构象信息可提升性质预测表现，其表征具有一定物理可解释性。

Abstract: It is well established that molecular substructures can contribute unequally to chemical and physical properties, with specific motifs or side chains often playing dominant roles. Fragment-based representations have therefore emerged as an effective strategy for incorporating chemically meaningful inductive biases, as exemplified by molecular fingerprints and fragment-based attention mechanisms. However, many existing fragmentation-based machine learning methods rely on predefined chemical rules, which may yield suboptimal fragmentation for previously unseen or chemically unfamiliar structures and often overlook the importance of multiscale molecular representations. To address these limitations, we formulate molecular fragmentation as a radius-constrained optimization problem inspired by the k-center formulation, where the number of clusters is not fixed a priori but emerges from the specified radius, and propose a geometry-based fragmentation method that partitions arbitrary molecular or material structures using largely geometric information, without relying on predefined chemical fragmentation rules. The method provides flexible control over fragment size, enabling the construction of multiscale fragment representations for scale-adaptive modeling. Building on this framework, we develop a geometric fragment-based attention mechanism that integrates multiscale structural information and incorporate it into a multimodal machine learning model combining p-SMILES representations with three-dimensional (3D) conformational information. Experiments on a benchmark polymer dataset show that incorporating 3D conformational information improves prediction accuracy for several properties over models based exclusively on p-SMILES representations. Explainable artificial intelligence analyses further provide insights into the physical relevance of the learned representations.

</details>

### [52] [Noether's Second Theorem and the Pareto Front Structure in Multi-Criteria Variational Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06920)
*Evgeny Nikulchev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文建立了诺特定理（Noether's theorem）第二定理与多准则变分问题（multi-criteria variational problems）帕累托前沿（Pareto front）结构之间的严格联系：在共同规范李群（gauge Lie group）下不变的一族泛函，其帕累托前沿的正则部分包含于加权欧拉–拉格朗日方程（weighted Euler–Lagrange equations）解之并集，且帕累托最优截面上诺特流（Noether currents）的组合守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为非线性动力学与控制中的多准则优化（multi-criteria optimization）提供解析理论基础，借助对称性（symmetry）与守恒律（conservation law）刻画帕累托前沿的结构，而非仅依赖数值多目标算法。

Method: 方法上，作者证明规范对称性通过比安基型恒等式（Bianchi-type identities）对拉格朗日乘子（Lagrange multipliers）施加额外约束、降低可容许权重空间的维数，并据此导出两种实用途径：规范固定（gauge fixing）降维与带惩罚项（penalty terms）的标量化（scalarization）。理论在三个经典问题上验证：双准则线性二次调节器（linear-quadratic regulator）、含极小极大准则的模型预测控制（model predictive control）问题，以及带控制约束的双积分器（double integrator）。

Result: 结果表明，三个算例中帕累托前沿均满足精确守恒律，且其解析结构可由问题对称性恢复；规范函数（gauge functions，即零拉格朗日量 null Lagrangians）可在动力学破坏对称性时恢复不变量，控制约束（control constraints）不破坏底层守恒律而仅限制可容许集；所提方法得到的帕累托前沿与理论曲线偏差极小，优于已知多目标算法。

Conclusion: 结论认为，诺特第二定理为多准则变分问题中帕累托前沿的解析刻画提供了新基础，并显示出在非线性动力学与控制中进行解析化多准则优化的潜力。

Abstract: This paper establishes a rigorous connection between Noether's second theorem and the structure of Pareto fronts in multi-criteria variational problems. We prove that for a family of functionals invariant under a common gauge Lie group, the regular part of the Pareto front is contained in the union of solutions to weighted Euler--Lagrange equations, and that on Pareto-optimal sections, combinations of Noether currents are conserved. Gauge symmetries are shown to impose additional constraints on the Lagrange multipliers via Bianchi-type identities, reducing the dimension of the admissible weight space. Two practical approaches are derived from this result: dimensionality reduction via gauge fixing and scalarization with penalty terms. The theory is validated on three classical problems: the linear-quadratic regulator with two criteria, a model predictive control problem with a minimax criterion, and the double integrator with control constraints. In each case, the Pareto front is shown to satisfy an exact conservation law, and the analytical structure of the front is recovered from the symmetries of the problem. The results demonstrate that gauge functions (null Lagrangians) can restore invariants even when the dynamics break the symmetry, and that control constraints do not destroy the underlying conservation laws but merely restrict the admissible set. The proposed approach yields a Pareto front with minimal deviation from the theoretical curve, outperforming known multi-objective algorithms, and provides a new foundation for the analytical treatment of multi-criteria optimization in nonlinear dynamics and control.

</details>


### [53] [Quantifying the remaining search space in analogue optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07968)
*Jona Nagerl,Younes Naceur,Natalia G. Berloff*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文提出一种确定性的、针对具体实例的上界，用于界定能量优于给定返回态（超出指定裕度）的二进制构型数量，并结合半定能量区间构成对改进幅度与多重性的互补性证书（complementary certificate），框架还可推广至连续相空间的Haar体积界与构型空间区域分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试模拟优化器（analogue optimiser）不仅需认证返回能量与最优值的接近程度，还需知晓可能残留多少更优构型；而半定松弛（semidefinite relaxation）虽给出基态能量与可观测量的严格界，却几乎不揭示残余搜索空间的大小。

Method: 将变分-Chernoff约化（variational-Chernoff reduction）与所得配分函数（partition function）的消除界（elimination bound）相耦合，构造二进制构型数的确定性上界；并推广该框架以获得剩余连续相空间Haar体积的界，进而分辨二进制与连续两种情形下的构型空间区域。

Result: 与精确枚举（exact enumeration）及独立Haar采样的比较在小规模实例上与所给界一致；在N=1000的数值实验中，即使最大的族中位数界也将潜在改进的二进制构型限制在全构型空间的10^{-144}以下，相应Haar体积的最大上界约为10^{-499}量级。

Conclusion: 所提证书可同时刻画任何可能改进的幅度与多重性，为模拟优化器的原生性能评估提供严格的残余搜索空间界，并适用于二进制与连续两类设定。

Abstract: Benchmarking analogue optimisers requires certifying not only how close a returned energy is to the optimum, but also how many better configurations may remain. Semidefinite relaxations provide rigorous bounds on the ground-state energy and selected observables, yet reveal little about the size of this residual search space. We introduce a deterministic, instance-specific upper bound on the number of binary configurations whose energies improve upon a returned state by a prescribed margin. Combined with a semidefinite energy interval, this yields a complementary certificate of both the magnitude and multiplicity of any possible improvement. To judge an analogue optimiser's native performance, we provide a generalisation of the framework to obtain bounds on the Haar volume of the remaining continuous phase-space. To further characterise the remaining search space, we extend our bounds to resolve regions in configuration space in both the binary and continuous setting. Our approach couples a variational-Chernoff reduction with an elimination bound of the resulting partition function. Comparisons with exact enumeration and independent Haar sampling are consistent with the bounds on small instances. In numerical experiments at $N=1000$, even the largest family-median bound restricts the potentially improving binary configurations to less than $10^{-144}$ of the full configuration space, while the largest upper bound on the corresponding Haar volume is of order $10^{-499}$.

</details>

Motivation: 研究动机在于将随机复金兹堡-朗道动力学的重整化（renormalisation）与解的性质推广到谐振子情形及完整的空间亚临界区间，并与纯耗散（purely dissipative）模型建立联系。

Conclusion: 复金兹堡-朗道动力学与纯耗散复Φ^4模型在统计意义下等价，但等价关系沿复时间轴（complex axis of time）成立。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct the complex Ginzburg-Landau dynamic associated with the harmonic oscillator, with a random force which is white in time and covers the full subcritical range in space. We are able to renormalise the non-linearity using the same constant as the one used to renormalise the problem associated to the Laplacian. By an extension of the problem to complex times, we are able to prove that the solution of the complex Ginzburg-Landau is equal in law to the solution of the purely dissipative case, i.e. the associated complex $Φ^4$ model, but along a complex axis of time.

</details>


### [55] [The singular Gaussian scaling limit for a tagged particle in the infinite-dimensional Dyson model at $β=2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08365)
*Hirofumi Osada,Kohei Suzuki*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文在逆温度 β=2、以 sine$_2$ 点过程为初值的无限维 Dyson 布朗运动中，对被标记粒子的涨落证明了对数型中心极限定理，涨落阶为 √log t，并将其尺度极限识别为对应零 Hurst 参数的奇异高斯场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典短程相互作用粒子系统的涨落呈多项式阶，而长程对数排斥可能带来更强的刚性（rigidity），需刻画这种刚性下被标记粒子涨落的真实阶数与极限规律，并寻找奇异高斯场（singular Gaussian field）的具体实现途径。

Method: 研究对象为无限维 Dyson 布朗运动（infinite-dimensional Dyson Brownian motion），设定逆温度（inverse temperature）β=2，初始分布取 sine$_2$ 点过程（sine$_2$ point process）；在此基础上对被标记粒子（tagged particle）的涨落建立对数中心极限定理（logarithmic central limit theorem），并分析其尺度极限。

Result: 被标记粒子的涨落阶为 √log t，属亚扩散（subdiffusive）行为，显著慢于经典短程相互作用系统的多项式涨落，体现了长程对数排斥所致刚性；其尺度极限（scaling limit）被识别为零 Hurst 参数（zero-Hurst parameter）对应的奇异高斯场。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了一种由被标记粒子尺度极限实现的奇异高斯场，兑现了 Dürr、Goldstein 与 Lebowitz（Comm. Pure Appl. Math. 38 (1985)）提出的构造设想，并揭示对数排斥刚性对涨落阶数的定量影响。

Abstract: We prove a logarithmic central limit theorem for a tagged particle in infinite-dimensional Dyson Brownian motion at inverse temperature $β=2$, starting from the sine$_2$ point process. The fluctuation is of order $\sqrt{\log t}$, which is subdiffusive and substantially slower than the polynomial fluctuations of classical short-range interacting particle systems, reflecting the rigidity induced by long-range logarithmic repulsion. We further identify the scaling limit as the singular Gaussian field corresponding to the zero-Hurst parameter, which provides a realisation of the singular Gaussian field as a scaling limit of a tagged particle raised by Dürr, Goldstein and Lebowitz (Comm. Pure Appl. Math. 38 (1985)).

</details>


### [56] [A Simple Approach to the Complexity of Mean Field Ising Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08374)
*Benjamin McKenna,Qiang Wu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种计算离散自旋玻璃（discrete spin glasses）中局部最优（local optima）与带隙态（gapped states）的简单方法，并以此计算混合伊辛自旋玻璃与 Ghatak-Sherrington 模型的退火复杂度（annealed complexity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球形自旋玻璃（spherical spin glasses）的几何已借助 Kac-Rice 公式得到较好理解，但该公式不直接适用于离散自旋系统，因此伊辛自旋玻璃（Ising spin glasses）几何的数学认识仍很有限。

Method: 针对一大类离散自旋玻璃，构造一个计数局部最优与带隙态的简单方法；并将其应用于含外场等势项的混合伊辛自旋玻璃以及 Ghatak-Sherrington 模型，同时计算能量约束下的复杂度（energy-constrained complexity）。

Result: 所计算的退火复杂度复现了数学与物理文献中若干已有结果作为特例；对纯伊辛自旋玻璃的带隙态，发现一个类似球形情形的临界能量阈值（critical energy threshold）。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于建立带隙态的一种无序持久性（disorder persistence），并识别出 3-spin 与 4-spin 自旋玻璃之间的转变。

Abstract: The geometry of spherical spin glasses is relatively well understood, thanks in large part to the Kac-Rice formula. However, this formula does not directly apply to discrete spin systems, and much less is known mathematically about the geometry of Ising spin glasses. In this paper, we present a simple method for counting local optima and gapped states in a broad class of discrete spin glasses. To illustrate its scope, we compute the annealed complexities ("configurational entropies," in the physics language) of mixed Ising spin glasses with potentials, such as external fields, and of the Ghatak-Sherrington model. These computations recover several existing results in the mathematics and physics literature as special cases. We can also compute energy-constrained complexity, finding a critical energy threshold analogous to the spherical case, but for gapped states of pure Ising spin glasses. Finally, we use our method to establish a form of disorder persistence for gapped states, identifying a transition between $3$-spin and $4$-spin glasses.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典湍流理论中 Richardson 色散律与反常耗散通常依赖于特定的流动假设，本文旨在证明其在更一般的随机 Hölder 连续速度场框架下依然成立，从而放宽对速度场正则性与可积性的要求。

Method: 研究对象为具有受控扫掠（controlled sweeping）性质的随机 Hölder 连续速度矢量场，通过数学分析方法证明粒子间距随时间的期望平方标度律及示踪物（tracer）的反常耗散性质。

Result: 在该速度场类中，粒子分离的均方随时间呈立方增长，即 E R_t^2 ~ t^3，符合 Richardson 色散律（Richardson dispersion law）；同时相应示踪物表现出反常耗散（anomalous dissipation）。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Richardson 色散与反常耗散可在较弱的随机 Hölder 连续速度场假设及受控扫掠条件下严格建立，拓展了经典结果的适用范围。

Abstract: The present paper establishes the Richardson dispersion law, predicting the particle separation with time according to $\E R_t^2 \sim t^3$, as well as anomalous dissipation for the associated tracer, for a random Hölder continuous velocity vectorfield with controlled sweeping.

</details>


### [58] [Hypocoercivity for a thermostatted Boltzmann equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07999)
*Josephine Evans,Alexander Pilakoutas*

Main category: math.AP

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热浴温度在空间中变化会在气体中驱动非平衡能量流（nonequilibrium energy current），此前的适定性与收敛性结果多针对等温或线性化情形，缺乏对非线性自碰撞与空间非均匀热浴耦合的系统分析。

Method: 对具有各向同性截断核（isotropic cutoff kernel）的麦克斯韦分子（Maxwell molecules）与硬势（hard potentials）情形，在统一的高斯型上界与高斯矩界初值类中建立估计，并给出自碰撞相对于热浴碰撞须足够弱的定量判据；估计对固定正温度上下界之间的热浴分布一致成立。

Result: 证明方程整体适定（global well-posedness）并指数收敛到同一稳态；对三维环面上任意非恒定热浴分布，稳态均不可能为局部麦克斯韦分布（local Maxwellian）。此外，仅与热浴碰撞时相对熵（relative entropy）单调不增；加入自碰撞后，任意小扰动可使相对熵初期增大，随后仍指数回落。

Conclusion: 空间非均匀热浴驱动的玻尔兹曼气体具有唯一非平衡稳态且吸引性稳健，但相对熵不再单调，表明耗散结构因自碰撞项而出现短暂的非单调行为。

Abstract: We study the Boltzmann equation for a gas on the three-dimensional torus. Its particles collide with one another and with a thermal reservoir whose temperature varies in space. The reservoir heats the gas in some regions and cools it in others, driving a nonequilibrium energy current. For the isotropic cutoff kernel, with Maxwell molecules or hard potentials, we prove global well-posedness and exponential convergence to the same stationary state for initial data with a common Gaussian upper bound and Gaussian moment bound. These bounds determine how weak self collisions must be relative to bath collisions. The estimates are uniform over bath profiles between fixed positive temperature bounds. For any nonconstant reservoir profile on the three-dimensional torus, this stationary state cannot be a local Maxwellian. When particles collide only with the bath, relative entropy to the stationary state cannot increase. Once self collisions are added, arbitrarily small perturbations can initially increase it, even though they later return exponentially.

### [59] [Critical collapse remains discretely self-similar at large D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07007)

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在任意有限维 D>3 的 Einstein-Klein-Gordon 模型中，临界黑洞形成时空解呈现离散自相似性（discrete self-similarity），其特征回响周期（echoing period）Delta 在无穷维极限下仍保持有限，并给出 Delta=1.6439+10.37/D 的大维展开拟合及显式大 D 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物质在黑洞形成阈值处坍缩时，时空通常涌现出连续或离散对称性，其形式依赖物质内容；本文旨在厘清 Einstein-Klein-Gordon 模型中离散自相似性的回响周期随维数 D 的演化，特别是无穷维极限行为，以检验近期关于该极限的猜想。

Method: 结合解析（analytical）与数值（numerical）方法，将计算推至 D=200 的高维情形，并构造显式的大 D 模型（large-D model）用于分析无穷维极限。

Result: 得到拟合关系 Delta=1.6439+10.37/D-O(1/D^2)；据此预测当 D→∞ 时 Delta 收敛为有限值，与近期提出的猜想（认为 Delta 在无穷维发散或消失）相反。

Conclusion: Einstein-Klein-Gordon 临界解的离散自相似回响周期在无穷维极限下保持有限，而非按近期猜想发散；该结论由高至 D=200 的数值与解析结果及显式大 D 模型共同支持。

Abstract: When matter collapses at the threshold of black hole formation spacetime typically has an emergent continuous or discrete symmetry, depending on the matter content. For the Einstein-Klein-Gordon model in any finite dimension D>3 the associated critical spacetime solutions exhibit discrete self-similarity, characterized by the echoing period Delta. Combining analytical and numerical results with dimensions up to D=200 we predict Delta remains finite at infinite dimension D, contrary to a recent conjecture. We find the fit Delta=1.6439+10.37/D-O(1/D^2) and provide an explicit large-D model.

</details>


### [60] [Analytic Marginalization Over Calibration Uncertainties in Gravitational Wave Inference and Application to Black Hole Ringdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07023)
*David Dzingeleski,Aaron Zimmerman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作将频域校准边缘化（calibration marginalization）推广至时域并采用基样条（basis splines）实现解析边缘化，用于黑洞铃宕（ringdown）时域分析。对 GW250114 仍发现双模式强证据；对 GW250207 纳入校准效应后铃宕推断与全信号一致，但无第二模式证据，相对论偏离约束不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器校准存在不确定性，是引力波推断（gravitational wave inference）中的系统误差来源之一。标准处理多在频域进行，而时域铃宕分析需要相应的时域校准边缘化方法。

Accurate inference of these properties is crucial, as many astrophysical, nuclear, and multimessenger studies rely on gravitational wave constraints. This framework provides a robust, systematic approach for neutron star parameter estimation, enabling more reliable inference for both current and future gravitational wave observations.

The balance requires the anisotropic stress to decay as $a^{-2}$, $a$ being the cosmological scale factor, which we combine with the gauge-field continuity equation. Abstract: In this work, we revisit the tidal Love numbers (TLNs) of several classes of exotic compact objects (ECOs) using the recently derived full perturbed junction conditions. Abstract: This thesis investigates the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe within modified gravity theories, with particular emphasis on symmetric teleparallel gravity and its extensions, including $f(Q)$, $f(Q, B)$, and $f(Q, φ)$ gravity. It explores the cosmological dynamics of these theories using dynamical systems analysis, stability techniques, and the Noether symmetry approach. The study examines the existence and stability of cosmological critical points, background evolution, and linear cosmological perturbations to understand the different phases of cosmic evolution. It also investigates the role of observational constraints and Big Bang nucleosynthesis in assessing the viability of modified gravity models. Furthermore, the application of Noether symmetries provides a systematic approach to identifying viable functional forms and deriving exact cosmological solutions. Overall, this thesis examines the theoretical and cosmological viability of modified teleparallel gravity as an alternative framework for describing the observed accelerated expansion of the Universe.

Abstract: Detecting single gravitons is possible through the extraction of discrete energy quanta from passing gravitational waves. For decades, this was considered hopeless, with estimates suggesting a detector the size of Jupiter. Here, I explain how these earlier estimates relied on the highly inefficient electron ionization process, while the absorption through the full mass quadrupole of a macroscopic system can bring graviton detection within experimental reach. Operating in the LIGO frequency band solves the challenges of graviton sources and noise suppression, allowing gravitons to be extracted from known passing gravitational waves and detection events to be heralded by classical gravitational-wave observations. Combined with the ability to resolve single energy quanta in macroscopic systems, such as in quantum acoustics, this opens a realistic path toward graviton detection. I also discuss the general limitations of particle detection in establishing an underlying quantum theory, illustrated by an energy-nonconserving semiclassical alternative. Nevertheless, the semiclassical alternative is not standard classical wave physics, and extracting $E=\hbarω$ from a passing wave constitutes bona fide particle detection. This opens the way not only to the first detection of individual gravitons, but also to experimentally probing linearized quantum gravity and the graviton counting statistics of gravitational waves.

</details>

The power spectrum is computed with the same response and Gaussian initial state, thereby fixing the statistical normalization. A complementary local freeze-out reduction makes the interaction coefficients and two geometric shape contributions explicit. Comparison along an evolving near-attractor trajectory indicates that unequal-time finite-inertia propagation is crucial for quantitatively predicting the intrinsic bispectrum. The analysis also derives the slow-roll-suppressed nonlinear $δN$ conversion, together with the Arnowitt--Deser--Misner constraints and cubic vertices that characterize the direct gravitational effects of NMDC. Abstract: We present a self-contained coordinate-matrix formulation of General Relativity in the harmonic gauge, providing a compact, index-free representation of the gravitational field equations within the algebra of $4\times 4$ real matrices $M_4$. By defining musical isomorphism operators ($\sharp, \flat$), metric adjoints ($\dagger$), and matrix-valued derivative vectors ($\bm{\mathcal{B}}, \bm{\mathcal{C}}, \bm{\mathcal{D}}^\sharp$), the Ricci tensor is recast into an algebraic matrix structure dominated by the Laplace--Beltrami operator $\Box_{\mathrm{LB}} \coloneqq \operatorname{tr}(\mathcal{G}^\sharp \partial^2)$, where $\partial^2 \coloneqq \bm{\partial}\bm{\partial}^T$ represents the matrix Hessian operator of second partial derivatives. We derive the explicit algebraic relation connecting $\bar{\mathcal{T}}$ with the classical Landau--Lifshitz energy-momentum complex $\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{LL}}$, and express the associated local conservation law in a simple matrix divergence form, $\bm{\partial}^T \left((-g)(\mathcal{T}^{\sharp} + \mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{LL}}^{\sharp})\right) = \bm{0}^T$. Abstract: We examine the field produced by a stationary current loop encircling a static Bocharova--Bronnikov--Melnikov--Bekenstein (BBMB) black hole and the response of charged test particles. From the dipole projection of Maxwell's equations, we obtain closed-form interior and exterior radial functions, and we test them against a multipole expansion of the localized source whose radial functions are also elementary. Every horizon-regular mode vanishes at the degenerate horizon $r_+=M$, so the magnetic flux through the horizon is zero. For a loop at $r_0=10M$, the dipole errors in the equatorial potential and polar field remain below $10\%$ for $r\lesssim0.42\,r_0$ and $r\gtrsim3.02\,r_0$, but reach about $40\%$ and $80\%$ close to the loop. Using the resulting potential, we study the effective potential, circular orbits, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), a binding-energy proxy, and epicyclic frequencies. Because the dipole field reverses its equatorial direction across the loop, a positive magnetic coupling $\mathcal{B}$ acts oppositely on the two sides. Above $\mathcal{B}_c\simeq0.0119$, the prograde radially stable orbits split into two bands separated by an unstable gap. At $\mathcal{B}=0.2$, the ISCO moves from $4M$ to $2.97M$, the exterior band begins at $45.46M$, and the ISCO binding-energy proxy grows from $8.14\%$ to $23.39\%$, compared with $5.72\%$ for neutral particles around a Schwarzschild black hole. For negative coupling, exterior orbits can be radially stable but vertically unstable. Higher multipoles preserve the large-coupling trends but shift the near-loop values appreciably, so the near-loop results apply to the projected source rather than to a thin ring.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate whether absorption can enhance morphological differences between images of different spacetimes, particularly when their critical impact parameters are similar. Using a phenomenological thick-accretion-flow model with an effective absorption coefficient $χ_0$, we compare Schwarzschild black-hole images with those of Bardeen, Hayward, and Ayón-Beato--García black holes. In the face-on Schwarzschild--Bardeen comparison with $g/M=0.5$, the dominant brightness shifts from a narrow lensed feature to a broader outer feature at $χ_0M\simeq$0.585 and 0.717, respectively. Near the first transition, the integrated difference between independently normalized images increases by $23.5$% on a common physical grid and by $46.6$% after rescaling by the respective critical impact parameters. Enhancement occurs across all three regular-black-hole families and over a broad range of the sampled flow parameters and viewing inclinations, although its magnitude and occurrence depend on the prescribed flow. Asynchronous brightness-peak transitions alone are insufficient to guarantee enhancement. These results suggest that the opacity dependence of image morphology may provide information complementary to the critical-curve scale, potentially helping distinguish spacetimes with similar characteristic shadow sizes. Assessing this possibility observationally requires physically consistent, frequency-dependent radiative transfer.

We find that increasing $p_{\mathrm{eff}}$ systematically shifts orbital radii outward and modifies the rational parameter $q$. Within the test-particle and quadrupole approximations, we estimate GW signals from an extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) system at a luminosity distance of $D_L=8000\,\mathrm{pc}$. The characteristic strains reach $\sim10^{-18}$ in the mHz band, partially overlapping the nominal LISA sensitivity range. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在为原初黑洞建立一个受控、局域且准静态的热力学描述，并厘清其平衡态热产生与密度扰动坍缩两种形成机制的主次关系，同时刻画形成后的蒸发-吸积演化。

Method: 将局部留数规定与引力动力学的热力学化相结合：对非极端球对称视界，证明亚纯一形式（meromorphic one-form）的正留数直接确定逆霍金温度（inverse Hawking temperature），并由第一定律（first law of thermodynamics）在标准归一化下复原结果；形成后演化采用细致平衡质量定律（detailed-balance mass law）与自由能梯度流（free-energy gradient flow）表述。

Result: 在标准归一化下由第一定律得到标准施瓦西温度（Schwarzschild temperature）与贝肯斯坦-霍金熵（Bekenstein-Hawking entropy）；所得熵定义离壳正则指数（off-shell canonical exponent），表明辐射主导宇宙中 PBH 的平衡态热产生被指数压低，标准密度扰动坍缩判据仍为主要形成机制；并证明广义热力学第二定律（generalized second law of thermodynamics）成立、移动质量充当瞬时蒸发-吸积阈值，最后给出留数作小解析形变时温度、熵、热鞍点（thermal saddle）与成核指数（nucleation exponent）的一阶修正。

Conclusion: 该框架自洽地把留数表述与黑洞热力学及形成后动力学统一起来，支持密度扰动坍缩作为主导形成通道，并将 PBH 演化表示为以质量为瞬时阈值的自由能梯度流；同时还给出解析形变下各热力学量的一阶响应。

Abstract: We establish a controlled, local, and quasi-stationary thermodynamic framework for neutral, nonrotating primordial black holes (PBHs) by combining a local residue prescription with the thermodynamicization of gravitational dynamics. For a nonextremal spherical horizon, we demonstrate that the positive residue of a meromorphic one-form directly determines the inverse Hawking temperature. Applying the first law of thermodynamics recovers the standard Schwarzschild temperature and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy under standard normalization. The resulting entropy defines an off-shell canonical exponent, which reveals that equilibrium thermal production of PBHs is exponentially suppressed in a radiation-dominated universe, thereby confirming that the standard density-perturbation collapse criterion remains the primary formation mechanism. For post-formation evolution, we adopt a detailed-balance mass law, prove the validity of the generalized second law of thermodynamics, and express the evolution dynamics as a free-energy gradient flow where the moving mass acts as an instantaneous evaporation-accretion threshold. Finally, we derive explicit first-order shifts for the temperature, entropy, thermal saddle, and nucleation exponent under small analytic deformations of the residue.

</details>


### [79] [An Orbital Curvature for ISCOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07875)
*Poulami Mukherjee,Chandrasekhar Bhamidipati,Sudipta Mukherji*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文利用黑洞时空测地线方程中圆轨道作为相图不动点的事实，构建了一种“轨道几何（orbital geometry）”，以轨道能量、角动量和角速度作为状态变量，通过响应函数及其标量曲率刻画圆轨道族的稳定性结构，并将最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）识别为曲率奇异点。该框架在四维Schwarzschild与五维Schwarzschild-AdS时空中的实例得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆轨道作为测地线方程相图的不动点，其稳定性结构缺乏统一的几何刻画方式；本文旨在建立一个能统一描述圆轨道稳定性与ISCO临界性的几何框架。

Method: 以轨道能量、角动量和角速度构成状态空间，引入响应函数（response functions）描述圆轨道对黑洞背景及自身角运动变化的反应，并计算关联的标量曲率（scalar curvature）作为几何诊断量。
