Conclusion: 轨道响应几何为刻画圆轨道稳定性与ISCO临界性提供了统一的几何框架。

Abstract: Building on the fact that circular orbits are the fixed points of the phase portraits arising from the geodesic equations on a black hole spacetime, we develop a general orbital geometry, which captures the stability structure of orbits. The construction uses the orbital energy, angular momentum and angular velocity as state space variables, with response functions encoding how the circular orbits react to changes in the black hole background and in their angular motion. The associated scalar curvature provides a geometric diagnostic of certain distinguished points of the circular orbit family, including, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO), where it becomes singular. Across the ISCO, the relevant orbital response functions change sign as the system passes from the stable to the unstable circular orbit branch, and typically vanish at marginal stability. We illustrate the framework for Schwarzschild black holes in asymptotically flat four dimensions, and five dimensional Schwarzschild AdS spacetimes. In the four-dimensional case, the ISCO is associated with a degeneration of the energy response sector, while in the five dimensional AdS case it is detected through the angular response. The AdS example also exhibits a separate curvature singularity at a point which coincides with the minimum temperature of the black hole background. These results show that the orbital response geometry provides a unified geometric framework for characterizing the stability of circular orbits and criticality of ISCOs.

</details>


### [80] [Can $n_s$ hint at a parity-odd sector of gravity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07957)
*Christian Dioguardi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在度量-仿射引力（metric-affine gravity）框架下，研究含非最小耦合标量场的慢滚暴胀（slow-roll inflation）。对畸变（distortion）积分消去后，动能项可产生有限场值处的极点（poles），二阶极点对应α-吸引子（α-attractors），三阶极点需宇称奇（parity-odd）扇区，给出可调的张标比与谱指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索度量-仿射引力中射影不变（projectively invariant）理论对暴胀（inflation）标量扇区的修正，特别是畸变场消去后产生的动能项极点如何影响暴胀预言，并考察能否得到区别于标准α-吸引子的可观测信号。

Method: 采用最多含畸变二次项、曲率线性项、无非传播挠率（torsion）与非度性（non-metricity）的一般射影不变理论，非最小耦合标量场，聚焦ξ-吸引子（ξ-attractors）类（由单一函数f(φ)确定标量扇区）。积分消去非动力学畸变，得到动能修正k(φ)∝f′(φ)²乘以f(φ)的多项式之比，对极点分类并推导p=2、p=3极点的有效耦合条件，再数值分析暴胀（如f(φ)∝φ²）。

Result: 二阶极点实现α-吸引子，α由留数（residue）确定，且在宇称偶（parity-even）扇区已可得；三阶极点需宇称奇扇区，当宇称奇二次耦合为零时动能函数分裂为两个最多二阶极点的独立项。三阶极点暴胀给出r∝N*^(-3/2)（幅度可调）与n_s≈1−3/(2N*)，谱指数略大于α-吸引子预言且处于ACT、SPT与DESI联合数据偏好区域中心。远离极点时还发现普适吸引子——反线性平台（inverse-linear plateau），n_s≈1−4/(3N*)、r∝N*^(-4/3)，可由BICEP/Keck检验。

Conclusion: 度量-仿射引力中畸变诱导的动能极点提供了实现α-吸引子及三阶极点暴胀的机制，后者与当前ACT/SPT/DESI数据相符；远离极点的反线性平台是可被BICEP/Keck检验的另一普适预言。

Abstract: We study slow-roll inflation in the context of metric-affine gravity. We consider the most general projectively invariant metric-affine theory at most quadratic in the distortion and linear in curvature with no propagating torsion and non-metricity and couple it non-minimally to a scalar field. We focus on the class of $ξ$-attractors, in which a single function $f(φ)$ fixes the whole scalar sector. Integrating out the non-dynamical distortion generates a correction to the kinetic term $k(φ)$ which is $f'(φ)^2$ times a ratio of polynomials in $f(φ)$. The new kinetic term can develop poles at finite field values, where the distortion field equations degenerate. We classify the poles and derive the conditions on the effective couplings for poles of order $p=2$ and $p=3$, the maximal order allowed in this setting. We then proceed to study inflation. Second-order poles realize $α$-attractors with $α$ fixed by the residue and are already available in the parity-even sector of the theory. Third-order poles instead require the parity-odd sector: whenever the parity-odd quadratic couplings vanish, the kinetic function splits into two independent terms with poles of order at most $p=2$. Third-order pole-inflation, as we confirm numerically for $f(φ)\proptoφ^2$, yields $r\propto N_*^{-3/2}$ with a tunable amplitude and $n_s\simeq 1-\tfrac{3}{2N_*}$, a spectral index mildly larger than the $α$-attractor prediction and well centered in the region preferred by the current combination of ACT, SPT and DESI data. Moreover, when inflation happens far away from the pole we also find a further universal attractor, an inverse-linear plateau, with $n_s \simeq 1-\frac{4}{3N_*}$ and $r\propto N_*^{-4/3}$, testable with the forthcoming BICEP/Keck observations.

</details>

*Hassan Hassanabadi,Soroush Zare*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有暗物质尖峰研究多采用无限延展或边界非光滑的密度分布，难以在无表面层（surface layer）条件下与外部Schwarzschild真空严格匹配，且对有限支撑与质量再分布如何影响黑洞观测缺乏受控模型。本文旨在建立边界正则、总质量可控的有限尖峰平衡构型以填补这一空白。

Result: 所得时空由内部真空间隙、零径向压强的物质区与外部Schwarzschild真空三部分构成。存在充分性界限，保证内边缘位于Schwarzschild边缘稳定半径（marginal-stability radius）及以外时上述性质成立，并确保无质量标量势为正。在共同质量预算下的比较显示，边界正则性与径向聚集度共同影响引力响应，阴影与光线偏折依赖于整体红移与质量标定。

Conclusion: 该平滑截断剖面为研究有限支撑与质量再分布如何影响黑洞观测量提供了受控的平衡模型；但锥度尺度、形成机制与集体稳定性仍需额外物理输入才能确定。

Abstract: We introduce a finite dark matter spike around a static, spherically symmetric black hole, with a general power-law index and an Einstein-cluster stress tensor. Smooth inner depletion and outer truncation ensure that the density and its first radial derivative vanish at both boundaries, while a single normalization preserves the prescribed exterior mass. The resulting spacetime contains an inner vacuum gap, a matter region with vanishing radial pressure, and an outer Schwarzschild vacuum. We derive the enclosed mass, density maximum, local logarithmic slope, and matched metric without surface layers, including exact polynomial-root representations of the matter-region metric for spike indices $7/3$ and $3/2$. Energy conditions and radial stability of the constituent circular orbits constrain the admissible configurations. A sufficient bound guarantees these properties for an inner edge at or beyond the Schwarzschild marginal-stability radius and also ensures positivity of the massless-scalar potential. Comparisons at a common mass budget examine how boundary regularity and radial concentration jointly affect the gravitational response. Shadows and light deflection also depend on the global redshift and mass calibration. Scalar waves matched across the three regions with one infinity-normalized time coordinate distinguish local gap-redshift effects from propagation through the complete geometry. The smoothly truncated profile provides a controlled equilibrium model for investigating how finite support and mass redistribution affect black hole observables; its taper scales, formation, and collective stability require additional physical input.

</details>


### [82] [Parity-violating scalar-tensor theory revisited: complete basis up to $d=4$ and Hamiltonian analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08191)
*Yun-Chuan Zhang,Yu-Min Hu,Xian Gao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在导数阶 d=4 以内构造了宇称破坏（parity-violating）的一般协变标量-张量（GST）与空间协变引力（SCG）单项式的完备基，并给出两者的对应关系、分部积分关系及哈密顿约束分析。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在该框架内构造仅含三个自由度的宇称破坏标量-张量理论，需要 d≤4 的完备单项式基及对各高阶导数项传播自由度数目的约束分析。

Method: 完整列举各种导数划分下 d≤4 的宇称破坏 GST 与 SCG 单项式，利用分部积分（integration by parts）约化多项式型拉氏量，推导两套基之间的对应关系并检验其独立性，随后对含失效速度（lapse velocity）与外曲率时间导数（time derivative of extrinsic curvature）的单项式配合标准爱因斯坦-希尔伯特动能项和依赖失效的势项做哈密顿约束分析（Hamiltonian constraint analysis）。

Result: 每种描述在 d=3 有 1 个、d=4 有 13 个线性独立单项式；拉氏量层面得到 6 个分部积分关系，将多项式型拉氏量约化为 1 个 d=3 与 7 个 d=4 单项式（含一般系数函数）。7 个 d=4 SCG 单项式中 5 个既不含失效速度也不含外曲率时间导数；正则分支上，含失效速度项传播 4 个自由度，含外曲率速度项传播 6 个自由度（无论单独出现还是与失效速度项共存）。

Conclusion: 结果给出了该框架下构造 d≤4、含三个自由度的宇称破坏标量-张量理论的完备基，宇称破坏拉氏量由 6 个 GST 多项式张成：1 个 d=3 与 5 个 d=4 多项式。

Abstract: We construct complete bases of parity-violating generally covariant scalar-tensor (GST) and spatially covariant gravity (SCG) monomials up to derivative grade $d=4$. Including all derivative partitions, each description contains one $d=3$ and thirteen $d=4$ linearly independent monomials. At the level of Lagrangians, we find six integration-by-parts relations that reduce polynomial-type Lagrangians to combinations of one $d=3$ and seven $d=4$ monomials with general coefficient functions. We derive the correspondence between the two bases, retaining general coefficients and total derivatives, and establish their independence under integrations by parts. Among the seven $d=4$ SCG monomials, five contain neither the lapse velocity nor the time derivative of the extrinsic curvature. For the two remaining monomials, we perform a Hamiltonian constraint analysis with a standard Einstein-Hilbert kinetic term and a lapse-dependent potential. On the regular branches considered, the lapse-velocity term propagates four degrees of freedom (DOFs), while the extrinsic-curvature-velocity term propagates six DOFs, whether present alone or together with the lapse-velocity term. These results provide a complete basis for constructing parity-violating scalar-tensor theories with three DOFs up to $d=4$ within this setting, with the parity-violating Lagrangian spanned by six GST polynomials: one $d=3$ and five $d=4$ polynomials.

</details>


### [83] [Instabilities of Simpson-Visser black bounces sourced by a phantom scalar and nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08322)
*Kirill A. Bronnikov,Rustam Ibadov,Jutta Kunz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Simpson-Visser“黑洞-虫洞（black-bounce）”时空在球对称单极扰动下的线性稳定性，发现所有研究的时空均不稳定，并计算了指数增长率；同时揭示了Darboux变换在虫洞喉部处理中此前未被注意的非物理极点问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simpson-Visser度规统一描述正则黑洞（regular black holes）与虫洞（wormholes），其稳定性是理解该类时空物理合理性的关键问题，而此前针对虫洞情形的稳定性分析可能存在被忽视的技术缺陷。

Method: 以幻影标量场（phantom scalar field，含自相互作用势）与非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics, NED）磁场为源，将线性稳定性问题化为含有效势的类薛定谔方程（Schrödinger-like equation）的边值问题；针对虫洞喉部约2/z²壁形势导致Darboux变换产生非物理极点的问题，采用Cremona、Pizzocchero与Sarbach风格的规范不变（gauge-invariant）方法导出正则的主方程（master equation）。

Result: 所有考察的时空均不稳定：黑洞情形结果直接可得；虫洞情形下，喉半径可变模式始终存在，当a/(2M)≳4.98时存在两个实增长喉模式，在更小a时合并为复共轭对（振荡不稳定性）；并发现三个例外点（exceptional points）划分不同的扰动区域。此外，当a/(2M)从两侧趋近1时，主导的黑洞与虫洞不稳定模式趋于同一极限增长率Mκ≃0.10121。

Conclusion: Simpson-Visser黑洞与虫洞在单极扰动下普遍存在线性不稳定性；Darboux变换在虫洞喉部问题中的适用性受a/(2M)≲4.98限制，规范不变方法可规避该困难并给出正则的有效势描述。

Abstract: We study the linear stability of Simpson-Visser ``black-bounce'' space-times (regular black holes and wormholes) under spherically symmetric (monopole) perturbations, taking as a source a phantom scalar field with a self-interaction potential and a magnetic field within nonlinear electrodynamics (NED). The metric contains two parameters: the mass $M$ and the bounce parameter $a >0$, such that $a \leq 2M$ corresponds to black holes and $a > 2M$ to wormholes. The stability problem reduces to a boundary-value problem for a Schrödinger-like equation with a certain effective potential. All space-times under study are found to be unstable, and the corresponding exponential growth rates are calculated. For black holes the results are obtained without complications, while the wormhole stability problem faces difficulties that seem to be previously unnoticed. Thus, the Darboux transformation, used to remove the wall shaped $\approx 2/z^2$ in the effective potentials at wormhole throats ($z$ is the tortoise coordinate), creates nonphysical poles outside the throat for $a/(2M) \lesssim 4.98$. We circumvent this difficulty by using a gauge-invariant approach in the spirit of Cremona, Pizzocchero and Sarbach, leading to a master equation with a simple and regular potential. Its further analysis reveals unstable modes: the mode with a variable throat radius is always present; for $a/(2M) \gtrsim 4.98$ there are two real growing throat modes, which merge into a complex-conjugate pair (oscillatory instability) for smaller $a$. A further analysis reveals three exceptional points that separate ranges of $a/(2M)$ with different perturbation regimes. Furthermore, the leading unstable black-hole and wormhole modes approach the same limiting growth rate $Mκ\simeq 0.10121$ as $a/(2M)$ approaches unity from either side.

</details>


### [84] [When Can Pulsar Timing Distinguish a Propagation Mass from a Weak Longitudinal Background?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08382)
*Kun Zhou,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 弱纵向背景可在角响应上模仿引力波传播质量的信号。基于NANOGrav 15年数据的压缩高斯实验给出定量刻画：在虚警概率10^-3下，允许无质量背景含弱纵向成分会使最优拒绝功效至少损失16.29个百分点，而阵列由20颗脉冲星扩至40颗可回收至少7.62个百分点；此外质量截止可先被识别，其后在一频率单元内的位置才可分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波传播质量的探测依赖角响应特征，但弱纵向背景成分可能产生相似的角响应，从而构成混淆（ambiguity）。作者旨在量化这种简并性对质量测量与模型判别的影响。

Method: 构建基于NANOGrav 15年计时数据的压缩高斯实验（compressed Gaussian experiment），在不同纵向振幅、零质量与正质量背景下，考察虚警概率（false-alarm probability）固定时的最优拒绝功效（optimal rejection power）与质量识别功效。

Result: 虚警概率10^-3时，20脉冲星子阵列中允许弱纵向成分使指定源的最优拒绝功效至少下降16.29个百分点；同背景水平下扩至40颗脉冲星可回收至少7.62个百分点。低背景实验中，跨越连续纵向振幅范围与三个注入背景水平，某一检验对零质量的拒绝功效至少为97.38%（两个相距1.08%的质量任一），但对具体质量的识别功效至多1.584%。

Conclusion: 质量截止（cutoff）可在其于频率单元内的位置被分辨之前先被识别，即质量存在的可探测性与质量参数的精确定位具有不同的可分辨性层级。

Abstract: A weak longitudinal background can imitate the angular response of a gravitational-wave propagation mass. We quantify this ambiguity in a compressed Gaussian experiment constructed from the NANOGrav 15-year timing data. At a false-alarm probability of $10^{-3}$, allowing a weak longitudinal component in a massless background costs at least 16.29 percentage points of optimal rejection power for a specified source in a 20-pulsar subarray. Expanding the array to 40 pulsars at the same background level recovers at least 7.62 points. In a separate low-background experiment, we allow the same continuous range of longitudinal amplitudes at zero and positive mass. Across that range and three injected background levels, one test rejects zero mass with at least 97.38\% power at either of two masses separated by 1.08\%. With false assignment limited to $10^{-3}$ at the other mass, the power to identify either mass is at most 1.584\%. A cutoff can thus be identifiable before its position within a frequency cell can be resolved.

</details>


### [85] [Torsion talks only to spin: the CMB temperature as a test of energy exchange in Einstein-Cartan cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08422)
*Fernando Izaurieta,Samuel Lepe,Cristian Quinzacara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用宇宙微波背景（CMB）温度测量约束扭转（torsion）宇宙学中的能量交换分配问题：若能量交换"普适"分配给包括光在内的所有组分，则预测的T(z)偏低约7%–8.5%且无自由参数，与PKS 1830−211、CO吸收体及Planck星系团数据明显不符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转（torsion）宇宙学中当几何与多个物质组分交换能量时，究竟哪个组分获得能量尚无定论；作者检验由度量-仿射（metric-affine）宇宙学拟合DESI DR2所隐含的"普适"能量分配是否与实测CMB温度-红移关系相容。

Method: 依据Einstein-Cartan理论中各扇区的Noether恒等式（Noether identities）判定守恒性：能量-动量不守恒正比于该组分自身自旋（spin），故无自旋的最小耦合光应守恒。将"普适"分配模型的T(z)预言（要求光子保持黑体谱，如FIRAS所要求）与z=0.886处PKS 1830−211分子吸收测得的T、CO吸收体与Sunyaev-Zel'dovich星系团数据以及Planck星系团分 bin 进行对比。

Result: "普适"分配模型无自由参数地预言z≈0.9处背景温度比T0(1+z)低7%、z≳3处低8.5%；PKS 1830−211测得5.08±0.10 K而预言4.78 K，结合CO吸收体以Δχ²=13支持标准标度关系，18个Planck星系团bin给出χ²=50.8（对比15.5）；同时原初各向异性的谱形改变为Planck所排除。若改由暗物质承载自旋，则拟合参数下z<3时H(z)变化小于10⁻⁴且T(z)恢复标准。

Conclusion: 数据显著排除"普适"共享能量交换的图像，支持按自旋比例分配的方案（光不获得能量）；将自旋赋予暗物质可在保持T(z)标准的同时几乎不改变低红移H(z)。

Abstract: The cosmic microwave background temperature at $z=0.886$ is known to $2\%$ from molecular absorption towards PKS~1830$-$211; CO absorbers and Sunyaev-Zel'dovich clusters extend the measurement to $z\simeq3$. We use them on a question that cosmologies with torsion leave open. When the geometry exchanges energy with several matter components, which of them receives it? In Einstein-Cartan theory the Noether identities of each sector fix the answer. A sector's energy-momentum fails to be conserved only in proportion to its own spin, so minimally coupled light, which has none, is conserved whatever the torsion does. A metric-affine cosmology fitted to DESI DR2 distributes the exchange ``universally'' among all fluids, radiation included; the photons then gain almost a third of their energy. If the photons stay a blackbody, as FIRAS requires of any source set by local conditions, the model predicts a background colder than $T_{0}(1+z)$ by $7\%$ at $z\simeq0.9$ and by $8.5\%$ at $z\gtrsim3$, with no free parameter. PKS~1830$-$211 gives $5.08\pm0.10\,\mathrm{K}$ against a predicted $4.78\,\mathrm{K}$; with the CO absorbers it favours the standard scaling by $Δχ^{2}=13$, and 18 Planck cluster bins give $χ^{2}=50.8$ versus $15.5$. Under the same reading the primary anisotropies would change their spectral shape by amounts Planck excludes. If dark matter carries the spin instead, $H(z)$ moves by less than $10^{-4}$ below $z=3$ at the fitted parameters and $T(z)$ is standard again.

</details>

### [86] [Continuum Photonic Axion Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07893)
*Mario G. Silveirinha*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文研究在无晶格的反演对称均匀电磁连续介质（electromagnetic continuum）中实现轴子拓扑（axion topology）的条件，提出一个仅由零波矢与无穷波矢极限下材料响应谱性质定义的连续反转不变量，并指出常规旋光（gyrotropic）型响应在一阶微扰下无法打开轴子能隙，而特定奇磁电耦合（odd magnetoelectric coupling）可以。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子拓扑此前主要在晶格光子晶体中被讨论，作者希望探究在无非晶格结构的均匀反演对称电磁连续介质中能否同样实现轴子拓扑，从而厘清实现该相所需的材料响应条件。

Method: 引入一个连续反转不变量（continuum inversion invariant），其仅依赖材料响应在零波矢与无穷波矢极限下的谱性质；并在第一阶微扰理论（first-order perturbation theory）框架下分析旋光型材料响应对轴子能隙的约束。借助晶体光子轴子绝缘体（photonic axion insulator）有效响应与中心对称交变磁体（centrosymmetric altermagnet）光学响应之间的类比，识别出一类可打开轴子能隙的空间色散奇磁电耦合。

Result: 标准旋光型材料响应受到强约束，在相当一般的条件下无法在一阶微扰理论中打开轴子能隙；而基于上述类比所识别的空间色散奇磁电耦合则能够打开轴子能隙。

Conclusion: 结果表明电磁连续介质中存在非互易机制（nonreciprocal mechanisms）的层级结构：均匀旋光性对应 Chern 相，而奇磁电耦合对应轴子相（axion phases）。

Abstract: Here, I study the conditions for realizing an axion topology in a homogeneous inversion-symmetric electromagnetic continuum, in the absence of a crystalline lattice. I introduce a continuum inversion invariant expressed solely in terms of the spectral properties of the material response in the zero- and infinite-wave-vector limits. Surprisingly, it is found that standard gyrotropic-type material responses are strongly constrained and, under fairly general conditions, cannot open an axion gap within first-order perturbation theory. Based on an analogy between the effective response of crystalline photonic axion insulators and the optical response of centrosymmetric altermagnets, a class of spatially dispersive odd magnetoelectric couplings that can open an axion gap is identified. These findings suggest an interesting hierarchy of nonreciprocal mechanisms in electromagnetic continua: while uniform gyrotropy relates to Chern phases, odd magnetoelectric coupling relates to axion phases.

</details>


### [87] [A Passive Intelligent Metasurface: A Network Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07894)
*Tristan Lawrie,Gregor Tanner,Luke Bennetts*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作基于量子图论（quantum graph theory）的散射框架，推导出无源超表面（passive metasurface）配置的精确解析表达式，可逐通道闭式求解各通道长度与散射参数，从而实现指定的反射波场，并在一维与二维超表面上分别演示了分束、导束、完美吸收与成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能超表面（intelligent metasurface）方案依赖含增益的主动超材料（active metamaterials），或需以计算代价高昂的逆问题求解来获得最优配置，因此亟需一种无需增益、无需数值反演的解析设计途径。

Method: 采用量子图论（quantum graph theory）的散射框架，将超表面建模为终止周期波导晶格的通道阵列，每个通道为长度 ℓ_m 的管，末端顶点的散射参数 λ_m 决定向壁面的损耗比例；由返回与离开晶格的波幅之比，逐通道给出长度与散射参数的闭式精确解。

Result: 解析构造给出一维与二维超表面实例：一维超表面终止二维晶格，实现高斯光束（Gaussian beams）的导束、分束与完美吸收；二维超表面终止三维晶格，在晶格内部某平面上生成指定图像；各情形均获得产生相应效果的精确边界属性。

Conclusion: 当波幅比的模不超过 1 时超表面为无源（passive），该条件为无源性提供了判据；所提方法以闭式解替代逆问题优化，为无源超表面的解析设计提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Intelligent metasurfaces enable advanced wave manipulation, including negative refraction, focusing, beam splitting and beam steering, with significant potential for practical applications. However, conventional approaches often rely on active metamaterials with gain, or demand computationally intensive solutions of inverse problems to find the optimal configuration. In this work, we use the scattering framework of quantum graph theory to derive an exact, analytic expression for configuration of a passive metasurface that produces a prescribed reflected wave field. The metasurface terminates a periodic lattice of waveguides and consists of an array of channels, each a tube of length $\ell_m$ ending in a vertex whose scattering parameter $λ_m$ sets how much of the wave is lost to the wall. For any prescribed incident and reflected field, the exact lengths and scattering parameters follow channel by channel, in closed form, from the ratio of the wave amplitudes returning to and leaving the lattice, and the metasurface is passive whenever this ratio does not exceed unity in modulus. We demonstrate the construction for both a one-dimensional and a two-dimensional metasurface. The one-dimensional metasurface terminates a two-dimensional lattice and steers, splits or perfectly absorbs Gaussian beams. The two-dimensional metasurface terminates a three-dimensional lattice and generates a prescribed image in a plane inside the lattice. In each case, we obtain the exact boundary properties that produce the effect.

</details>

### [88] [Multi-Probe Cosmological Constraints on Clustering DBI Dark Energy Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08008)
*Samit Ganguly,Surhud More,Goutam Manna,Kazuharu Bamba,Eduardo Guendelman,Abhijit Bhattacharyya*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文对一类聚类快子（tachyonic）Dirac-Born-Infeld（DBI）暗能量模型开展多探针观测检验，其非正则动能项导致亚光速声速，使暗能量扰动在次视界尺度上增长；结果显示标准参数与ΛCDM在2σ内一致，贝叶斯证据在各超新星样本下均偏好DBI（Pantheon+为中等偏好），但模型无法进入幽灵（phantom）区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于检验具有非正则动能项的DBI暗能量模型能否同时解释背景膨胀与线性扰动，并回应DESI DR2对有效状态方程穿越w=-1的偏好。

Method: 采用同时追踪背景演化与线性扰动的多流体（multifluid）框架，结合嵌套采样（nested sampling），使用Planck 2018压缩CMB距离先验、DESI DR2 BAO、宇宙计时器（cosmic chronometers）、fσ8增长数据及三组Ia型超新星汇编（Pantheon+、DES-SN5YR、Union3）进行约束。

Conclusion: CPL参数化、Om(z)、statefinder层级与增长指数四项独立诊断均指向真实的动态暗能量而非宇宙学常数；热历史保持不变，早期暗能量可忽略。但受w_φ≥-1限制，模型无法进入幽灵区，符合单极小耦合标量场的no-go定理，因而无法跟随DESI DR2对w=-1穿越的偏好。

Abstract: We present a multi-probe observational analysis of a clustering tachyonic Dirac-Born-Infeld~(DBI) dark energy model. Its noncanonical kinetic term gives a subluminal sound speed, so dark energy perturbations can grow on subhorizon scales. Working in a multifluid framework that tracks both the background expansion and linear perturbations, we constrain the model with nested sampling, using Planck 2018 compressed CMB distance priors, DESI DR2 BAO, cosmic chronometers, $fσ_8$ growth data, and three Type Ia supernova compilations: Pantheon$+$, DES-SN5YR, and Union3. The standard cosmological parameters agree with $Λ$CDM within $2σ$, with DBI preferring a slightly lower $H_0$ and higher $Ω_{m0}$, with the largest shift being $\simeq1.8σ$ in $H_0$ for Pantheon$+$. The Bayesian evidence favours DBI in every compilation, moderately for Pantheon$+$ ($Δ\ln\mathcal{Z}\simeq3.4$) and inconclusively for DES-SN5YR and Union3 ($Δ\ln\mathcal{Z}\simeq0.6$-$0.7$). The field thaws naturally. Dark energy perturbations stay several orders of magnitude below matter perturbations, yet the noncanonical sound speed still alters the Hubble friction term, raising $S_8$ by only $\approx0.5σ$ relative to $Λ$CDM. Four independent diagnostics, namely the CPL parametrization, $Om(z)$, the statefinder hierarchy, and the growth index, all point to genuinely evolving dark energy rather than a cosmological constant. The thermal history is left intact, with negligible early dark energy. However, one limitation remains. Because $w_φ=\dotφ^2-1\geq-1$, the phantom regime is out of reach, which is in line with the no-go theorem for a single minimally coupled scalar field, so the model cannot follow the DESI DR2 preference for an effective equation of state crossing $w=-1$.

</details>

*Opal Issan,Lucas José,Matt Landreman,Jonathan Citrin,Brendan Tracey*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导出适用于任意整数场周期数 N≥1 的闭式非轴对称磁流体力学平衡解，突破此前解仅限 N≤2 的限制，并为零旋转变换情形构造了匹配的外部真空场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的闭式非轴对称磁流体力学平衡解最多只有两个场周期（field periods, N≤2），不足以作为验证三维磁流体力学平衡代码的充分基准，因此需要推广到任意场周期数。

Method: 解析推导闭式（closed-form）非轴对称磁流体力学平衡解，解具有嵌套环向磁通面、非恒定压强与闭合磁力线；对反射对称的 ι=0 平衡，进一步构造边界两侧磁场连续匹配的外部真空场（exterior vacuum field）。

Result: 所得平衡对任意整数 N≥1 成立，旋转变换（rotational transform）可取零（ι=0，N≥1）或整数（ι=±N，N≥3）；反射对称的 ι=0 解具有跨等离子体边界的连续磁场。

Conclusion: 该工作将闭式三维平衡基准从 N≤2 推广至任意 N，并为自由边界（free-boundary）平衡代码提供了带匹配真空场的验证基准。

Abstract: Several closed-form non-axisymmetric magnetic fields with nested toroidal flux surfaces have recently been discovered as exact benchmarks for verifying three-dimensional magnetohydrodynamic equilibrium codes. However, all of these solutions have at most two field periods ($N \le 2$), i.e., identical segments that repeat toroidally. Here, we derive closed-form non-axisymmetric magnetohydrodynamic equilibria for an arbitrary integer number of field periods $N \geq 1$. The equilibria have non-constant pressure and closed magnetic field lines with either zero rotational transform ($ι= 0$) for $N \geq 1$ or integer rotational transform ($ι= \pm N$) for $N \ge 3$. For the reflection-symmetric $ι= 0$ equilibria, we also construct a matched exterior vacuum field with continuous magnetic field across the plasma boundary, providing a benchmark for free-boundary equilibrium codes.

</details>

### [90] [Amplitude Analysis of the Invariant-Mass Spectra of Centrally Exclusively Produced $π^+π^-$ and $K^+K^-$ Pairs in $pp$ Collisions at $\sqrt{s}=510$ GeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07086)
*Aranya Giri,Barak Schmookler,Rene Bellwied*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文对 STAR 实验在 √s=510 GeV 中心独家 pp 碰撞中测量的 π⁺π⁻ 与 K⁺K⁻ 不变质量谱进行振幅分析（amplitude analysis），并用比值方法检验共振态（resonance）的味依赖（flavor dependence）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用前向标记质子（Roman Pot）的中心独家（central exclusive）双介子产生过程，可在高 RHIC 能量下检验共振态产生机制，并与低能数据比较其味依赖行为。

Method: 对双介子不变质量谱做振幅拟合：π⁺π⁻ 道采用 f₀(500)、ρ⁰(770)、f₀(980)、f₂(1270)、f₀(1500)、f₀(1710) 六个共振态，K⁺K⁻ 道采用 f₀(980)、f₂(1270)、f₂′(1525)、f₀(1710) 四个共振态；并计算各共振态与干涉项（interference term）的基准截面（fiducial cross section）及其相对总拟合截面的比值。

Result: π⁺π⁻ 拟合得到 χ²/ndf=121.712/57，并给出 m_{f₀(980)}=0.989 GeV、Γ=0.091 GeV；K⁺K⁻ 拟合得到 χ²/ndf=14.662/15。提取的比值被跨衰变道比较，并与先前测量作定性对照。

Conclusion: 在最高 RHIC 碰撞能量下导出了 f₀(1500) 与 f₀(1710) 一致的味依赖关系，且与低能数据相符。

Abstract: We present an amplitude analysis of the $π^{+}π^{-}$ and $K^{+}K^{-}$ invariant-mass spectra from central exclusive $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=510~\mathrm{GeV}$, measured by the STAR experiment with forward-scattered protons tagged in the Roman Pot detectors. The $π^{+}π^{-}$ channel is described with $f_{0}(500)$, $ρ^{0}(770)$, $f_{0}(980)$, $f_{2}(1270)$, $f_{0}(1500)$ and $f_{0}(1710)$ resonances with $χ^{2}/\mathrm{ndf}=121.712/57$. The fit also yields $m_{f_{0}(980)}=0.989$ GeV and $Γ=0.091$ GeV. The $K^{+}K^{-}$ channel is described with $f_{0}(980)$, $f_{2}(1270)$, $f_{2}^{\prime}(1525)$ and $f_{0}(1710)$ resonances with $χ^{2}/\mathrm{ndf}=14.662/15$. The fiducial cross sections for the individual resonances and interference terms were calculated, together with their ratios relative to the total fitted cross section. These ratios were compared across decay channels and qualitatively with previous measurements. A consistent flavor dependence of $f_{0}(1500)$ and $f_{0}(1710)$ has been derived for the highest RHIC collision energies, which is in agreement with low energy data.

</details>

*Jialin Yu,Stefan Knirck,Gabe Hoshino,Rakshya Khatiwada,Alex Lapuente,David W. Miller,Andrew Sonnenschein*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 首次针对44–52 μeV（10.7–12.5 GHz）质量区间的固定极化暗光子（dark photon, DP）暗物质开展搜寻，利用GigaBREAD数据并采用极化匹配似然分析，未发现信号，给出动能混合参数（kinetic mixing parameter）上限约χ~10⁻¹²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往GigaBREAD及其他碟形天线（dish antenna）搜寻均假设暗光子暗物质极化随机；但由类错位机制（misalignment-like mechanism）产生的矢量暗物质可能保持近似固定极化，随地球自转产生恒星日调制（sidereal modulation），需针对该情形重新分析。

Method: GigaBREAD为室温宽带碟形天线实验，暗光子在圆柱形金属发射面上转化为普通光子并聚焦至射频接收机；研究采用极化匹配似然（polarization-matched likelihood），显式计入固定极化在探测器轴上的时间依赖投影，对已有数据进行重新分析。

Result: 未发现固定极化暗光子信号的证据，在动能混合参数上给出约χ~10⁻¹²量级的上限；该上限比在固定极化假设下直接重标度（rescaling）此前GigaBREAD结果所得的朴素上限约优20%。

Conclusion: 结果支持对暗光子极化状态进行显式建模可提升搜寻灵敏度，并为该质量区间固定极化矢量暗物质提供更严格的实验约束。

Abstract: We present the first search for fixed-polarization dark photon (DP) dark matter in the 44-52$μ\mathrm{eV}$ mass range, corresponding to 10.7-12.5GHz. GigaBREAD is a room-temperature broadband dish antenna experiment in which DP convert to ordinary photons on a cylindrical metallic emission surface and are focused onto a radio-frequency receiver. Previous searches, including GigaBREAD and several other dish antenna searches, have assumed randomly polarized DP dark matter. However, vector dark matter produced by a misalignment-like mechanism may retain an approximately fixed polarization, leading to sidereal modulation of the detector response as the Earth rotates. We reanalyze the GigaBREAD data using a polarization-matched likelihood that accounts for the time-dependent projection of a fixed DP polarization onto the detector axis. We find no evidence for a fixed-polarization DP signal and set upper limits on the kinetic mixing parameter at the level of $χ\sim10^{-12}$. These limits are about $20\%$ better than a naive limit obtained by rescaling the previous GigaBREAD limit under the fixed-polarization assumption.

</details>


### [92] [Search for dark matter axions at 1-2 micro-eV with a RADES-DarkQuantum haloscope at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07806)
*Alberto Arcusa Puente,Sergio Arguedas Cuendis,Marcos Buetas Mata,Sergio Calatroni,Cristian Cogollos,Alejandro Díaz-Morcillo,Babette Döbrich,Jacob Egge,Elisa Gabbrielli,Javier Galindo Guarch,Jose María García-Barceló,Benito Gimeno,Yikun Gu,Sophia Hollick,Igor Garcia Irastorza,David Kittlinger,Adrian Lambies-Asensio,Axel Lindner,Manuel Meyer,Adrián Muñoz-Urán,Jose R. Navarro-Madrid,Tobias Ortmann,Sagar Paul,Angel Rodriguez,Kristof Schmieden,Laura Segui,Wolfgang Wernsdorfer,Mina Yami*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文提出利用CERN的M1超导磁体搭建光晕仪（haloscope），以连续覆盖约1.2–1.8 μeV质量区间搜寻银河系轴子及轴子类粒子暗物质，并称其有望在该未探索区间对QCD轴子模型给出高影响力乃至决定性的检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD轴子（QCD axion）是暗物质最具吸引力的候选之一，但约1–2 μeV质量范围基本未被探索，构成明显的实验空白。

Method: 提出使用CERN的M1超导磁体（superconducting magnet）部署光晕仪（haloscope），对银河系轴子与轴子类粒子（axion-like particle）暗物质进行搜寻，实现约1.2–1.8 μeV的连续频率覆盖；并利用CERN加速器当前长期停机（long shut-down）提供的时间窗口。

Result: 在保守假设下，实验可在扫描范围的相当部分达到QCD轴子带（QCD axion band）；在乐观探测器性能下，可在大范围质量区间对KSVZ与DFSZ等基准模型（benchmark models）具备灵敏度。

Conclusion: 即便在保守情形下该实验也能产出高影响力结果，并有望在尚未探索的1–2 μeV质量区间对轴子暗物质范式（axion dark matter paradigm）作出决定性检验。

Abstract: The QCD axion remains one of the most compelling candidates for the dark matter of the Universe, yet the mass range around $1$\text{--}\SI{2}{\micro\electronvolt} remains essentially unexplored. In this paper, we describe a proposal to employ CERN's M1 superconducting magnet to deploy a haloscope to perform a search for galactic axion and axion-like particle dark matter providing continuous coverage from approximately 1.2 to 1.8 \SI{}{\micro\electronvolt}. Under conservative assumptions, the experiment can reach the QCD axion band over a significant fraction of the scanned range, while optimistic detector performance would allow sensitivity to benchmark models such as KSVZ and DFSZ over a large part of the explored mass interval. With the current long shut-down of CERN's accelerators providing a unique and timely window, such an experiment will deliver high-impact results even in conservative scenarios and potentially delivering a definitive test of the axion dark matter paradigm in the unexplored $1$\text{--}\SI{2}{\micro\electronvolt} mass range.

</details>


### [93] [Data-quality monitoring of luminosity blocks with ATLAS Open Data: an open benchmark and a learned within-run detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07819)
*Renjie Wang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作构建了基于ATLAS开放数据（ATLAS Open Data）的公开基准（public benchmark），用于可比较地评估LHC逐亮度段（luminosity block）的数据质量监测方法，并给出基于残差特征与梯度提升分类器的检测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC的数据质量监测（data-quality monitoring）逐亮度段决定哪些碰撞数据进入物理分析，但各实验的监测数据不公开，导致方法无法公开比较。

Method: 基于2015–2016年√s=13 TeV质子-质子对撞的ATLAS开放数据构建基准：217个run、39828个亮度段的1289-bin直方图（喷注、电子、光子、缪子及由它们计算的缺失横向动量），注入8类三级强度的探测器缺陷，并采用仅用更早run训练与校准的时间顺序协议（time-ordered protocol）；检测器使用相对每run稳健参考的残差（residuals）上的位置不变特征与梯度提升分类器（gradient-boosted classifier），并以分裂保形预测（split-conformal prediction）校准。

Result: 直方图形状在run间差异远大于run内（χ²/n_dof中位数5.9对1.1），跨run的参考与自编码器（autoencoder）失效；在1.0%假阳性率下，该方法对注入缺陷亮度段的检出率为43%，匹配滤波扫描（matched-filter scan）为25%，已知缺陷模式的单直方图检验为63%；对训练中未见的缺陷类型效率降至18%，加入模式无关的合成探测器故障后升至34%，而已知类型代价为3个百分点。

Conclusion: 该公开基准使LHC数据质量监测方法得以开放比较，并表明残差加监督分类结合保形校准是有效基线，但跨缺陷类型的泛化仍是主要挑战。

Abstract: Data-quality monitoring at the LHC decides, luminosity block by luminosity block, which collision data enter physics analyses, yet its methods cannot be compared openly because the experiments' monitoring data are not public. We construct a public benchmark from the ATLAS Open Data release of the 2015--2016 proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$~TeV: 1289-bin histograms of reconstructed jets, electrons, photons, muons and the missing transverse momentum computed from them, for 39\,828 luminosity blocks of all 217 runs in the Good Run Lists; eight types of detector defects injected at event level at three strengths; real-data labels from the Good Run Lists and the liquid-argon calorimeter error flag; and a time-ordered protocol in which every detector is trained and calibrated on earlier runs only. Histogram shapes differ far more between runs than within a run (median $χ^2/n_\mathrm{dof}$ of 5.9 against 1.1), so references and autoencoders built across runs fail, and all competitive detectors use residuals with respect to a robust reference of each run. A gradient-boosted classifier on position-invariant features of these residuals, trained on synthetic defects and calibrated by split-conformal prediction, detects 43\% of the luminosity blocks with injected defects at a false-positive rate of 1.0\%, against 25\% for a matched-filter scan and 63\% for a single-histogram test that knows the defect pattern. For defect types excluded from its training, its efficiency falls to 18\%; adding pattern-agnostic synthetic detector failures to the training raises it to 34\%, above the scan, at a cost of three percentage points on the known types.

</details>

### [95] [Development and performance of a transformer-based lepton isolation algorithm with the ATLAS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08194)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: PLIT 是一种基于 Transformer 的提示轻子（prompt lepton）鉴别算法，利用径迹信息提升多轻子末态分析中提示轻子与非提示/误鉴别轻子的区分能力，并已在 ATLAS Run 3 数据上完成效率刻度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轻子末态测量受到非提示轻子（non-prompt leptons）与误鉴别轻子（misidentified leptons）的显著本底影响，需要更有效的轻子隔离（lepton isolation）鉴别手段。

Method: PLIT 将轻子候选者关联的带电粒子径迹（charged-particle tracks）信息与轻子自身属性结合，采用 Transformer 神经网络捕捉径迹间相关性以增强鉴别；性能用模拟的顶夸克对（top-quark pair）产生事例评估，并与既有隔离算法比较。

Result: 相比此前算法，PLIT 在保持对非提示轻子高排除率的同时提升了提示轻子效率；针对电子与缪子分别定义了工作点（operating points）。

Conclusion: 基于 LHC 上 ATLAS 实验在 √s=13.6 TeV 质子-质子碰撞中记录的 Run 3 数据，利用 Z→l⁺l⁻ 事例对效率进行刻度，给出数据与模拟的修正因子（correction factors），可供物理分析使用。

Abstract: The Prompt Lepton Isolation Tagger (PLIT) is a transformer-based algorithm designed to distinguish prompt leptons (electrons and muons) produced in the primary decays of electroweak gauge bosons from non-prompt leptons produced in hadron decays and from misidentified leptons. It is primarily used in measurements targeting multilepton final states with important backgrounds from non-prompt and misidentified leptons. PLIT exploits information from charged-particle tracks associated with the lepton candidate, together with lepton properties, using a transformer neural network to identify correlations between the tracks and improve the discrimination. Its performance is evaluated using simulated top-quark pair production events and compared with that of previously used lepton isolation algorithms, demonstrating improved prompt lepton efficiency while maintaining high rejection of non-prompt leptons. This article presents the development, performance, and calibration of PLIT for electrons and muons in proton--proton collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}$=13.6 TeV recorded by the ATLAS experiment at the Large Hadron Collider. Operating points are defined for both electrons and muons, and their efficiencies are calibrated using $Z\to l^+l^-$ events in Run 3 data, providing data-to-simulation correction factors for physics analyses.

</details>


### [96] [Search for Higgs boson production in association with another Higgs or an exotic scalar boson in the $b\bar{b}W^{+}W^{-}$ single lepton final state at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08241)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS 利用 LHC Run 2 全部 13 TeV、140 fb⁻¹ 数据搜寻双希格斯玻色子（Higgs boson pair）产生，衰变道为 H→bb̄ 与 H→WW*→ℓνqq̄；未观测到显著信号，95% 置信水平上限为标准模型（Standard Model）预期的 35 倍，并对超出标准模型（Beyond the Standard Model）的共振 X→SH 产生给出质量依赖的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双希格斯玻色子产生可检验标准模型下希格斯势的自耦合（self-coupling），同时为超出标准模型的重标量共振态（resonant heavy scalars）提供探针。

Method: 采用 ATLAS 探测器 2015–2018 年 13 TeV 质子-质子对撞数据，积分亮度（integrated luminosity）140 fb⁻¹；针对非共振（non-resonant）双希格斯产生按标准模型动力学进行分析，同一事例选择（event selection）亦用于搜寻 X→SH、S→W⁺W⁻、H→bb̄ 的共振过程。

Result: 未观测到双希格斯玻色子产生的证据，95% 置信水平（confidence level）上限设为标准模型预言的 35 倍；对 m_X 在 350–3000 GeV、m_S 在 170–2500 GeV 范围内的多个质量组合给出共振 X→SH 产生的上限。

Conclusion: 该衰变道在当前数据量下尚不足以观测标准模型双希格斯产生，但可对超出标准模型的共振重标量产生施加强限制。

Abstract: A search for Higgs boson pair production where one Higgs boson decays via $H \to b \bar{b}$ and the other Higgs boson decays via $H \to WW^*\to \ell νq \bar{q}\quad (\ell=e,μ)$ is presented. This search uses the complete Run 2 data sample recorded by the ATLAS detector at the LHC from 2015 to 2018 at a centre-of-mass energy of 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $140$ fb$^{-1}$. The analysis looks for non-resonant Higgs boson pair production assuming Standard Model dynamics. No evidence for Higgs-boson pair production is observed and an upper limit at 95% confidence level is set at 35 times the Standard Model prediction. The same selection is used to target Beyond the Standard Model theories predicting the resonant production of the heavy scalars, $X$ and $S$, with $m_X > m_S > m_H$, undergoing the process $X \to SH, S \to W^+W^-, H \to b \bar{b}$. Limits are set on the resonant $X \to SH$ production for several combinations of the two scalar's masses within the $m_X$ mass range $350-3000$ GeV and $m_S$ mass range $170-2500$ GeV.

</details>

### [97] [Stability and Relaxation of Electron-Phonon Systems at Strong Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07014)
*Noga Bashan,Erez Berg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用准经典 Kadanoff-Baym 方程（quasiclassical Kadanoff-Baym equation）研究强耦合下 Migdal-Eliashberg 理论（Migdal-Eliashberg theory, MET）的动理学性质，发现非马尔可夫效应（non-Markovian effects）可消除马尔可夫量子玻尔兹曼近似下出现的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前在马尔可夫量子玻尔兹曼近似（Markovian quantum Boltzmann approximation）中，强耦合 MET 存在动力学不稳定性，可能被误认为超导（superconductivity）的先决限制；需澄清这是否为近似伪迹。

Method: 采用准经典 Kadanoff-Baym 方程处理电子-声子相互作用（electron-phonon interactions）的强耦合动理学，计入非马尔可夫效应，并分析长时间弛豫动力学（long-time relaxation dynamics）。

Result: 非马尔可夫效应下该不稳定性消失；在德拜频率（Debye frequency）与耦合强度之间的中间温区，弛豫率（relaxation rate）随温度线性变化且斜率为普朗克（Planckian）值，主导弛豫模式获得较大虚部，导致强振荡衰减。

Conclusion: 任何先于超导出现的不稳定性必须来自 MET 之外，如声子软化（phonon softening）或有限电子带宽下的极化子效应（polaronic effects）；特征声子频率并非 Tc 的基本上限，多轨道电子-声子系统（multi-orbital electron-phonon systems）存在提升 Tc 的空间。

Abstract: We study the kinetic properties of Migdal-Eliashberg theory (MET) for electron-phonon interactions at strong coupling. Previously, the system was found to be dynamically unstable at large couplings within a Markovian quantum Boltzmann approximation. Using the quasiclassical Kadanoff-Baym equation, we show that this instability is absent when non-Markovian effects are taken into account. We also analyze the long-time relaxation dynamics at strong-couplin, and find a clear imprint of the non-Markovian nature of the dynamics: in an intermediate temperature range between the Debye frequency and the coupling strength, the relaxation rate becomes linear in temperature with Planckian slope, while the leading relaxation mode acquires a large imaginary component, producing strongly oscillatory decay. Our results imply that any instability which preempts superconductivity must arise from effects beyond MET, such as phonon softening or polaronic effects at finite electronic bandwidth. This shows that the characteristic phonon frequency is not a fundamental bound on $T_c$, and leaves open a route to higher $T_c$ in multi-orbital electron-phonon systems.

</details>


### [98] [Agnostic and efficient detection of $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry-protected topological phases in projected entangled-pair states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07030)
*Alexandre Chaduteau,Hao Chen,Juraj Hasik,Titus Neupert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种与虚拟空间对称表示及键维无关的高效方法，可在热力学极限下直接检测相互作用二维 Z2 对称保护拓扑（symmetry-protected topological, SPT）相，并从无限投影纠缠对态（infinite projected entangled-pair states, PEPS）张量中显式提取 SPT 标签的上链（cocycle）数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPT 相的分类已较为完善，但实际问题在于检测：给定相互作用哈密顿量的基态、对称群 G 及其表示，能否高效判断该态是否处于相对 G 的 SPT 相。一维已有实用判据，而二维方法此前局限于有限尺寸系统或需预先已知张量网络的虚拟对称性显式形式。

Method: 以基态的无限 PEPS 表示为出发点，构造一种不依赖 PEPS 张量虚拟空间对称表示形式（agnostic）的检测流程；该方法不受张量键维限制，且直接在热力学极限下工作，无需有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 数值上演示了该流程，能够刻画远离不动点波函数处的相边界，并从 PEPS 张量中显式提取 SPT 标签的上链数据。

Conclusion: 该研究为二维相互作用 Z2 SPT 相提供了一种实用、高效且普适的检测手段，将 SPT 检测从有限尺寸或依赖虚拟对称性先验的情形推广至热力学极限下的通用张量网络框架。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases are the simplest example of quantum phases that cannot be detected by local order parameters. Their classification is by now well-established from multiple perspectives, a prominent one being via tensor network states. The practical question, however, is not mere classification, but detection: given the ground state of an interacting Hamiltonian and a symmetry group $G$ along with its representation, is there an efficient way to tell if the state is in a SPT phase with respect to $G$? While there exist such practical tests in one spatial dimension (1D), methods in 2D have so far been restricted to finite-size systems or require an explicit form of virtual symmetries of the tensor networks representing the ground state. Here, we develop an agnostic and efficient method to detect interacting $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 2D SPT phases, using an infinite projected entangled-pair (PEPS) representation of the ground state. The method is agnostic to the symmetry representation in the virtual space of the PEPS tensor and applicable independent of the bond dimension of the tensors. Moreover, it is efficient as it does not require finite-size scalings: it works directly in the thermodynamic limit. We numerically demonstrate the procedure by mapping out phase boundaries away from fixed-point wavefunctions. We extract the SPT label cocycle data explicitly from the PEPS tensor.

</details>


### [99] [Exact magnetic ground states and competing interlayer exchanges in layered kagome magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07131)
*Vladimir Antropov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用含六个层间交换作用的Heisenberg模型研究层状kagome磁体基态，证明长程交换可使磁序在螺旋—铁磁—螺旋间切换，并指出若干可观测量不足以唯一确定六个交换常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状kagome磁体的磁基态由层间交换作用决定，但Rosenfeld–Mushnikov三交换模型对更长程交换的描述能力有限，需厘清长程交换对磁序类型的真实影响。

Method: 采用包含六个层间交换相互作用的Heisenberg模型；对具相同对角Fourier交换函数的各向同性双子晶格模型作解析论证，并结合基态相图、谐波稳定性、磁场响应及对单个交换作用的敏感性分析。

Result: 对任意交换程长，各向同性双子晶格模型存在共面单波矢基态；扩展三交换模型后，长程作用可将优势磁序由螺旋变为铁磁再变回螺旋，而非仅改变螺旋周期；传播矢量、层内角、饱和场与面内磁化率均不足以唯一确定六个交换常数。

Conclusion: 层间长程交换显著改变基态磁序类型，常规可观测量无法唯一反演六个交换常数，结果为基于第一性原理交换作用预测非共线磁序提供依据。

Abstract: Magnetic ground states in layered kagome magnets are investigated using a Heisenberg model with six interlayer exchange interactions. For isotropic two-sublattice models with identical diagonal Fourier exchange functions, a coplanar single-wave-vector ground state is shown to exist for arbitrary exchange ranges. Extending the three-exchange model of Rosenfeld and Mushnikov reveals that longer-range interactions can change the preferred magnetic order from spiral to ferromagnetic and back to spiral, rather than merely modifying the spiral period. The analysis includes ground-state phase diagrams, harmonic stability, magnetic-field response, and sensitivity to individual exchange interactions. The propagation vector, internal layer angle, saturation field, and planar susceptibility are also found to be insufficient to determine all six exchange constants uniquely. These results provide a basis for predicting noncollinear magnetic order from first-principles exchange interactions.

</details>


### [100] [Extensions of the Haldane gap proof: higher dimensions, near degeneracy, fermions and tensor-network states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07400)
*Tarun Grover*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文将OpenAI关于自旋1海森堡链能隙的"纯度自举（purity bootstrap）"论证推广至任意空间维度、费米子系统与张量网络态，利用时空对偶（space-time duality）从有限系统信息导出低能态劈裂的上界与能隙的非零性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenAI近期证明周期边界自旋1海森堡链在热力学极限下存在谱隙（spectral gap），其核心是由有限系统谱推出能隙的纯度不等式；本文旨在考察该论证的若干推广形式及其适用范围。

Method: 将纯度自举论证推广到任意空间维度，主要结果利用对称性生成元与对称性扭曲（symmetry twists）之间的时空对偶，从有限系统信息得到近简并低能态劈裂的上界及其上方非零能隙；进一步推广至费米子系统，并应用于张量网络态（tensor-network states）。

Result: 对2+1维场中环面码（toric code in a field）与2+1维横场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model），有限计算确定了分别具有四个和两个低能态的参数区间，其劈裂对任意大系统尺寸L分别被e^{-cL}与e^{-cL^2}从上方界定，而上方能隙在热力学极限下保持非零；费米子推广在2+1维费米子模型中确立了对称质量生成（symmetric mass generation）相；对Jian与Zaletel提出的对称正方晶格自旋1态，证明了所有局域算符连通关联（connected correlations）的指数衰减。

Conclusion: 纯度自举论证可超越一维玻色情形，推广至任意维度、费米子系统与张量网络态，为从有限系统计算严格约束热力学极限下的低能谱结构（简并劈裂与能隙）提供了统一框架。

Abstract: A recent remarkable paper by OpenAI showed that the spin-one Heisenberg chain with periodic boundary conditions has a spectral gap in the thermodynamic limit. The proof elegantly deduces this gap from the Hamiltonian's spectrum on a finite system. This basic idea is an inequality involving the purity of the original Gibbs state and a dual Gibbs state that is defined via space-time rotation. Here we consider a few generalizations of this purity bootstrap argument. We first extend it to arbitrary spatial dimensions. Our main result uses the space-time duality between symmetry generators and symmetry twists to obtain upper bounds on the splitting of nearly degenerate low-energy states, together with a nonzero gap above them, from finite-system information. As applications, we show that finite-system calculations for the 2+1-D toric code in a field and the 2+1-D transverse-field Ising model establish parameter regimes with four and two low-lying states whose splittings are bounded from above by $e^{-cL}$ and $e^{-cL^2}$ respectively for arbitrarily large system size $L$, while the gap above these states remains nonzero in the thermodynamic limit. Next, we extend it to fermionic systems and use a finite calculation to establish a symmetric mass generation phase in a $2+1$-D fermionic model. Finally, we apply the bootstrap to tensor-network states and establish exponential decay of connected correlations of all local operators in the symmetric square-lattice spin-one state introduced by Jian and Zaletel.

</details>


### [101] [Observable-Based Learning of Effective Entanglement Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07353)
*Siyi Yang,Yuzhi Liu,Yi-Ming Ding,Zheng Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出可观测匹配（observable-matching）框架，用量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）响应估计学习相互作用系统的有效纠缠哈密顿量（entanglement Hamiltonian, EH），无需预设空间函数形式即可在1D/2D复现纠缠熵，并在(2+1)D O(3) Wilson–Fisher临界点揭示非均匀耦合重整化与负的次领头修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用量子多体系统的纠缠哈密顿量除少数可解析情形外难以确定，且既有构造（如晶格Bisognano–Wichmann, LBW）依赖预设的空间耦合剖面，缺乏对超出该剖面的空间分辨耦合的刻画手段。

Method: 建立可观测匹配框架，以量子蒙特卡洛给出的响应估计为训练信号；先用等时关联（equal-time correlations）标定LBW构造的整体前置因子，再在不假定具体函数形式的前提下学习空间分辨耦合，训练仅使用局域关联。

Result: 学得的EH在1D复现基准von Neumann熵与二阶Rényi熵、在2D复现二阶Rényi熵；对(2+1)D O(3) Wilson–Fisher临界点的强键切割，自由形式学习优于标定后的LBW拟设，并揭示空间非均匀的耦合重整化；学得的EH还可给出该情形下的von Neumann熵，显示负的次领头修正。

Conclusion: 可观测驱动的学习可作为构造有效纠缠哈密顿量的通用途径，突破预设空间剖面的限制并适用于高维相互作用系统，同时为常规数值方法难以直接计算的von Neumann熵提供获取手段。

Abstract: Determining the entanglement Hamiltonian (EH) of an interacting quantum many-body system remains a fundamental challenge beyond special analytically tractable cases. Here we develop an observable-matching framework for learning effective EHs, which we demonstrate using response estimates from quantum Monte Carlo. As applications of this framework, we first calibrate the overall prefactor of the lattice Bisognano--Wichmann (LBW) construction using equal-time correlations and then learn spatially resolved couplings beyond its prescribed profile, without assuming a specific functional form for their spatial dependence. Trained solely on local correlations, the learned EHs reproduce benchmarked von Neumann and second Rényi entropies in $1$D and second Rényi entropies in $2$D. For a strong-bond cut associated with special surface criticality at the $(2+1)$D $O(3)$ Wilson--Fisher critical point, free-form learning outperforms the calibrated LBW ansatz in predicting the second Rényi entropy and reveals spatially nonuniform coupling renormalizations. Importantly, the learned EH also provides access to the von Neumann entropy in this setting, revealing a negative subleading correction and extending previous Rényi entropy findings to the von Neumann case. Directly evaluating the von Neumann entropy in such $(2+1)$D interacting quantum many-body systems remains challenging for conventional numerical methods. These results establish observable-based learning as a route to constructing effective entanglement Hamiltonians beyond prescribed spatial profiles in higher-dimensional interacting systems.

</details>


### [102] [Interfacial polarity control of anomalous Hall effect in LaCoO3-SrIrO3 heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07501)
*Yeonjae Lee,Chanyoung Shin,Youngdo Kim,Jinyoung Kim,Jaeung Lee,Miyoung Kim,Sangjae Lee,Changyoung Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过独立调控层堆叠顺序与原子终止方式控制SrIrO3-LaCoO3界面的极性诱导电荷符号，证明界面极性可作为主动设计变量实现反常霍尔效应（AHE）的开关调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化物异质界面极性（interfacial polarity）虽可驱动界面电荷重分布，但其在调控拓扑输运现象（如反常霍尔效应，anomalous Hall effect, AHE）中的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过独立设计层堆叠序列（layer stacking sequence）与原子终止（atomic termination）来调控SrIrO3-LaCoO3界面极性诱导电荷的符号；利用原位角分辨光电子能谱（in-situ angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）直接观测能带移动，并对四种异质结构组成的系统矩阵开展磁输运测量。

Result: ARPES通过不同界面构型间对比鲜明的能带移动，直接确认SrIrO3中诱导电荷的符号反转；磁输运测量显示AHE可被清晰地开启与关闭——仅在电子掺杂条件下出现，在空穴掺杂的SrIrO3中被抑制。

Conclusion: 该AHE响应源于由极性控制的SrIrO3能带填充（band filling）所主导的、内禀的贝里曲率（Berry curvature）驱动机制；界面极性由此从被动的结构结果转变为工程化氧化物异质结中拓扑量子态的主动设计变量。

Abstract: Interfacial polarity in oxide heterostructures can drive robust charge redistribution across the interface, yet its role in tailoring topological transport phenomena like the anomalous Hall effect (AHE) remains largely unexplored. Here, we control the sign of polarity-induced charge in SrIrO3-LaCoO3 interfaces by independently engineering the layer stacking sequence and the atomic termination. In-situ angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy directly confirms the sign reversal of the induced charge in SrIrO3 through the contrasting electronic band shifts between different interfacial configurations. Magneto-transport measurements across a systematic matrix of four distinct heterostructures reveal that the AHE can be cleanly toggled on and off: it emerges exclusively under electron-doped conditions, while remaining suppressed for hole-doped SrIrO3. Our results establish that this response originates from an intrinsic, Berry-curvature-driven mechanism governed by polarity-controlled band filling of SrIrO3. These findings shift interfacial polarity from a passive structural consequence to an active design variable for engineering topological quantum states in oxide heterostructures.

</details>


### [103] [Negligible Kitaev interaction in the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiBr$_2$ revealed by neutron spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07597)
*Hao Xu,Shufan Cheng,Yanyan Shangguan,Song Bao,Junbo Liao,Bo Zhang,Zihang Song,Shuai Dong,Maofeng Wu,Shinichi Itoh,Daichi Ueta,Jinsheng Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用飞行时间中子谱学测量 NiBr2 的磁激发，发现其磁振子色散可由含至第三近邻海森堡交换、层间耦合与单离子各向异性的最小双线性哈密顿量定量描述，无需引入 Kitaev 相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiX2（X = 卤素）家族中键依赖相互作用（bond-dependent interactions）的来源尚需厘清，特别是溴比碘轻是否导致配体介导自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）减弱、进而削弱 Kitaev 相互作用。

Method: 采用飞行时间中子谱学（time-of-flight neutron spectroscopy）在宽广能量与动量范围内测量范德瓦尔斯反铁磁体（van der Waals antiferromagnet）NiBr2 的磁激发，并用基于最小双线性哈密顿量的线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory）进行定量拟合。

Result: 观测到定义良好的磁振子色散（magnon dispersions）；模型包含至第三近邻的海森堡交换（Heisenberg exchange）、层间耦合及单离子各向异性（single-ion anisotropy）项，即可定量再现实验数据，无需 Kitaev 相互作用。

Conclusion: NiBr2 被确立为以海森堡相互作用为主导的范德瓦尔斯反铁磁体；结果支持配体自旋轨道耦合支配 NiX2 家族内键依赖相互作用强度的观点。

Abstract: Using time-of-flight neutron spectroscopy, we have mapped out the magnetic excitations in the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiBr$_2$ over a broad range of energies and momenta. The observed well-defined magnon dispersions are quantitatively reproduced by linear spin-wave theory based on a minimal bilinear Hamiltonian that includes Heisenberg exchange interactions up to third-nearest neighbors, interlayer couplings, and a single-ion anisotropy term, with no need to invoke a Kitaev interaction. This finding is consistent with the fact that bromine is lighter than iodine, leading to weaker ligand-mediated spin-orbit coupling in NiBr2 than in NiI2, and consequently a reduced Kitaev interaction. Our results establish NiBr$_2$ as a Heisenberg-dominated van der Waals antiferromagnet and support the view that the ligand spin-orbit coupling governs the strength of the bond-dependent interactions within the NiX$_2$ (X = halogen) family.

</details>


### [104] [Ground-state properties of uniaxial magnetic model for antiferromagnetic 1/1 periodic approximants to icosahedral quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07660)
*Shinji Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: No AI summary available.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No AI summary available.

Method: No AI summary available.

Result: No AI summary available.

Conclusion: No AI summary available.

Abstract: The ground-state properties of the uniaxial magnetic model in the 1/1 periodic approximant to the icosahedral quasicrystal with antiferromagnetic interactions for the nearest-neighbor bond $J_1$ and the next-nearest-neighbor bond $J_2$ are analyzed theoretically. By performing numerically-exact calculation, we have determined the ground-state phase diagram for the interaction strength $J_2/J_1$ and the magnetic anisotropy defined within the mirror plane reflecting the effect of the crystalline electric field. We have discovered non-collinear magnetic structures, whose magnetic space groups are identified as the antiferromagnetic orders $I_{\rm P}m'\bar{3}'$ and $R_{\rm R}\bar{3}$ with the magnetic propagation vector $(111)$ as well as the antiferromagnetic orders $Im'm'm'$ and $R\bar{3}'$ with the magnetic propagation vector $(000)$ and the ferrimagnetic orders $Im'm'2$, $I2'2'2$, $P2'/m'$, and $P\bar{1}$. By analyzing each ground state with degeneracy, we have revealed the magnetic as well as topological properties, which are useful to analyze experimental observations of rare-earth-based 1/1 approximants considering the effect of magnetic domains.

</details>


### [105] [Photoinduced switching of orbital-selective Mott states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07681)
*M. Lisiecki,P. Werner,J. Herbrych*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究基于双轨道 Hubbard-Kanamori 模型，利用矩阵乘积态方法证明定制脉冲可驱动轨道间电荷转移，近乎反转轨道占据并交换局域/巡游特性，从而实现光致轨道选择性的反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道选择性 Mott 相（orbital-selective Mott phase）中不同轨道的局域与巡游特性差异显著，如何主动调控并反转这种轨道选择性、进入长寿命非平衡态，是亟需微观机制的问题。

Method: 采用矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state）方法模拟初始处于轨道选择性 Mott 相的双轨道 Hubbard-Kanamori 模型，施加定制脉冲激发，并分析单光子和双光子激发通道及啁啾（chirping）效应。

Result: 定制脉冲激发引起轨道间电荷转移，使轨道占据近乎反转；单粒子谱快照显示轨道局域与巡游特性发生互换；在低频共振附近施加啁啾可增强电荷转移。

Conclusion: 研究确立了反转轨道选择性并获取多轨道体系长寿命非平衡态的微观途径。

Abstract: We investigate light-driven inversion of orbital selectivity in a two-orbital Hubbard-Kanamori model initially in an orbital-selective Mott phase. Using the matrix-product-state we show that tailored pulse excitations drive interorbital charge transfer that nearly reverses the orbital occupations. Single-particle spectral snapshots exhibit changes consistent with an interchange of the localized and itinerant characters of the orbitals. We analyze the relevant one- and two-photon excitation channels and show that chirping around the lower-frequency resonance enhances the transfer. These results establish a microscopic route to reversing orbital selectivity and accessing long-lived nonequilibrium states in multiorbital systems.

</details>


### [106] [Contrasting fate of holes introduced by Fe or In in kagome metal CoSn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07848)
*Véronique Brouet,Laure Thomarat,Patrick Le Fèvre,Ethan Saiman,Jean-Pascal Rueff,Oleg Rubel,Victor Balédent,François Bertran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该摘要研究CoSn这一具有平带（flat bands）的kagome金属（kagome metal）中，通过Co/Fe与Sn/In两种替代方式引入空穴对能带的影响；发现In替代Sn使能带近似刚性上移，而Fe替代Co导致意外的下移，并归因于Fe局域磁矩（local magnetic moment）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoSn的平带位于费米能级（Fermi level, EF）下方约0.2 eV，将其移过EF可提供研究平带对电子性质影响的罕见机会，因此需要考察在体系中引入空穴（hole doping）的可行途径。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）与Co K边X射线吸收（X-ray absorption）实验，并结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，对比Co/Fe与Sn/In两种替代方式的效果。

Result: In替代Sn引起能带向EF的近刚性上移，符合空穴掺杂预期；Fe替代Co则出现意外的下移。DFT计算复现了这两种相反趋势，并将Fe的异常行为归因于其局域磁矩：磁矩使空穴局域在Fe杂质上，并对邻近Co原子施加吸引性静电势。

Conclusion: 两种替代均使弱色散态（weakly dispersing states）接近EF：In替代时对应Co kagome格子的平带，Fe替代时对应磁性Fe杂质周围的部分局域态（partly localized states）。

Abstract: CoSn is a kagome metal with well-defined flat bands 0.2 eV below the Fermi level EF. Moving them across EF would offer a rare opportunity to investigate their impact on electronic properties. Using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and X-ray absorption at the Co K-edge, we examine two routes to introduce holes in this compound, through Co/Fe or Sn/In substitutions. Substituting In for Sn produces an almost rigid upward shift of the bands toward EF, as expected for hole doping, whereas substituting Fe for Co leads to an unexpected downward shift. Density functional theory calculations reproduce these opposite trends and trace the unusual behavior of Fe to its local magnetic moment. The moment keeps the hole on the Fe impurity and imposes an attractive electrostatic potential on neighboring Co atoms. Overall, both substitutions bring weakly dispersing states close to EF, the flat bands of the Co kagome lattice upon In substitution and partly localized states around magnetic Fe impurities upon Fe substitution.

</details>


### [107] [Non-Perturbative Resolution of Luttinger's Theorem Violations in Strongly Correlated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07933)
*Nazzareno Africani,Herbert Eßl,Alessandro Toschi,Michele Fabrizio*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文针对强关联体系中Luttinger定理（Luttinger's theorem）被违背的悖论，给出一个非微扰解，指出违背并不要求骨架图（skeleton diagram）表示失效，且Luttinger积分本身是与Luttinger-Ward泛函（Luttinger-Ward functional）相关的拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体物理中，Luttinger定理将电子数与格林函数（Green's function）对数单值性（monodromy）对应的Luttinger计数相联系，并在有限磁场下经Streda公式联系霍尔电导；强关联体系中报道的违背现象构成明显悖论，且长期存在争议。

Method: 摘要表明作者提供了一种非微扰（non-perturbative）解法，用以说明所报道的Luttinger定理违背无需骨架图表示失效，并将Luttinger积分刻画为拓扑不变量（topological invariant），考察其与Luttinger-Ward泛函及Matsubara轨迹（Matsubara trajectory）零频不连续性的关系。

Result: 结果表明，Luttinger定理的违背可在骨架图表示依然成立的前提下发生；Luttinger积分（衡量粒子数与Luttinger计数失配的量）是直接绑定于Luttinger-Ward泛函而非仅绑定于格林函数的真实拓扑不变量；该不变量与ln G(iε)的单值性一样，仅源于Matsubara轨迹的零频不连续。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了多体理论中长期存在的争议点，阐明Luttinger定理违背与骨架图表示的有效性并不矛盾，并确立了Luttinger积分的拓扑性质及其与Luttinger-Ward泛函、Matsubara轨迹零频结构的本源联系。

Abstract: Many-body physics faces an apparent paradox concerning violations of Luttinger's theorem in strongly correlated systems. The theorem relates the electron number to the Luttinger count -- the monodromy of the logarithm of the imaginary-frequency Green's function $G(iε)$ -- and, at finite magnetic field, connects the Hall conductivity to the magnetic-field derivative of the Luttinger count via the Streda formula. Here, we provide a non-perturbative solution to this paradox. We demonstrate that the reported violations of Luttinger's theorem do not require a breakdown of the skeleton diagram representation of physical quantities, thereby resolving a long-standing point of contention in many-body theory. Furthermore, we show that the Luttinger integral, which accounts for the mismatch between the particle number and the Luttinger count, is itself a genuine topological invariant directly tied to the Luttinger-Ward functional $Φ[G]$ rather than to the Green's function alone. We demonstrate that this invariant, like the monodromy of $\ln G(iε)$ itself, stems exclusively from the zero-frequency discontinuity of the Matsubara trajectory.

</details>


### [108] [Key role of orbital exchange in CoO magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08065)
*Leonid V. Pourovskii,Yuriy Mokrousov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用基于密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场（DFT+DMFT）的从头算力定理方法，构建了CoO的完整低能有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian），首次系统量化了轨道交换相互作用（orbital exchange），并证明其主导近邻项、解释磁矩倾斜与INS谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoO反铁磁相中Co离子具有显著轨道矩（orbital moment），适合轨道电子学（orbitronics）应用，但涉及轨道自由度的格点间交换相互作用（intersite exchange）缺乏定量结果，其在CoO磁性中的作用尚不明确。

Method: 采用从头算力定理方法（ab initio force-theorem approach），基于密度泛函理论（DFT）与动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）结合，推导包含轨道交换项的低能有效哈密顿量，并直接建模非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering, INS）强度。

Result: 发现可观的轨道相互作用，其中四极轨道交换（quadrupolar orbital exchange）为占主导的近邻项；所得共线反铁磁序的磁矩偏离赝立方<001>方向，轨道贡献约占总磁矩的三分之一，与实验估计一致；磁矩倾斜源于轨道交换与自旋-轨道交换（spin-orbital exchange）的联合效应，同一相互作用亦导致最低激发带的显著劈裂；INS中柱状结构可作为轨道交换的清晰指纹。

Conclusion: 轨道交换在CoO磁性中起核心作用，为理解复杂集体激发（collective excitations）及其输运性质提供基础，并对轨道电子学领域的应用具有潜在推动作用。

Abstract: Co ions in the antiferromagnetic phase of CoO are known to possess a substantial orbital moment, making this material promising for orbitronic applications. However, intersite exchange interactions involving these orbital degrees of freedom remain poorly quantified, and their role in CoO magnetism is unclear. Here, we derive a full low-energy effective Hamiltonian for CoO, including orbital exchange terms, using an ab initio force-theorem approach based on DFT+dynamical mean-field theory. We find sizable orbital interactions, with quadrupolar orbital exchange emerging as the dominant nearest-neighbor term. The collinear antiferromagnetic order obtained from the effective Hamiltonian features magnetic moments tilted away from the pseudocubic <001> direction and an orbital contribution amounting to about one-third of the total moment, in agreement with experimental estimates. We show that the moment tilting originates from the combined effect of orbital and spin-orbital exchange interactions. The same interactions also produce a prominent splitting of the lowest excitation band. By directly modeling the inelastic neutron scattering (INS) intensity, we further show that the characteristic column-like structures previously observed in CoO INS provide a clear fingerprint of orbital exchange. We also discuss possible impact of our findings for further advances in the field of complex collective excitations, their transport properties and applications in the realm of orbitronics.

</details>


### [109] [Spin supersolid in triangular lattice easy-axis spin-1/2 antiferromagnet: a fermionic parton perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08270)
*Meng Zeng,Roderich Moessner,Shu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于π通量狄拉克自旋液体（π-flux Dirac spin liquid）构建费米子部分子（parton）平均场理论，研究三角晶格上自旋-1/2易轴XXZ反铁磁体的超固（supersolid）序，并用随机相位近似（RPA）分析集体激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨三角晶格易轴XXZ反铁磁体中超固序的具体构型（Y态与Y'态）及其竞争机制，并与近期数值和实验结果对照。

Method: 采用基于π通量狄拉克自旋液体的部分子平均场理论，由在位自旋子（spinon）相互作用项与近邻各向异性自旋耦合产生平均场序；集体模式通过随机相位近似（RPA）获得。

Result: 在有限参数区域内，Y态与Y'态（序平面内旋转90°的Y态）均作为自洽解存在；平均场能量偏好Y'态，尽管两者在经典极限下严格简并。考虑集体模式涨落的高阶修正后，Y态可具有更低能量；施加指向磁矩的局域场或沿易轴的均匀场等小外场亦可在平均场层面稳定Y态。RPA给出激发谱在M点的roton极小，以及位于无能隙Goldstone模式之上的有隙赝Goldstone模式，与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 该唯象模型通过调节自旋子相互作用强度与自旋耦合各向异性可描述Y态超固序及其谱特征，并为与近期数值和实验结果的比较提供理论框架。

Abstract: We study the supersolid order of the spin-1/2 easy-axis XXZ antiferromagnet on the triangular lattice using a fermionic parton mean-field theory built on the $π$-flux Dirac spin liquid, with the mean-field order generated by an on-site spinon interaction term and nearest-neighbor (anisotropic) spin couplings. The collective modes are obtained using the random-phase approximation (RPA). In a finite parameter region, we find the Y state as a self-consistent solution, together with the competing Y' state, which is a $90^\circ$-rotated Y state inside the order plane. The mean-field energy favors Y', even though the two states are exactly degenerate in the classical limit. However, the Y state can become lower in energy when higher-order corrections due to the fluctuations of the collective modes are considered. The Y state can also be stabilized on the mean-field level by applying small external fields, which we do in two different ways: moment-aligned local field and uniform field along the easy axis. In both cases, the RPA calculation for the stabilized Y state gives a roton minimum at the $M$ point in the excitation spectrum. A gapped pseudo-Goldstone mode above the gapless Goldstone mode is obtained as well, which has also been observed experimentally. Furthermore, we investigate the effects of tuning the spinon interaction strength and the anisotropy in spin couplings in our phenomenological model. Comparison with the recent numerical and experimental results is also discussed.

</details>


### [110] [Multimagnon bound states in the polarized phase of anisotropic antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08349)
*A. El Mendili,M. E. Zhitomirsky*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作精确求解单轴反铁磁体高场极化相中的双磁子束缚态问题，并发展大自旋强耦合方法，揭示由单离子各向异性稳定的多磁子束缚态及其玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚所诱导的多极相层级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单轴反铁磁体高场极化相中单离子束缚磁子态（single-ion bound magnon states）的形成与低能激发行为，澄清束缚磁子对凝聚如何驱动向自旋向列相（spin-nematic phase）等新奇的转变。

Method: 精确求解两粒子问题以确定束缚磁子对在连续谱（continuum）上下方的色散关系；利用Gershgorin圆定理（Gershgorin circle theorem）给出束缚能的严格界限；并发展强耦合方法（strong-coupling approach）描述大自旋易轴反铁磁体中的n磁子单离子束缚态（n≤2S）。

Result: 对自旋-1易轴系统，当单离子各向异性与交换作用的比值超过阈值时，总动量为零的双磁子束缚态成为低能激发；随磁场降低，磁子对发生凝聚并驱动向自旋向列相的转变。多磁子激发的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensation）产生由单离子各向异性稳定的场诱导多极相（multipolar phases）层级结构。

Conclusion: 该理论为单轴反铁磁体中的束缚磁子与场诱导多极相提供了定量描述，并与Na2BaNi(PO4)2和Sr2CoGe2O7的近期实验比较，展示了其定量预测能力。

Abstract: We investigate single-ion bound magnon states in the high-field polarized phase of uniaxial antiferromagnets. By solving the two-particle problem exactly, we determine the dispersion of bound magnon pairs both below and above the continuum. We also obtain rigorous bounds on their binding energies by using the Gershgorin circle theorem. For spin-1 easy-axis systems, two-magnon bound states with zero total momentum emerge as low-energy excitations once the ratio of single-ion anisotropy to exchange exceeds a threshold value. As the field decreases, magnon pairs condense, driving a transition into a spin-nematic phase. Beyond the two-magnon problem, we develop a general strong-coupling approach that describes $n$-magnon single-ion bound states ($n\leq 2S$) in large-$S$ easy-axis antiferromagnets. The Bose-Einstein condensation of these multi-magnon excitations yields a rich hierarchy of field-induced multipolar phases stabilized by single-ion anisotropy. Comparisons with recent experiments on Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ and Sr$_2$CoGe$_2$O$_7$ illustrate the quantitative power of our theory.

</details>


### [111] [Direct transition from a fractional Chern insulator to a topological superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08475)
*Taige Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文给出数值证据，表明在排斥相互作用陈能带（Chern band）中，随带宽增大，填充 ν=1/3 处可由 Laughlin 分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator, FCI）连续直接转变为 c_-=3/2 拓扑超导体（topological superconductor, TSC）。作者为此发展了推广的超导-涡旋增殖（vortex proliferation）理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注的核心问题是拓扑超导电性的起源：是否存在不依赖弱耦合费米面不稳定性（Fermi-surface instability）的机制。作者试图在排斥相互作用陈能带体系中寻找分数量子陈绝缘体到拓扑超导体的直接连续相变证据，并检验其是否具有任意子（anyonic）起源。

Method: 采用数值方法在 ν=1/3 填充的排斥相互作用陈能带中调节带宽，寻找 FCI 与 c_-=3/2 TSC 之间的相变。理论上发展了推广的超导-涡旋增殖理论：磁平移（magnetic translation）将涡旋组织为六个谷（six valleys），保持平移的增殖需要引入额外规范场（gauge fields）。

Result: 数值上发现该连续直接相变，且临界点处电子关联长度（electron correlation length）在偏离参考费米面的动量处发散。所发展的理论重现了临界谱的动量结构，并刻画了发散的电子关联；当偏离公度填充进行掺杂时，理论给出超导穹顶（superconducting dome）。

Conclusion: 结果支持该拓扑超导性具有任意子起源（anyonic origin），且无需弱耦合费米面不稳定性即可出现。该工作为分数陈绝缘体与拓扑超导体之间的直接连续转变提供了数值与理论相互印证的图像。

Abstract: We find numerical evidence for a direct continuous transition from a Laughlin fractional Chern insulator (FCI) to a $c_-=3/2$ topological superconductor (TSC), driven by increasing bandwidth at filling $ν=1/3$ in a repulsively interacting Chern band. At the critical point, the electron correlation length diverges at momentum away from the reference Fermi surface. We develop a generalized theory of superconducting-vortex proliferation for this transition. Magnetic translation organizes the vortices into six valleys, so translation-preserving proliferation requires additional gauge fields. The resulting theory reproduces the momentum structure of the critical spectrum and captures the divergent electron correlation. Upon doping away from commensurate filling, the theory gives rise to a superconducting dome. These results are consistent with an anyonic origin of the topological superconductivity in the absence of a weak-coupling Fermi-surface instability.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Universal Negative Correlation between Nonreciprocity and Superconducting Transition Width in FeTe-Based Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07116)
*Annie G. Wang,Saswata Mandal,Zi-Jie Yan,Bomin Zhang,Xiaoda Liu,Hongtao Rong,Deyi Zhuo,Pu Xiao,Lok-Kan Lai,Zhiyuan Xi,Ke Wang,Chao-Xing Liu,Cui-Zu Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过分子束外延（MBE）生长多种FeTe基超导异质结构，系统测量发现磁手性各向异性系数γ对顶层材料的晶体结构、晶格常数、厚度、拓扑性与磁性均不敏感，而与超导转变宽度ΔT呈负相关，从而确立ΔT作为调控非互易输运（nonreciprocal transport）的统一参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非中心对称超导体（noncentrosymmetric superconductors）中的非互易电荷输运可探测对称性破缺与超导涨落，并支撑超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect）；然而既有研究虽多，决定其幅度大小的因素仍不明确。

Method: 采用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy, MBE）合成一大类FeTe基超导异质结构，顶层覆盖拓扑平庸与拓扑非平庸、层状与非层状、磁性与非磁性材料；通过系统性一阶与二阶谐波输运测量（first- and second-harmonic transport measurements）提取磁手性各向异性系数（magnetochiral anisotropy coefficient）γ，并结合基于无序的理论模型进行分析。

Result: γ对顶层的晶体结构、晶格常数、厚度、拓扑性与磁性不敏感，但与超导转变宽度ΔT呈负相关；对化学计量比FeTe超导薄膜的对照测量显示非互易性显著更弱，证实界面反演对称性破缺（interfacial inversion-symmetry breaking）的关键作用。

Conclusion: 在反演对称性破缺程度可比的前提下，界面无序的降低使超导转变更陡峭，并在Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）转变区间显著增强非互易输运，由此确立ΔT为跨FeTe基异质结构的统一调控参数，调和了此前相互分歧的观测结果，并为增强非互易超导响应与探索超导二极管效应提供通用框架。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal charge transport in noncentrosymmetric superconductors probes broken symmetries and superconducting fluctuations and underpins the superconducting diode effect. Despite extensive studies, the factors governing its magnitude remain unclear. In this work, we use molecular beam epitaxy (MBE) to synthesize a broad family of FeTe-based superconducting heterostructures with top layers spanning topologically trivial versus nontrivial, layered versus nonlayered, and magnetic versus nonmagnetic materials. Through systematic first- and second-harmonic transport measurements, we find that the magnetochiral anisotropy coefficient $γ$ is insensitive to the top-layer crystal structure, lattice constant, thickness, topology, and magnetism, but exhibits a negative correlation with the superconducting transition width $ΔT$. Control measurements on superconducting stoichiometric FeTe films reveal much weaker nonreciprocity, confirming the essential role of interfacial inversion-symmetry breaking. Combined with a disorder-based theoretical model, our results establish a universal relationship: for comparable inversion-symmetry breaking, reduced interfacial disorder sharpens the superconducting transition and strongly enhances nonreciprocal transport in the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) transition regime. These findings identify $ΔT$ as a unifying parameter governing nonreciprocity across FeTe-based heterostructures, reconcile previously disparate observations, and establish a general framework for engineering enhanced nonreciprocal superconducting responses and exploring superconducting diode effects in nonlinear quantum devices.

</details>


### [113] [Electrically tunable superconducting dimensionality in stripe-ordered monolayer MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07302)
*Tianyu Qiu,Ziyi Han,Fei Yang,Keyu Wei,Liguo Dou,Yong Zhang,Dekun Yang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Xuefeng Wang,Fucong Fei,Fengqi Song,Yanyan Shangguan,Shaochun Li,Jinsheng Wen,Hong Yao,Chaoxing Liu,Binghai Yan,Lin He,Xiaoxiang Xi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 单层1T′-MoTe₂中条纹电荷密度波将低能电子态重构为准一维通道，使超导关联呈现方向选择性；静电掺杂可削弱横向绝缘响应，实现由准一维向二维相位相干超导的可逆调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维度决定集体量子态（collective quantum states）的稳定性，包括库珀对（Cooper pairs）能否建立超导所需的相位相干（phase coherence）。但固体中电子维度通常由晶体结构固定，难以可逆调控，因此需要寻找可在单一晶层内电学调控电子维度的机制。

Method: 以单层1T′-MoTe₂为对象，结合拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）与扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy）识别条纹序，并利用方向性输运测量（directional transport）与隧穿谱（tunneling spectroscopy）探测各向异性的超导与绝缘行为；进一步通过静电掺杂（electrostatic doping）调控横向响应。

Result: 观测到条纹电荷密度波（stripe charge-density wave）将低能电子态重构为准一维（quasi-one-dimensional）通道；超导关联（superconducting correlations）局限于条纹方向，垂直方向呈绝缘行为。静电掺杂削弱横向绝缘响应，驱动系统进入相位相干的二维超导（two-dimensional superconductivity）态。

Conclusion: 条纹电荷序（stripe charge order）可重构超导维度，并在单一晶层内实现电学可调，表明电荷序是调控电子维度与超导相干性的有效手段。

Abstract: Dimensionality governs the stability of collective quantum states, including whether Cooper pairs can establish the phase coherence required for superconductivity. In solids, however, electronic dimensionality is usually encoded in the crystal structure and is difficult to control reversibly. Here we show evidence that monolayer 1$T^{\prime}$-MoTe$_2$ develops a stripe charge-density wave that reorganizes its low-energy electronic states into quasi-one-dimensional channels. Raman spectroscopy and scanning tunneling microscopy identify the stripe order, whereas directional transport and tunneling spectroscopy reveal superconducting correlations confined to the stripe direction and insulating behavior perpendicular to it. Electrostatic doping weakens the transverse insulating response and drives the system into phase-coherent two-dimensional superconductivity. These results show that stripe charge order can reconfigure superconducting dimensionality and make it electrically tunable within a single crystalline layer.

</details>


### [114] [Fermi-volume anomaly in quartet condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07601)
*Yixin Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文研究含四体序（quartet order）的四分量费米系统中密度与费米体积（Fermi volume）的关系，用 Luttinger-Ward 泛函对四体源相位缠绕的响应来表达费米体积计数的修正，并在一维、二维、三维中讨论其符号与大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卢廷格定理（Luttinger theorem）所给出的密度—费米体积对应在存在多体序（尤其是四体序）时是否仍然成立尚不清楚，需要刻画无配对序的规则零温分支上费米体积计数的偏离。

Method: 将费米体积计数的修正表示为 Luttinger-Ward 泛函对四体源（quartet source）相位缠绕（phase winding）的响应，其首项为四粒子态与四空穴态的加权差；对高斯顶点（Gaussian vertex）与抛物色散（parabolic dispersion）进行计算，并在固定密度下求解一维 Dyson 方程，比较由费米点得到与由低温着装谱（dressed spectra）得到的计数修正。

Result: 在参数范围内，该修正使费米体积在一维扩张、在三维收缩；二维的符号依赖动量截断（momentum cutoff）。在固定四体振幅与顶点峰值归一化下，修正强烈依赖顶点宽度，而费米子熵（fermionic entropy）变化很小；另考察了线性配对响应（linear pairing response），其可限制未配对分支延续到零温。

Conclusion: 四体序导致的费米体积计数修正具有显著的维度与截断依赖性，且对顶点宽度敏感，表明该类系统中密度与费米体积的对应关系需作实质性修正；线性配对响应则可能决定无配对分支在零温下的可延续性。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between density and Fermi volume in a four-component Fermi system with quartet order. For a regular zero-temperature branch without pair order, we express the correction to the Fermi-volume count in terms of the response of the Luttinger-Ward functional to a phase winding of the quartet source. Its leading term is a weighted difference between four-particle and four-hole states. For a Gaussian vertex and parabolic dispersion, we find that the correction expands the Fermi volume in one dimension and contracts it in three dimensions in the parameter range studied. The sign in two dimensions depends on the momentum cutoff. We solve the one-dimensional Dyson equation at fixed density and compare the counting correction obtained from the Fermi points with that evaluated from the dressed spectra at low temperature. At fixed quartet amplitude and peak normalization of the vertex, the correction depends strongly on the vertex width, whereas the fermionic entropy changes little. We also examine the linear pairing response, which can limit the continuation of the unpaired branch to zero temperature.

</details>


### [115] [Majorana corner modes and tunable braiding in an altermagnet-superconductor heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07645)
*Yingli Gao,Kai Zhang,Ling-Zheng Meng,Junpeng Hou,Ning Li,Ya-Jie Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出在蜂窝晶格的交变磁体-超导体异质结（altermagnet-superconductor heterostructure）中实现马约拉纳角模（Majorana corner modes），无需净外磁场，并利用交变磁各向异性构建Clifford门集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实现与操控马约拉纳角模通常依赖Zeeman场（Zeeman fields），但会抑制超导配对（superconducting pairing）；交变磁体（altermagnetism）提供了在零净外磁场下实现该模式的新途径，且已有研究多局限于方晶格。

Method: 构建蜂窝晶格交变磁体-超导体异质结的理论方案，采用低能边缘理论（low-energy edge theory）框架，分析不同非等价边缘上的有效低能边缘质量（effective low-energy edge masses），并给出bearded与zigzag两种边缘终止方式下角模出现的条件。

Result: 交变磁性在蜂窝晶格各非等价边缘上产生不同的有效边缘质量，与方晶格情形形成对比；在bearded和zigzag边缘终止下均确立了马约拉纳角模的涌现条件，并可通过调节交变磁各向异性（altermagnetic anisotropy）与化学势（chemical potential）显式构造完整的Clifford门集合。

Conclusion: 该异质结为马约拉纳角模的实现与操控提供了有前景的平台，并展示了其在拓扑量子计算门操作方面的潜力。

Abstract: Zeeman fields are usually required for realizing and manipulating Majorana corner modes at the cost of suppressed superconducting pairing. Altermagnetism offers a new route toward realizing Majorana corner modes under zero net external magnetic fields. Here we propose a theoretical scheme to host Majorana corner modes in a honeycomb-lattice altermagnet-superconductor heterostructure. In contrast to previous studies on square lattices, altermagnetism yields distinct effective low-energy edge masses on various inequivalent edges of the honeycomb lattice. Within a low-energy edge-theory framework, we establish the conditions under which Majorana corner modes emerge for both bearded and zigzag edge terminations. We further explicitly construct a complete set of Clifford gates by tuning the altermagnetic anisotropy and chemical potential of the heterostructure. Our results provide a promising platform for the realization and manipulation of Majorana corner modes.

</details>


### [116] [Signature of charge-3e tunneling in twisted cuprate Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07708)
*Yuying Zhu,Heng Wang,Zechao Wang,Zhiyuan Sun,Qi-Kun Xue,Ding Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作在扭转铜氧化物约瑟夫森结（twisted cuprate Josephson junctions）中观测到夏皮罗台阶（Shapiro steps）出现 nhf/3e 的新序列，表明存在以三个电子（3e）为单元的约瑟夫森隧穿过程，区别于传统 2e 库珀对（Cooper pairing）与非常规 4e 配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约瑟夫森效应夏皮罗台阶的标准公式以 2e 为基本单元，偏离该公式可能揭示非常规物理（如 4e 超导）。作者旨在检验扭转双层体系中是否存在奇数电荷单元的隧穿过程。

Method: 在扭转铜氧化物约瑟夫森结中测量微波辐照下的夏皮罗台阶序列，并系统考察其随微波频率与功率、温度以及电流退火（current annealing）的依赖关系。
