Result: 观测到 nhf/3e 的新台阶序列；频率/功率、温度与电流退火依赖性一致指向 hf/3e 作为夏皮罗台阶的基本单元，表明存在涉及三个电子（3e）的约瑟夫森隧穿。

Conclusion: 奇数电荷（3e）成束行为不同于由偶数电荷复合玻色子（composite bosons）承载的已知超导过程，凸显扭转双层体系中竞争电荷序（competing charge orders）之间的重要相互作用。

Abstract: Cooper pairing is a fundamental aspect of superconductivity and results in charge transport with a basic unit of 2e (e is the electron charge). This basic unit can manifest itself as the denominator in the formula for Shapiro steps of ac Josephson effect-nhf/(2e), where h is the Planck constant, f is the ac frequency and n is an integer. Violation of this standard formula may suggest unconventional physics, such as nhf/4e for charge-4e superconductivity. Here, we report discovery of a new sequence of nhf/3e in twisted cuprate Josephson junctions. The microwave frequency/power, temperature, and current annealing dependences of this exotic sequence consistently point to hf/3e as the unit for Shapiro steps, indicating a Josephson tunneling process involving a trio of electrons (3e). This bunching of an odd number of charges distinguishes itself from well-established superconducting processes, which are carried by composite bosons made of an even number of charges. The unusual observations highlight the important interplay between competing charge orders in twisted bilayer systems.

</details>


### [117] [Pressure-Induced Phase Transition and Superconducting Properties of LiPdH$_2$: A First-Principles Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07989)
*Zahra Alizadeh,Yue-Wen Fang,M. R. Mohammadizadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算研究了氢化物超导体 LiPdH₂ 中四方 P4/mmm 相与菱方 R̄3m 相的竞争关系，预测在约 220 GPa 发生压力诱导相变，且 R̄3m 相具有最高约 46 K（谐近似）/28 K（非谐）的超导转变温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢基超导体的超导性能与结构稳定性、氢原子轨道活性及量子效应密切相关，需厘清 LiPdH₂ 不同相之间的竞争机制以优化其超导电性。

Method: 采用第一性原理（first-principles）方法，对比四方 P4/mmm 与菱方 R̄3m 两相的热力学稳定性，并在谐近似（harmonic approximation）与非谐（anharmonic）水平下计算超导转变温度 T_c，同时考察离子量子效应（ionic quantum effects）与非谐效应。

Result: 在约 220 GPa 处发生压力诱导相变，R̄3m 相在热力学稳定性上超过 P4/mmm；R̄3m 相预测为超导，T_c 在谐近似下最高 46 K、非谐水平下 28 K。P4/mmm 相在 200 GPa 以内稳定，但电子性质更刚性、氢参与较弱，超导性能较低。

Conclusion: 离子量子效应与非谐效应在稳定 P4/mmm 相及塑造其超导性质中起决定作用，且随压力迅速减弱；结构稳定性、氢轨道活性与量子效应之间的相互作用是优化氢基超导体超导性的关键。

Abstract: We present a detailed first-principles investigation of the superconducting hydride LiPdH$_2$, focusing on the competition between its tetragonal $P4/mmm$ and rhombohedral $R\bar{3}m$ phases. Our results reveal a pressure-induced phase transition near 220 GPa, where $R\bar{3}m$ overtakes $P4/mmm$ in thermodynamic stability. The $R\bar{3}m$ phase is predicted to be superconducting, with $T_{\rm c}$ values of up to 46 K in the harmonic approximation and 28 K at the anharmonic level. In contrast, although the $P4/mmm$ phase is stable up to 200 GPa, it maintains a more rigid electronic character with weaker hydrogen involvement, resulting in lower superconducting performance. Ionic quantum and anharmonic effects play a decisive role in stabilizing $P4/mmm$ and shaping its superconducting properties, whereas these effects diminish rapidly with pressure. These findings highlight the interplay between structural stability, hydrogen orbital activity, and quantum effect in optimizing the superconductivity of hydrogen-based superconductors.

</details>


### [118] [Microscopic Origin and Josephson Signatures of $d$-Wave Superconductivity in Monolayer FeSe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08037)
*Jiong Mei,Lili Wang,Yu-Jie Sun,Kun Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验约束的多轨道理论（multiorbital theory）揭示，SrTiO₃衬底上单层FeSe中Fe d_xy轨道在费米能级附近的显著贡献与最近邻跃迁主导，可产生无节点（nodeless）d波超导配对，并建立约瑟夫森隧道（Josephson tunneling）空间干涉的选择定则用于鉴别配对对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层FeSe/SrTiO₃具有仅电子型费米面（electron-only Fermi surface）的高温超导性，其非常规配对机制尚不明确；近期约瑟夫森扫描隧道显微镜（Josephson scanning tunneling microscopy）证据提示无节点d波超导，需从微观层面厘清Fe d_xy轨道的作用。

Method: 采用实验约束的多轨道理论：以拟合准粒子干涉（quasiparticle interference）和角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy）的紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）为基础，结合巡游自旋涨落计算（itinerant spin-fluctuation calculations）与强耦合交换方法（strong-coupling exchange approaches），并推导空间约瑟夫森隧道的对称性选择定则。

Result: 模型显示费米能级附近存在显著d_xy特征且最近邻跃迁占主导，不同于铁基超导惯常的动力学阻挫（kinetic frustration）图像；两类计算均支持稳健的d波配对，其带间配对分量（interband pairing components）使准粒子谱完全有能隙（fully gapped）；选择定则给出键中心极大值与镜面对称强制的干涉零点（mirror-enforced interference zeros），可区分d波与竞争s波。

Conclusion: 对称性强制的节点线（symmetry-enforced nodal lines）在Fe位杂质附近仍存在，涡旋周围d波与s波约瑟夫森图案的显著反差在镜面保护丧失后依然保留；结果将非常规d_xy电子结构与无节点d波超导联系起来，并证明空间约瑟夫森干涉（spatial Josephson interference）在多轨道超导体配对对称性判定中的效力。

Abstract: Monolayer FeSe on SrTiO$_3$ exhibits high-temperature superconductivity with an electron-only Fermi surface, raising fundamental questions about its unconventional pairing mechanism. Motivated by recent Josephson scanning tunneling microscopy evidence for nodeless $d$-wave superconductivity, we investigate the microscopic role of the Fe $d_{xy}$ orbital using an experimentally constrained multiorbital theory. A tight-binding model fitted to quasiparticle interference and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy reveals substantial $d_{xy}$ character near the Fermi level and dominant nearest-neighbor hopping, in contrast to the conventional kinetic-frustration picture of iron-based superconductors. Both itinerant spin-fluctuation calculations and strong-coupling exchange approaches favor a robust $d$-wave pairing state, whose interband pairing components allow a fully gapped quasiparticle spectrum. We further establish a symmetry-based selection rule for spatial Josephson tunneling, revealing characteristic bond-centered maxima and mirror-enforced interference zeros that distinguish $d$-wave pairing from competing $s$-wave states. These symmetry-enforced nodal lines survive near Fe-site impurities, while a pronounced contrast between the $d$- and $s$-wave Josephson patterns persists around vortices despite the loss of mirror protection. Our results connect the unconventional $d_{xy}$ electronic structure to nodeless $d$-wave superconductivity and demonstrate the power of spatial Josephson interference for resolving pairing symmetry in multiorbital superconductors.

</details>


### [119] [Experimental Evidence for Type-I Superconductivity in $\mathrm{OsB_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08239)
*Yogesh Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过交流磁化率（AC susceptibility）、比热（specific heat）与场扫描磁滞测量，证明二硼化锇（OsB₂）实际上是罕见的非单质第一类超导体（Type-I superconductor），而非此前认为的低κ第二类超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自MgB₂超导性发现以来，金属二硼化物（metal diborides）备受关注；其中正交结构（orthorhombic，空间群Pmmn）的OsB₂此前被归类为低κ第二类超导体（low-κ Type-II superconductor，Tc≈2.1 K），但其超导类型归属存在重新检验的必要。

Method: 采用交流磁化率（χac）在静态直流磁场下的测量、比热（C/T）测量以及场扫描（field-ramped）转变的磁滞测量三类实验手段进行判定。

Result: 观测到三项主要实验特征：(i) 直流磁场下χac中出现显著的微分顺磁效应（Differential Paramagnetic Effect, DPE）峰，指示中间态（intermediate state）；(ii) 比热中出现场诱导的向发散型一级相变（first-order phase transition）的转变；(iii) 场扫描转变中显著的磁滞（magnetic hysteresis），表明存在过冷（supercooling）。

Conclusion: 上述证据共同确立OsB₂为一种罕见的非单质第一类超导体（nonelemental Type-I superconductor），修正了其此前作为低κ第二类超导体的分类。

Abstract: Since the discovery of superconductivity in $\mathrm{MgB_2}$, metal diborides have attracted significant attention. Among these, osmium diboride ($\mathrm{OsB_2}$), crystallizing in an orthorhombic structure (space group $Pmmn$) with puckered boron layers, has been classified as a low-$κ$ Type-II superconductor ($T_c \approx 2.1~\mathrm{K}$). Here, we report experimental evidence establishing that $\mathrm{OsB_2}$ is in fact a rare nonelemental Type-I superconductor. Our identification is supported by three primary experimental hallmarks: (i) a pronounced Differential Paramagnetic Effect (DPE) peak in AC susceptibility ($χ_{ac}$) measurements under static DC magnetic fields, signaling the intermediate state; (ii) a field-induced crossover to a divergent first-order phase transition in specific heat ($C/T$); and (iii) pronounced magnetic hysteresis in field-ramped transitions indicative of supercooling.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Information flow control in hybrid quantum networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07440)
*Michael Siomau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子纠缠（quantum entanglement）如何调控复杂网络（complex networks）的动力学，表明长距离纠缠链路可改变网络的稳态有效连通性（effective connectivity），并通过初始纠缠分布实现经典集体行为的平衡控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子资源（quantum resources）能够改变传统网络的动力学特征与运行行为，而缺乏直接连接的节点之间需借助长距离纠缠实现信息传递，因此有必要理解纠缠对网络集体动力学的调控机制。

Method: 通过考察信息流控制机制（information flow control mechanism），分析长距离纠缠链路对网络稳态有效连通性的修正作用，并针对线性响应控制（linear response control）与李雅普诺夫泛函可控性（Lyapunov functional controllability）两种不同控制方案推导相应的纠缠阈值（entanglement thresholds）。

Result: 长距离纠缠不仅改变网络的稳态有效连通性，还可用于抑制不期望的经典动力学（classical dynamics）；通过调整初始分布的纠缠，经典集体行为（classical collective behavior）能够得到良好平衡，并给出两类控制方法所对应的纠缠阈值。

Conclusion: 初始纠缠的分布式调控可作为平衡网络经典集体行为的有效手段，且不同控制策略对纠缠强度存在各自的阈值要求，为量子资源赋能网络动力学控制提供了依据。

Abstract: Quantum resources can change the dynamical features and operational behaviors of conventional networks. Long-distance entanglement supports information transfer between nodes lacking a direct connection. This entanglement link not only modifies the stationary effective connectivity of the network but can also be used to suppress undesirable classical dynamics. By examining information flow control mechanism, we demonstrate that classical collective behavior can be well-balanced by altering the initially distributed entanglement. We show the entanglement thresholds for two distinct control approaches: linear response control and Lyapunov functional controllability.

</details>


### [121] [Discrete quadrature spectrum of composite bosons: Weyl relation, intensity correlations, and binned homodyne detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07776)
*Itzhak Roditi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在一个可精确求解的配对模式模型（paired-mode model）中比较了三种探测手段对复合玻色子（composite boson）的判定：经典驱动、周期分箱的正交分量统计，以及强度关联g^(2)。作者引入Weyl缺陷（Weyl defect），其具有精确乘积公式及基于Schmidt矩的受控稀薄展开，并发现分箱观测量对单个Schmidt本征值穿过箱边界呈分段常数式敏感，与g^(2)行为并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由两个费米子（fermion）构成的复合玻色子在不同测量下可表现为基本粒子或复合粒子，需要辨明哪些探测手段能可靠刻画其“基本性/复合性”。摘要据此动机，考察不同观测量对同一模型的判据是否一致。

Method: 在可精确求解的配对模式模型中对比三类探针：经典驱动、周期分箱的正交分量统计（binned quadrature statistics）与强度关联g^(2)。借助Weyl缺陷的精确乘积公式与Schmidt矩稀薄展开分析分箱观测量的收敛性，并用谱滤波（spectral filter）与极性修正（polar correction）恢复精确的Pauli代数，再通过受宣布的联合投影（heralded joint projection）从两个独立副本的真空中提取逻辑单态（logical singlet）。

Result: 分箱观测量随Schmidt权重呈分段常数并在平坦谱阶梯上缓慢且非单调收敛；有限模式（Schmidt数约3）例子在Weyl缺陷为量级1时仍以百分比水平成功，而接近1的g^(2)并不能控制分箱代数。平坦谱情形下构造强烈收敛至振子对应物，其真空重叠在各尺度严格为正；对可和Schmidt振幅的无限谱，除可数个滤波截断外均收敛，截断处的正性仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: 复合玻色子的“基本性”判定依赖于所选探针：Weyl缺陷具有良好解析结构，而分箱统计与g^(2)不能等量替代。谱滤波加极性修正可恢复精确Pauli代数并实现逻辑单态提取，但无限谱下截断处正性（positivity）问题尚待解决。

Abstract: A composite boson made of two fermions can look elementary under one measurement and composite under another. In an exactly solvable paired-mode model we compare three probes: classical drives, periodically binned quadrature statistics, and intensity correlations $g^{(2)}$. The Weyl defect has an exact product formula and a controlled dilute expansion in the Schmidt moments. The binned observables are instead piecewise constant in the Schmidt weights and sensitive to single eigenvalues crossing bin edges; on the flat-spectrum ladder they converge slowly and nonmonotonically over the computed range. A spectral filter and a polar correction recover an exact Pauli algebra; a heralded joint projection then extracts a logical singlet from the vacua of two independent copies of the composite, with probability set by the accepted weight and the mixedness of the filtered reduced qubit. Finite-mode examples with Schmidt number about three succeed at the percent level despite order-one Weyl defects; near-unit $g^{(2)}$ does not control the binned algebra. For flat spectra the construction converges strongly to its oscillator counterpart, whose vacuum overlap is strictly positive at every scale; for infinite spectra with summable Schmidt amplitudes it converges at all but countably many filter cutoffs, positivity at the cutoffs studied remaining open.

</details>


### [122] [Exponential mixing time for the Davies dynamics of the quantum random energy model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08064)
*Giacomo De Palma,Emanuele Mingione*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：在Davies动力学（Davies dynamics）下，量子随机能量模型（QREM）在自旋玻璃相（β>β_c且Γ<Γ_c(β)）中的热化混合时间（mixing time）随系统规模呈指数增长，几乎必然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QREM的平衡自旋玻璃相具有冻结、局域化的低能结构，但其在开放系统动力学层面的表现缺乏严格刻画；本文旨在给出这一结构在热化时间上的严格动力学证据。

Method: 利用Davies动力学（单量子比特Pauli系统-浴耦合），结合Manai–Warzel的QREM局域化定理将稀疏深经典构型的几何转移至低能本征态，再通过Davies特有的通量估计（flux estimate）把中间谱区Gibbs权重指数小与瓶颈两侧矩阵元指数小转化为混合时间的指数下界。

Result: 对任意β>β_c及0≤Γ<Γ_c(β)（满足e^{ββ_c}=2cosh(βΓ_c(β))），混合时间以系统规模指数增长，几乎必然；加入标准零Bohr频率退相干项后界不变，且结果对所有满足细致平衡（detailed balance）与多项式上界、不假设本原性（primitivity）的Davies速率一致成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为QREM在量子转变线以下整个平衡自旋玻璃相中冻结、局域化低能结构提供了严格的开放系统动力学体现。

Abstract: We prove exponentially slow thermalization for the quantum random energy model (QREM) under Davies dynamics. The QREM is the $n$-qubit Hamiltonian $H=ΓT+U$, where $U$ assigns independent Gaussian energies with variance equal to the system size to computational-basis configurations and $T$ is the transverse field generated by single-qubit Pauli $X$ operators. Let $β_c=\sqrt{2\log 2}$ be the freezing inverse temperature of the classical random energy model. For every inverse temperature $β>β_c$ and every transverse-field strength $0\leqΓ<Γ_c(β)$, where $e^{ββ_c}=2\cosh(βΓ_c(β))$, we show that the mixing time of the dissipative Davies dynamics associated with single-qubit Pauli system--bath couplings grows exponentially with the system size, almost surely; the bound is unchanged if the standard zero-Bohr-frequency dephasing terms are included. The result is uniform over all Davies rates satisfying detailed balance and a polynomial upper bound and does not assume primitivity.
  In the spin-glass phase, the Gibbs weight is carried by a sparse family of deep classical configurations that are separated by an extensive Hamming distance. A localization theorem for the QREM [Manai, Warzel, Comm. Math. Phys. 402, 1259 (2023)] transfers this geometry to the corresponding low-energy quantum eigenstates. Consequently, the Gibbs weight of the intermediate spectral sector is exponentially small, while single-qubit bath operators have exponentially small matrix elements between the two macroscopic sides of the bottleneck. A Davies-specific flux estimate then converts these two facts into an exponential lower bound on the mixing time. This gives a rigorous open-system dynamical manifestation of the frozen, localized low-energy structure of the QREM throughout its equilibrium spin-glass phase below the quantum transition line.

</details>


### [123] [Improving Gravitationally Induced Entanglement in Interferometric Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07153)
*Alireza Maleki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对用于探测引力诱导纠缠的斯特恩—盖拉赫（Stern–Gerlach, SG）与马赫—曾德尔（Mach–Zehnder, MZ）物质波干涉仪进行系统几何优化与对比分析，推导各构型引力相位的精确解析表达式，并给出设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过探测大质量粒子间引力诱导纠缠（gravitationally induced entanglement）以检验引力的量子性质，是重要研究路径；但干涉仪几何设计对相位积累与探测可行性的影响尚缺乏系统分析。

Method: 推导SG与MZ各构型引力相位的精确解析解，进行几何优化；对比两种SG取向（线性、平行）与三种MZ取向（平移对称、镜像对称、平行臂），并开展蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）误差分析与贝尔不等式违背（Bell violation）评估。

Result: 几何优化不可或缺：忽略几何比例优化可使累积相位及纠缠可探测性降低达数个量级。平行臂SG与MZ构型相位积累最大且性能近乎等价；平移对称MZ存在相消干涉零点（blind operating points），检测失效。蒙特卡罗分析表明平行臂构型在制造公差下更稳健。

Conclusion: 几何优化、尤其是平行臂设计，应作为近期引力诱导纠缠实验的重要设计考量。

Abstract: Proposals to detect gravitationally induced entanglement between massive particles offer a promising pathway toward probing the quantum nature of gravity. We perform a systematic geometric optimization and comparative analysis of Stern--Gerlach (SG) and Mach--Zehnder (MZ) matter-wave interferometers for this purpose, deriving exact analytical expressions for the gravitational phase of each configuration. Our first key result is that geometric optimization is indispensable. For commonly assumed experimental parameters, neglecting the optimization of geometric ratios can suppress the accumulated phase, and hence the detectability of entanglement, by up to several orders of magnitude. Second, we provide a complete comparative analysis of two SG orientations (linear and parallel) and three MZ orientations (translation-symmetric, mirror-symmetric, and parallel-arms). The parallel-arms SG and MZ configurations tend to yield the largest phase accumulations and are nearly equivalent in performance, whereas the translation-symmetric MZ setup exhibits destructive-interference nulls that act as blind operating points where detection becomes ineffective. These observations highlight the importance of geometry-aware design. A Monte Carlo error analysis of realistic fabrication tolerances indicates that the parallel-arms configurations are generally more robust, maintaining low relative phase uncertainty over broad parameter intervals. Furthermore, an evaluation of Bell violations assesses the feasibility and signal-to-noise ratio of each configuration. Our results identify geometric optimization, particularly through parallel-arms designs, as an important consideration for near-term experiments targeting gravitationally induced entanglement.

</details>


### [124] [Dualities in one-dimensional quantum lattice models: Lie groups, Schur-Weyl duality and symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08293)
*Dante Bosgoed,Frank Verstraete,Laurens Lootens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一个对连续对称性（continuous symmetries）进行规范化的系统框架，并证明Schur-Weyl对偶（Schur-Weyl duality）可作为该构造在开边界条件下的自然实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将此前关于幺正融合范畴（unitary fusion category）对称模型对偶性的工作推广到连续对称性，并统一理解有限与连续对称性对偶的共同结构。

Method: 限制于紧致李群（compact Lie group）对称性，为一维量子晶格模型构造对偶模型；利用矩阵乘积算符（matrix product operators, MPO）显式构造对偶理论与（非可逆）对称性之间的 intertwiner，并通过所有可能的扭曲边界条件（twisted boundary conditions）联系对偶理论的完整谱。

Result: 当对偶作用于G对称模型的边界希尔伯特空间时，出现Peter-Weyl变换；框架还表明，有限与连续对称性的对偶均依赖于键代数（bond algebra）与晶格上对称性表示之间作为双中心化子（double centralizers）的关系，并以U(1)与SU(2)对称量子晶格模型为例加以说明。

Conclusion: 论文主张对偶非可逆自发对称性破缺（dual non-invertible spontaneous symmetry breaking）是对称张量网络算法（symmetric tensor network algorithms）背后的机制，并将该框架视为既有融合范畴对偶工作的推广。

Abstract: We present a systematic framework for gauging continuous symmetries, and show that the Schur-Weyl duality arises naturally as a realization of this construction with open boundary conditions. Restricting to compact Lie group symmetries, we construct dual models for one-dimensional quantum lattice models exhibiting those symmetries. We demonstrate that matrix product operators enable the explicit construction of intertwiners between the dual theories and their (non-invertible) symmetries. With closed boundary conditions, we can relate the full spectrum of these dual theories by considering all possible twisted boundary conditions.We illustrate how the Peter-Weyl transform appears when the duality acts on the boundary Hilbert space of a $G$-symmetric model. The presented framework generalizes previous work on dualities for unitary fusion category symmetric models. It exemplifies how dualities for both finite and continuous symmetries rely on the relation between a bond algebra and a representation of the symmetry in the lattice as double centralizers. We argue that dual non-invertible spontaneous symmetry breaking is the mechanism that underlies symmetric tensor network algorithms. We illustrate these results for $U(1)$- and $SU(2)$-symmetric quantum lattice models.

</details>


### [125] [Testing Gravitationally-Induced-Entanglement with Massive Particles and Quantum Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08313)
*Germain Tobar,Markus Aspelmeyer,Magdalena Zych*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种实验方案，利用微米级悬浮粒子与超精密核钟阵列的集体相互作用，检验引力能否使粒子与量子钟阵列产生纠缠（entanglement）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物质波干涉仪（matter-wave interferometer）仅常规检验固定质量的空间相干性，尚缺乏对主动引力质量（active gravitational mass）量子化的实验检验。

Method: 通过悬浮粒子（levitated particle）与核钟（nuclear clocks）阵列之间的集体相互作用，设计可在实验室观测引力介导纠缠（gravitationally mediated entanglement）的可行性方案。

Result: 该装置探测的是真正相对论性效应（relativistic effect），即需要内部时钟能量对主动引力质量作出贡献，而固定质量间的牛顿相互作用（Newtonian interaction）无法产生此类效应。

Conclusion: 若观测到该纠缠，将为主动引力质量的量子化性质提供证据，而这是此前任何实验均未检验过的，其意义不同于对固定质量空间相干性的常规检验。

Abstract: We present an experimental proposal to test whether gravity can entangle a levitated particle with an array of quantum clocks. By harnessing the collective interaction between a micrometer-scale particle and an array of ultra-precise nuclear clocks, our scheme offers a conceivable path to observing gravitationally mediated entanglement with quantum clocks in the laboratory. Crucially, this setup will probe a genuinely relativistic effect one that requires the contribution of internal clock energy to active gravitational mass and cannot arise from the Newtonian interaction between fixed masses. Therefore, observing this entanglement would provide evidence for the quantised nature of active gravitational mass -- which has not been tested in any prior experiment -- rather than for spatial coherence of fixed masses,which is routinely tested in matterwave interferometers

</details>


### [126] [Monopoles, Scattering and Generalized Symmetries on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07960)
*Oliver O'Brien,Atsushi Ueda,Etienne Granet,Henrik Dreyer,Frank Verstraete*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在Quantinuum H2量子计算机上实现了磁单极子散射（magnetic monopole scattering）的量子自旋链类比，通过结合局域准粒子波包制备协议与穿越对偶缺陷（duality defect）的时间演化，实现了局域粒子到非局域弦状激发（non-local string-like excitations）的高效转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算已发展到可观测有趣多体量子现象的阶段，磁单极子佯谬（magnetic monopole paradox）最近才借助广义对称性（generalized symmetries）的语言得到解决，作者希望将这一抽象理论构造在量子硬件上具体实现和验证。

Method: 作者设计了一种在基态之上制备局域准粒子波包的新协议，并将其与跨越对偶缺陷的时间演化相结合，从而将局域粒子转化为非局域弦状激发；该方案在横场Ising模型（transverse-field Ising model）及簇模型（cluster model）的对称保护拓扑（symmetry-protected topological, SPT）相中进行了演示，实验平台为Quantinuum System Model H2。

Result: 在横场Ising模型与簇模型SPT相中均成功演示了粒子到弦（particle-to-string）的转换，表明对偶缺陷与广义对称性可作为制备和探测非局域激发的有效资源。

Conclusion: 研究确立了对偶缺陷和广义对称性作为量子硬件上制备与探测非局域激发的实用资源（practical resources），为利用广义对称性框架研究多体量子现象提供了可操作的实验路径。

Abstract: Quantum computers have advanced to the stage that interesting many-body quantum phenomena can be observed. A striking example is the magnetic monopole paradox, a problem that was only solved recently using the language of generalized symmetries. Here we demonstrate a quantum spin chain analogue of magnetic monopole scattering on Quantinuum System Model H2. We implement a novel protocol for preparing a localized quasiparticle wavepacket on top of the ground state. By combining this protocol with time evolution across a duality defect, we efficiently convert local particles into non-local string-like excitations. We demonstrate this particle-to-string conversion in the transverse-field Ising model and a symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phase of the cluster model. Our results establish duality defects and generalized symmetries as practical resources for preparing and probing non-local excitations on quantum hardware.

</details>


### [127] [Polynomial-time classical simulation of boson and fermion sampling under thermal loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08444)
*Armando Angrisani,Hela Mhiri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作为热损耗（thermal loss，含纯损耗）下的玻色采样（boson sampling）与费米子采样（fermion sampling）建立统一经典模拟框架，证明在任意固定非零总损耗率下可在多项式时间内以逆多项式全变差误差完成采样，从而削弱噪声条件下量子计算优势（quantum computational advantage）的稳健性主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样实验是展示量子计算优势的主要途径，但其对实验噪声的稳健性尚未被完全理解；本文旨在系统刻画热损耗噪声下玻色与费米子采样的经典可模拟性边界。

Method: 构建热损耗（含纯损耗）下玻色与费米子采样经典模拟的共同框架，针对Fock态玻色采样、固定均匀压缩的高斯玻色采样（Gaussian boson sampling）及带magic输入的费米子采样，在Haar随机干涉仪下于总损耗率固定非零时给出多项式时间采样算法；在非Haar随机情形下引入理想输出分布的平坦性（flatness）假设，得拟多项式时间模拟；对每层恒定热损耗结合平坦性假设得到对数深度下的多项式时间模拟。

Result: 对上述三类采样任务，在任意固定非零总损耗率下实现多项式时间采样，全变差误差为逆多项式，且对Haar随机干涉仪以高概率成立；Fock态玻色采样的界同时覆盖稀疏与饱和区域，在接近完全输入填满时更强——当仅有限数目的输入模式为空时，Haar随机干涉仪对应的噪声输出随模式数增长收敛到与干涉仪无关的分布。

Conclusion: 热损耗使玻色与费米子采样在宽参数范围内经典可模拟，表明量子计算优势在这些噪声条件下缺乏稳健性；所得界的加强与干涉仪无关的收敛现象进一步揭示了损耗主导下的输出分布结构。

Abstract: Sampling experiments are a leading approach to demonstrating quantum computational advantage, yet their robustness to experimental noise is not completely understood. We develop a common framework for classical simulation of boson and fermion sampling under thermal loss, including pure loss. For Fock-state boson sampling, Gaussian boson sampling with fixed uniform squeezing, and fermion sampling with magic inputs, we obtain polynomial-time sampling at inverse-polynomial total variation error, for any fixed nonzero overall loss rate, and with high probability over Haar-random interferometers. A milder flatness assumption on the ideal output distribution suffices for quasipolynomial-time simulation without Haar randomness. With constant thermal loss per layer, the same flatness assumption gives polynomial-time simulation at logarithmic depth. Our bounds for Fock-state boson sampling cover both dilute and saturated regimes and become stronger near full input filling: when only a bounded number of input modes are empty, the noisy output for Haar-random interferometers converges, as the number of modes grows, to a distribution independent of the interferometer.

</details>


### [128] [Provable circuit-level thresholds for non-abelian topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08488)
*Yuanjie Ren,Margarita Davydova,Shankar Balasubramanian,Andreas Bauer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出在任意二维非手性拓扑序（2D non-chiral topological order）中实现量子存储（quantum memory）的显式方案，基于Turaev-Viro路径积分构造校验子提取电路（syndrome extraction circuits）与在线解码（decoding on the fly）经典算法，并证明其在校验子级局部随机噪声模型下具有阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅可推断其目标是将量子存储的实现推广至任意二维非手性拓扑序这一广泛物相类别，而非局限于特定模型。

Method: 方法包含三部分：以Turaev-Viro路径积分为基础构造校验子提取电路；设计用于在线解码（on-the-fly decoding）的经典算法；并给出向拓扑逻辑门（topological logical gates）推广的讨论方案。

Result: 方案被证明在局部随机电路级噪声模型（local stochastic circuit-level noise model）下具有阈值（threshold），即存在可容忍的噪声容限。

Conclusion: 该工作为任意二维非手性拓扑序提供了通用量子存储构造框架，并指出其可进一步扩展以实现拓扑逻辑门，为通用容错量子计算提供基础。

Abstract: We provide an explicit scheme to realize a quantum memory in any 2D non-chiral topological order. For this, we construct syndrome extraction circuits based on the Turaev-Viro path integral as well as a classical algorithm for decoding on the fly. We prove that our scheme has a threshold under a local stochastic circuit-level noise model and discuss how to generalize it to implement topological logical gates.

</details>


### [129] [FNO-Based Inverse Co-Design of Semiconducting Quantum-Dot Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06994)
*Marek Rychetsky,Valentin Duruisseaux,Freya Shah,David L. Craig,Ming Ni,Georgios Katsaros,Taylor L. Patti,Anima Anandkumar,Natalia Ares*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个可微分逆向协同设计（differentiable inverse co-design）框架，联合优化栅极电压与栅电极版图，用于静电定义半导体量子点（quantum dots）。其核心是以傅里叶神经算子（Fourier neural operator, FNO）代理模型替代传统静电仿真器，实现毫秒级可微分评估，并据此在满足制造约束下反向设计出不同形状的双量子点限制构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 栅极定义半导体量子点（gate-defined semiconductor quantum dots）是可扩展自旋量子处理器（spin-based quantum processors）的有力候选，但其工作特性依赖光刻栅极布局与外加电压等参数。现有器件架构主要依靠反复制造与实验测试逐步改进，难以穷尽设计空间，限制了对具备特定目标性质量子比特的获取。

Method: 采用半经典托马斯–费米（Thomas–Fermi）静电仿真器生成参考数据，训练傅里叶神经算子（FNO）代理模型以预测二维电荷密度场（charge-density field）。以受限电荷的目标空间分布定义目标量子点几何，并将该FNO嵌入带制造约束的梯度式设计回路，联合优化栅极电压与栅电极版图。

Result: FNO 复现电荷密度的相对 L² 测试误差仅为 0.6%，单次设计的 GPU 推理相对 CPU 物理仿真器实现约 17,000 倍加速，批量推理可进一步提升性能。嵌入梯度设计回路后，FNO 能由相同初始条件生成纵向与横向拉长的双量子点限制几何。

Conclusion: 该工作表明可对量子点形状与位置进行工程化调控，为在制造之前设计具备不同量子比特相关操作特性的器件提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Gate-defined semiconductor quantum dots are promising building blocks for scalable spin-based quantum processors, however their operational properties depend on several fabrication and control parameters, such as lithographic gate layout and applied voltages. Existing device architectures have been refined through repeated fabrication and experimental testing, producing layouts that support reliable device operation. However, this process does not exhaustively explore the design space, limiting the ability to obtain qubits with targeted properties. We address these challenges by presenting a differentiable inverse co-design framework for electrostatically defined quantum dots that jointly optimises gate voltages and the gate electrode layout. A semiclassical Thomas--Fermi electrostatic simulator provides reference data for a Fourier neural operator (FNO) surrogate, which predicts the two-dimensional charge-density field. The desired spatial distribution of the confined charge defines the target quantum-dot geometry for inverse design. The FNO reproduces charge densities with a relative $L^2$ test error of just 0.6\% and enables millisecond-scale differentiable evaluations. Relative to the CPU-based physics simulator, the FNO achieves a 17,000-fold speed-up on GPU for a single design, with further performance gains enabled by batched inference. When embedded in a gradient-based design loop with fabrication constraints, the FNO can produce both vertically and horizontally elongated double-dot confinement geometries from the same initial conditions. The ability to engineer these distinct confinement configurations demonstrates control over quantum-dot shape and position, providing a route towards designing devices with distinct qubit-relevant operating properties prior to fabrication.

</details>


### [130] [Nonlocal magic of fermions is bounded by entanglement fluctuations and is logarithmic for free fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07003)
*Raghav G. Jha,Dengyao Luo,Alexander F. Kemper,Bojko N. Bakalov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 自由费米子态虽经典可模拟，但作为量子比特态却携带大量魔法（magic），其中部分是局域幺正无法消除的非局域魔法。本文证明此前观察到的非局域 stabilizer Rényi 熵随纠缠费米子对数线性增长，仅是限制在高斯（Gaussian）幺正下的假象：允许保持费米宇称的一般局域非高斯幺正后，非局域魔法从线性降为对数增长，其根源在于纠缠谱的统计性质，由纠缠容量（capacity of entanglement）刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由费米子态经典可模拟，却具有大量 stabilizer 电路所缺的魔法资源，其中部分是非局域的。已有结果表明，若局域操作限于高斯幺正，非局域魔法随部分纠缠费米子对数 m 线性增长；本文旨在检验这一线性增长是否本质，还是高斯限制造成的人为效应。

Method: 比较局域高斯幺正与保持费米宇称的一般局域非高斯幺正将 m 个自由费米子对转化为费米子 Bell 对的效果，以保真度衰减率和非局域 stabilizer Rényi 熵为指标；并引入纠缠容量 C_E（纠缠哈密顿量的方差）作为纠缠谱权重分散程度的度量，建立非局域魔法与 C_E 的上界关系。

Result: 局域高斯幺正至多在三对时最小化非局域 stabilizer Rényi 熵，从四对起失效。一般非高斯幺正达到 Bell 对的保真度衰减不快于 1/√m，而高斯情形为指数衰减，使非局域魔法由随 m 线性降为对数增长。任何费米子态的非局域魔法至多随 C_E 对数增长；自由费米子对的 C_E 独立相加，故 C_E 仅随 m 线性增长。

Conclusion: 自由费米子态（含 Hartree–Fock 与 BCS 平均场态）的魔法几乎全部是局域的，非局域魔法远小于高斯限制下的估计，线性增长是非高斯资源缺失导致的假象。

Abstract: Free-fermion states are classically simulable, yet, written as qubits, they typically carry extensive magic, the quantum resource that stabilizer circuits lack. Part of this magic is nonlocal: local unitaries acting separately on two parts of the system cannot remove it. If these local unitaries are restricted to Gaussian ones, the nonlocal magic grows linearly with the number $m$ of partially entangled fermion pairs. We show that this growth is an artifact of the Gaussian restriction: local Gaussian unitaries minimize the nonlocal stabilizer Rényi entropy for up to three pairs, but not from four pairs on. General local non-Gaussian unitaries that respect fermion parity take $m$ free-fermion pairs to fermionic Bell pairs with a fidelity that decays at most as $1/\sqrt m$ compared to exponential decay with Gaussian unitaries, so the nonlocal magic drops from linear to logarithmic in $m$. The reason is statistical --- Bell pairs have equal Schmidt weights, that is, a fully degenerate entanglement spectrum, and a state can stay far from them only if its Schmidt weights are spread over many orders of magnitude. This spread is measured by the capacity of entanglement $C_E$, the variance of the entanglement Hamiltonian, and the nonlocal magic of any fermionic state grows at most logarithmically with $C_E$. Free-fermion pairs add to $C_E$ independently, so $C_E$ grows only linearly in $m$, and this leads to the stabilizer fidelity decreasing at most as $1/\sqrt m$. Almost all the magic of free fermions, including Hartree--Fock and BCS mean-field states, is therefore local.

</details>


### [131] [Threshold-Free Symmetry-Enriched Topological Quantum Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07017)
*Abhijat Sarma,Ruochen Ma,Cenke Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明非阿贝尔对称富化（non-Abelian symmetry enrichment）可使环面码（toric code）存储在任何键错误概率下免受非相干电噪声影响，并给出高效最小权重解码器（minimum-weight decoder），在 N≥40 时逻辑错误率随系统尺寸指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用非阿贝尔对称性保护拓扑量子存储，使其在任意非相干电噪声强度下仍保持逻辑信息，并探讨其与强到弱自发对称破缺（strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking）的联系。

Method: 方法上，将蜂窝晶格（honeycomb lattice）上的环面码与 Sp(N) 转子耦合，使电任意子（electric anyons）携带基本表示（fundamental representation），并构造高效的最小权重解码器。

Result: 结果表明，当 N≥40（一个保守的充分界）时，逻辑错误率随系统尺寸指数衰减；同一解码器还在二维相应局域退相干下排除了 Sp(N) 基本通道中的强到弱自发对称破缺。

Conclusion: 结论是这种构造可推广至其他对称群，包括 SU(N)，说明非阿贝尔对称富化是一种具有普适潜力的纠错保护机制。

Abstract: We show that non-Abelian symmetry enrichment can protect a toric code memory against incoherent electric noise at any bond error probability $p\in[0,1]$. For a toric code on the honeycomb lattice coupled to $Sp(N)$ rotors, with electric anyons carrying the fundamental representation, we construct an efficient minimum-weight decoder whose logical error probability decays exponentially with system size for $N\ge40$, a conservative sufficient bound. The same decoder also rules out strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking in the fundamental channel of $Sp(N)$ under the corresponding local decoherence in two dimensions. The construction extends to other symmetries, including $SU(N)$.

</details>


### [132] [Single-shot ground-state preparation via strongly coupled baths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07021)
*Liang-Hong Mo,Jerome Lloyd,Yuxuan Zhang,Dmitry A. Abanin,Biao Lian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出在强系统-热库耦合（strong system-bath coupling）下构造冷却协议，可在单个冷却周期内实现量子多体系统基态与热态的精确制备，并将自由费米子（free-fermion）动力学精确约化为二能级散射问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制备量子多体系统（quantum many-body systems）的基态与热态是量子模拟（quantum simulation）的核心任务；已有耗散协议（dissipative protocols）多依赖弱耦合（weak coupling），导致冷却缓慢。作者意在突破弱耦合限制、实现快速且高精度的态制备。

Method: 采用动态拉曼式调节辅助能级（ancilla energy levels）并结合时变耦合（time-dependent couplings），优化冷却误差与物理时间之间的权衡；对一般自由费米子系统将动力学精确映射为二能级散射问题，并据此构造“Möbius-Ingham”协议族，以抑制非绝热加热（non-adiabatic heating）。

Result: 在无能隙谱（gapless spectra）下达到最优误差标度，在有能隙谱（gapped spectra）下随周期时长呈近指数级抑制主导加热误差；自由费米子基准中处于稳态误差-冷却时间的 Pareto 前沿（Pareto frontier）；在相互作用 Ising 链、t-V 链及二维 Fermi-Hubbard 模型中亦表现有效，并可扩展至单周期制备自由费米子热态（Gibbs state）。

Conclusion: 强耦合单周期冷却协议为基态与热态制备提供了高效方案，兼具理论可解析性（二能级散射约化）与跨模型（自由与相互作用体系）的数值有效性，并可推广至 Gibbs 态制备。

Abstract: Preparing ground and thermal states of quantum many-body systems is a central task in quantum simulation. Dissipative protocols have emerged as a promising solution for both near-term and fault-tolerant hardware, but most constructions rely on weak system-bath coupling, leading to slow cooling. Here, we construct cooling protocols at strong system-bath coupling, and demonstrate accurate state preparation within even a single cooling cycle. Our protocols combine dynamically ramped ancilla energy levels with time-dependent couplings to optimize the trade-off between cooling error and physical time. For a general class of free-fermion systems, we reduce the dynamics exactly to a two-level scattering problem: ground-state cooling becomes a control problem of maximizing resonant excitation transfer while suppressing non-adiabatic heating. We then construct a family of ``Möbius-Ingham'' protocols, which reach optimal error scaling for gapless spectra and almost-exponential suppression of the leading heating error for gapped spectra, as a function of the cycle period. In our free-fermion benchmarks, Möbius-Ingham cooling lies on the Pareto frontier between steady-state error and cooling time among the controls tested. We further demonstrate its effectiveness in interacting Ising and $t$-$V$ chains and the two-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard model. Finally, we extend our construction to Gibbs state preparation, showing how free-fermion thermal states can be prepared in a single cycle.

</details>


### [133] [Ground-State Dressing and Delayed Feedback of a Chiral Giant Atom in Ultrastrong Waveguide QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07036)
*Giuseppe Musolino,Daniele Lamberto,Salvatore Savasta,Federico Roccati*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究超强耦合于一维波导两个空间分离点位的手性巨原子（chiral giant atom）；当相对耦合相位为π/2时，总谱密度与耦合点间距无关，而方向性分量并非如此，且虚光子云保持手性，可在耦合突断时释放为方向性辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究巨原子（giant atom）与一维波导多点耦合中，间距与相对耦合相位如何影响谱密度、基态缀饰与自发辐射等超强耦合（ultrastrong coupling）效应及其手性（chirality）。

Method: 采用变分极化子变换（variational polaron transformation）处理手性巨原子与一维波导的超强耦合，并推导极化子表象下的自发辐射动力学。

Result: 在相对耦合相位π/2处，总谱密度及其方向性分量表现出不同的间距依赖性：总谱密度与耦合点间距无关；重整化跃迁频率（renormalized transition frequency）与总基态缀饰（ground-state dressing）继承这一无关性，而虚光子云（virtual-photon cloud）仍保持手性。耦合突然关断时，该光子云以方向性辐射形式释放。

Conclusion: 在忽略局域光子势（local photonic potential）后，极化子框架下的记忆核（memory kernel）在同一相位下亦与距离无关，表明π/2相位可使若干关键物理量对耦合点间距不敏感。

Abstract: We study a chiral giant atom ultrastrongly coupled to a one-dimensional waveguide at two spatially separated points. At a relative coupling phase π/2, the total spectral density is independent of the distance between the coupling points, whereas its directional components are not. Using a variational polaron transformation, we show that the renormalized transition frequency and the total ground-state dressing inherit this independence, while the virtual-photon cloud remains chiral. A sudden switch-off of the coupling releases this cloud as directional radiation. We also derive the spontaneous-emission dynamics in the polaron frame, showing that, after neglecting the local photonic potential, the memory kernel becomes distance independent at the same phase.

</details>


### [134] [Thermally-driven quantum reservoir computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07187)
*Thiago Werlang,Daniel Valente*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种以热浴时变温度编码数据注入的储层计算（reservoir computing）范式，推导了任意固定读出（readout）的非线性响应精确表达式，并证明记忆-非线性权衡（memory-nonlinearity trade-off）是结构必然性而非可调动力学区制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发热限制计算，但耗散等物理限制因素原则上可转为资源；储层计算中能量耗散正是衰减记忆（fading memory）的来源，故其探索将数据直接编码于热浴温度、构建"热原生"量子储层计算的可能性。

Method: 将数据注入编码在热浴的时间依赖温度上；推导任意固定读出的非线性响应精确表达式；证明记忆-非线性权衡的结构必然性（注入数据的同一耦合同时强制平衡化）；并以驱动单量子比特（单自由参数）与耦合双量子比特（离散对称性破缺）为例进行说明。

Result: 单量子比特案例以单一自由参数验证两项结果；耦合双量子比特中离散对称性破缺使十五维空间对单一可观测者开放，但该可观测者仅能恢复略过半（just over half）的信息。

Conclusion: 结果反驳"大希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）必然带来计算能力"的误解；记忆-非线性权衡应从储层动力学转移至测量通道（measurement channel），可观测者选择与热浴工程（bath engineering）而非哈密顿量控制，成为热原生量子储层计算机的物理杠杆。

Abstract: Heat generation limits computation, classical and quantum, from single logic gates to entire data centers. However, an in principle limiting physical factor can sometimes be turned into a resource. In reservoir computing, energy dissipation is what gives a reservoir its fading memory. Here, we introduce a paradigm where the data injection itself is encoded on the time-dependent temperature of a thermal bath. We derive an exact expression for the nonlinear response of any fixed readout, and prove a rigorous memory-nonlinearity trade-off which turns out to be a structural necessity, rather than a tunable dynamical regime, since the same coupling that injects data also enforces equilibration. We illustrate both results with a driven qubit, using a single free parameter, and with a pair of coupled qubits, where the breaking of a discrete symmetry opens a fifteen-dimensional space to a single observable, yet that observable alone recovers just over half of it. This refutes the misconception that large Hilbert spaces guarantee computational power. Our results relocate the memory-nonlinearity trade-off from the reservoir's dynamics to its measurement channel, pointing to observable choice and bath engineering (instead of Hamiltonian control) as the physical levers for a thermally-native quantum reservoir computer.

</details>


### [135] [Frustration-freeness is decidable but unprovable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07337)
*Naoto Shiraishi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究"给定哈密顿量是否对所有系统尺寸都无阻挫（frustration-free）"这一判定问题，证明其可判定性依赖于固定的局域希尔伯特空间维度，且存在无法在ZFC公理体系内证明的无阻挫哈密顿量，并通过截断尺寸不可计算性调和这两个看似矛盾的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无阻挫性（frustration-free property）在AKLT模型、Kitaev环面码（toric code）等对称保护拓扑相与拓扑序范式中具有核心地位，但"给定哈密顿量是否对所有系统尺寸均无阻挫"这一基本问题的可判定性此前未被确立。

Method: 采用数学证明方法：一方面证明对任意固定的局域希尔伯特空间维度（local Hilbert space dimension）存在与哈密顿量无关的有限系统尺寸截断（cutoff），使该尺寸处的无阻挫性蕴含所有尺寸的无阻挫性；另一方面构造一个无阻挫性无法在ZFC公理内证明的哈密顿量。

Result: 得到两个看似矛盾的结论：无阻挫性对任意固定局域维度是可判定的（decidable）；同时存在无阻挫性在ZFC中不可证明的哈密顿量。二者通过证明该截断虽有限但作为局域维度的函数是不可计算的（uncomputable）而得以调和。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了量子多体物理中可判定性（decidability）与可证明性（provability）之间存在意想不到的根本性区分。

Abstract: The frustration-free property is valuable in quantum many-body systems, which states that its ground states simultaneously minimize all local interaction terms, even when these terms act on overlapping degrees of freedom. Prominent examples include the AKLT model and Kitaev's toric code model, which serve as paradigmatic models of symmetry-protected topological phases and topological order, respectively. A fundamental question is whether a given Hamiltonian is frustration-free for all system sizes. Here we establish two seemingly contradictory results concerning this decision problem. First, we prove that frustration-freeness is decidable for any fixed local Hilbert space dimension. Specifically, there exists a finite, Hamiltonian-independent system-size cutoff such that frustration-freeness at this cutoff size guarantees frustration-freeness at all system sizes. Second, on the other hand, we also show that there exists a frustration-free Hamiltonian whose frustration-freeness cannot be proved within the standard axioms of mathematics (ZFC). We reconcile these seemingly contradictory results by showing that the cutoff, although finite for each local Hilbert space dimension, is uncomputable as a function of that dimension. Our findings reveal an unexpected fundamental distinction between decidability and provability arising in quantum many-body physics.

</details>


### [136] [Higher-order topological quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07401)
*Motohiko Ezawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种耦合光子晶体（photonic crystal）的二阶拓扑量子电池（second-order topological quantum battery），在含光学损耗（optical loss）与选择性非线性增益（nonlinear gain）的非厄米（non-Hermitian）条件下，实现量子化遍历能（ergotropy）与完美功提取，并借助镜像对称（mirror symmetry）保护的相位同步实现集体储能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池（quantum battery）作为利用叠加（superposition）与相干性（coherence）等真实量子特性的下一代储能器件，其在现实非厄米损耗条件下的拓扑保护储能与功提取机制尚待阐明。

Method: 构建基于呼吸-Kagome模型（breathing-Kagome model）的光子晶体，将二阶拓扑量子电池与之耦合；在所有格点引入光学损耗，仅在输出格点选择性引入非线性增益，从而在真实非厄米条件下考察系统的拓扑相变（topological phase transition）。

Result: 系统在遍历能与充电功率（charging power）上均展现出清晰的拓扑相变；在拓扑相中实现完美功提取，其遍历能最大值呈量子化（quantized）特征并可作为良好的拓扑相指标；三角形几何将能量相干地分配至两个量子电池，镜像对称保护的相位同步（phase synchronization）带来集体储能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，非厄米条件下的二阶拓扑量子电池可实现量子化的完美功提取与集合式协同储能，为拓扑量子电池的设计与拓扑相判别提供了可行范式。

Abstract: A quantum battery is a next-generation energy-storage device that exploits genuinely quantum properties such as superposition and coherence. We propose a second-order topological quantum battery coupled to a photonic crystal described by the breathing-Kagome model, where optical loss is present on all lattice sites while nonlinear gains are introduced selectively at the output sites. Under these realistic non-Hermitian conditions, the system exhibits a clear topological phase transition manifested in both the ergotropy and the charging power. Remarkably, perfect work extraction is realized in the topological phase, where the maximum value of the ergotropy is quantized and acts as a good topological-phase indicator. The triangular geometry coherently distributes energy into two quantum batteries, where mirror-symmetry-protected phase synchronization gives rise to collective energy storage.

</details>


### [137] [Coupling between molecular exchange qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08058)
*Filippo Troiani,Jason S. R. McCoombs,Athanassios K. Boudalis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将自旋三角形（spin triangle）中的手性（chirality）与部分自旋和（partial spin sum）等标量自由度研究拓展至由两个自旋三角形通过交换或偶极相互作用耦合形成的二聚体（dimer）；在弱耦合极限下建立有效的两比特（two-qubit）图像并给出相应哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋三角形已被视为定义和操控手性、部分自旋和等标量自由度的候选体系，但由两个三角形构成的耦合二聚体是否可在弱耦合下由简化的两比特模型描述尚待阐明。

Method: 在两个自旋三角形经交换相互作用（exchange interaction）或偶极相互作用（dipolar interaction）耦合的框架下，论证弱耦合极限（weak-coupling limit）可引入有效两比特模型，并将有效哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）表示为三角形间底层物理相互作用的函数；随后将该两比特哈密顿量与微观哈密顿量（microscopic Hamiltonian）进行基准对比（benchmark）。

Result: 推导出了有效两比特哈密顿量随三角形间物理相互作用变化的显式表达式，并与微观模型进行了对照验证；进一步讨论了三角形间耦合对不同手性或部分自旋和状态之间自旋-电跃迁（spin-electric transition）的可观测效应。

Conclusion: 弱耦合自旋三角形二聚体可由有效两比特模型刻画，该模型为分析三角形间耦合如何调控以标量自由度为编码对象的状态及其自旋-电跃迁提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Spin triangles are promising systems for defining and manipulating scalar degrees of freedom, such as chirality, partial spin sums, or combinations between the two. Here we extend this analysis to dimers formed by pairs of spin triangles, coupled by exchange or dipolar interactions. We show that the weak-coupling limit warrants the introduction of an effective two-qubit picture, and derive the expressions of the related Hamiltonians as a function of the underlying physical interaction between the triangles. We benchmark this two-qubit Hamiltonian against the microscopic one, and discuss the observable effects of the inter-triangle coupling on the spin-electric transitions between states with different chiralities or partial-spin sums.

</details>


### [138] [Non-Unitary Quantum Butterfly Effect: Theory and Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08152)
*Paul Argyle,Djamil Lakhdar-Hamina,Sarah H. Miller,Victor Galitski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将时序外对易子（out-of-time-order correlator, OTOC）推广至非幺正（non-unitary）系统，在含局域投影测量的Haar随机砖墙量子比特/量子dit线路中解析与数值计算无限温度系综平均OTOC，并借助转移矩阵（transfer matrix）与蒙特卡洛方法揭示其指数衰减及因果扰乱前沿（causal scrambling front）内信号衰减的非幺正特性，同时在Quantinuum离子阱量子计算机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTOC是刻画量子信息扰乱（quantum scrambling）与多体动力学的重要工具，但既有研究主要针对幺正（unitary）演化；实际硬件中存在测量等非幺正噪声，因此需要一个能刻画非幺正信道对OTOC衰减影响的推广框架，并为硬件噪声的累积效应提供定量基准。

Method: 针对掺入局域投影测量（local projective measurement）的Haar随机砖墙量子dit线路（Haar-random brickwork qudit-circuit），计算无限温度下的系综平均OTOC；采用转移矩阵（transfer matrix）解析求解其渐近行为，并发展蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）框架数值计算OTOC；随后通过量子模拟及Quantinuum囚禁离子量子计算机直接估计OTOC。

Result: OTOC在渐近意义上呈指数衰减，且衰减速率取决于测量速率；在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下、测量稀疏时，OTOC近似呈高斯形状。唯象上对应因果扰乱前沿的形成：与幺正情形不同，信号在因果锥（causal cone）内部即发生衰减。实验上，尽管存在硬件噪声，仍分辨出幺正与非幺正两种OTOC唯象行为。

Conclusion: 解析结果刻画了局域非幺正信道（local nonunitary channel）如何影响OTOC衰减，为一大类硬件噪声对多体动力学的累积效应提供了定量基准；所建立的解析、蒙特卡洛与量子模拟/量子硬件相结合的方法体系可推广用于非幺正量子动力学研究。

Abstract: We present a generalization of the out of time order correlator (OTOC) for non-unitary systems. For a Haar-random brickwork qudit-circuit, peppered with local, projective measurement, we calculate the infinite-temperature, ensemble-average OTOC. Utilizing transfer matrix machinery, we find that the OTOC asymptotically decays exponentially and that the decay depends on the rate of measurement. In the thermodynamic limit, with sparse measurement, the OTOC approximately exhibits a Gaussian shape. Phenomenologically, this corresponds to the development of a causal scrambling front where unlike the unitary case, the signal decays in the bulk of the causal cone. We develop a Monte Carlo framework to compute the OTOC. We then estimate the OTOC directly via quantum simulation and on Quantinuum's trapped-ion quantum computer. We resolve the unitary and non-unitary OTOC phenomenology despite the presence of hardware noise. Our analytic results characterize how local nonunitary channels affect OTOC decay, providing a quantitative benchmark for the cumulative effects of a broad class of hardware noise on many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [139] [Disorder-blind Mpemba acceleration of quantum state preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08424)
*M Nishanth,Shreyas Sadugol,Payman Mahmoudi,Alexander Yosifov,Aditya Iyer,Roopayan Ghosh,Jinzhao Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在无相位退相干的无序 XX 链（dephased disordered XX chain）中，一种仅依赖链几何与粒子数的“盲”初态制备（blind preparation）无需知晓无序实现或其分布即可实现强姆潘巴效应（strong Mpemba effect），并给出失效概率的系综级预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用强姆潘巴效应加速弛豫需抑制最慢模式，通常依赖对系统的先验知识；但在无序系统（disordered systems）中该模式样本依赖，固定制备往往残留慢模式重叠（slow-mode overlap），因此需要一种无需无序信息的普适制备策略。

Method: 构造由干净链（clean chain）设计的盲制备，仅使用链几何与粒子数。在强退相干极限下将密度动力学映射为电阻链（resistor chain），并用精确的逆生成元恒等式（inverse-generator identity）把残余重叠与空间电阻分布（spatial resistance profile）联系起来。

Result: 盲制备可在典型样本中虽起始更远离稳态却最终反超参考态。发现两个阈值：有限平均电阻即可实现扩散，但残余重叠仅在电阻方差有限时才随链长按 L^{-1/2} 衰减，稀有弱键（rare weak links）会减慢该衰减；并预测盲制备胜出的样本比例。强退相干下的有限尺寸数值计算（含相互作用）支持两种标度区间。

Conclusion: 结果将残余重叠与电阻统计量（均值与方差）定量关联，给出盲制备可靠性的系综级判据，并提供制备电路与密度读出方案以实验检验残余重叠。

Abstract: Using the strong Mpemba effect to accelerate relaxation by initial-state design requires suppressing the slowest mode, which typically needs prior knowledge of the system. However, in disordered systems, this mode is sample-dependent, so a fixed preparation generally retains a slow-mode overlap. We show that a ``blind'' preparation, designed from the clean chain without knowledge of the disorder realisation or its distribution, can start farther from stationarity yet overtake a reference in typical samples of a dephased disordered XX chain. It requires only the chain geometry and the particle number. At strong dephasing, the density dynamics maps to a resistor chain, and an exact inverse-generator identity links the residual overlap to the spatial resistance profile. We find two distinct thresholds: a finite mean resistance suffices for diffusion, but the residual overlap decays as $L^{-1/2}$ with chain length $L$ only when the resistance variance is finite; rare weak links slow this decay. We predict the fraction of samples in which the blind preparation wins, which is an ensemble-level measure of its reliability. Finite-size calculations support both scaling regimes with interactions at strong dephasing. We give preparation circuits and a density readout for the residual overlap.

</details>

### [140] [Constrained Hamiltonian Formulation of Kinetic Theory for Relativistic Spin-$1/2$ Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08213)
*Jie Zhou,Ying Shan Zhao,Yifeng Sun*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文利用约束哈密顿形式（constrained Hamiltonian formalism）为外电磁场中的相对论性自旋-1/2粒子构建了无碰撞动理学理论，给出协变动理学方程、非正则泊松括号（noncanonical Poisson brackets）与不变测度，并导出电荷、熵及能量-动量/角动量的局域守恒与平衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 描述非平衡自旋输运需要一种能一致纳入自旋动力学（spin dynamics）与相空间结构（phase space structure）的动理学理论，而现有框架难以形式一致地同时处理二者。

Method: 采用约束哈密顿形式（constrained Hamiltonian formalism）处理外电磁场中的相对论性自旋-1/2粒子，推导协变动理学方程及其在独立物理相空间变量下的形式，并给出相应的非正则泊松括号（noncanonical Poisson brackets）与不变测度（invariant measure）。

Result: 对有质量粒子，自旋约束与电磁场的相互作用使粒子速度一般不平行于其动能动量（kinetic momentum），并修正动量与自旋演化；约化相空间中的不变测度即使在场含时情况下也满足精确的Liouville恒等式，进而给出电荷与Boltzmann熵的局域守恒，以及能量-动量和总角动量的平衡方程。全局平衡条件与协变统计力学（covariant statistical mechanics）及Wigner函数方法一致；无质量情形退化为标准的手征动理学方程（chiral kinetic equations）与相空间测度。

Conclusion: 该框架为非平衡条件下的自旋输运提供了自洽的哈密顿描述，并兼容已有协变统计力学、Wigner函数及手征动理学结果。

Abstract: Describing spin transport away from equilibrium requires a kinetic theory that consistently incorporates spin dynamics and phase space structure. We construct such a collisionless kinetic theory for relativistic spin-$1/2$ particles in external electromagnetic fields using the constrained Hamiltonian formalism. The formulation yields both a covariant kinetic equation and its form in independent physical phase space variables, together with the associated noncanonical Poisson brackets and invariant measure. For massive particles, the interplay between the spin constraints and the electromagnetic field makes the particle velocity generally nonparallel to its kinetic momentum and modifies the momentum and spin evolution. The invariant measure in the reduced phase space obeys an exact Liouville identity even for time-dependent fields. This identity and the equations of motion give local conservation of charge and Boltzmann entropy, as well as balance equations for energy-momentum and total angular momentum. The global equilibrium conditions are consistent with covariant statistical mechanics and the Wigner function approach. The massless construction recovers the standard chiral kinetic equations and phase space measure. This framework provides a consistent Hamiltonian description of spin transport beyond equilibrium.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>
