### [141] [Constraining tau-sterile neutrino mixing with high-energy astrophysical neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05170)
*Mauricio Bustamante,Qinrui Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用TeV–PeV天体物理中微子的味成分（flavor composition）测量，可在全质量范围内限制惰性中微子与ν_τ的混合参数|U_τ4|²，当前IceCube数据给出90%置信水平下|U_τ4|²<0.88，未来望远镜有望将其压低至0.12–0.14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惰性中微子（sterile neutrino）是否与三个活跃中微子混合仍是开放问题，其中与τ中微子的混合|U_τ4|²约束最弱，因为地面实验只能间接且在有限惰性中微子质量范围内探测。天体物理中微子提供了不同的探测途径。

Method: 天体源几乎不产生ν_τ，但宇宙学距离上的振荡（oscillations）会使约三分之一转化为ν_τ；惰性中微子混合会减少到达地球的ν_τ比例。作者在源成分自由的前提下，拟合IceCube测量的中微子味成分以限制|U_τ4|²。

Result: 拟合给出90%置信水平下|U_τ4|²<0.88；与ν_e或ν_μ的惰性混合几乎不可探测。结合现有及计划的望远镜（含IceCube-Gen2与HUNT），该上限到2040年可降至0.24–0.29，2050年降至0.12–0.14。

Conclusion: 天体物理中微子味成分对|U_τ4|²敏感且与惰性中微子质量无关。到2050年，此类上限可与部分现有地面实验限制相当，但仍不及最强的地面约束。

Abstract: Whether a sterile neutrino mixes with the three active ones remains an open question. Its mixing with the tau neutrino, $|U_{τ4}|^2$, is the least constrained, since terrestrial experiments probe it only indirectly and over limited ranges of sterile-neutrino mass. We show that TeV-PeV astrophysical neutrinos probe it differently. Their sources make almost no $ν_τ$, but oscillations over cosmological distances turn about one third of them into $ν_τ$ before they reach Earth. Mixing with a sterile neutrino would make these $ν_τ$ scarcer, lowering the $ν_τ$ fraction that neutrino telescopes measure, whatever the sterile-neutrino mass. In contrast, sterile mixing with $ν_e$ or $ν_μ$ is nearly undetectable. Fitting the flavor composition measured by IceCube, with the composition at the sources left free, we find $|U_{τ4}|^2 < 0.88$ at 90% C.L. Combining IceCube with present and planned neutrino telescopes, including IceCube-Gen2 and HUNT, could lower this limit to 0.24-0.29 by 2040 and to 0.12-0.14 by 2050. By 2050, these limits would match some present terrestrial limits, though not the strongest.

</details>


### [142] [QED corrections to $e^+e^-\toπ^+π^-(γ)$ with amplitude-level resummation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07067)
*Stéphane Delorme,Charles Earnshaw,Frank Krauss,Alan Price,Nora Salone,Andrzej Siódmok*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文在 KKMC 与 SHERPA 两个独立蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）事件发生器中，对 $e^+e^-\to\pi^+\pi^-(\gamma)$ 过程给出 QED 辐射修正的高精度实现与比对。一阶截面两者一致至 $\lesssim0.04\%$；二阶修正带宽随能量增大，从 CMD 能区的 $\sim0.005\%$ 升至 10 GeV 的 $\sim0.9\%$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足高精度对撞实验对 $e^+e^-\to\pi^+\pi^-(\gamma)$ 截面与分布的需求，亟需评估 QED 辐射修正的理论不确定度，并通过独立实现之间的交叉检验验证其可靠性。

Method: 采用点状标量 $\pi$ 介子并乘以 $\pi$ 介子形状因子（pion form factor），将全阶软光子指数化（soft-photon exponentiation）与严格的一、二光子树图振幅（tree amplitudes）结合，纳入含初末态相干干涉的完整单圈修正（one-loop correction），并在二阶计入光子双圈顶点（two-loop vertices）及单光子发射的严格单圈修正；理论系统参数用于估计缺失的高阶项。

Result: 一阶基准截面在两个发生器间一致至 $\lesssim0.04\%$，各分布在统计精度内相符；二阶修正带在 CMD 约 $\sim0.005\%$，4 GeV 处 $\sim0.4\%$，10 GeV 处 $\sim0.9\%$。

Conclusion: 两套独立实现的高阶一致性支持将该 QED 修正框架用于高精度截面计算；同时针对辐射返回（radiative return）中一阶修正为量级一的情形提出了新的处理规则。

Abstract: We present QED corrections to $e^+e^-\toπ^+π^-(γ)$ in two independent Monte Carlo event generators, KKMC and SHERPA, for point-like scalar pions dressed with the pion form factor. All-order soft-photon exponentiation is combined with exact one- and two-photon tree amplitudes, the complete one-loop correction including the initial-final boxes with coherent interference, and at second order the photonic two-loop vertices and the exact one-loop correction to one-photon emission. At first order their fiducial cross sections agree to $\lesssim0.04\%$ and their distributions within statistical precision. The second-order band is $\sim0.005\%$ for CMD, $\sim0.4\%$ at $4$ GeV and $\sim0.9\%$ at $10$ GeV. Theory nuisance parameters estimate missing higher orders, and we construct a new rule for treating radiative return, where first-order corrections are of order one.

</details>


### [143] [Testing the Pseudo-Dirac Nature of Atmospheric Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07281)
*Olivia M. Bitter,André de Gouvêa,Srubabati Goswami,Supriya Pan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究DUNE类探测器利用大气中微子通量探测中微子“赝狄拉克（pseudo-Dirac）”性质的灵敏度，通过对能谱与角分布的修正确定可探测参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赝狄拉克中微子（pseudo-Dirac neutrinos）具有两个近简并、强混合的活性-惰性态之间极小的质量平方差，对应极长的振荡长度，可作为探针检验中微子本质；大气中微子覆盖从数十公里到地球直径的宽广基线，因此适合探测此类振荡。

Method: 以类DUNE（Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment）探测器为对象，通过测量大气中微子通量，考察与ν3态相关的一个新振荡频率对中微子能量谱和角分布的修正，并据此确定可探测的参数空间。

Result: 研究给出了大气中微子在类DUNE探测器下对赝狄拉克参数空间的探测范围，并分析了这些测量对由较小质量平方差表征的次主导“太阳”振荡（solar oscillations）的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 大气中微子测量可用于检验中微子的赝狄拉克本质，并同时约束次主导太阳振荡的影响。

Abstract: Pseudo-Dirac neutrinos are characterized by extremely small mass-squared differences between two nearly degenerate, strongly-mixed, active-sterile states. These differences are associated with new very-long oscillation lengths and provide a probe of the nature of neutrinos. Atmospheric neutrinos can probe these oscillations as they allow access to a broad range of baseline distances, from tens of kilometers to the Earth's diameter. We investigate the sensitivity of an experiment like the Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE) to the pseudo-Dirac nature of neutrinos through measurements of the atmospheric neutrino flux. We concentrate on the modifications to the atmospheric neutrino energy and angular spectra induced by one new oscillation frequency associated with the $ν_3$ state and determine the parameter space that can be probed with atmospheric neutrinos at a DUNE-like detector. We also comment on the sensitivity of these measurements to the sub-leading "solar" oscillations, which are also characterized by a relatively small mass-squared difference.

</details>


### [144] [Natural Dark Matter in Generalized No-scale $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ at the HL-LHC and LUX-ZEPLIN Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07385)
*Tianjun Li,James A. Maxin,Dimitri V. Nanopoulos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 结合翻转 $SU(5)$ 模型 $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ 与广义无标度超引力（generalized No-Scale SUGRA），亚 200 GeV 中性微子与右手超轻子的 bulk 区域仍可行，且其自然暗物质（NDM）可同时被 LUX-ZEPLIN 与 HL-LHC 检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: bulk 区域曾是 1980 年代超对称（SUSY）先驱的发现目标，但此后被认为已被排除，迫使采用精细调节的暗物质方案以满足精确的遗迹丰度测量；本文重新审视该区域是否仍可行。

Method: 将 $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$（翻转 $SU(5)$ 加上弦论导出的 TeV 标度额外 $10+\overline{10}$ 矢量样多重态与带电矢量样单态 flippon）与广义无标度超引力相结合，并给出与现有实验（含 LHC Run 3 的胶子微子与标夸克限制）一致、可在 LUX-ZEPLIN 与 HL-LHC 观测的参数空间。

Result: 该组合解决了 SUSY 电弱精细调节问题，并包含仅通过湮灭即满足 Planck 卫星 5$\sigma$ 观测的自然暗物质；bulk 中预测的亚 200 GeV 中性微子与右手超轻子并未超出探测范围，同时质量大于 600 GeV 的电弱微子也被允许。

Conclusion: 亚 200 GeV 中性微子与右手超轻子的 bulk 区域在本模型中仍然可行；LUX-ZEPLIN 千日实验（预计 2026-27 年事件）将推动 HL-LHC（2030 年运行）加以确认。

Abstract: Is a supersymmetric bulk region with sub-200 GeV neutralinos and right-handed sleptons still feasible? Initially the bulk was the discovery target of the supersymmetry (SUSY) pioneers in the 1980s, though reality crashed those ambitions over the subsequent years and decades, presumptively excluding these light neutralinos and sleptons, inviting finely tuned dark matter scenarios to satisfy emerging high-precision relic density measurements. The answer to this important question is $yes$, if we consider the GUT model $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$, namely, flipped $SU$(5) supplemented with a string derived TeV-scale extra $10+\overline{10}$ vector-like multiplet and charged vector-like singlet $(XE,XE^c)$, dubbed flippons. We pair $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ with effective supernatural supersymmetry (SUSY), known as generalized No-Scale Supergravity (SUGRA). This merging of $\mathcal{F}$-$SU(5)$ with generalized No-Scale SUGRA solves the SUSY electroweak fine-tuning problem and includes natural dark matter (NDM) that meets the latest 5$σ$ Planck satellite observations through annihilation only. The NDM in this model is imminently under test by the LUX-ZEPLIN 1000-day experiment, thus prospective observed events by LUX-ZEPLIN in 2026-27 will compel confirmation by the high-luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) energizing in 2030. Therefore, we present the parameter space observable at both LUX-ZEPLIN and the HL-LHC that is consistent with all presently operating experiments, including the LHC Run 3 gluino and squark constraints. Our results predict sub-200 GeV neutralinos and right-handed sleptons in the bulk that are not beyond detection, though more massive electroweakinos greater than 600 GeV are also allowed.

</details>


### [145] [Estimating Spectrum-Free Information Bounds for Generative Surrogates Beyond Conditional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07826)
*Chaitanya Dumbre,Sourabh Dube*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出高能物理中条件生成代理模型（conditional generative surrogates）常用的1D边缘散度（marginal divergence）指标会奖励“条件崩塌”（conditional collapse），并提出以连续排名概率技巧分数（Continuous Ranked Probability Skill Score, CRPSS）为核心的评估框架，辅以k近邻（k-NN）外推估计条件信息上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多依赖1维边缘散度等单一指标；在CMS模拟基准上，一个忽略条件变量的采样器在KL与Wasserstein-1下均优于训练过的混合密度网络（Mixture Density Network, MDN），且在四项目标中的两项上无法与条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching, CFM）模型稳定区分，说明此类指标不足以反映条件建模质量。

Method: 借用预报检验（forecast verification）中的CRPSS，以精确边缘分布为参考对模型打分；分析全局技巧分数对评估谱（evaluation spectrum）的依赖性，并在条件变量分箱（bin）内报告分数。进一步提出数据驱动的k-NN外推方法，用于估计条件信息所能达到的最优CRPS。

Result: 全局技巧分数显著依赖评估谱；在条件变量分箱内该依赖性基本消失。MDN与CFM在全部四个目标上的CRPS均与k-NN外推给出的最优估计一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明当前架构已达到条件变量所提供的信息量——至少就这些逐目标（per-target）分数而言；1D边缘指标不能作为条件生成代理模型的充分评价依据。

Abstract: Conditional generative surrogates in high-energy physics, such as models that predict reconstructed jets from partons, are often evaluated on $1\text{D}$ marginal divergences among other metrics. Taken alone these metrics can reward conditional collapse. On a CMS simulation benchmark, a sampler that ignores the conditioner beats a trained Mixture Density Network (MDN) on all four target marginals under both KL and Wasserstein-1, and cannot be stably separated from a Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) model on two of the four. We instead score the models with the Continuous Ranked Probability Skill Score (CRPSS) from forecast verification, taking the exact marginal as the reference. We show how global skill depends on the evaluation spectrum, and we also report it within bins of a conditioning variable, where that dependence largely goes away. We then introduce a data-driven $k$-Nearest-Neighbor ($k$-NN) extrapolation that estimates the best CRPS the conditioning information allows. Both models agree with this estimate on all four targets, which suggests that current architectures have reached the information available in the conditioner, at least as far as these per-target scores can tell.

</details>


### [146] [Testing the Spin of New Physics with Top Precision Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08459)
*André Lessa,Vinícius Rocca,Veronica Sanz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过对顶夸克对产生过程的精确测量，可在三个顶夸克相关（top-philic）新物理情景中区分标量、矢量费米子与Z′媒介粒子的自旋与动力学特性；虽标量与矢量费米子在低能有效场论下不可区分，但其动量依赖干涉图样在微分分布中留有可测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量不仅能发现新物理（new physics）的存在，还能揭示产生该新物理的动力学机制，而这一信息在传统总截面或低能有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）框架下往往被掩盖，因此有必要研究微分测量对虚拟新物理态自旋与动力学的探测能力。

Method: 在顶夸克对（top-pair）产生过程中比较三种顶夸克相关情景：彩色标量顶夸克伙伴（colored scalar top partner）、彩色矢量类费米子（colored vector-like fermion）以及自旋为一的Z′媒介粒子；前两者通过圈修正（loop corrections）贡献，Z′通过树图（tree level）贡献，并利用顶夸克横向动量（transverse momentum）谱分析其干涉图样。

Result: 标量与矢量类费米子情景在低能下匹配到相同的有效算符结构（effective operator structure），无法用EFT方法区分；但在阈值附近或以上，其完整圈振幅呈现不同的动量依赖干涉图样（interference patterns）。横向动量谱显示共振效应与圈诱导效应较易区分，而标量与矢量类假设之间的区分更为困难，但其解析结构差异仍在分布形状中留下可测信息。

Conclusion: 精确微分测量可在虚拟新物理态未被直接重建的情况下探测其自旋与动力学，结果支持发展更强的多维分析（multidimensional analyses）以提升区分能力。

Abstract: Precision measurements can reveal not only the presence of new physics, but also information about the dynamics that generated it. We study this question in top-pair production by comparing three top-philic scenarios: a colored scalar top partner, a colored vector-like fermion, and a spin-one $Z^\prime$ mediator. The first two contribute through loop corrections, while the $Z^\prime$ contributes at tree level. We show that the scalar and vector-like fermion scenarios match onto the same effective operator structure at low energies,making their distinction unfeasible using the EFT approach. However, near or above threshold their full loop amplitudes develop distinct momentum-dependent interference patterns. Using the top transverse momentum spectrum we find that resonant and loop-induced effects are relatively easy to distinguish, whereas separating the scalar and vector-like hypotheses is substantially more challenging. Nevertheless, their different analytic structures leave measurable information in the shape of the distribution. These results illustrate how precision differential measurements can probe the spin and dynamics of virtual new states even when they are not directly reconstructed, and motivate more powerful multidimensional analyses.

</details>


### [147] [To Infinity and Beyond: Maximizing Entanglement and Emergent Symmetries in $N$ Higgs Doublet Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07005)
*Spencer Chang,Gabriel Jacobo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文考察“希格斯散射中味纠缠最大化”与“势函数增强镜像对称性”之间的关联：在N个希格斯双重态模型中推广出U(2)^N镜像对称，并在三个希格斯双重态模型中给出反例，说明需附加约束才能维持该关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于近期观察——在两希格斯双重态模型（two Higgs doublet model）中，希格斯散射的味纠缠（flavor entanglement）最大化会导致势函数（若下有界）具有增强的镜像对称性（mirror symmetry）U(2)^2——论文意在检验该关联是否普遍成立，并寻找维持它的条件。

Method: 将分析推广至N个希格斯双重态模型（N Higgs doublet models），构造具有U(2)^N对称性的镜像模型推广；聚焦三个希格斯双重态模型，考察在势函数下有界条件下味纠缠最大化与对称性之间的关系，并检验规范化（gauging）对称性后对最大纠缠的要求。

Result: 在三个希格斯双重态模型中，存在一个下有界势函数解，其味纠缠最大化但仅具有最小U(2)对称性，构成反例；若要求规范化对称性之后仍保持最大纠缠，则该反例被排除，最大纠缠与扩展对称性之间的关联得以恢复。

Conclusion: 最大味纠缠与增强镜像对称性之间的联系并非普遍成立，需要附加约束；在三希格斯双重态模型中，“规范对称性下仍保持最大纠缠”可作为恢复该联系的有效约束条件。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the recent observation that maximizing flavor entanglement in Higgs boson scattering in a two Higgs doublet model leads to parameter space where the potential, if bounded from below, has an enhanced mirror symmetry $U(2)^2$. Looking at $N$ Higgs doublet models, we find a generalization of the mirror models, with $U(2)^N$ symmetry. Focusing on three Higgs doublet models, we determine that there is also a bounded potential solution that maximally entangles but has only the minimal $U(2)$ symmetry. This counterexample demonstrates that additional constraints must be imposed to maintain the connection between maximal entanglement and extended symmetries. As a potential constraint, for the three Higgs doublet model, we show that requiring maximal entanglement still occurs after gauging the symmetries eliminates the counterexample, thus restoring the connection.

</details>


### [148] [Can Two-Point Correlations Determine Gravitational Waves from Sound Waves?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07877)
*Guangshang Chen,Jianpeng Dai,Yang Xiao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出仅凭流体速度两点相关函数（two-point correlation function）在一般情况下不足以唯一确定宇宙一阶相变（first-order phase transition）产生的引力波（gravitational waves, GWs）频谱，并进一步论证在真实的随机气泡成核与生长声源中，连通高阶贡献在线性声学传播阶段可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声波被认为是宇宙一阶相变引力波的主要来源，而其频谱通常在高斯近似（Gaussian approximation）下由速度两点相关函数预测；但引力波源是速度的二次量，连通四点相关（connected four-point correlations）也可能贡献，因此需要检验两点重建的可靠性。

Method: 通过两个解析玩具模型（analytical toy examples）说明两点相关并不总能唯一决定频谱；随后构建基于随机气泡成核与生长（stochastic bubble nucleation and growth）的真实声源模型，分析连通贡献在线性声学传播（linear acoustic propagation）及宇宙膨胀（cosmic expansion）影响下的相对量级。

Result: 两个玩具模型显示仅用速度两点相关无法唯一确定GW频谱；在真实随机气泡声源中，连通贡献在后续线性声学传播阶段变为次主导（subleading），但宇宙膨胀会限制这种抑制效果。

Conclusion: 结果界定了两点相关重建何时可靠，并说明高阶速度相关与声发射持续时间（duration of acoustic emission）对引力波预测的影响。

Abstract: Sound waves are expected to be a dominant source of gravitational waves (GWs) from cosmological first-order phase transitions. Their spectrum is commonly predicted using the fluid velocity two-point correlation function under the Gaussian approximation. However, since the GW source is quadratic in velocity, connected four-point correlations may also contribute to the GW spectrum. Through two analytical toy examples, we demonstrate that velocity two-point correlations alone does not always uniquely determine the GW spectrum. Moving beyond these idealized examples, we investigate a realistic acoustic source based on stochastic bubble nucleation and growth, proving that the connected contribution becomes subleading during subsequent linear acoustic propagation, although cosmic expansion limits this suppression. These results clarify when two-point reconstruction is reliable and how higher-order velocity correlations and the duration of acoustic emission affect GW predictions.

</details>


### [149] [Enhancing Gravitational Waves from Global Strings via a First-Order Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07416)
*Yongtao Jia,Ligong Bian,Huai-Ke Guo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文首次实现宇宙学一阶相变（first-order phase transition）中随机相位真真空气泡成核、膨胀、碰撞并产生整体弦网络（global string network）的（3+1）维格点模拟（lattice simulation），并据此重新评估轴子（axion）暗物质丰度、暗辐射限制与引力波（gravitational wave）信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多针对二阶相变（second-order phase transition）情形，一阶相变中气泡碰撞生成弦网络的非线性动力学尚缺乏（3+1）维格点验证，其对轴子谱与引力波产额的影响不明确。

Method: 开展（3+1）维格点模拟，令随机相位的真真空气泡成核、膨胀并碰撞，形成整体弦网络；由模拟得到的弦标度演化与轴子谱（axion spectrum）推算暗物质遗迹丰度、大爆炸核合成（big bang nucleosynthesis）给出的暗辐射限制，以及引力波与轴子的发射率之比。

Result: 弦网络进入标度区（scaling regime），其对数增长系数小于二阶相变情形的取值；引力波与轴子发射率之比约为二阶相变情形的100倍，由此得到的引力波谱可被脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing array）及空间与地面干涉仪（interferometer）探测。同时给出对轴子衰变常数f_a与质量m_a的约束。

Conclusion: 一阶相变产生的整体弦网络显著改变轴子—引力波产额的相对比例，使该情景成为脉冲星计时阵列与干涉仪可检验的引力波源，并为f_a与m_a提供新的限制途径。

Abstract: We perform the first (3+1)-dimensional lattice simulations of a cosmological first-order phase transition (PT) in which true-vacuum bubbles with random phases nucleate, expand, and collide, producing a network of global strings. We find that the string network enters the scaling regime, and the coefficient of the logarithmic growth is smaller than the value obtained for a second-order PT. From the axion spectrum, we derive the dark-matter relic abundance and the dark-radiation bound from big bang nucleosynthesis on the axion decay constant $f_a$ and mass $m_a$. The ratio of the gravitational wave emission rate to the axion emission rate is found to be about two orders of magnitude larger than in the second-order case, and the resulting gravitational wave spectrum can be probed by pulsar timing arrays as well as by space- and ground-based interferometers.

</details>


### [150] [Algorithmic Construction of Vector-Valued Modular Forms and Application to an $A_4\times\mathbb{Z}_4$-invariant Model of Leptons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08387)
*Q. Bonnefoy,M. Rausch de Traubenberg,V. Saulquin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种构造有限模群向量值模形式（Vector-Valued Modular Forms, VVMFs）的算法及配套代码，利用Burnside-Brauer定理确定能生成所有高权形式的极小生成集，并以A_4×Z_4轻子模型作示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规做法是先构造前几个VVMFs再用张量积生成其余形式，但系统性地找出足以生成全部高权形式的极小VVMF集合存在困难；对新群的研究还需处理各类细节问题。

Method: 用户提供目标群的群论与表示论基本描述，代码借助Burnside-Brauer定理确定极小VVMF集合，依据该函数理论的一般结果显式构造该集合，再通过张量积（tensor products）生成任意其他VVMF，并自动处理新群研究中可能出现的注意事项（caveats）。

Result: 该方法复现了近期模味对称性（modular flavor symmetries）现象学文献中已研究的有限模群（finite modular groups）的已知结果，并在额外测试的群上同样适用；通过基于未被探索的有限模群A_4×Z_4的轻子（lepton）模型演示了算法与代码的运行。

Conclusion: 该算法与配套代码为有限模群VVMF的系统构造提供了自动化工具，可扩展到新的群，并支持模味对称性建模中的实际应用。

Abstract: We present an algorithmic procedure that constructs the Vector-Valued Modular Forms (VVMFs) of finite modular groups, as well as its implementation in a companion code. The usual method consists in building the first few VVMFs, whose tensor products then generate the others. We address the difficulty of systematically finding the minimal set of VVMFs that suffices to produce all higher-weight ones, and we propose a method to identify it that makes use of the Burnside-Brauer theorem. For any given group, whose basic group-theoretic and representation-theoretic description is provided by the user, the code determines the minimal set of VVMFs, explicitly builds it using general results of the theory of such functions, then performs the tensor products that generate any other VVMF. Importantly, it automatically takes into account all caveats that might arise during the study of new groups. Our approach reproduces the known results for the finite modular groups explored in the recent phenomenological literature on modular flavor symmetries, and works for additional groups which we tested. We illustrate our algorithm and the working of our code by studying a model of leptons based on the unexplored finite modular group $A_4\times\mathbb{Z}_4$.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Spectral Regimes of Truncated sinc-Power and Pulse-Train Kernels: Edge Exponents, Per-Edge Plunges, and the Landau-Widom Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08401)
*Unnikrishna Pillai*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究带限信号在有限窗口下的经典集中问题（concentration problem），系统刻画带宽形状变形（加权、分裂、收缩、重叠、自相似）时特征值聚簇与过渡区（plunge）的变化，指出边界描述子（edge descriptor）由常数、指数、整数直至分形维数逐级演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Slepian–Landau–Pollak–Widom 框架仅针对单一矩形带，其特征值在 0 与 1 附近聚簇，中间存在宽度随窗口长度对数增长的过渡区。本文动机在于把该结论推广到带形被一般化变形的设定，考察聚簇与过渡区的普适性是否保持。

Method: 以带限算子的核（kernel）为对象，通过逐元素幂运算平滑带边、取不相交的多个频带副本、将每个带收缩为直线、令频带重叠以及构造自相似带等变形方式，分析相应特征值聚簇结构与过渡区指数、系数及层次结构。

Result: 平滑带边使过渡区被幂律边替代，指数为幂次减一；不相交频带使过渡区宽度按带边数量成比例扩大，系数为每条边的常数，与 Landau–Widom 多区间定理一致；频带收缩为直线时退化为经典有限秩算子；重叠频带产生多级阶梯；自相似带形成由 Cantor 维数控制的分簇层级。在平滑边处，重标度特征值不再坍缩到单一曲线。

Conclusion: 边界描述子（edge descriptor）依次由常数过渡为指数、整数与分形维数，构成统一分类；对单一频带，经典结果表明全部特征滤波器零点位于单位圆上，且阶数升高时带内零点增多，故主特征向量表现为低通滤波器（lowpass filter）。

Abstract: A signal band-limited to one rectangular band and observed through a finite window is the classical concentration problem of Slepian, Landau, Pollak, and Widom. The eigenvalues of the operator cluster near one and near zero, separated by a transition called the plunge. The plunge width grows like the logarithm of the window length, with a universal coefficient of one over pi squared set by the band-edge jump. This paper follows the clusters and the plunge as the band is deformed. An elementwise power of the kernel smooths the band edge, and the plunge gives way to a power-law edge with exponent one less than the power. Disjoint copies of the band widen the plunge in proportion to the number of band edges, as the multiple-interval theorem of Landau and Widom implies, so the coefficient is a per-edge constant. Shrinking each band to a line gives the classical finite-rank operator. Overlapping bands give a multilevel staircase, and a self-similar band gives a cluster hierarchy governed by the Cantor dimension. At the smooth edge, rescaled eigenvalues do not collapse onto one curve. The edge descriptor passes from a constant to an exponent to an integer to a fractal dimension. For the single band, classical results place all eigenfilter zeros on the unit circle, with more of them inside the band as the order rises, so the dominant eigenvectors act as lowpass filters.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [A hierarchy of physics-embedded neural operators for structural dynamic surrogate modelingA hierarchy of physics-embedded neural operators for structural dynamic surrogate modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07059)
*Amir Ansrollahi,Mehmet Sefa Orak*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出模态有理映射（Modal Rational, MR）与物理引导模态算子网络（PGMON），在MOSAIC有界谱差异框架下实现结构频率响应重建；在钢桁架塔与锥形壳梁两类算例中平均位移时程误差分别为0.73–0.76%与0.029–0.116%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构响应代理模型需在优化或健康状态变化中捕捉移动共振、相位变化与非等幅传递通道；物理嵌入学习（physics-embedded learning）旨在以更低成本逼近有限元（FEA）级精度。

Method: MR将结构特征映射为模态量以解析重建频响；PGMON在此基础上加入有界频率、阻尼与残余柔度修正；MOSAIC指含结构GELU与Anchored-SIREN修正的有界谱差异框架，MOSAIC-MR与MOSAIC-PGMON分别叠加该差异项。

Result: 在5%参考模态阻尼下，两类各20000样本设计总体（80 m钢桁架塔、十站点变质量锥形壳梁）的平均位移时程误差为塔0.73–0.76%、梁0.029–0.116%；误差分析区分了学习模态差异、截断与激励加权三种来源。

Conclusion: 结果为物理结构化预测提供支持，但证据限于固定拓扑（fixed-topology）与基准测试，对泛化性、计算节省与附加修正的收益须谨慎主张；后续工作是围绕该架构开发并验证神经算子（neural operators）。

Abstract: Structural-response surrogates must capture moving resonances, phase changes, and unequal-amplitude transfer channels during optimization or health-state changes. Physics-embedded learning seeks FEA-level accuracy at lower cost by embedding physics in inputs, forward passes, losses, and backpropagation. We propose Modal Rational (MR), mapping structural features to modal quantities for analytical frequency-response reconstruction. The Physics-Guided Modal Operator Network (PGMON) adds bounded frequency, damping, and residual-flexibility corrections. Modal Operator with Structural GELU and Anchored-SIREN Correction (MOSAIC) denotes the bounded spectral-discrepancy framework used here; MOSAIC-MR and MOSAIC-PGMON add such discrepancies. They support finite-dimensional maps and physical decoders for future neural operators; evidence is fixed-topology, not a completed operator comparison. Two 20,000-design populations are studied: an 80 m steel truss tower with variable member areas and attached masses, and a tapered shell beam with ten variable station masses. Archived tests cover ten designs and seven acceleration histories per structure; they are benchmarks, not seismic-design assessments. With 5% reference modal damping, MR, PGMON, and MOSAIC give mean displacement-history errors of 0.73-0.76% for the tower and 0.029-0.116% for the beam, against declared band-limited modal references. Supplied modal frequencies are distinguished from design-only use; unequal parameter counts and training settings are explicit. Error analysis separates learned modal discrepancies, truncation, and excitation weighting, explaining acceleration and difficult-tower limits and risks of blind use. Results support physically structured prediction but limit claims about generalization, computational savings, and added corrections. Next is developing and validating neural operators around these architectures.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Space of Geometric Gibbs Measures: Uniqueness, Ergodicity, and Teichmüller Complex Manifold Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08384)
*Yunping Jiang*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文在既有几何吉布斯测度（geometric Gibbs measures）工作的基础上证明其唯一性，从而完成所有几何吉布斯测度空间上 Teichmüller 复流形（Teichmüller complex manifold）结构的构造，并借助焊接技术（welding technique）揭示其与动力拟圆（dynamical quasicircles）上调和测度（harmonic measures）的联系，进而得到渐近共形刚性（asymptotic conformal rigidity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前引入几何吉布斯测度，用以在 Teichmüller 度量（Teichmüller metric）下完备化经典吉布斯测度（classical Gibbs measures）空间；本文旨在补齐该构造中唯一性这一关键环节，并探索该测度类的进一步几何与遍历性质。

Method: 核心手段为证明几何吉布斯测度的唯一性，并运用焊接技术（welding technique）建立几何吉布斯测度与动力拟圆上调和测度之间的对应关系。

Result: 确立了几何吉布斯测度的唯一性，完成了所有几何吉布斯测度空间上 Teichmüller 复流形结构的构造；通过上述测度联系，得到动力拟圆的渐近共形刚性。

Conclusion: 论文为几何吉布斯测度遍历性质（ergodic properties）的系统研究奠定基础，并将其纳入 Teichmüller 复流形与调和测度的统一框架之中。

Abstract: Building on our previous work, in which geometric Gibbs measures were introduced to complete the space of classical Gibbs measures under the Teichmüller metric, we establish the uniqueness of geometric Gibbs measures in this paper. This result completes the construction of a Teichmüller complex manifold structure on the space of all geometric Gibbs measures. Using the welding technique, we uncover a connection between geometric Gibbs measures and harmonic measures on dynamical quasicircles. This connection allows us to establish asymptotic conformal rigidity for these dynamical quasicircles. Finally, we establish a foundation for the systematic study of the ergodic properties of geometric Gibbs measures.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Where heat flows and where to change it: gauge-free sensitivity maps of thermal conductivity in disordered solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06958)
*D. A. Drabold,C. Ugwumadu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文在谐波（Allen–Feldman）理论框架下提出两种局域热导描述：一是将电导率κ投影到原子上的映射（存在"规范"依赖性），二是κ对原子质量与键刚度的精确响应（满足Euler求和规则）。在非晶与副晶硅中二者呈反相关，可归因于局域软度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热管理材料设计不仅需要总热导数值，还需知道热量在何处流动，因此需要局域化的热流描述；同时原子投影方式依赖于势能如何在原子间划分（规范），需检验其可靠性与替代方案。

Method: 开发两种局域描述：κ的原子投影（考察规范依赖），以及κ对每个原子质量和每条键刚度的精确响应（含三项精确项，其中包含热流映射）。在非晶硅及副晶硅中，结合ACE、GAP-18、Keating力常数进行验证，并用谐波振动振幅刻画局域软度，辅以32768原子大体系复现。

Result: 规范依赖属中等：单点约为空间标准差的23%，半径0.4 nm区域为9.5%，另有κ的0.8%均匀偏移。两张映射呈反相关：热流少处重原子降低κ，热流多处则倾向升高。谐波振动振幅可同时预测两种映射；室温下固定振幅后反相关基本消失；约100 K以下仍存在软度无法捕捉的成分。

Conclusion: 软区（热流小）通过相距约1–2 meV的振动模式混合起作用；副晶硅中晶粒每原子导热更多而由无序基体主导响应。响应映射可指导分散取代原子的放置，并能定位限制非晶/晶界面输运的区域。

Abstract: Designing materials for thermal management requires knowing where heat flows, not only how much. Within harmonic (Allen$--$Feldman) theory we develop two local descriptions and test them on amorphous silicon. The first, a projection of the conductivity $κ$ onto atoms, depends on how the potential energy is divided among atoms (a "gauge"); we find the dependence moderate, 23% of the map's spatial standard deviation at single sites and 9.5% over regions of radius 0.4 nm, apart from a uniform offset of 0.8% of $κ$. The second avoids the division altogether. It is the exact response of $κ$ to the mass of each atom and the stiffness of each bond, it obeys an Euler sum rule, and it contains the heat-flow map as one of three exact terms. In amorphous and paracrystalline silicon, with ACE, GAP-18 and Keating force constants, the two maps are anti-correlated: where little heat flows a heavier atom lowers $κ$, and where much heat flows it tends to raise it. The explanation is local softness. The harmonic vibrational amplitude of each atom predicts both maps, and at room temperature the anti-correlation largely disappears at fixed amplitude, in a 32,768-atom replica as well; below about 100 K a component remains that softness does not capture. Thus soft regions, which carry little heat, act through the mixing of vibrational modes about 1$--$2 meV apart. In paracrystalline silicon the grains conduct more heat per atom, while the disordered matrix controls the response. Exact recalculations show that the response map guides the placement of dispersed substituents and locates the region that limits transport across an amorphous/crystalline interface.

</details>


### [155] [Energy-Based Anisotropic Hysteresis Modeling for Permanent Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06980)
*Walter Friza,Manfred Kaltenbacher*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过将能量型矢量play模型（vector play model）中粒子的球形钉扎区域替换为半轴代表方向相关钉扎力的椭球，实现对永磁体各向异性磁滞行为（anisotropic hysteresis behaviour）的建模，并加入无磁滞部分的角度依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永磁体的各向异性磁滞行为建模需要能够表征易磁化方向（easy magnetization directions）的矢量磁滞模型（vector hysteresis models），而原有能量型矢量play模型的球形钉扎区域无法体现方向依赖性。

Method: 将每个粒子的球形钉扎区域（pinning region）替换为椭球，其半轴表示方向相关的钉扎力；重点处理矢量play模型更新公式（update formula）在几何上一致的适配，并为模型的无磁滞部分（anhysteretic part）引入角度依赖性。

Result: 模型与NdFeB磁体的实测数据进行了对比，能够复现磁场方向相对易轴（easy axis）倾斜至30°的测量磁滞回线（loops）。

Conclusion: 椭球形钉扎区域的推广使能量型矢量play模型可描述各向异性材料，并在NdFeB磁体倾斜磁化情形下获得与测量一致的结果。

Abstract: Modeling the anisotropic hysteresis behaviour of permanent magnets requires vector hysteresis models capable of representing preferred (easy) magnetization directions. This work extends the energy-based vector play model to anisotropic materials by replacing the spherical pinning region of each particle with an ellipsoid, whose semi-axes represent direction-dependent pinning forces. Special attention is paid to the geometrically consistent adaptation of the update formula for the vector play model. The anhysteretic part of the model is also given an angular dependency. The model is compared to measurements taken from a NdFeB magnet and reproduces the measured loops for field inclinations of up to 30° from the easy axis.

</details>


### [156] [Sudden disappearance of a bright exciton in solid helium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07097)
*Fatema Mohamed,Lucia Reining,Matteo Gatti*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示固氦（solid helium）中激子（exciton）光谱存在强烈的角度依赖：在特定动量转移方向上，显著的激子峰被完全抑制，该效应源于电子-空穴交换相互作用（electron-hole exchange interaction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有激子研究多集中于波矢视为零的光学光谱，而波矢依赖的激子光谱在理论与损失函数（loss function）测量上均缺乏探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以固氦为原型体系，基于理论分析考察激子光谱随波矢方向的变化，并归因于电子-空穴交换相互作用；同时讨论压力依赖并对照已有实验证据。

Result: 发现特定动量转移方向上激子峰被完全抑制的显著角度依赖；在布拉格点（Bragg points）附近，波矢方向的极小改变即可导致光谱剧烈变化，呈现反直觉效应。

Conclusion: 作者阐释了激子本质如何使该现象成为可能，预测了其他材料中出现强烈角度依赖的条件，并指出该效应可作为实验验证的依据。

Abstract: Excitons, bound electron-hole pairs, are investigated extensively in optical spectra, where their wave vector is considered to be vanishing. Excitonic spectra as a function of wave vector are much less explored, both theoretically and with measurements of the loss function. Here, we uncover a striking angular dependence of excitonic spectra for the prototypical case of solid helium, where a prominent excitonic peak is completely suppressed for a momentum transfer in certain directions. The effect is entirely due to the electron-hole exchange interaction. This phenomenon leads to a counter-intuitive effect close to Bragg points, where an infinitesimal change of direction of wave vector is sufficient to change the spectrum dramatically. We explain in which way the nature of excitons makes such a scenario possible. We discuss the pressure dependence, point to experimental evidence for our findings, and predict under which circumstances dramatic angular dependencies may also be found in other materials.

</details>


### [157] [Barlow Packings All Have Maximum Packing Fraction: Why it is so Hard to Find Barlow Structures Other than fcc or hcp?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07197)
*Andres Robles-Navarro,Shaun Cooper,Alex Travesset,Peter Schwerdtfeger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用格点理论研究由堆垛密排层形成的周期Barlow堆积，发现内聚能、平衡晶格常数、体模量、爱因斯坦频率与振动熵均随fcc堆垛分数近似线性变化，据此为中间Barlow堆垛在自然界罕见提供热力学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间堆垛的周期Barlow堆积在自然界中罕见，其成因缺乏简洁的热力学解释；本文旨在检验不同Barlow堆垛之间性质随fcc堆垛分数变化的规律，并据此说明其稀缺性。

Method: 采用格点理论（lattice theory）处理由二维六角密排层堆垛形成、满足Xi≠Xi+1约束的周期Barlow堆积(ABC…)∞，原子间作用取广义(n,m)-Lennard-Jones势；并结合声子色散计算，以及对N=2,3,4,12的氩Barlow堆积含色散力的密度泛函（density-functional）计算进行对照。

Result: 所有堆积的内聚能介于hcp（(AB)）与fcc（(ABC)）之间，且随fcc堆垛分数近似线性变化；平衡晶格常数、体模量、爱因斯坦频率与振动熵呈类似准线性趋势。对标准(12,6)势，fcc振动熵最高、hcp最低，其余Barlow堆积居中，与既往粘性硬球蒙特卡洛（sticky-hard-sphere Monte Carlo）模拟一致；氩的密度泛函计算亦印证内聚能的近线性依赖。

Conclusion: 上述趋势意味着自由能（free energy）近似线性依赖于fcc堆垛分数，hcp与fcc为两端点；这种近线性几乎不提供稳定中间堆垛的驱动力，从而为中间Barlow堆垛在自然界稀少提供了简单热力学解释。例外情形为hcp与fcc能量近简并时，或本模型未包含的多体相互作用（many-body interactions）偏好其他堆垛时。

Abstract: We apply lattice theory to periodic Barlow packings $(X_{1}X_{2}\dots X_{N})_{\infty}$ where $X_{i}\in\{A,B,C\}$ and $X_{i}\neq X_{i+1}$, formed by stacking two-dimensional hexagonal close-packed layers, interacting via generalized $(n,m)$-Lennard-Jones potentials. The cohesive energies of all packings lie between those of hexagonal close-packed (hcp, $(AB)$) and face-centered cubic (fcc, $(ABC)$) structure and vary almost linearly with the fcc stacking fraction. The equilibrium lattice constant, bulk modulus, Einstein frequency, and vibrational entropy show similarly near-linear trends. For the standard $(12,6)$ potential, fcc has the highest vibrational entropy and hcp the lowest, with other Barlow packings falling between them, consistent with previous sticky-hard-sphere Monte Carlo simulations and supported by phonon dispersion calculations. These trends imply an approximately linear dependence of free energy on fcc stacking fraction, with hcp and fcc as endpoints. This provides a simple thermodynamic explanation for why intermediate Barlow stackings are rare in nature: near-linearity offers little driving force to favor them. Exceptions may arise when hcp and fcc are nearly degenerate in energy or when many-body interactions, absent from the present model, favor another stacking. Density-functional calculations including dispersion forces for argon Barlow packings with periods $N=2,3,4$, and $12$ corroborate the predicted near-linear cohesive energy dependence.

</details>


### [158] [Dark-State Coherent Control of Excitons in TMD Monolayers: A Reduced Intravalley Lindblad Framework for WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07315)
*Mauricio Martinez,Martin A. Mosquera*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文建立按耦合拓扑（coupling topology）区分的Lindblad框架，研究固定质心动量下三能级激子流形（excitonic manifold）中的暗态相干（dark-state coherence）。在hBN封装WSe₂的Ξ流形中，太赫兹脉冲先于弱光脉冲的对射顺序可实现向暗激子的转换；V流形则因辐射衰减丧失相干，作为无保护参照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁诱导透明（EIT）与受激拉曼绝热通道（STIRAP）的性能依赖耦合拓扑：Ξ方案可使相干绕过有损耗的中间态，而V方案中相干由两个均会衰减的激发态承载。本文旨在为二维半导体激子的暗态相干控制提供与材料相关的最小基线与可复现的流形选取方法。

Method: 构建按拓扑区分的Lindblad框架，描述固定质心动量下单个激子与声子、辐射场及稀薄激子气体的耦合；通过系统筛选光学亮度（optical brightness）、激子内偶极强度与态紧致度从激子谱中选取Ξ与V流形，使选择过程可复现并可迁移至其他材料。

Result: 在hBN封装WSe₂的Ξ流形中，按反直觉STIRAP顺序施加太赫兹脉冲后接弱光脉冲，可通过暗态跟随将激发转换为光学暗激子，而有损耗的亮中间激子近乎保持空置；转换受中间态发射而非非绝热耦合限制，并由太赫兹支路的单一绝热性参数控制。激子气体的交换平均场在中密度下使双光子共振移动超出转换容忍度；级联透明窗口中心提供原位校准，并确定其为真EIT而非Autler–Townes劈裂的控制强度条件。V流形在辐射时间尺度上失去相干。

Conclusion: 结果给出二维半导体中激子在辐射极限下暗态相干控制的最小化、材料特异性基线，并表明拓扑分辨的Lindblad处理可支持可复现的流形选取与EIT/ Autler–Townes机制的判别。

Abstract: Dark-state coherence in driven three-level systems underlies electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT) and stimulated Raman adiabatic passage (STIRAP). Their performance depends on the coupling topology: in a ladder ($Ξ$) scheme the coherence can bypass a lossy intermediate state, whereas in a $V$ scheme it is carried by two excited states that both decay. We develop a topology-resolved Lindblad framework for driven three-level excitonic manifolds at fixed center-of-mass momentum, in which a single exciton couples to phonons, the radiation field and a dilute exciton gas. The $Ξ$ and $V$ manifolds are selected from the exciton spectrum by systematically screening optical brightness, intra-excitonic dipole strength and state compactness, making the selection reproducible and transferable to other materials. Within this framework, in the $Ξ$ manifold of hBN-encapsulated WSe$_2$, a weak optical pulse, preceded by a terahertz pulse in the counterintuitive STIRAP order, converts the excitation it creates into an optically dark exciton by dark-state following, while the lossy bright intermediate exciton stays nearly empty. The conversion is limited by emission from that intermediate state rather than by non-adiabatic coupling, and is controlled by a single adiabaticity parameter of the terahertz leg. The exchange mean field of the exciton gas shifts the two-photon resonance by more than the conversion tolerates at moderate densities; the center of the cascade transparency window provides an in situ calibration, and we identify the control strengths at which it is true EIT rather than Autler--Townes splitting. The $V$ manifold loses its coherence on the radiative timescale and serves as the unprotected reference. These results provide a minimal, material-specific baseline for dark-state coherent control of excitons in two-dimensional semiconductors in the radiative limit.

</details>


### [159] [Cross-Family Mode Coupling and Localization Reversal in Phononic Crystal Defect Dimers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07422)
*Xinlin Xu,Junji Kato*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文研究了声子晶体（phononic crystals）中双缺陷二聚体的局域化反转（localization reversal）现象，指出其由模态族交换（modal-family exchange, $B_1$ 单态与 $E$ 双重态）驱动，并与缺陷质量差中的族间竞争相平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷耦合可调控振动的空间分布，但邻近模态族（modal families）之间的相互作用如何决定缺陷偏好（defect preference）尚不清楚，因此需要厘清局域化反转的机制。

Method: 构建单缺陷支持邻近 $B_1$ 单态（singlet）与 $E$ 双重态（doublet）的二聚体模型，通过软化一个缺陷并比较偏轴（off-axis）与对角（diagonal）布局，结合跨族刚度与质量块的删除测试、固定态下观测项删减测试，以及仅保留完整单态-双重态空间的标称算符（nominal operators）进行预测。

Result: 反转伴随模态族交换发生，$B_1$ 与 $E$ 对缺陷质量差的贡献在阈值处近乎抵消；删除跨族刚度与质量块会消除反转，而固定态下省略跨族观测项则保留反转。偏轴软化端点处，独立通道预测的偏好缺陷与全阶解相反（频率仅差 0.16%）；标称算符预测的软化分支阈值误差低于 1.1%。

Conclusion: 局域化反转与模态族交换及物理缺陷质量平衡相关联，且频率吻合本身并不能保证局域化预测的准确性。

Abstract: Defects in phononic crystals confine elastic vibrations within bandgaps, enabling waveguiding, sensing, and energy harvesting. Coupling defects offers control over the spatial distribution of vibration, but how interactions between nearby modal families determine defect preference remains unclear. We examine localization reversal in dimers whose single defect supports a nearby $B_1$ singlet and $E$ doublet: softening one defect shifts a continued modal branch toward its unchanged partner. In off-axis and diagonal layouts, this reversal accompanies family exchange, with opposing $B_1$ and $E$ contributions to the defect-mass difference nearly cancelling at the threshold. Deleting cross-family stiffness and mass blocks removes reversal, while omitting cross-family observable terms at fixed states preserves it, identifying modal redistribution as the dominant mechanism. At the off-axis softening endpoint, independent channels predict the opposite preferred defect despite a $0.16\%$ frequency difference from the full-order solution. Nominal operators retaining the complete singlet-doublet space predict selected softening-branch thresholds with errors below $1.1\%$ against independent full-order sign brackets, without fitting detuned trajectories. These results link localization reversal to modal-family exchange and physical defect-mass balance, and show that frequency agreement alone does not guarantee localization accuracy.

</details>


### [160] [PolyGPT: A Decoder-Only Transformer for Polymer Electrolyte Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07438)
*Tanmay Sarkar Akash,Siddhartha Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: PolyGPT（38.3M 参数的 Transformer）可依据性质目标、化学特征包含/排除请求与电解质领域条件生成聚合物重复单元；电解质条件化虽略微降低 Tg 预测误差，却显著加剧重复生成并削弱各项控制效果，表明单一性质误差不足以评价条件生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物电解质（polymer electrolyte）设计需同时控制材料性质与化学结构，而现有条件生成模型的评价方式是否充分尚不明确。

Method: 构建 3830 万参数的 Transformer 模型 PolyGPT，从可选性质目标（如低玻璃化转变温度 Tg）、指定化学特征的包含或排除请求及电解质领域条件生成重复单元（repeat unit）；按验证损失（validation loss）选取模型，对比无条件生成与各类条件生成的结构有效性、唯一性（uniqueness）、预测器估计误差与目标命中率。

Result: 无条件与仅电解质条件的结构有效性分别为 99.46% 与 99.59%，但电解质条件化使有效输出唯一性由 99.99% 降至 51.74%；Tg 目标 246.11 K 下平均绝对误差仅由 26.97 K 微降至 26.07 K，而 50 K 容差内产出独立有效聚合物的比例由 82.35% 降至 32.31%（要求未见于训练目录时为 59.90% 与 19.71%）；指定特征命中率由 94.27% 降至 31.27%。

Conclusion: 领域条件化在性质误差上的微小改善掩盖了重复性上升、训练目录新颖性（training-catalog novelty）下降与组合设计请求满足度降低；条件聚合物生成的评价须同时考虑性质误差、重复性、新颖性及多重请求是否被满足。

Abstract: Designing polymer electrolytes requires control over both material properties and chemical structure. Here, we present PolyGPT, a Transformer with 38.3 million parameters that generates polymer repeat units from optional property targets, requests to include or exclude a specified chemical feature, and an electrolyte-domain condition. Using the model selected by validation loss, we compared unconditional generation with requests for electrolyte-associated structures, a low glass-transition temperature (Tg), or both. Unconditional and electrolyte-only generation achieved structural validity of 99.46% and 99.59%, respectively. However, electrolyte conditioning increased repetition: uniqueness among valid outputs fell from 99.99% to 51.74%. At a Tg target of 246.11 K, we compared generation with and without electrolyte conditioning, and adding the electrolyte condition slightly reduced the predictor-estimated mean absolute error from 26.97 to 26.07 K, but the fraction of all attempts yielding distinct valid polymers within 50 K of the target fell from 82.35% to 32.31%. When we also required absence from the training catalog, these yields were 59.90% and 19.71%, respectively. The lower yield persisted with a tighter 25 K tolerance. Domain conditioning also weakened control of inclusion requests: the mean fraction of attempts producing valid polymers containing the requested chemical feature fell from 94.27% to 31.27%. Across nine checkpoints, earlier models sometimes produced more distinct, catalog-unseen polymers meeting the Tg target: 10,000 checkpoint model with electrolyte conditioning and 30,000 checkpoint model without it. Together, these results show why property error alone is insufficient for evaluating conditional polymer generation. It must be considered alongside repetition, training-catalog novelty, and whether combined design requests are satisfied.

</details>


### [161] [Half-integer quantum anomalous Hall effect in MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ via non-magnetic extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07844)
*Igor P. Rusinov,Tatiana V. Menshchikova,Evgueni V. Chulkov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理方法研究MnBi$_2$Te$_4$反铁磁拓扑绝缘体薄膜与非磁性GeBi$_2$Te$_4$（Bi$_2$Te$_3$）覆盖层接触时的电子结构与霍尔响应，提出可实现对半整数量子反常霍尔相（half-integer quantum anomalous Hall phase）的异质结设计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对MnBi$_2$Te$_4$（0001）中两个狄拉克锥（Dirac cones）均受交换劈裂（exchange splitting）影响、难以呈现半整数量子化霍尔相应的问题，寻求通过界面设计调控其拓扑响应并拓宽效应可观测温区。

Method: 基于从头算（ab-initio）方法，构建MnBi$_2$Te$_4$反铁磁拓扑绝缘体薄膜与非磁性GeBi$_2$Te$_4$或Bi$_2$Te$_3$覆盖层组成的异质结，计算其电子结构与霍尔响应。

Result: 所提出的结型设计消除了MnBi$_2$Te$_4$（0001）其中一个狄拉克锥的交换劈裂，从而呈现半整数量子反常霍尔相；该方案可提高效应观测温度。

Conclusion: 该设计方案丰富了MnBi$_2$Te$_4$化合物的拓扑性质多样性，且适用于其他层状反铁磁拓扑绝缘体；通过替换覆盖层与磁性拓扑绝缘体薄膜，亦可建立半量子化霍尔相。

Abstract: Within an ab-initio approach, we study the electronic structure and Hall response of the antiferromagnetic topological insulator film of MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ in contact with non-magnetic GeBi$_2$Te$_4$ (Bi$_2$Te$_3$) overlayer. The proposed junction design eliminates the exchange splitting one of the Dirac cones of the MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ (0001) thus manifesting a half-integer quantum anomalous Hall phase. The described recipe allows to enlarge temperature of the effect observation, enriches the diversity of topological properties of the MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ compound and is appropriate for other layered antiferromagnetic topological insulators. Applying the proposed heterostructure design, the half-quantized Hall phase can be established with employing other overlayer and magnetic topological insulator films.

</details>


### [162] [Zero-Point Renormalization Reshapes the Shift Current: The Role of Electron-Phonon Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08019)
*Yang Li,Zuzhang Lin,Zhikuan Wang,Chengzhi Wu,Zeyu Jiang,Bing Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文基于第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），在单层 MoS2 中揭示由量子虚声子耦合（quantum virtual-phonon coupling）导致的能带重整化位移电流（band-renormalized shift current, BRSC），表明位移电流（shift current）不能仅由能带色散决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对位移电流的理解主要依赖单粒子图像（single-particle picture），即纯由能带色散决定；虽有研究探讨激子、磁振子、声子等直接激发准粒子的多体效应，但几乎所有固体中普遍存在、对输运尤为关键的虚声子耦合对电子态的重整化尚未被讨论。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，在单层 MoS2 中识别并计算该缺失通道——能带重整化位移电流（BRSC），考察零点重整化（zero-point renormalization）对位移电流谱的影响，并分析应变与温度调控。

Result: 仅零点重整化、在无携带动量的光激发声子且能带色散几乎不变的情况下，即显著重塑位移电流谱：出现源于动量不同态之间声子辅助光跃迁的低能峰，高能特征明显红移（redshift）；仅 1.0% 应变即可强烈调控 BRSC，升温则使其增强。

Conclusion: 研究结果推翻“仅由能带结构决定”的范式，确立电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）为非线性光电流不可或缺的要素，并为下一代光电子响应的工程化开辟了应变-热调控途径。

Abstract: Shift current (SC) drives the bulk photovoltaic effect, holding promise for near-instantaneous, low-dissipation photodetection and energy conversion. The current understanding of SC relies primarily on a single-particle picture, in which the SC is determined purely by band dispersion. While many-body effects from directly excited quasiparticles, such as excitons, magnons and phonons, have been explored, the renormalization of electronic states by quantum virtual-phonon coupling --- inherent in essentially all solids and particularly decisive for transport --- remains unaddressed. Here, using first-principles calculations, we identify this missing channel --- band-renormalized shift current (BRSC) --- in monolayer MoS2. We find that the zero-point renormalization alone, without any polarization-carrying photoexcited phonons and with barely any alteration of the band dispersion, dramatically reshapes the SC spectrum: emergent low-energy peaks arise from phonon-assisted optical transitions between momentum-distinct states, with a pronounced redshift of higher-energy features. A mere 1.0% strain sharply tunes the BRSC, whereas raising the temperature enhances it. Our findings overturn the band-structure-only paradigm, establishing electron-phonon coupling as an indispensable ingredient for nonlinear photocurrent and opening a strain-thermal route for engineering next-generation optoelectronic responses.

</details>


### [163] [Heat Transport Mechanisms in MOF-5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08024)
*Zezhu Zeng,Xia Liang,Hengtai Zheng,Aron Walsh,David E. Manolopoulos*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: MOF-5 中同时存在长寿命声子与连接体振动，其分裂谱表明声子准粒子近似（phonon quasiparticle approximation）失效；热输运由低频类粒子传播与类波相干两条通道共同贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属有机框架（metal-organic frameworks, MOFs）热导率的计算值与实测值长期存在偏差，且声子准粒子图像在其低维、强非谐结构中的适用性存疑，需澄清其微观热输运机制。

Method: 结合模式投影分子动力学谱（mode-projected molecular dynamics spectra）与谱 Wigner 热输运（spectral Wigner heat transport）方法，区分并量化类粒子与类波两类输运通道；另构建唯象外禀散射模型（phenomenological extrinsic scattering model）解释计算与实测热导率的差异。

Result: 低频类粒子声子传播在 200–500 K 显著减弱，而类波声子相干性几乎不变，并在 500 K 时贡献约一半本征热导率；外禀散射模型在量级与温度依赖上均可解释计算与实测热导率的不一致；ZIF-8 与 HKUST-1 的连接体振动亦呈现准粒子失效特征。

Conclusion: MOF 热输运不能仅用声子准粒子图像描述，需纳入类波相干贡献与外禀散射效应；声子准粒子失效可能是 MOFs 的共性特征，而非 MOF-5 特有。

Abstract: We find that MOF-5 supports both long-lived phonons and linker vibrations whose split spectra signal a breakdown of the phonon quasiparticle approximation. Combining mode-projected molecular dynamics spectra with spectral Wigner heat transport, we resolve two contrasting transport channels: low-frequency particle-like phonon propagation decreases strongly from 200 to 500 K, whereas wave-like phonon coherence remains nearly constant and supplies about half the intrinsic conductivity at 500 K. A phenomenological extrinsic scattering model provides a plausible explanation for the discrepancy between calculated and measured conductivities in terms of both its magnitude and temperature dependence. We also identify signatures of phonon quasiparticle breakdown in the linker vibrations of ZIF-8 and HKUST-1, indicating that this feature extends to other metal-organic frameworks.

</details>


### [164] [Ferroelectric control of magnetic coupling in a Cr dimer adsorbed on $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08055)
*J. F. Ospina,S. Di Napoli,M. A. Barral*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）研究3d过渡金属原子及二聚体在单层α-In₂Se₃上的吸附，发现Cr二聚体的磁耦合（magnetic coupling）可被铁电极化（ferroelectric polarization）翻转显著调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电衬底（ferroelectric substrate）可通过极化依赖的局域成键与电子结构变化调控低维体系磁相互作用，但原子级定义的二维杂化体系中该机制尚需明确。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，系统考察3d过渡金属原子与二聚体在单层α-In₂Se₃两个极性表面上的吸附，并借助自旋密度（spin density）、投影电子态（projected electronic states）与Wannier哈密顿量（Wannier Hamiltonians）分析磁耦合来源。

Result: Cr在两种极性表面均形成稳定二聚体；一种极化下反铁磁态（AFM）占优，相反极化下铁磁态（FM）与AFM近乎简并。分析显示直接Cr-Cr跃迁（hopping）减弱、Cr-Se杂化（hybridization）与Se自旋极化增强。

Conclusion: 铁电极化通过调节直接交换与衬底介导交换（substrate-mediated exchange）之间的平衡来调控磁相互作用，展示了铁电偏振在原子级定义二维杂化体系中的磁调控能力。

Abstract: Ferroelectric substrates offer a route to controlling magnetic interactions in low-dimensional systems through polarization-dependent changes in local bonding and electronic structure. Here, using first-principles calculations, we investigate the adsorption of 3$d$ transition-metal atoms and dimers on monolayer $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ and identify Cr as a stable dimer configuration on both polar surfaces. Reversing the ferroelectric polarization drives a pronounced change in its magnetic coupling: the AFM state is favored for one polarization, whereas the FM and AFM configurations become nearly degenerate for the opposite one. Analysis of the spin density, projected electronic states, and Wannier Hamiltonians reveals concomitant changes in the direct Cr-Cr hopping and in the interaction with the bridging Se atom. In particular, enhanced Cr-Se hybridization and Se spin polarization, together with a strong reduction of the direct two-center Cr-Cr hopping, point to a polarization-dependent balance between direct and substrate-mediated exchange contributions. These results show how ferroelectric polarization can tune magnetic interactions in an atomically defined 2D hybrid system.

</details>


### [165] [The polar discontinuity picture in nickelate superlattices: comparison of the insulating and metallic phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08067)
*Edith Simmen,Jonathan Amara,Philippe Ghosez,Nicola A. Spaldin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文以第一性原理（first-principles）方法研究 BaTiO₃ 与 SmNiO₃ 或 BiNiO₃ 超晶格中的形式电荷极性不连续（formal-charge polar discontinuity），发现绝缘态与金属态镍酸盐（nickelate）的界面响应在定性上高度相似，金属屏蔽电荷对表观极性不连续影响很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿（perovskite）异质结中的极性不连续已在 LaAlO₃/SrTiO₃ 等体系中被充分研究，近期在形式带电层位于金属中的体系亦观察到相关迹象，因此需要厘清金属态下极性不连续图像是否仍然适用。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，对比 BaTiO₃/BiNiO₃（绝缘相）与 BaTiO₃/SmNiO₃、BaTiO₃/BiNiO₃（金属镍酸盐相）超晶格的结构、态密度（density of states）及界面响应。

Result: 在绝缘 BiNiO₃ 超晶格中识别出极性不连续的结构与态密度特征，并发现其通过 BiNiO₃ 电荷歧化（charge disproportionation）模式的改变实现部分屏蔽（partial screening），与先前 BaTiO₃/SmNiO₃ 研究类似；金属相对应体系虽存在屏蔽电荷，但界面响应与绝缘对应物定性上高度相似。

Conclusion: 金属屏蔽电荷对表观形式电荷极性不连续影响甚微；但用极性不连续图像解释和量化金属镍酸盐/铁电体（ferroelectric）界面的屏蔽时存在实际局限，需谨慎对待。

Abstract: We present a first-principles study of the formal-charge polar discontinuity in superlattices of BaTiO$_3$ interfaced with SmNiO$_3$ or BiNiO$_3$, comparing the results for the insulating and metallic nickelate phases. Polar discontinuities arising from differently charged layers in perovskites are well-studied in heterostructures such as LaAlO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$, and recently, signatures consistent with such a polar discontinuity have been observed in systems where the formally charged layers would reside in a metal. We first identify signatures of the polar discontinuity in the structure and the density of states of superlattices with BaTiO$_3$ and insulating BiNiO$_3$, and find partial screening by modification of the BiNiO$_3$ charge disproportionation pattern analogous to our previous study on BaTiO$_3$/SmNiO$_3$. We then examine superlattices constructed from the corresponding metallic nickelate phases and find that, despite the presence of screening charges, the qualitative interfacial response is remarkably similar to that of the insulating counterparts. We show that the metallic screening charges have little influence on the apparent formal-charge polar discontinuity and discuss practical limitations of the polar-discontinuity picture when interpreting and quantifying screening at metallic nickelate/ferroelectric interfaces.

</details>


### [166] [Finite-element reconstruction of molten-zone dynamics and chemical segregation in optical floating-zone grown Ni--Mn--Ga](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08250)
*Denys Musiienko,Taras Kovaliuk,Petr Harcuba,Oleg Heczko,Ross H. Colman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种在透明蓝宝石坩埚内进行的光学浮区法（optical floating-zone method），用于生长均质 Ni–Mn–Ga 磁性形状记忆合金（magnetic shape-memory alloys）单晶，并结合瞬态有限元传热模型（finite-element heat-transfer model）与熔区质量平衡计算解释成分偏析（composition segregation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ni–Mn–Ga 单晶的均质生长受不一致熔融（incongruent melting）、Mn 反应活性及纵向成分偏析限制，需要改进生长工艺并理解偏析机理。

Method: 采用蓝宝石坩埚约束的光学浮区法以稳定熔区并保留光学加热与可视化监控；用 COMSOL Multiphysics 建立瞬态有限元传热模型，并与有效熔区质量平衡（mass-balance）计算耦合，以解算凝固速度与凝固前沿几何。

Result: 晶体生长重现性与宏观几何可控性改善；模型表明局部凝固速度（solidification velocity）并非由设定平移速度固定，而是随熔区几何演化显著变化；EBSD 对淬火生长界面的分析独立支持计算所得凝固前沿几何，耦合质量平衡计算再现了实测成分分布的主要特征。

Conclusion: 偏离理想 Scheil–Pfann 行为源于熔区几何与凝固条件的演化；经实验约束的模型可重建难以直接观测的生长条件，并为蓝宝石约束浮区法生长不一致熔融合金提供优化基础。

Abstract: Homogeneous single-crystal growth of Ni--Mn--Ga magnetic shape-memory alloys is limited by incongruent melting, Mn reactivity, and longitudinal composition segregation. We report a modified optical floating-zone method in which growth is performed inside a transparent sapphire crucible, stabilising the molten zone while preserving optical heating and visual monitoring. The resulting crystals show improved growth reproducibility and controlled macroscopic geometry. To interpret the measured segregation profiles, a transient finite-element heat-transfer model in COMSOL Multiphysics was coupled to an effective molten-zone mass-balance calculation. The model reveals that the local solidification velocity is not fixed by the imposed translation speed but varies strongly as the molten-zone geometry evolves. The calculated solidification-front geometry is independently supported by EBSD analysis of a quenched growth interface, while the coupled mass-balance calculation reproduces the dominant features of the measured composition profiles. These results show that deviations from ideal Scheil--Pfann behaviour arise from the evolving molten-zone geometry and solidification conditions. The experimentally constrained model therefore provides a practical means of reconstructing otherwise inaccessible growth conditions and a basis for optimisation of sapphire-confined floating-zone growth of incongruently melting alloys.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [A Wigner-Lindblad phase space model for open quantum dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07608)
*O. Morandi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Weyl量子化与Wigner相空间描述，构建了开放量子系统的粒子-热浴碰撞模型，通过超对称量子力学因子化约化哈密顿量并引入含位置依赖摩擦与扩散的广义Wigner–Lindblad动力学模型，并在数值模拟中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究开放系统（open system）与热浴（thermal bath）接触时的碰撞量子动力学（collisional quantum dynamics），并克服标准量子Fokker–Planck模型在长时间行为上的非物理假象。

Method: 提出基于Weyl量子化（Weyl quantization）原理与Wigner相空间（Wigner phase space）描述的粒子-热浴相互作用模型；利用超对称量子力学（supersymmetric quantum mechanics）技术因子化约化哈密顿量，构造任意势场下的系统依赖产生与湮灭算符；对Moyal星积（Moyal star product）作二阶半经典展开，将经典Fokker–Planck弛豫机制扩展至含Lindblad量子随机跃迁的Wigner–Lindblad动力学框架。

Result: 建立了广义Wigner–Lindblad动力学模型（含位置依赖摩擦与扩散项）的数学适定性（well-posedness），数值模拟表明该模型消除了标准量子Fokker–Planck模型在四次双势阱（quartic double-well traps）中的非物理长时间假象，准确刻画2D分子势（4D相空间）中的耗散量子态局域化（localization），并有效评估InAs/InP共振隧穿二极管（resonant tunneling diodes）中的电子输运与俘获时间。

Conclusion: 该框架为开放量子系统提供了一种兼顾经典弛豫机制与Lindblad量子随机跃迁的统一相空间描述，具有数学适定性并在多类势场与器件场景中得到数值验证。

Abstract: We investigate the collisional quantum dynamics of an open system in contact with a thermal bath. We propose a particle-bath interaction model based on the Weyl quantization principle and the Wigner phase space description of quantum evolution. By factorizing the reduced Hamiltonian of the open system via supersymmetric quantum mechanics techniques, we construct system-dependent creation and annihilation operators for arbitrary potential landscapes. This allows us to extend the classical Fokker--Planck relaxation mechanism to incorporate Lindblad quantum stochastic jumps within the Wigner phase-space formalism. Through a semiclassical expansion of the Moyal star product to second order, we obtain a generalized Wigner--Lindblad kinetic model featuring position-dependent friction and diffusion terms. We establish the mathematical well-posedness of the Lindblad problem and we validate our approach through numerical simulations. Our model resolves unphysical long-time artifacts of standard quantum Fokker--Planck models in quartic double-well traps, accurately captures dissipative quantum state localization in 2D molecular potentials (4D phase space), and effectively evaluates electron transport and trapping times in InAs/InP resonant tunneling diodes.

</details>


### [168] [The Zhukovskiy-Volterra gyroscope: geometry and reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07789)
*Vladimir Dragović,Borislav Gajić,Božidar Jovanović*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对带陀螺的刚体定点运动的Zhukovskiy可积情形进行辛约化（symplectic reduction），将其化为球面 $S^{n-1}$ 上的可积磁流（integrable magnetic flow）；同时给出Euler陀螺（Zhukovskiy–Volterra陀螺）的几何解释并将其约化至二维球面上的可积磁流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机，仅指出研究对象为 $\mathbb R^n$ 中带陀螺刚体定点运动的Zhukovskiy可积情形及Euler陀螺的Zhukovskiy几何解释。

Method: 采用辛约化（symplectic reduction）方法，分别处理 $\mathbb R^n$ 中Zhukovskiy可积情形与Zhukovskiy–Volterra陀螺系统。

Result: Zhukovskiy可积情形被约化为球面 $S^{n-1}$ 上的可积磁流；Zhukovskiy–Volterra陀螺系统被约化为二维球面上的可积磁流，并给出其几何解释。

Conclusion: 带陀螺刚体定点运动的可积系统可经由辛约化统一表述为球面上的可积磁流，维数由 $\mathbb R^n$ 情形降至 $S^{n-1}$，并在二维球面情形得到具体对应。

Abstract: We describe a symplectic reduction of the Zhukovskiy integrable case of motion of a rigid body about a fixed point with a gyroscope in $\mathbb R^n$ to an integrable magnetic flow on a sphere $S^{n-1}$. We also present Zhukovskiy's geometric interpretation of motion of the Euler top with a gyroscope, aka the Zhukovskiy--Volterra gyroscope, and perform a symplectic reduction of this system to an integrable magnetic flow on a $2$-dimensional sphere.

</details>


### [169] [Topology-Inspired Interfaces in Non-Local Mass-Spring Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07885)
*Robyn G. Edge,Timothy A. Starkey,Gregory J. Chaplain*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本研究将质量-弹簧 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）模型推广至保持双原子单胞手性对称性（chiral symmetry）的超近邻（beyond-nearest-neighbour, BNN）耦合，揭示耦合范围与强度对体响应、拓扑相与界面态的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近邻（nearest-neighbour, NN）相互作用限制了离散晶格动力学与拓扑的设计自由度，作者希望借助非局域耦合（non-local couplings）突破该约束，并理解其在拓扑与界面层面的后果。

Method: 在质量-弹簧 SSH 模型中引入保持手性对称性的 BNN 连接，解析其范围与刚度对体能的影响，并考察非局域界面引起的本征边界微扰及动力学/拓扑异质介质间界面。

Result: 可达绕数（winding number）受晶格连通度上界约束，而弹簧刚度决定具体拓扑相；BNN 界面引入局部边界微扰，异质界面出现由带隙诱导（band-gap-induced）的界面态局域化不对称。

Conclusion: 界面构型可作为控制局域界面模存在性与性质的手段，通过改变界面可调节模式频率、局域化程度及其与体带的杂化（hybridisation）。

Abstract: Non-local couplings offer a means of engineering the dynamics and topology of discrete lattices beyond the constraints of nearest-neighbour (NN) interactions. Here, we extend the mass-spring Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) model to incorporate beyond-nearest-neighbour (BNN) connections that preserve the chiral symmetry of the diatomic unit cell, investigating how their range and strength shape the bulk response. We show that the accessible winding numbers are bounded by the lattice connectivity, while the spring stiffnesses select the specific topological phase. We further investigate the challenges posed by non-local interfaces, where the extended connectivity inherently introduces local boundary perturbations. The dynamic freedom afforded by BNN couplings is exploited by examining interfaces between dynamically and topologically dissimilar bulk media, revealing a band-gap-induced asymmetry in the localisation of interface states. We show that the interfacial configuration provides a means of controlling the existence and character of localised interface modes, with changes to the interface enabling the tuning of mode frequencies, localisation and hybridisation with the bulk bands.

</details>


### [170] [Universal $x$-$y$ duality in equivariant topological recursion and quartic Kontsevich model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08018)
*Alexander Hock,Jakob Lindner,Raimar Wulkenhaar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在等变拓扑递归（equivariant topological recursion）框架下研究 $x$-$y$ 对偶（$x$-$y$ duality）、其参数化版本及辛对偶（symplectic duality），并给出四次数 Kontsevich 矩阵模型的全亏格（all-genus）猜想解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑递归可生成无穷族多微分（multi-differentials），而 $x$-$y$ 对偶提供了连接两族多微分的显式途径；作者希望将这一对偶结构推广到等变情形，并用其刻画 Kontsevich 矩阵模型的分区函数与谱曲线。

Method: 在等变拓扑递归设定下分析 $x$-$y$ 对偶、参数化版本与辛对偶，显式研究对偶族的极点结构（pole structure）并给出各类极部分的递归公式；同时为任意势 $P$ 的 Kontsevich 矩阵模型分区函数构造与 IZHC 积分特征展开相关的 Fock 空间实现（Fock space realisation），由此得到相对隐式谱曲线 $(X,Y)$ 的两族互为 $X$-$Y$ 对偶的多微分。

Result: 与经典拓扑递归不同，对偶族在群作用的固定点处以及由群作用确定的反对角线（anti-diagonals）上出现额外极点；解 $Y(X)$ 对势 $P(\Phi)=\frac{\lambda}{4}\Phi^4$ 的四次模型成立，并识别出将其与 $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$-等变谱曲线联系的对偶变换网络与辛平移（symplectic shifts）。

Conclusion: 该构造给出四次 Kontsevich 模型的一个猜想性全亏格解，并复现了此前在亏格 $g\leq 1$ 已建立的递归结构。

Abstract: Topological recursion is a universal procedure that generates an infinite family of multi-differentials with different interpretations in various areas of mathematics. Recently, a new understanding of topological recursion has emerged from $x$-$y$ duality, which relates two such families through explicit formulae. We study $x$-$y$ duality, its parametrised version, and symplectic duality in the setting of equivariant topological recursion. We explicitly analyse the pole structure of the resulting dual families and provide recursive formulae for the different types of polar parts. In contrast to classical topological recursion, the dual family has additional poles at fixed points of the group action, and at anti-diagonals determined by the group action.
  We show that the partition function $Z_P(E)=\int_{H_N} e^{-N\,\mathrm{Tr}(EΦ^2+P(Φ))}dΦ$ of the Kontsevich matrix model with arbitrary potential $P$, where $dΦ$ is the Lebesgue measure on Hermitian $N\times N$ matrices and $E$ a positive matrix, admits a Fock space realisation related to the character expansion of the IZHC integral. The Fock space framework provides two mutually $X$-$Y$ dual families of multi-differentials relative to an implicitly defined spectral curve $(X,Y)$. The solution $Y(X)$ succeeds for the quartic Kontsevich model with potential $P(Φ)=\fracλ{4}Φ^4$. We identify a network of duality transformations and symplectic shifts which relate the quartic Kontsevich model with the $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$-equivariant spectral curve. The proposed construction yields a conjectural all-genus solution to the quartic Kontsevich model, which reproduces recursive structures previously established for genus $g\leq 1$.

</details>


### [171] [Fitness advantages, prediction of resistances and protocol design: exploiting the synergy between mathematical modelling and statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08059)
*Giulia Chiari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出整合数学建模与统计推断的框架，用于刻画克隆选择、预测放射抗性并优化放疗方案；通过255,150次计算机模拟揭示演化动力学偏好高增殖克隆、放疗选择抗性克隆并降低异质性，并给出不同患者特征下的最优方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤演化与治疗抗性由克隆异质性（clonal heterogeneity）、选择压力（selective pressure）与治疗干预之间的相互作用驱动，需要可量化手段同时理解克隆选择机制并指导个体化放疗设计。

Method: 构建多克隆动力学模型（multi-clonal dynamical model），纳入增殖、资源竞争、表观遗传不稳定性（epigenetic instability）与克隆特异性放射敏感性；并对255,150次计算机模拟（in silico simulations）进行统计分析。

Result: 演化动力学倾向选择高增殖克隆，而放疗选择放射抗性（radioresistance）群体并降低肿瘤异质性；研究进一步刻画肿瘤特征对耐药轨迹的影响，并识别肿瘤控制、毒性与抗性选择之间的权衡关系（trade-offs）。

Conclusion: 在考虑剂量、分割方式（fractionation）与时机的前提下，可为不同患者特征确定最优方案；治疗史（treatment history）会影响二线治疗（second-line therapies）疗效，表明机制建模与统计推断结合可支撑癌症演化理解与个体化放疗设计。

Abstract: Tumour evolution and treatment resistance are driven by the interplay between clonal heterogeneity, selective pressures, and therapeutic interventions. In this work, we propose an integrated mathematical and statistical framework to investigate clonal selection, predict radioresistance, and optimize radiotherapy protocols. We develop a multi-clonal dynamical model incorporating proliferation, resource competition, epigenetic instability, and clone-specific radiosensitivity. Through the statistical analysis of 255,150 in silico simulations, we identify key biological determinants of treatment response and characterize the effects of evolutionary competition and radiotherapy on clonal composition. Our results show that evolutionary dynamics favour highly proliferative clones, whereas radiotherapy selects radioresistant populations and reduces tumour heterogeneity. We further investigate how tumour characteristics influence resistance trajectories and identify trade-offs between tumour control, toxicity, and resistance selection across different treatment schedules. Optimal protocols are determined for distinct patient profiles, accounting for dose, fractionation, and timing. Finally, we demonstrate how treatment history affects the efficacy of second-line therapies. Overall, our findings highlight the potential of combining mechanistic modelling and statistical inference to understand cancer evolution and support personalized radiotherapy design.

</details>


### [172] [Multifractal scaling of quantum eigenfunctions in the thermodynamic limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08087)
*Jonathan P. Keating,Henrik Ueberschaer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在维数 $d\geq 5$ 下，于热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）中证明了与点状凸起势（point-like bump potentials）薛定谔算子本征函数相关测度的多重分形标度（multifractal scaling），并显式给出分形指数随矩参数变化的函数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学文献长期预期此类本征函数测度在高维（$d\geq 5$）呈现多重分形行为，本文旨在为该预期提供严格的数学证明。

Method: 研究对象为含点状凸起势的薛定谔算子（Schrödinger operators）本征函数相关测度，在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）框架下分析其多重分形标度（multifractal scaling），并显式计算分形指数作为矩参数的函数；证明依赖对散射体位置的弱无序条件（weak disorder condition）与对本征值序列的强耦合条件（strong coupling condition）。

Result: 在所设条件下，$d\geq 5$ 时相关测度满足多重分形标度，分形指数可显式表示为定义各阶矩的参数（矩参数）的函数。

Conclusion: 结果与物理学文献在高维情形下的长期预期一致，为该多重分形现象提供了严格依据；其适用范围受弱无序与强耦合两项条件的约束。

Abstract: We prove multifractal scaling of measures related to the eigenfunctions of Schrödinger operators with point-like bump potentials, in the thermodynamic limit, in dimension $d\geq 5$. This is consistent with a longstanding expectation in the physics literature in these dimensions. The fractal exponents are computed explicitly as a function of the parameter defining the moments. Our result requires a weak disorder condition on the location of the scatterers and a strong coupling condition on the sequence of eigenvalues.

</details>


### [173] [Overcompressive shocks in a $2\times2$ hyperbolic system of conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08292)
*A. P. Chugainova,R. R. Polekhina,D. V. Treschev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究弹性杆中描述小幅纵向-扭转波的2×2双曲守恒律系统的过压缩激波（overcompressive shock），借助各向异性耗散矩阵的粘性正则化方法，通过相图定性分析证明在参数平面某一区域内存在二参数族的异宿轨道，从而确认模型容许属于Liu分类第一类的过压缩激波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于确认该弹性杆波动模型是否容许过压缩激波（overcompressive shock），并刻画其激波结构；过压缩激波在2×2双曲守恒律系统中较为特殊，需借助粘性正则化（viscous regularization）判定其可容许性与类型归属。

Method: 采用带各向异性耗散矩阵（anisotropic dissipative matrix）的粘性正则化方法处理该系统，并对相应行波方程的相图（phase portrait）进行定性分析，以考察连接平衡点的异宿轨道（heteroclinic trajectory）。

Result: 相图定性分析表明，在参数平面六个区域之一中存在表示过压缩激波结构的二参数族异宿轨道；据此确认该模型容许过压缩激波，且按Liu分类（Liu's classification）属于第一类。

Conclusion: 该弹性杆纵向-扭转波模型确实容许过压缩激波，其结构由二参数族异宿轨道刻画，并在Liu分类中归为第一类；结果为该类双曲守恒律系统中过压缩激波的数学刻画提供了依据。

Abstract: Overcompressive shocks in a $2\times2$ hyperbolic system of conservation laws describing small-amplitude longitudinal-torsional waves in an elastic rod are investigated. A viscous regularization method with an anisotropic dissipative matrix is applied to the system. A qualitative analysis of phase portraits has revealed that a two-parameter family of heteroclinic trajectories, representing the structure of an overcompressive shock, exists in one of six domains in the parameter plane. It is established that the model admits overcompressive shocks, which belong to the first type according to the classification of Liu.

</details>


### [174] [Oriented V-systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08326)
*Maali Alkadhem,Misha Feigin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出“定向∨-系统（oriented ∨-system）”概念，它给出定向结合方程（oriented associativity equations）的一类解，推广了 Veselov 的 WDVV 方程 ∨-系统解，并在子系统、限制运算下封闭，实例涉及非本原复反射群（imprimitive complex reflection groups）的多参数族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Veselov 的 ∨-系统可构造 WDVV 方程的解，但这一解类需要向定向结合方程框架推广，并需要更丰富的构造来源。

Method: 将定向∨-系统定义为线性空间中向量与余向量对（vector and covector pairs）的一种特殊构型，用其构造定向结合方程的解，并研究其在取子系统（subsystems）与限制（restrictions）下的封闭性。

Result: 证明了定向∨-系统类在取子系统和限制下封闭，并给出包含与非本原复反射群相关的多参数族在内的实例。

Conclusion: 定向∨-系统是 WDVV 方程 ∨-系统解在定向结合方程中的自然推广，其封闭性质与复反射群实例为构造该类方程的解提供了系统性途径。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of an oriented $\vee$-system which gives a class of solutions of the oriented associativity equations generalising Veselov's $\vee$-system solutions of WDVV equations. An oriented $\vee$-system is a special configuration of pairs of a vector and a covector in a linear space. We show that the class of oriented $\vee$-systems is closed under taking subsystems and restrictions. We provide examples which include a multiparameter family related to imprimitive complex reflection groups.

</details>

### [175] [Exact and Near-Exact Inverse Renormalization Group with Constrained Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07641)
*Dimitrios Bachtis*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该工作证明约束马尔可夫过程（constrained Markov processes）在不动点作用量处可精确逆转变换重整化群（renormalization group），并在其邻域近似精确，且在三维 φ⁴ 量子场论中实现从 V=16³ 到 V=128³ 的无临界慢化构型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆重整化群（inverse renormalization group）缺乏严格的理论保证与计算稳定性手段，尤其在高维系统中由粗晶格构型生成细晶格构型时难以避免临界慢化（critical slowing down）。

Method: 以在细晶格块内采样、并施加与粗晶格自由度一致的约束的蒙特卡罗方法（Monte Carlo）构造约束马尔可夫过程；解析证明其与重整化群逆变换的精确/近似精确对应关系，并在逆重整化后的构型上直接优化重整化群变换以保持计算稳定性。

Result: 对任意变换解析给出了精确性条件；在三维 φ⁴ 量子场论（quantum field theory）、V=16³ 的无关联构型基础上，生成 V=128³ 的近平衡无关联构型并提取两个临界指数（critical exponents），测得动态临界指数 z=0.03(2)，无临界慢化。

Conclusion: 约束马尔可夫过程为逆重整化群提供了理论保证与实用算法，可在高维系统中高效生成大体积构型并用于临界指数测定。

Abstract: We establish conditions for exactness of the inverse renormalization group via constrained Markov processes, a class of Monte Carlo simulations which sample the degrees of freedom within a block on a fine lattice under the constraint that they remain consistent with the corresponding degree of freedom on the coarse lattice according to a forward renormalization group transformation. We show analytically, for an arbitrary transformation, that constrained Markov processes correspond to an exact inversion of the renormalization group at the fixed point action, and to a near-exact inversion in the vicinity of the fixed point. We then optimize renormalization group transformations directly on inversely renormalized configurations to effectively map the action onto itself and ensure computational stability in the generation of configurations for high-dimensional systems. Starting from a set of uncorrelated configurations for the three-dimensional $φ^{4}$ quantum field theory at lattice volume $V=16^{3}$, we invert the renormalization group with constrained Monte Carlo to construct a set of uncorrelated, near-equilibrium configurations for $V=128^{3}$ and extract two critical exponents of the system. The inversion exhibits no critical slowing down, with dynamic critical exponent $z=0.03(2)$.

</details>


### [176] [The 2d Ising Model on Hyperbolic Lattices and the Role of Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07045)
*Pallabi Dey,Debasish Banerjee,Arnab Kundu,Ritam Sinha*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该文利用蠕虫算法（worm algorithm）与Metropolis模拟，研究规则双曲镶嵌（regular hyperbolic tessellations）上铁磁Ising模型临界行为对边界条件的依赖。结果表明边界条件的选择与"内部—全系统"可观测量的区分对非可均格点（non-amenable lattices）上的临界现象刻画至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲格点属非可均格点（non-amenable lattices），其边界占比不趋于零，边界条件可能显著影响临界行为；因此需系统比较开放边界与接线边界（wired boundaries）下的标度表现，厘清边界效应与体相性质的区分。

Method: 在若干规则双曲镶嵌上以蠕虫与Metropolis模拟研究铁磁Ising模型；开放边界与通过共同辅助自旋（auxiliary spin）实现的接线边界进行对照，考察磁化率（susceptibility）的体积型有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）、Binder crossings与磁化率峰，并分析中心—辅助自旋关联、中心自旋对均匀磁场的响应及弱边界场的分层响应。

Result: 开放边界下磁化率呈体积型有限尺寸标度，其非平均场（non-mean-field）标度指数在四种镶嵌上近似稳定，支持共同普适类（universality class）的可能；Binder crossings与磁化率峰比较显示显著有限尺寸效应。接线边界下中心—辅助自旋关联呈径向标度，与平均场磁化指数一致；中心自旋对均匀场的响应进一步检验了δ=3；弱边界场的分层响应显示诱导磁化向内部增强。

Conclusion: 边界条件的选取以及内部观测量与全系统观测量之间的区分，是刻画非可均格点上临界现象的关键因素；不同边界设定可能指向不同的有效标度图像。

Abstract: We investigate the role of boundary conditions in the critical behaviour of the ferromagnetic Ising model on regular hyperbolic tessellations. Using worm and Metropolis simulations, we compare open boundaries with wired boundaries implemented through a common auxiliary spin. For open boundaries, the susceptibility exhibits volume-based finite-size scaling with non-mean-field scaling exponents that are approximately stable across four tessellations, supporting the possibility of a common universality class. Comparisons with Binder crossings and susceptibility peaks reveal substantial finite-size effects. For wired boundaries, the central-to-auxiliary spin correlator exhibits radial scaling consistent with a mean-field magnetization exponent. The central-spin response to a uniform magnetic field provides a further consistency test of $δ=3$. Layer-resolved responses to a weak boundary field also reveal an enhancement of the induced magnetization towards the interior. Our results highlight the importance of boundary conditions and the distinction between interior and whole-system observables in characterizing critical phenomena on non-amenable lattices.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [The lattice Boltzmann machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07139)
*Hua-Dong Yao,Sauro Succi*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文主张格子玻尔兹曼（lattice Boltzmann, LB）方法的灵活性源于其作为一种由规则驱动的信息处理机器的"生成性"，其更新过程与残差网络、Transformer同属迭代弛豫动力学，只是算符由守恒律与对称性固定而非从数据学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LB方法被广泛视为灵活高效的输运现象求解器，但其灵活性来源缺乏机制层面的解释；作者希望揭示这种灵活性背后的生成性本质，并据此界定该方法的适用边界与扩展准则。

Method: 作者提出更新过程的三条性质足以促使宏观闭合定律从底层迭代中涌现，物理内容由"目标（target）"决定；通过随机目标机器、无格点的图（graph）上交通过程训练网络，以及阻尼失效的极限情形三类论证展开。

Result: 随机抽取目标的机器生成由其矩（moments）预测的输运定律；在无格点的图上具备同样三条性质的网络经交通过程训练后自行生成守恒律；该机制存在一个精确极限，此时非守恒模（non-conserved modes）的阻尼失效，而格点上宏观定律对此毫无预警。

Conclusion: 在此视角下，LB是一种"格子玻尔兹曼机（lattice Boltzmann machine）"，其信息规则由物理而非训练提供；三条性质给出了新要素（无论来自物理还是数据）进入LB模型时应保持的规范。

Abstract: The lattice Boltzmann (LB) method is widely regarded as a very flexible and efficient solver for a broad class of transport phenomena in fluids, soft matter and related fields. In this paper, we posit that such flexibility is a direct result of its generative nature as a rule-driven information-processing machine. Its update, a relaxation toward a local target followed by a shift along the lattice, belongs to the same class of iterated relaxation dynamics as residual networks and transformers, with the difference that in LB the operators are fixed by conservation and symmetry rather than learned from data. We show that three properties of this update are sufficient to promote the emergence of closed macroscopic laws from the underlying iteration process, the physical content of the target deciding the specific law in question. A machine whose target is drawn at random generates the transport law predicted from its moments. A network given the same three properties on a graph, where there is no lattice, is trained on a traffic process and generates a conservation law of its own. The mechanism has a precise limit, where the damping of the non-conserved modes fails, and on the lattice the macroscopic law gives no warning of it. In this reading, LB is a lattice Boltzmann machine whose informing rules are supplied by physics rather than by training. Its three properties then state what a new ingredient should preserve when it enters an LB model, whether it comes from physics or from data.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Langmuir kinetics and totally asymmetric volume exclusion in linear chains via Fock space formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07053)
*Luis D. S. Silva,Arthur M. S. Carvalho,Francisco A. G. Almeida,Gerson C. Duarte-Filho*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文用精确的Doi-Peliti场论算符方法（Doi-Peliti field-theoretic operator formulation），在Fock空间中求解有限长TASEP-LK链（≤16格点）的稳态，绕过全矩阵对角化的高计算复杂度，揭示有限尺寸涨落抹平奇异畴壁并导致连续相变交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动扩散系统（driven-diffusive systems）如TASEP-LK是交通流、传送网络与介观流道的基元模型，但有限链中的随机涨落与空间关联难以用平均场（mean-field）准确刻画，而直接矩阵方法计算代价过高。

Method: 采用Fock空间中的精确Doi-Peliti场论算符表述，借助非对称非厄米矩阵（asymmetric non-Hermitian matrices）的双正交本征向量（biorthonormal eigenvectors）分解，由准哈密顿量（quasi-Hamiltonian）的核直接计算稳态，避免全矩阵对角化与时依赖矩阵指数运算。

Result: 精确离散计算显示有限尺寸随机涨落自然抹平奇异畴壁（domain walls），产生平滑密度剖面与连续相变交叉；有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）表明局部剖面随k→∞系统性地趋近连续场论预言的尖锐畴壁；慢动力学区中两点关联产生强格点间累积量，使平均场系统性地低估联合占据概率达50%。

Conclusion: 局域随机涨落与空间关联主导介观输运系统及短交通走廊的稳态动力学，因此此类体系建模需要发展解析方法而非依赖标准平均场近似。

Abstract: Driven-diffusive systems combining spatial exclusion and bulk exchange, such as the totally asymmetric simple exclusion process coupled with Langmuir kinetics (TASEP-LK), serve as foundational models for particle transport in vehicular traffic, conveyor networks, and mesoscopic flow channels.
