In this work, we employ an exact Doi-Peliti field-theoretic operator formulation in Fock space to evaluate steady-state observables, continuous phase landscapes, and spatial correlations in finite linear chains up to $k = 16$. By introducing a decomposition in terms of biorthonormal eigenvectors for asymmetric non-Hermitian matrices, the stationary state is calculated directly from the kernel of the quasi-Hamiltonian, completely bypassing the computationally prohibitive complexity of full matrix diagonalization and time-dependent matrix exponentiation. Across distinct rate regimes and global parametric scans, our exact discrete calculations demonstrate that finite-size stochastic fluctuations naturally smear out singular domain walls, producing smooth density profiles and continuous phase crossovers. Finite-size scaling illustrates how local profiles systematically steepen toward the sharp domain wall predicted by continuum field theory as $k \to \infty$. Furthermore, two-point correlation analysis reveals that in slow-kinetics regimes, spatial fluctuations generate strong inter-site cumulants, causing standard mean-field approximations to systematically underestimate joint occupancy probabilities by 50\%. These findings emphasize the necessity of analytical methods for modeling mesoscopic transport systems and short traffic corridors where local stochastic fluctuations and spatial correlations dominate the stationary dynamics.

</details>


### [179] [Percolation of discorectangles oriented predominantly along two mutually perpendicular directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07484)
*Yuri Yu. Tarasevich*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将有限厚度细长物体（discorectangle，即"体育场"形状）沿两个相互垂直方向的"马耳他十字"取向分布下的渗流问题从零厚度棒推广到有限厚度情形，基于排除面积解析式与特征值方法给出阈值近似关系，并由蒙特卡洛模拟证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果主要针对零厚度棒（zero-thickness sticks）的渗流；实际物体具有有限厚度，需考察厚度如何改变渗流阈值（percolation threshold）及归一化曲线的普适性。

Method: 采用"马耳他十字"（Maltese cross）角分布（由参数α刻画），基于discorectangle对的排除面积（excluded area）解析表达式，用特征值方法（eigenvalue method）估计渗流阈值；将连通性矩阵表示为棒矩阵加一个由排除面积常数部分 C=4lw+πw² 决定的秩1项。

Result: 得到近似关系 λ_max^DR ≈ λ_max^stick + C 与 n_c^DR ≈ n_c^stick/(1+C n_c^stick)；有限厚度移动绝对阈值并影响第一种归一化，而通过最大特征值表述的第二种归一化抵消加性几何贡献、仅保留取向依赖性，从而解释了归一化曲线的普适性。对长径比8、16、32的单位长度discorectangle开展的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟、不同区域尺寸、周期边界条件（periodic boundary conditions）与union–find标度分析均证实理论预测。

Conclusion: 有限厚度可通过排除面积的常数项以秩1修正纳入棒渗流框架，阈值的取向依赖性与厚度的加性几何贡献可分离，理论近似与数值模拟一致。

Abstract: The percolation of finite-thickness elongated objects---discorectangles (stadiums)---oriented predominantly along two mutually perpendicular directions is studied, extending recent results for zero-thickness sticks. The angular distribution is the ``Maltese cross'' parametrized by $α$. Based on the known analytic expression for the excluded area of a discorectangle pair, the percolation threshold is estimated by the eigenvalue method. The discorectangle connectivity matrix is represented as the stick matrix plus a rank-1 term determined by the constant part of the excluded area, $C=4lw+πw^{2}$, which gives the approximate relations $λ_{\max}^{\mathrm{DR}}\approxλ_{\max}^{\mathrm{stick}}+C$ and $n_c^{\mathrm{DR}}\approx n_c^{\mathrm{stick}}/(1+C n_c^{\mathrm{stick}})$. Finite thickness shifts the absolute thresholds and affects the first normalization, whereas the second normalization, written through maximum eigenvalues, cancels the additive geometric contribution and preserves only the orientational dependence, explaining the universality of the normalized curves. Monte Carlo simulations of unit-length discorectangles with aspect ratios 8, 16, and 32, various domain sizes, periodic boundary conditions, and union--find scaling analysis confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [180] [Galois conjugation, droplet growth, and the differential transcendence of the Ising model in nonzero field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08033)
*Ran Luo,Jiahua Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明：在任意非零无量纲磁场（dimensionless field）$H=h/(k_BT)\neq 0$ 下，正方晶格 Ising 模型（square-lattice Ising model）的自由能（free energy）与磁化强度（magnetization）不满足关于耦合 $K=J/(k_BT)$ 的代数微分方程（algebraic differential equation）。该结论排除了 Onsager 型公式及一大类特殊函数（special-function）表达式，并将此前遗留的可数多个例外场也一并消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正方晶格 Ising 模型仅在零磁场下可精确求解，非零磁场下精确解（exact solution）的可能范围长期未定。此前工作仅留下可数多个易逸度（fugacity）为代数整数的场未决，本文旨在填补这一缺口并界定非零场下精确解的不可能性边界。

Method: 采用伽罗瓦共轭（Galois conjugation）与反向自旋液滴（droplets of reversed spins）的大阶增长分析。在单位圆之外的共轭易逸度处，近方形液滴产生与代数微分方程不相容的系数增长；并进一步证明在全压强级数（pressure series）中该增长无法被抵消。

Result: 对每个固定的 $H\neq 0$，自由能与磁化强度均不满足代数微分方程，从而排除 Onsager 型公式与广泛的特殊函数表达。自由能结论亦适用于 Lieb 晶格（Lieb lattice）以及等长链修饰、均匀耦合与场的正方晶格；并在任意 $d\ge2$ 维中，对满足 $e^{-2|H|}$ 存在大于 1 的实代数共轭的场给出解决。

Conclusion: 非零磁场下正方晶格 Ising 模型的精确解不可能具有 Onsager 型或广义特殊函数形式，此前遗留的例外场被完全消除，结果可推广至若干相关晶格与更高维情形，明确了该模型精确可解性的界限。

Abstract: The square-lattice Ising model is exactly solvable in zero magnetic field, but the scope of exact solutions at nonzero field remains unresolved. We show that, at every fixed dimensionless field $H=h/(k_BT)\ne 0$, its free energy and magnetization obey no algebraic differential equation in the coupling $K=J/(k_BT)$. This excludes Onsager-type formulas and broad classes of special-function expressions. Previous work left open a countable set of fields with algebraic-integer fugacities. We eliminate these exceptions using Galois conjugation and the large-order growth of droplets of reversed spins. At conjugate fugacities outside the unit circle, nearly square droplets produce coefficient growth incompatible with an algebraic differential equation. We prove that cancellations cannot remove this growth in the full pressure series. The free-energy result also holds for the Lieb lattice and square lattices with equal-length chain decorations and uniform coupling and field. In every dimension $d\ge2$, we additionally resolve fields for which $e^{-2|H|}$ has a real algebraic conjugate greater than one.

</details>


### [181] [Electrical conductivity of a two-dimensional two-component medium: discretization and effective medium approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07491)
*Yuri Yu. Tarasevich*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在二维双组分介质中，采用离散化方案与有效介质近似（effective-medium approximation, EMA）建立无量纲有效电导率的三次方程；与200×200电阻网络直接计算对比，无关联无序下符合良好，关联无序下出现显著偏差且类渗流拐点右移，质疑关联无序EMA方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机各向同性二维双组分介质的有效电导率（effective conductivity），并检验有效介质近似（EMA）在无关联与关联无序（correlated disorder）条件下的适用性，同时解释此前随机纳米线网络（random nanowire networks）中报道的偏差。

Method: 将方形晶格叠加于随机介质，每格点取多数相的电导率并以四个电阻的星形结构替代，相邻格点由电导率为σ1、σ2或调和平均σ12=2σ1σ2/(σ1+σ2)的等效电阻连接，概率分别为(1-p)^2、p^2、2p(1-p)；结合Foster定理与EMA导出无量纲有效电导率x的闭合方程，并化为系数依赖p与电导率比r的三次方程（cubic equation）。

Result: EMA预测与周期性侧边界、上下Dirichlet条件的200×200电阻网络直接计算相比：无关联无序时符合良好；在按相邻节点颜色的离散化规则赋值的关联无序情形下出现显著偏差，类渗流拐点（percolation-like inflection point）向1/2右侧移动。

Conclusion: 结果对关联无序情形的EMA方法有效性提出质疑，并有助于解释随机纳米线网络中先前报道的偏差。

Abstract: The effective conductivity of a two-dimensional two-component medium is studied within a discretization scheme and the effective-medium approximation (EMA). A square lattice is superimposed on a random isotropic medium; each cell is assigned the conductivity of its majority component, and the cell is replaced by a star of four resistors. Neighboring cells are connected by an equivalent resistor whose conductivity is $σ_1$, $σ_2$, or the harmonic mean $σ_{12}=2σ_1σ_2/(σ_1+σ_2)$, with probabilities $(1-p)^2$, $p^2$, and $2p(1-p)$, respectively. Applying Foster's theorem and the EMA yields a closed equation for the dimensionless effective conductivity $x$ and, after transformation, a cubic equation whose coefficients depend on $p$ and the conductivity ratio $r$. The EMA predictions are compared with direct calculations on a $200\times200$ resistor network with periodic lateral boundary conditions and Dirichlet conditions on the top and bottom. For uncorrelated disorder, good agreement is found. For correlated disorder, when resistor conductivities are assigned according to the discretization rule from the colors of adjacent nodes, significant deviations appear and the percolation-like inflection point shifts to the right of $1/2$. The results question the validity of the correlated-disorder EMA approach and help explain discrepancies previously reported for random nanowire networks.

</details>


### [182] [Thermodynamic geometry of three coupled Ising spins: exact curvature, frustration and bond anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08072)
*Zahra Ebadi,Nayyere Einali Saghavaz,Omid Yahyayi Monem,Hosein Mohammadzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文以任意两两耦合、纵向场中的三个伊辛自旋为对象，给出了 Fisher-Rao（Ruppeiner）几何的闭式解，并表明热力学曲率可作为精确基态简并（ground-state degeneracy）的极灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学曲率（thermodynamic curvature）通常只在热力学极限下检验其物理含义，而小自旋团簇本身即具有物理意义（独立分子三聚体样品、少比特量子模拟器），且是唯一能闭式获得几何并完整呈现其对相互作用拓扑依赖的情形。

Method: 研究三个伊辛自旋（three Ising spins）在纵向场中带任意两两耦合时的 Fisher-Rao（Ruppeiner）几何，该团簇在开链（open chain）与几何阻挫三角（geometrically frustrated triangle）之间连续插值；对对称三角形给出标量曲率（scalar curvature）的闭式表达式，并推导低温曲率平台的一般公式。

Result: 对称三角形的曲率在无相互作用线及一条与耦合无关的固定场温比线上为零，后者与单自旋磁化率的拐点重合，其近零行为严格线性，反映解析性（analyticity）而非临界标度；阻挫反铁磁区曲率在零温下仍为有限负值，该平台与简并基态流形（degenerate ground manifold）到第一激发态之间磁化含量的变化相关；任意键各向异性（bond anisotropy）会在一个仅对数依赖于各向异性的交叉温度以下破坏平台；铁磁侧零温曲率为所有耦合普适，各向异性团簇中附加的零线追踪场致激发能级交叉。

Conclusion: 小自旋团簇的热力学曲率可闭式求得并直接映射到能级结构，因而是精确基态简并的极灵敏探测器（对键各向异性仅呈对数依赖），同时提示曲率的零值行为可能源于解析性而非临界性。

Abstract: Thermodynamic curvature is widely used as a diagnostic of interactions and phase transitions, yet its interpretation is usually tested only in the thermodynamic limit. Small spin clusters are physically relevant in their own right, since a macroscopic sample of independent molecular trimers or a few-qubit quantum simulator is governed exactly by the few-spin partition function. They also provide the only setting in which the geometry can be obtained in closed form together with its full dependence on the interaction topology. We study the Fisher-Rao (Ruppeiner) geometry of three Ising spins with arbitrary pairwise couplings in a longitudinal field. This cluster interpolates continuously between an open chain and a geometrically frustrated triangle. For the symmetric triangle we obtain the scalar curvature in closed form. It vanishes on the noninteracting line and on a line of fixed field-to-temperature ratio that does not depend on the coupling, which coincides with the inflection point of the single-spin susceptibility. The near-zero behaviour is therefore exactly linear and reflects analyticity rather than critical scaling. In the frustrated antiferromagnetic regime the curvature remains finite and negative down to zero temperature. A general low-temperature formula relates this plateau to the change in magnetization content between the degenerate ground manifold and its first excitation. Any bond anisotropy destroys the plateau below a crossover temperature that depends only logarithmically on the anisotropy, so the curvature is an extremely sensitive detector of exact ground-state degeneracy. On the ferromagnetic side the zero-temperature curvature is universal for all couplings, whereas additional zero lines in anisotropic clusters track field-induced crossings of excited levels.

</details>


### [183] [Typical number and stability of equilibria in large non-reciprocal complex systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08324)
*Bertrand Lacroix-A-Chez-Toine,Pierre Le Doussal,Valentina Ros*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有竞争性梯度与非梯度（非互易）高斯力场的 N≫1 维动力学模型，计算按稳定性指标分解的淬火复杂度（quenched complexity），并证明其仅在完全非互易极限下与退火复杂度（annealed complexity）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注随机高维动力学中大量局域平衡态（local equilibria）的典型数目问题，尤其是非互易分量（non-reciprocal component）如何影响平衡态计数的典型性，以区分平均计数与典型实现之间的差异。

Method: 模型由两项竞争构成：一项驱动系统向平凡平衡态松弛，另一项为促进大量局域平衡态涌现的高斯力场（Gaussian force field），后者含可调强度的非互易分量。作者计算按稳定性指标（stability index）分解的淬火复杂度（quenched complexity）——典型平衡态数目的指数增长率，并与退火复杂度——平均平衡态数目的指数增长率——进行比较。

Result: 两种复杂度仅在完全非互易极限（fully non-reciprocal limit）下重合；对任何有限互易程度，典型实现所含平衡态数目比平均计数预测的指数级更少，且互易性增强会扩大这一差异。淬火复杂度显式依赖于随机力场的协方差（covariance）与非互易程度，退火层面所见的普适性（universality）在此失效。

Conclusion: 平均平衡态计数虽能正确定位拓扑平凡化转变（topology trivialization transition）的位置，但无法刻画该转变附近平衡态的典型组织方式，也无法给出主导平衡态的稳定性指标。

Abstract: We consider a prototypical class of dynamics of $N\gg 1$ degrees of freedom subject to two competing terms, one favoring relaxation to a trivial equilibrium and one taking the form of a Gaussian force field promoting the emergence of many local equilibria. The latter is a non-gradient field, i.e. it includes a non-reciprocal component with a tunable strength. For this model, we compute the quenched complexity, i.e. the exponential growth rate of the typical number of equilibria, resolved by stability index. We compare it with the annealed complexity, i.e. the exponential growth rate of the average number of equilibria. We show that the two coincide only in the fully non-reciprocal limit: for any finite degree of reciprocity, typical realizations contain exponentially fewer equilibria than predicted by the average count. Increasing reciprocity enhances the discrepancy between the two. The quenched complexity depends explicitly on the covariance of the random force field and on the degree of non-reciprocity: the universality observed at the annealed level breaks down. The average counting of equilibria correctly identifies the location of the topology trivialization transition, but it does not capture the typical organization of equilibria near this transition, nor the stability index of the dominant equilibria.

</details>


### [184] [Asymptotic analysis of the dynamical field-field correlation function of the impenetrable Bose gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08350)
*Frank Göhmann,Karol K. Kozlowski,Mikhail D. Minin,Alexander Weiße*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究不可穿透玻色气体（impenetrable Bose gas）在由准动量分布（quasi-momentum distribution）刻画的一般平衡态下两点关联函数（two-point correlation function）的长时、大距离渐近行为，给出由弛豫率（relaxation rate）、对数修正与振幅构成的渐近展开，并计算了两费米点情形下的首个次领头修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多集中于特定系综（如吉布斯系综）下的关联函数渐近行为，而本文希望推广到由准动量分布或填充分数（filling fraction）刻画的更一般平衡态，从而统一处理吉布斯系综（Gibbs ensemble）与广义吉布斯系综（generalized Gibbs ensemble）等情形。

Method: 采用解析方法研究两点关联函数的长时间、大距离渐近展开；指出展开的整体结构取决于费米点（Fermi points，即准动量分布取值为 1/2 的点）的数目，并据此确定领头渐近行为的组成要素。

Result: 领头渐近行为由弛豫率（relaxation rate）、一个对数修正项（logarithmic correction）与一个振幅共同决定，三者均给出简单且显式的表达式；在两费米点情形下，进一步计算了首个次领头修正（first sub-leading correction）。

Conclusion: 不可穿透玻色气体两点关联函数的渐近结构由费米点数目分类刻画，所给显式表达式为吉布斯系综与广义吉布斯系综等一般平衡态提供了统一的渐近描述。

Abstract: We study the long-time, large-distance asymptotic behaviour of the two-point correlation function of fields of the impenetrable Bose gas in equilibrium states characterized by a quasi-momentum distribution (or filling fraction). This includes, in particular, the cases of Gibbs and generalized Gibbs ensembles. The overall structure of the asymptotic expansion depends on the number of Fermi points, those points for which the quasi-momentum distribution takes the value $1/2$. The leading asymptotic behaviour is determined by a relaxation rate, a logarithmic correction term and an amplitude for which we provide simple and explicit expressions. In the case of two Fermi points the first sub-leading correction is calculated as well.

</details>


### [185] [Solvable macroscopic models for populations of type I excitability neurons with Gaussian noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07714)
*Maria V. Ageeva,Denis S. Goldobin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文针对受加性高斯噪声（additive Gaussian noise）驱动的二次积分-发放神经元（quadratic integrate-and-fire neuron, QIF）模型，基于首达时间（first passage time）问题给出平均峰间期（mean interspike interval）的解析解，并据此构建放电率（firing rate）与群体平均膜电位（population-mean membrane potential）的解析表达式，进而推广至参数服从洛伦兹分布（Lorentzian distribution）的异质群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I 型兴奋性神经元（type I excitability）在分岔点附近由 QIF 模型刻画，而噪声下的 QIF 在统计物理、随机稳定性及一维淬火无序（quenched disorder）量子力学中均有广泛出现；已有理论多依赖扩散近似（diffusion approximation），难以处理有效噪声强度依赖于参数分布的稀疏网络情形，因此需要更一般的解析框架。

Method: 从首达时间问题出发，推导受加性高斯噪声 QIF 的平均峰间期解析解；以某一普适解析函数（universal analytic function）的实部与虚部表示放电率与群体平均膜电位；借助该函数将结果推广至洛伦兹分布参数的异质群体，并应用于化学与电突触（chemical and electrical synapses）相互作用及稀疏抑制网络（sparse inhibitory networks）中有效内源噪声（effective endogenous noise）的产生两个问题。

Result: 得到了平均峰间期、放电率与群体平均膜电位的解析表达式；对两个示例问题进行了理论实现与检验，其中稀疏抑制网络情形可覆盖有效噪声强度依赖洛伦兹分布参数的情景，而该情景在扩散近似下无法直接处理。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了噪声驱动 QIF 群体动力学的解析刻画，并借助普适解析函数与洛伦兹异质性推广超越扩散近似的适用范围，为突触相互作用与网络内源噪声研究提供了可实现的解析工具。

Abstract: The dynamics of type I excitability neurons near the bifurcation point are governed by the model of quadratic integrate-and-fire neuron (QIF). The model of QIF subject to white Gaussian noise is abundant in statistical physics, since it not only reasonably reproduces the dynamics of some biological systems but also emerges in the problems of stochastic stability and in quantum mechanics of one-dimensional systems with quenched disorder. We derive the solution for the mean interspike interval of a QIF subject to additive Gaussian noise in terms of analytic functions, from the first passage time problem. On the basis of this solution, we construct analytical expressions for the firing rate and population-mean membrane potential in terms of the real and imaginary parts of a certain universal analytic function. With this function, we generalize theoretical results to heterogeneous populations with Lorentzian-distributed parameters. Implementation of theoretical results is demonstrated and examined for two problems: the interplay between chemical and electrical synapses, and the emergence of effective endogenous noise in sparse inhibitory networks. For the latter case, it is possible to cover the scenarios where the effective noise intensity depends on Lorentzian-distributed parameters, which is not feasible directly with the diffusion approximation.

</details>


### [186] [Emergent Symmetry in Constant-Density Flocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07771)
*Hugues Chaté,Alexandre Solon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 二维恒密度群体（flocks）的有序相由单一角度场描述，是具无标度集体运动有序相的最简系统之一；计入此前被舍弃的项后，其 Nambu-Goldstone 模方程在大尺度上具有剪切与伽利略推进（Galilean boost）的联合不变性，该对称性在唯一吸引不动点处恢复，Ward 恒等式精确固定标度指数并经数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维恒密度群体的有序相仅有单一角度场，是研究集体运动无标度有序相的最简模型，但以往分析中部分方程项被舍弃，可能导致对称性与标度行为的描述不完整，因而有必要重新审视这些被丢弃项的作用。

Method: 采用场论与重整化群（renormalization group）式分析：将此前舍弃的项补回 Nambu-Goldstone 模（Nambu-Goldstone mode）的控制方程，识别大尺度上剪切（shear）与伽利略推进（Galilean boost）的联合对称性，并借助 Ward 恒等式（Ward identities）推导标度指数，辅以数值验证。

Result: 该联合对称性在裸层面（bare level）被破坏，但在唯一吸引不动点（attractive fixed point）处得以恢复；由 Ward 恒等式可精确确定标度指数（scaling exponents），数值结果与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 补全的方程所含附加项使二维恒密度群体模型呈现剪切–伽利略推进联合对称性，这一对称性在不动点处恢复并精确锁定标度指数，说明该对称性可作为刻画无标度有序相的核心原则。

Abstract: In two dimensions, the ordered phase of constant-density flocks is described by a single angle field, which makes them arguably the simplest systems with a scale-free ordered phase of collective motion. We show that the equation governing the corresponding Nambu-Goldstone mode, completed with terms previously discarded, possesses on large scales an invariance combining a shear and a Galilean boost. Broken at the bare level, this symmetry is restored at the unique attractive fixed point, and the associated Ward identities fix the scaling exponents exactly, as confirmed numerically.

</details>


### [187] [Masked diffusion as random sequential adsorption: an exactly solvable model for parallel decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07845)
*Adrian Baule*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文将掩码扩散模型（masked diffusion models）并行生成中的误差映射到驱动多粒子系统（driven many-particle systems）的统计物理框架，证明并行生成误差等于生成过程相对反向掩码过程的熵产（entropy production），并给出最优去掩码调度（unmasking schedule）为热力学度规（thermodynamic metric）下的测地线（geodesic）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型（autoregressive LLMs）逐词生成，而掩码扩散模型可并行揭示多个词元（token），但因同时揭示的词元相互独立采样而引入误差。论文旨在刻画这一并行性与误差之间的权衡，并寻找降低误差的生成调度方案。

Method: 把并行生成误差等价为生成过程相对于反向掩码过程的熵产；对马尔可夫数据（Markov data），将生成刻画为带标记间隙的随机顺序吸附过程（random sequential adsorption with labelled gaps）并给出任意步数的精确解；多步极限下，误差化为缓慢驱动系统的耗散（dissipation），去掩码调度对应驱动协议（driving protocol），步数对应持续时间，误差由基于词元间互信息（mutual information）闭式给出的热力学度规决定。

Result: 精确解表明文本相关性（correlations）越长，误差越集中于生成起始阶段；以较小步长起始的调度在路径误差与困惑度（perplexity）上均获益更多，且达到渐近行为所需步数更少；最优调度为该热力学度规的测地线。

Conclusion: 并行生成的误差具有统计物理意义下的熵产与耗散解释，去掩码调度可视为驱动协议，其优化归结为热力学度规上的测地线问题；文本相关性的长度与结构同时决定误差分布与输出质量。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models generate text by reversing a stochastic masking process. Unlike autoregressive LLMs, which generate text one token at a time, they can reveal many tokens in parallel, at the price of an error, since tokens revealed together are drawn independently. We show that this trade-off maps onto the statistical physics of driven many-particle systems. (i) For any data, the error of parallel generation is the entropy production of the generation process relative to the reversed masking process. (ii) For Markov data, generation is a random sequential adsorption process with labelled gaps, which we solve exactly for any number of steps. (iii) For many steps, the error becomes the dissipation of a slowly driven system: the unmasking schedule plays the role of the driving protocol, the number of steps that of its duration, and the error is set by a thermodynamic metric that we obtain in closed form from the mutual information between tokens. Optimal schedules are geodesics of this metric. The exact solution shows how correlations in the text shape both the error and the quality of the output. The longer the correlations, the more the error concentrates at the start of generation. Schedules that start with small steps then gain more, both in path error and in perplexity, and need fewer steps to reach their asymptotic behaviour.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.CB'></div>

# q-bio.CB [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Autotaxis of a Run-and-Tumble Agent via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07531)
*Ramesh Pramanik,Shradha Mishra,Sakuntala Chatterjee*

Main category: q-bio.CB

TL;DR: 该研究在动态化学环境中，用强化学习（reinforcement learning, RL）框架研究单个自趋化（autochemotactic）智能体的“跑-翻滚”（run-tumble）运动；结果显示 RL 能提升趋化（chemotaxis）性能，且性能对学习率与探索率呈非单调依赖，存在最优 RL 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注的问题是：在由分泌、扩散与降解共同决定、因而动态变化的吸引剂化学环境中，强化学习能否为单个自趋化智能体的跑-翻滚运动提供有效的导航策略，并提升其趋化性能。

Method: 采用强化学习框架，对能分泌并感知化学线索的单个自趋化智能体进行建模；在其化学环境因吸引剂分泌、扩散（diffusion）与降解（degradation）而动态变化的情形下，考察 RL 导航策略的有效性，并通过改变分泌率、降解率、扩散率以及学习率（learning rate）和探索率（exploration rate）等参数来刻画趋化性能。

Result: 与不使用 RL 的常规跑-翻滚趋化相比，使用 RL 时，智能体从随机初始位置出发找到有利区域（吸引剂峰值附近）的概率随时间增长更快，且长时间极限下达到更高值。趋化性能对学习率和探索率呈非单调依赖，表明存在最优 RL 策略以实现最高效导航。作者将这些观察解释为既有吸引剂环境与智能体自身生成的吸引剂分布（profile）之间相互作用的结果。

Conclusion: 强化学习可有效增强单个自趋化智能体的趋化表现，且其效果受学习与探索参数的调节，存在最优参数区间；观测到的非单调行为源于外部既有化学环境与智能体自生成化学场之间的耦合作用。

Abstract: We study the run-tumble motion of a single autochemotactic agent that secretes and senses chemical cues using a reinforcement learning (RL) framework. In a chemical environment that changes dynamically via secretion, diffusion, and degradation of attractant chemicals, we study the effectiveness of RL for a successful navigation strategy. We show that RL enhances chemotactic performance. Starting from a random initial position, the probability of finding the agent in the favorable region (near the attractant peak) increases faster with time and reaches a higher value in the long time limit when RL is used, compared to the case when the agent performs usual run-tumble chemotaxis without using RL. We also characterize the chemotactic performance of the agent by varying different parameters like rates of secretion, degradation, and diffusion. Importantly, we observe a non-monotonic dependence of the chemotactic performance on the learning and exploration rates, indicating the existence of an optimal RL strategy for the most efficient navigation. We explain these observations as a result of the interplay between the pre-existing attractant environment and the agent-generated attractant profile.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Embodied physical learning: a trained bacterial cell wall can guide organism behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07112)
*Nada Elmeligy,No Chen,J. M. Schwarz*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出“具身物理学习（embodied physical learning）”框架，将弹簧网络（spring network）模型嵌入可运动细菌智能体，训练细胞壁网络把碰撞载荷路由至鞭毛马达以调控逃逸行为。训练后的细胞在首次近壁翻滚中即可恢复理想侧向分辨控制器约89%的收益，且行为在数百次壁面接触内收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学习（physical learning）虽已证明无序弹簧网络可按局部规则训练以路由机械信号，但既有研究多停留于网络本身；该文关注训练基底本身如何影响生物体（如细菌）的运动行为，即学习效应能否从材料层面传递到智能体层面。

Method: 构建细菌细胞壁的弹簧网络模型并嵌入可运动的智能体（motile agent）；以细胞与表面碰撞所产生载荷作为刺激，通过局部规则训练网络将该载荷跨体路由至远端鞭毛马达（flagellar motors），驱动定子释放（stator release）并产生转向偏置；同时分析执行器自身动力学带来的约束、收敛速度及交联重塑（crosslink remodeling）下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在被困后的首次近壁翻滚中，训练后的细胞恢复了理想侧向分辨控制器（side-resolved controller）所提供优势的89%；行为在数百次壁面遭遇内收敛，与细胞单世代内持续接触结构所经历的情形相当；只要关键的承载张力键被保护，路由在连续交联重塑下仍可保持。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并验证了具身物理学习：刺激、执行器与后果均属于智能体自身，训练力尺度继承自智能体自身的感知而非外部施加；同时指出执行器自身动力学构成更紧的约束，即路由信号须在刺激窗口内完成切换。文中亦讨论了具身物理学习的实验检验途径。

Abstract: Physical learning studies how physical systems themselves can be trained to perform learning tasks using physical laws. For instance, disordered spring networks can be trained by local rules to route a mechanical signal from one site in the network to another. Disordered spring networks play key roles in many facets of cell biology, not just in terms of mechanics, but also in terms of motility. How then can training on the substrate itself impact the action of an organism, such as a bacterium? Here, we train a spring-network model of the bacterial cell wall and embed it in a motile agent. The stimulus is the load the cell generates by colliding with a surface. The trained wall routes that load across the body to distant flagellar motors, where it drives stator release, and the resulting turn biases the cell toward escape and delays subsequent surface contacts. On the first near-wall tumble after entrapment, the trained cell recovers 89% of the advantage an ideal side-resolved controller provides. We call this arrangement embodied physical learning: the stimulus, the actuator, and the consequences all belong to the agent, and the training force scale is inherited from the agent's own sensing rather than imposed. An actuator with its own kinetics imposes a second, tighter constraint: the routed signal must switch the actuator within the stimulus window. We also find that behavior converges within a few hundred wall encounters, comparable to what a cell in sustained contact with structure experiences in a single generation, and that the routing survives continuous crosslink remodeling provided crucial, tension-bearing bonds are protected. Experimental tests of embodied physical learning are discussed.

</details>


### [190] [Bearing, heading and the perceptual geometry of collective motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07214)
*Mohammad Salahshour*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出集体运动（collective motion）的"投影几何"理论：邻居的空间延展使其朝向仅通过投影几何进入耦合系数矩阵，经典朝向匹配（heading matching）仅是该矩阵对角线上的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集体运动模型假设动物转向以匹配邻居的旅行方向，谐波理论（harmonic theory）中邻居仅作为某一方位（bearing）上的刺激出现，忽略了邻居空间延展与朝向信息的作用方式。

Method: 构建以邻居方位与体型纵横比（aspect）为索引的耦合系数矩阵；并独立分析仅对位置（带延迟）作出响应的情形，其对齐系数由距离加权吸引剖面的斜率决定；随后在鱼群与行走鸽子的实测数据中检验，并设置对照实验。

Result: 在鱼与鸽子的社会性响应中（独立于行进方向测量身体朝向）可复原社会反应，包括鱼对邻居方位的响应；朝向超额（heading excess）依赖于转向的测量方式——鱼在"前向"响应下出现而在"居中"响应下不出现，鸽子中仍未解决；对照表明两种结果均不能识别直接的对齐规则。

Conclusion: 对齐（alignment）不必作为待测规则；动物的对齐与否可通过几何、知觉与行为三方面来源加以区分，从而成为可测量的科学问题。

Abstract: Traditional models of collective motion commonly assume that animals turn to match their neighbours' directions of travel. In the harmonic theory, decisions follow from a neural representation of direction, and a neighbour enters only as a stimulus at a bearing, the direction in which it lies. Here we show that a neighbour's spatial extent allows its orientation to enter through projection geometry alone, and the theory generates a matrix of coupling coefficients indexed by the neighbour's bearing and aspect. Responses to bearing alone form one edge of this matrix, and classical heading matching is a special case on its diagonal, one ray through a two-parameter family. Independently, an animal that responds only to neighbour position, perceived with a delay, acquires an alignment coefficient set by the slope of its distance-weighted attraction profile, so that it can align, anti-align or reverse sign. Alignment need not be a rule to be measured. In schooling fish and walking pigeons with body orientation measured independently of travel, social responses are recovered, including bearing responses in the fish. Heading excess depends on how turning is measured, appearing in the fish with forward but not centred responses and remaining unresolved in pigeons. Controls show why neither outcome identifies a direct alignment rule. In fish recordings with motion-assigned body polarity, heading-dependent structure departs from Vicsek form and fails the coefficient test for a common angular potential. Whether animals align thereby becomes a measurable question, with geometric, perceptual and behavioural origins that can be distinguished.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Nanoscale Inclined Four-electrode Ion Traps: Finite-Element Modelling, Floquet Stability, and Surface-Potential Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07260)
*Nahiyan Archa,Abhinand P.,Ahammed Shabeeb,Nikhil Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文通过有限元方法研究用于171Yb+的倾斜四叶片射频离子阱架构，结合赝势表征、矩阵Mathieu分析、Floquet稳定性评估与局域表面势建模，量化其运动模式与表面势响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型化囚禁离子系统需要能在纳入纳米尺度特征与表面诱导微扰的同时提供可控射频约束的电极架构。

Method: 采用有限元研究倾斜四叶片射频离子阱（电极面特征约99-150 nm、对置电极间距20 μm），在6度倾角、150 V射频幅值、15 MHz驱动频率下计算赝势，并用含非对角耦合项的完整射频势Hessian构建矩阵Mathieu方程，结合单周期数值传播与Floquet稳定性评估；另建立四源静电模型分析局域表面势影响。

Result: 主对角路径上的面内赝势势垒为3.430与3.389 meV；识别出两个主要运动分支，久期频率约1.8137与1.8181 MHz，第三个以轴向为主的分支名义频率约7.90 kHz但受Hessian残余迹与相位回归漂移限制；归一化响应系数随关联性在约0.5至1.5倍之间被抑制或增强。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了研究纳米尺度倾斜叶片电极架构及其运动特性与表面势响应的计算框架，并强调可靠三维阱表征所需的数值收敛要求。

Abstract: Developing miniaturised trapped-ion systems requires electrode architectures that provide controlled radio-frequency (RF) confinement while accounting for nanoscale features and surface-induced perturbations. Here, we present a finite-element investigation of an inclined four-blade RF ion-trap architecture for 171Yb+, combining pseudopotential characterisation, matrix Mathieu analysis, Floquet stability assessment, and localised surface-potential modelling. The architecture incorporates exposed electrode-face features of approximately 99-150 nm within an opposing-electrode separation of 20 um. At an electrode inclination of 6 degrees, an RF amplitude of 150 V, and a drive frequency of 15 MHz, the calculated pseudopotential exhibits directional in-plane barriers of 3.430 and 3.389 meV along the principal diagonal paths. The full RF-potential Hessian, including off-diagonal coupling terms, is used to construct the matrix Mathieu equation. Numerical propagation over one RF period identifies two dominant motional branches with secular-frequency estimates of 1.8137 and 1.8181 MHz. A third, predominantly axial branch exhibits a nominal frequency near 7.90 kHz, although its quantitative resolution is limited by the residual Hessian trace and phase-return drift. Additionally, a four-source electrostatic model is developed to investigate the effects of localised surface potentials on modal electric-field responses. Parametric analysis reveals correlation-dependent suppression and enhancement of normalised response coefficients, with multipliers ranging from approximately 0.5 to 1.5. This study establishes a computational framework for investigating nanoscale inclined blade-electrode architectures, their motional characteristics, and surface-potential responses, while highlighting numerical convergence requirements for reliable three-dimensional trap characterisation.

</details>


### [192] [Chiral-vibration-induced magnetism and circular dichroism in organic molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07511)
*Hao-Wen Xu,Cheng Xu,Zi-Yang Xu,Xiao-Long Yang,Hang Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作以密度泛函紧束缚结合阻尼简正模动力学，在单分子水平研究苯、晕苯和甲烷的振动逆法拉第效应与瞬态振动圆二色性，提出“圆偏振泵浦写入—瞬态VCD读出”的全光手性控制方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索分子尺度上磁性的全光调控与手性光学探测，理解圆偏振光如何驱动手性核运动并产生有效磁矩及可读出的手性响应。

Method: 结合密度泛函紧束缚（density-functional tight-binding, DFTB）计算与阻尼简正模动力学（damped normal-mode dynamics），以圆偏振光激发简并的红外活性振动模式，并比较苯、晕苯与甲烷三种分子体系。

Result: 圆偏振光激发合适的简并红外活性模式后，驱动产生手性核运动并形成有效磁矩；反转泵浦光的螺旋度（helicity）可反转振动手性与瞬态二色性响应的符号。分子对称性影响光诱导手性响应的表现。

Conclusion: 研究支持一种写入—读出（writing-readout）方案：圆偏振泵浦制备手性振动态，瞬态振动圆二色性（transient vibrational circular dichroism, VCD）探测其手性，为分子尺度全光磁性调控与手性光学探测提供平台。

Abstract: We investigate the vibrational inverse Faraday effect and transient vibrational circular dichroism in benzene, coronene, and methane at the single-molecule level. We combine density-functional tight-binding calculations with damped normal-mode dynamics. Circularly polarized light excites suitable degenerate infrared-active modes, driving chiral nuclear motion that produces effective magnetic moments. Reversing the pump helicity reverses the vibrational chirality and the sign of the transient dichroic response. These results support a writing-readout scheme in which circularly polarized pumping prepares a chiral vibrational state and transient vibrational circular dichroism probes its handedness. We further examine how molecular symmetry shapes the light-induced chiral response across the three molecular systems. This work provides a valuable platform for exploring all-optical control of magnetism and chiroptical probing at the molecular scale.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Confinement-induced bound state and effective potential for microwave-shielded ultracold molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07230)
*Linyi Zhang,Xiaoling Cui*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文研究纵向囚禁下微波屏蔽超冷分子的束缚态与有效势，证明纵向囚禁可诱导自由空间中不存在的束缚态，并提出含纵向完整相互作用效应的有效二维势，在更宽参数范围内准确预测结合能（包括远超囚禁频率的深束缚态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波屏蔽超冷分子（microwave-shielded ultracold molecules）在低维体系中有丰富物理，但纵向囚禁形成的准二维（quasi-two-dimensional, q2D）几何中束缚态与相互作用如何描述尚不清晰；简化二维势的适用性有限，需更准确的等效描述。

Method: 考察纵向囚禁下玻色型与费米型分子的束缚态形成，将纵向方向的完整相互作用效应纳入，构建有效二维势（effective 2D potential），并与其他简化二维势进行对比检验。

Result: 纵向囚禁促进玻色与费米分子的束缚态形成，产生自由空间中不存在的囚禁诱导束缚态（confinement-induced bound states）；所建有效势在微波耦合（microwave coupling）与囚禁强度（confinement strength）的更大参数范围内准确预测结合能（binding energy），甚至可预测结合能远超囚禁频率的深束缚态。

Conclusion: 所建立的有效势为高效探索微波屏蔽超冷分子的低维物理提供了可行途径，并优于简化二维势的描述能力。

Abstract: We investigate the bound state and effective potential of microwave-shielded ultracold molecules under longitudinal confinement, which effectively creates a quasi-two-dimensional (q2D) geometry. We find that the longitudinal confinement facilitates bound-state formation in both bosonic and fermionic molecules, leading to confinement-induced bound states that are absent in free space. To describe these bound states, we establish an effective 2D potential by incorporating the full interaction effect along the longitudinal direction. Compared with other simplified 2D potentials, the effective potential accurately predicts the binding energies of ultracold molecules over a much broader parameter regime of microwave coupling and confinement strength. Remarkably, it can even predict deep bound states with binding energies well exceeding the confinement frequency. By establishing this effective potential, our work paves the way for efficiently exploring the rich low-dimensional physics of microwave-shielded ultracold molecules.

</details>


### [194] [Initial heat-current decay versus thermal transport at a three-body resonance in a one-dimensional Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07615)
*Yixin Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 稀薄一维无自旋费米气体在三体共振（three-body resonance）附近的动力学弛豫与热输运被理论研究；发现使初始热流衰减率最大的共振位置高于使积分弛豫时间最小的位置，且更快初始衰减可与更慢的后续弛豫及更大的低频热响应共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清三体共振附近相互作用如何通过完整的真空散射振幅（vacuum amplitude）进入线性化玻尔兹曼碰撞算子（linearized Boltzmann collision operator），并区分不同时间尺度上的弛豫行为。

Method: 采用线性化玻尔兹曼方程框架，将有限程相互作用（finite-range interaction）的完整真空振幅纳入碰撞算子；考察动量矩（momentum moments）之间的耦合与碰撞算子的界（bounds），并以第四阶矩畸变作为探测手段。

Result: 初始热流衰减率极大与积分弛豫时间极小的共振位置彼此分离；在较高共振能量下，动量矩耦合将热流权重转移至慢模（slow mode）；两个共振位置对应的耗散响应随频率升高至少发生两次次序反转（reversals），碰撞算子的界证实了这些反转。

Conclusion: 弛豫尺度存在分离：更快的初始衰减可伴随更慢的后续弛豫与更大的低频热响应；第四阶矩畸变呈现相同的尺度分离，可作为通过动量分布（momentum distribution）进行实验探测的手段。

Abstract: We theoretically study kinetic relaxation and heat transport in a dilute one-dimensional spinless Fermi gas near a three-body resonance. The full vacuum amplitude of a finite-range interaction enters the linearized Boltzmann collision operator. The resonance position that maximizes the initial heat-current decay rate lies above the one that minimizes the integrated relaxation time. At higher resonance energies, coupling among momentum moments transfers heat-current weight to a slow mode. A faster initial decay can therefore coexist with slower subsequent relaxation and a larger low-frequency heat response. The dissipative responses at two resonance positions reverse their ordering at least twice as frequency increases. Bounds on the full collision operator confirm both reversals. A fourth-moment distortion exhibits the same separation of relaxation scales and provides a probe through the momentum distribution.

</details>


### [195] [Universal Phase Locking from Slowly Varying Inhomogeneity in Tunnel-Coupled One-Dimensional Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07657)
*Bence Fitos,Gábor Takács*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究结合截断Wigner近似（truncated Wigner approximation）与渐近分析（asymptotic analysis），揭示了一维Bose凝聚体中sine-Gordon模型的相位锁定源于缓慢变化的密度分布（density profile）所驱动的普适机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tunnel-coupled一维Bose凝聚体实验显示系统弛豫到相位锁定（phase-locked）态，这与均匀可积理论（homogeneous integrable theory）的预测相冲突，因此需要解释该锁定的物理来源。

Method: 将截断Wigner近似与渐近分析相结合，并借助线性化理论（linearised theory）分析密度分布对相对相位（relative phase）演化的影响。

Result: 在密度极大值附近的相对相位向零漂移（drift），该漂移在线性化理论中即已存在；其时间尺度仅由密度曲率（density curvature）决定，而与隧穿耦合（tunnel coupling）无关。按弛豫时间尺度（relaxation time scale）重新标度时间后，不同系统尺寸与密度分布的动力学坍缩为同一行为，非线性（non-linearity）则抑制来自边缘的激发回流。

Conclusion: 相位锁定由缓慢变化的密度分布经普适机制产生，可解释实验与均匀可积理论之间的冲突，并给出由密度曲率控制的普适弛豫时间尺度。

Abstract: The sine-Gordon model can be experimentally realised by tunnel-coupled one-dimensional Bose condensates. However, the experiments show relaxation to a phase-locked state, which conflicts with the predictions of the homogeneous integrable theory. Here we combine the truncated Wigner approximation with an asymptotic analysis, and show that this locking is produced by a slowly varying density profile through a universal mechanism, whereby the relative phase near the density maximum drifts towards zero. We show that this drift is already present in the linearised theory and its timescale is determined by the density curvature alone, while it is independent of the tunnel coupling. Rescaling time by the relaxation time scale collapses the dynamics for different system sizes and density profiles, while non-linearity suppresses the excitation returning from the edges.

</details>


### [196] [Coherent phase slips in coupled rings: from weak to strong interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08168)
*Axel Perez-Obiol,Malcolm G. Boshier,Luigi Amico,Juan Polo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文研究两个弱耦合超冷玻色子环格点（ring lattice）间环流（circulation）的相干转移：粒子数不变，持续电流（persistent current）在两环间来回振荡，并揭示弱相互作用与强相互作用下两种不同的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清环间持续电流相干转移背后的微观机制，并评估其在现有环格点实验中的可观测性与操控潜力。

Method: 结合Bose–Hubbard数值模拟及其Gross–Pitaevskii极限与量子相位模型（quantum phase model）极限进行理论分析，并提出制备初态的淬火（quench）协议。

Result: 弱相互作用下，各占据动量模独立隧穿，转移时间与环间隧穿成反比，伴随广延的粒子数涨落与分布于多个Schmidt分量的纠缠，并被自俘获（self-trapping）抑制；强相互作用、大填充的相位主导区中，环流经多体持续电流态间的二阶集体过程转移，转移时间在弱链接极限下与隧穿平方成反比，粒子数涨落保持O(1)。

Conclusion: 数守恒操作下可获取的纠缠至多约一比特（每个粒子数扇区为两种多体组态的相干叠加）；耦合环可作为研究相干量子相滑（coherent quantum phase slips）与操纵持续电流态的平台。

Abstract: We study the coherent transfer of circulation between two weakly coupled lattice rings of ultracold bosons, in which a persistent current initially prepared in one ring oscillates back and forth between the rings without net transfer of particles. Combining Bose--Hubbard simulations with their Gross--Pitaevskii and quantum phase model limits, we show that similar current oscillations arise from two distinct microscopic mechanisms. At weak interactions, particles in each occupied momentum mode tunnel independently between the rings, leading to a transfer time inversely proportional to the inter-ring tunnelling, extensive particle-number fluctuations and entanglement distributed over many Schmidt components; this mode-exchange dynamics is suppressed by self-trapping as the interactions increase. At stronger interactions and large filling, in the phase-dominated regime, circulation is instead transferred through a second-order collective process between many-body persistent-current states, with a transfer time scaling as the inverse square of the inter-ring tunnelling in the weak-link limit. Number fluctuations then remain of order one instead of growing with the particle number, and within each number sector the system evolves into coherent superpositions of two many-body configurations, distinguished by which ring carries the circulation. The entanglement accessible under number-conserving operations therefore reaches up to approximately one bit. We propose a quench protocol to prepare the initial state and estimate transfer times within reach of current ring-lattice experiments, identifying coupled rings as a platform to study coherent quantum phase slips and to manipulate persistent-current states.

</details>


### [197] [Dynamical effects induced by synthetic SU(1,1) gauge fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08447)
*Chenwei Lv*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文研究由 SU(1,1) 规范场（gauge field）诱导的波包与纠缠动力学，揭示非阿贝尔非紧致 Bloch 振荡（non-Abelian non-compact Bloch oscillations）中稳定振荡与不稳定放大之间的转变，并给出基于 Minkowski 空间双曲立体角的几何描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非紧致规范场（non-compact gauge fields）与开放系统中粒子或能量的交换密切相关，但其诱导的波包与纠缠动力学机制尚待阐明；本文旨在建立相应的几何框架以控制纠缠、选择性放大与能量转移。

Method: 分析 SU(1,1) 规范场下的波包演化，借助辅助一维量子问题的谱判定稳定性；并将 SU(1,1) 规范场引入玻色紧束缚模型（bosonic tight-binding model）得到玻色 Kitaev 模型，另提出在旋量凝聚体（spinor condensate）中的实验实现方案。

Result: 波包表现出非阿贝尔非紧致 Bloch 振荡，稳定振荡与不稳定放大的转变由辅助一维问题的谱决定；在不稳定绝热区，波包探测到 SU(1,1) 拓扑缺陷（topological defect），其放大率仅由 Minkowski 空间中的双曲立体角（hyperbolic solid angle）决定；玻色 Kitaev 模型的体积或超体积纠缠标度（volume/super-volume entanglement scaling）受规范场稳定性支配。

Conclusion: 结果可推广至其他非紧致群与更高维度，为纠缠控制、选择性放大及能量转移工程提供几何框架，并具备基于旋量凝聚体的实验可行性。

Abstract: Compact gauge fields are powerful tools for controlling quantum dynamics and topological phases of matter. Non-compact gauge fields are particularly relevant for open systems, where particles or energy are exchanged with the environment. Here, we investigate the wavepacket and entanglement dynamics induced by an $\mathrm{SU}(1,1)$ gauge field. The wavepacket undergoes non-Abelian non-compact Bloch oscillations, in which the spectrum of an auxiliary one-dimensional quantum problem determines the transition between stable oscillations and unstable amplification. In the unstable adiabatic regime, the wavepacket probes an $\mathrm{SU}(1,1)$ topological defect, and its amplification is determined solely by a hyperbolic solid angle in Minkowski space. Threading an $\mathrm{SU}(1,1)$ gauge field through a bosonic tight-binding model yields the bosonic Kitaev model, whose volume or super-volume entanglement scaling is governed by the stability of the gauge field. We further propose an experimental realization of an $\mathrm{SU}(1,1)$ gauge field in a spinor condensate. Our results can be generalized to other non-compact groups and higher dimensions, providing a geometric framework for entanglement control, selective amplification, and engineering energy transfer.

</details>


### [198] [Flat-band systems: A platform to realize the moments of Quantum Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08479)
*Rishi Gangadhar,Binayyak Roy,Vito Scarola,Sumanta Tewari*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 摘要提出在孤立平带（flat band）中制备高斯波包，施加弱恒力后，波包方差（wave-packet variance）随时间线性变化的领头阶项包含与量子度规迹（quantum-metric trace）梯度成正比的分量，从而将波包扩散作为量子几何（quantum geometry）的动力学探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度规（quantum metric）是布洛赫带（Bloch bands）的基本几何属性并支配多种物理现象，但实验上获取它仍具挑战性，因此需要一种可实验实现的探测手段。

Method: 在孤立平带中制备高斯波包并施加弱恒力，通过解析推导给出波包方差的领头阶时间演化；并以具有精确平带且贝里曲率（Berry curvature）为零的带隙 Lieb 晶格（gapped Lieb lattice）为例进行数值验证。

Result: 解析结果表明方差领头阶变化与时间成线性关系，并含正比于波包平均的量子度规迹梯度的贡献，该几何贡献在无能带色散时依然有限；数值结果与解析预言一致，确认了量子度规对波包扩散的贡献。

Conclusion: 所需晶格制备、可调跃迁幅度（tunable hopping amplitudes）、施加外力与密度测量均与成熟超冷原子（ultracold-atom）技术兼容，为观测该响应提供可行平台，并确立波包扩散作为平带系统量子几何的动力学探针。

Abstract: The quantum metric is a fundamental geometric property of Bloch bands that governs a wide range of physical phenomena, yet experimentally accessing it remains challenging. We show that the momentum-space variation of the quantum metric can be probed through the real-space dynamics of a Gaussian wave packet prepared in an isolated flat band. Following the application of a weak constant force, we demonstrate analytically that the leading order change in the wave-packet variance is linear in time and contains a contribution proportional to the wave-packet averaged gradient of the quantum-metric trace. This geometric contribution remains finite despite the absence of band dispersion. We illustrate the effect using a gapped Lieb lattice with an exactly flat middle band and vanishing Berry curvature, allowing the quantum-metric contribution to the spreading to be isolated. The numerical results agree with the analytical prediction and confirm the quantum-metric contribution to the wave-packet spreading. The required lattice preparation, tunable hopping amplitudes, applied force, and density measurements are compatible with established ultracold-atom techniques, providing a possible platform for observing the predicted response. Our results establish wave-packet spreading as a dynamical probe of quantum geometry in flat-band systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Topological Transitions of Evanescent Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07038)
*Ignacio Pavez,Tom Sheppard,Yoonseok Hwang,Hannah M. Price,Frank Schindler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究紧束缚哈密顿量（tight-binding Hamiltonians）带隙内倏逝波（evanescent waves）的能带结构，揭示除带边标准例外点（exceptional points）外，还可由无自旋时间反演对称性（spinless time-reversal symmetry）保护产生带隙内例外点，并用Z2指数刻画其拓扑性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上带隙内的衰减/增长模式被视为平凡，其复数波矢产生非厄米色散（non-Hermitian dispersion）的性质缺乏系统描述；本文旨在刻画这类倏逝波谱结构及其拓扑分类。

Method: 采用一维转移矩阵形式（transfer matrix formalism），结合连续模型、紧束缚模型及各向异性光子晶体（anisotropic photonic crystal）进行验证；通过调节模型参数分析带隙内例外点的成对成核过程（由纯虚动量支的交叉所介导），并引入赋予带隙内每个能量的Z2指数。

Result: 发现带隙内例外点可作为Z2指数的畴壁（domain walls）出现；参数调节可驱动拓扑相变，使带隙内例外点成对成核。束缚态（bound state）的尾部在能量位于带边与最近例外点之间时呈纯指数形式，而位于带隙内例外点对之间时则转为振荡形式，且其波矢不同于带边动量。

Conclusion: 倏逝波谱具备可供Z2指数诊断的拓扑结构，带隙内例外点及相应拓扑相变决定了束缚态尾部的空间行为；结果在连续、紧束缚及光子晶体模型中得到一致展示。

Abstract: We study the band structure of evanescent waves, that is, the decaying or growing solutions that live within the gaps of tight-binding Hamiltonians. Although the underlying Hamiltonian is Hermitian, these modes carry complex wavevectors and therefore exhibit a non-Hermitian dispersion. Using the transfer matrix formalism in one dimension, we show that, in addition to the standard exceptional points that appear at the band edges, a different type of exceptional point can arise inside the gap, protected by spinless time-reversal symmetry. Tuning model parameters drives a topological transition where such in-gap exceptional points are nucleated in pairs, mediated by a crossing between purely imaginary momentum branches. We show that this spectral structure is diagnosed by a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ index assigned to every energy inside the gap, with the in-gap exceptional points arising as its domain walls. A generic bound state in the system has a tail that is purely exponential when its energy lies between a band edge and the nearest exceptional point, but becomes oscillatory - with a wavevector differing from the band-edge momentum - when its energy lies between the pair of in-gap exceptional points. We illustrate these results using continuum and tight-binding models, as well as an anisotropic photonic crystal.

</details>


### [200] [Parity-Selective Quantum Interference in Unconventional Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07330)
*Yiyuan Chen,Xiao-Bin Qiang,Hai-Zhou Lu,Congjun Wu,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出量子干涉（quantum interference）可作为探测非相对论自旋劈裂（nonrelativistic spin splitting）宇称的对称性分辨探针，因偶宇称与奇宇称磁体呈现定性不同的弱（反）局域化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相对论自旋劈裂已成为非常规磁性态（unconventional magnetic states）的标志性特征，亟需能够分辨其宇称的探测手段。

Method: 通过分析量子干涉效应，考察偶宇称与奇宇称磁体中的弱（反）局域化（weak (anti-)localization）行为，并借助自旋群（spin group）描述时间反演对称的散射环路；进一步推导相应的磁电导（magnetoconductivity）公式。

Result: 偶宇称磁体可容纳由自旋群刻画的时间反演对称散射环路，产生可被自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）抑制的弱局域化；奇宇称磁体则具有隐藏的弱反局域化，可被自旋轨道耦合释放。

Conclusion: 结果将弱（反）局域化理论推广至非常规磁体，并为非常规自旋劈裂磁性态提供了实用的诊断方案。

Abstract: Nonrelativistic spin splitting has emerged as a defining feature of unconventional magnetic states. Here we show that quantum interference offers a symmetry-resolved probe for the parity of such spin splitting, as even- and odd-parity magnets exhibit qualitatively different weak (anti-)localization behaviors. Even-parity magnets can host time-reversal symmetric scattering loops depicted by the spin group, giving rise to a weak localization that can be suppressed by spin-orbit coupling. In contrast, odd-parity magnets have a hidden weak antilocalization that can be released by spinorbit coupling. We further derive the corresponding magnetoconductivity formulas to facilitate experimental explorations. Our results not only advance the weak (anti-)localization theory to unconventional magnets but also suggest a practical diagnostic of unconventional spin-split magnetic states.

</details>


### [201] [Prediction of antimagnon-polarons with intrinsic momentum-space level attraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07434)
*Chongzhou Wang,Zhengda Jing,Weichao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文预言了连续磁弹性介质中无需外部耗散浴、由内禀机制产生的动量空间能级吸引（level attraction），并提出“反磁振子-极化子（antimagnon-polaron）”概念，据此设计直流泵浦的可调单频声子激光器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子与声子/光子杂化形成的准粒子通常在动量空间表现能级排斥（level repulsion）；能级吸引则需反相通道，此前仅限于由耗散浴介导的零维系统，缺乏在连续介质中的本征实现方案。

Method: 在连续磁弹性介质（magnetoelastic medium）中引入自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit-torque, SOT）稳定的反磁振子，使其以相反手性（handedness）进动，从而直接反转磁弹性耦合，无需依赖外部环境；并用微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）进行验证。

Result: 该本征能级吸引导致独特的0/π锁相同步增长；微磁学模拟证实，SOT调控的增益与阻尼（gain and damping）平衡驱动了集体不稳定性（collective instability）。

Conclusion: 该机制最终实现连续可调、直流泵浦的单频声子激光器（phonon laser），为相干声学发射（coherent acoustic emission）提供了新途径。

Abstract: Propagating hybrid quasiparticles formed by magnons and phonons (magnon-polarons) or photons (magnon-polaritons) typically exhibit level repulsion in momentum space. Level attraction, by contrast, requires an anti-phase channel and has so far been restricted to zero-dimensional systems mediated by dissipative baths. Here we predict the antimagnon-polaron in a continuous magnetoelastic medium, which inherently exhibits momentum-space level attraction. The spin-orbit-torque (SOT)-stabilized antimagnon precesses with opposite handedness, directly reversing the magnetoelastic coupling without requiring an external environment. This intrinsic attraction yields a distinctive 0/π phase-locked simultaneous growth. Micromagnetic simulations confirm this picture, revealing that the SOT-controlled balance between gain and damping drives a collective instability. This ultimately enables a continuously tunable, dc-pumped, single-frequency phonon laser, establishing a novel route for coherent acoustic emission.

</details>


### [202] [Supermoiré Formation and Robust Topological Edge State in Helical Trilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07435)
*Hong Li,Huandong Chen,Seongyeon Youn,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Trithep Devakul,Kazuhiro Fujita,Abhay Narayan Pasupathy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用扫描隧道显微镜/谱（scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy, STM/STS）在扭转角约1.7°的螺旋三层石墨烯（helical trilayer graphene, HTG）中实现了超莫尔（supermoiré）畴及其电子结构的原子尺度可视化，证实了畴内平带、畴壁导电边缘态及子晶格极化反转所对应的拓扑变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两个周期近乎相等的莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattice）相遇时可形成由公度莫尔畴与畴壁构成的超莫尔结构；理论预测 HTG 的公度畴为不同拓扑绝缘体（topological insulator）、其间由导电畴壁网络分隔，但此前缺乏直接的实验证据。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜与扫描隧道谱（STM/STS）对扭转角约1.7°的 HTG 进行实空间成像与局域谱学测量，并将实验结果与理论计算（含谷保护拓扑边缘模式、子晶格极化）进行对比。

Result: 观测到样品弛豫形成超莫尔畴的原子尺度证据，每个畴内平带（flat band）与远程能带之间具有约40 meV的大能隙；畴壁沿其长度方向呈现谱学上均匀的边缘态，与谷保护拓扑边缘模式（valley-protected topological edge mode）计算高度吻合；通过成像子晶格极化（sublattice polarization）的反转，揭示了跨越畴边界的拓扑变化。

Conclusion: 实验证实 HTG 中平带的拓扑性质与石墨烯子晶格上的波函数局域化密切相关，并直接可视化超莫尔畴边界处的拓扑转变，为莫尔体系中拓扑畴壁网络的实空间研究提供了范例。

Abstract: When two materials with nearly matched lattice constants meet, they form commensurate domains separated by incommensurate domain walls that absorb the mismatch. Recently, this picture has been extended to moiré lattices: when two moiré superlattices have nearly equal periodicities, they can form a "supermoiré" consisting of commensurate moiré domains separated by domain walls. Helical trilayer graphene (HTG) is a particularly interesting example: theory predicts that its commensurate domains are distinct topological insulators, separated by a network of conducting domain walls. Here, we use scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy to visualize supermoiré domains and their electronic properties in HTG at a twist angle of $\sim1.7^\circ$. We find atomic-scale evidence of relaxation into supermoiré domains, each hosting flat bands separated from remote bands by large gaps of $\sim40$ meV. Domain walls exhibit spectrally uniform edge states along their length, in excellent agreement with calculations for valley-protected topological edge modes. The topological character of the flat bands is intimately tied to wavefunction localization on a graphene sublattice, and we reveal the topological change across a domain boundary by imaging the reversal of sublattice polarization.

</details>


### [203] [Probing Non-Abelian Topology through Quench Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07456)
*Zhenming Zhang,Tianyu Li,Wei Yi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对非阿贝尔拓扑绝缘体（non-Abelian topological insulator）的淬火协议（quench protocol），通过动力学天球（dynamic skyrmion）与能隙分辨的动态陈数（gap-resolved dynamic Chern number）系统重构多能带系统的完整非阿贝尔拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有淬火动力学研究多用于探测阿贝尔能带拓扑（Abelian band topology）或能带节点的欧拉类拓扑（Euler-class topology），而多能带系统中本征态相互交织、需用非对易拓扑荷（noncommutative topological charge）描述的非阿贝尔拓扑绝缘体，其淬火动力学尚不清楚。

Method: 构建针对非阿贝尔拓扑绝缘体的淬火协议，并发展能带投影方案（band-projection scheme），从淬火过程中涌现的准动量—时间流形（quasimomentum–time manifold）上的动态天球中提取能隙分辨拓扑；这些天球由一系列能隙分辨动态陈数刻画。

Result: 所提取的动态陈数可重构淬火后哈密顿量的完整非阿贝尔拓扑，包括全局非阿贝尔拓扑荷以及开边界条件下的边缘态模式（edge-state patterns）。

Conclusion: 该方案为多能带系统的非阿贝尔拓扑提供了动力学探测途径，并可借助光子（photons）或冷原子（cold atoms）平台实现。

Abstract: Quench dynamics offer valuable insights to topological matter, wherein the emergent topological phenomena serve as a dynamic probe to the topology of static Hamiltonians. While such a practice has been widely applied to detect the Abelian band topology or the Euler-class topology of band nodes, little is known regarding the quench dynamics of non-Abelian topological insulators in multiband systems, where the intertwining geometry of all eigenstates necessitates the description of noncommutative topological charges. Here we propose a quench protocol for non-Abelian topological insulators, so that the underlying topology can be systematically probed using dynamic signatures. Specifically, we develop a band-projection scheme to extract the gap-resolved topology from dynamic skyrmions in the emergent quasimomentum--time manifolds of the quench processes. These skyrmions are characterized by a series of gap-resolved dynamic Chern numbers, which enable us to reconstruct the full non-Abelian topology of the post-quench Hamiltonian, including the global non-Abelian topological charge and edge-state patterns under the open-boundary condition. Our proposal offers dynamical access to non-Abelian topology in multiband systems, and can be readily implemented using photons or cold atoms.

</details>


### [204] [Ultrafast Moiré Exciton Dynamics Revealed by Two-Dimensional Coherent Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07539)
*Deran Zhang,Zhexue Chen,Dongxue Chen,Tengfei Yan,Jianhua Jiang,Hui Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用二维相干光谱（two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy, 2DCS）直接分辨hBN封装WS₂/WSe₂异质结中莫尔限域（moiré-confined）层内A激子的七个分立能级，并揭示其两阶段热化（thermalization）过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔超晶格（moiré superlattices）可将激子（excitons）限制在分立能级，但其内部弛豫路径（relaxation pathways）被非均匀展宽（inhomogeneous broadening）掩盖，难以直接观测。

Method: 采用二维相干光谱（2DCS），在hBN封装的WS₂/WSe₂范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructure）中分辨莫尔限域层内WS₂ A激子的分立能级，并在皮秒延迟尺度追踪各能级布居（population），结合Kullback–Leibler散度（Kullback–Leibler divergence）分析分布演化。

Result: 分辨出七个分立能级，对应57.02 meV的限域深度（confinement depth）；观测到两阶段热化：1.2 ps内有效激子温度（exciton temperature）由171.8 K降至99.3 K，源于快速态内布居重分布，随后由激子—环境相互作用介导缓慢回归准平衡（quasi-equilibrium）；KL散度分析证实布居由非平衡态逐步转为玻尔兹曼分布（Boltzmann-distributed）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了态分辨（state-resolved）的莫尔激子动力学观测手段，为研究莫尔量子材料中的非平衡多体现象（nonequilibrium many-body phenomena）奠定基础。

Abstract: Moiré superlattices of van der Waals heterostructures confine excitons into discrete energy levels in finite-sized samples, yet their internal relaxation pathways remain obscured by inhomogeneous broadening. Here, using two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy (2DCS), we directly resolve seven discrete energy levels of the moiré-confined intralayer $\mathrm{WS}_{2}$ A exciton in an hBN-encapsulated $\mathrm{WS}_{2}/\mathrm{WSe}_{2}$ heterostructure, corresponding to a confinement depth of 57.02 meV. By tracking the population of these levels over picosecond delays, we uncover a two-stage thermalization process: an effective exciton temperature drops from 171.8 to 99.3 K within 1.2 ps through rapid intra-manifold population redistribution, followed by a slower recovery toward quasi-equilibrium mediated by exciton--environment interactions. Kullback--Leibler divergence analysis confirms the progressive transition from a non-equilibrium to a Boltzmann-distributed population. Our results establish state-resolved access to moiré exciton dynamics and provide a foundation for exploring nonequilibrium many-body phenomena in moiré quantum materials.

</details>


### [205] [Multi-Channel Non-Abelian Edge Pumping via Multi-Degeneracy Encircling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07594)
*Qingyang Mo,Jiacheng Sun,Zhongfu Li,Jiajun Li,Wange Song,Tao Li,Shuang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作基于硅光芯片（silicon photonic chip），通过在多能隙晶格（multi-gap lattice）中绝热环绕多个能带简并点，实验演示了多通道非阿贝尔边缘泵浦（multi-channel non-Abelian edge pumping），实现分布于不同物理边界上三个拓扑边缘模式（topologically protected edge modes）的非阿贝尔编织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔操作（non-Abelian operations）可实现光场的顺序依赖控制，但波系统中的实验实现长期局限于局域体态或同一边界模式，且往往依赖脆弱的能隙闭合简并（gap-closing degeneracies）；传统 Thouless 泵浦仅支持跨单一能隙的双通道边缘传输。

Method: 在硅光芯片上构建多能隙晶格，采用最小三能带“能带三角形”（band-triangle），其中由全局能带连通性（global band connectivity）联结两个相反电荷的外尔点（Weyl points）与一个零电荷简并点，从而同时产生异边界与同边界边缘通道；通过绝热环绕这些简并点并对通道进行排序（sequencing）来实现模式编织。

Result: 实验中实现了三个拓扑保护边缘模式在不同物理边界之间的非阿贝尔编织；整个过程中能隙保持完全打开（fully gapped），其保护来源于拓扑绕数（topological winding），而非维持的谱简并（spectral degeneracies）。

Conclusion: 能带三角形原理可系统地推广至任意通道数，为集成光子电路（integrated photonic circuits）中拓扑态的集体化、顺序可控路由提供了一条可扩展路径。

Abstract: Non-Abelian operations enable sequence-dependent control of light, yet experimental realizations in wave systems have remained confined to local bulk or same-boundary modes and have often required fragile gap-closing degeneracies. Conventional Thouless pumps, meanwhile, support only two-channel edge transfer across a single gap. Here we demonstrate multi-channel non-Abelian edge pumping on a silicon photonic chip by adiabatically encircling multiple band degeneracies in a multi-gap lattice. In a minimal three-band band-triangle, two opposite-charge Weyl points and one zero-charge degeneracy are linked by global band connectivity, yielding both opposite-boundary and same-boundary edge channels. Sequencing these channels produces non-Abelian braiding of three topologically protected edge modes that reside on distinct physical boundaries. The process remains fully gapped and is protected by topological winding rather than by maintained spectral degeneracies. The same band-triangle principle extends systematically to an arbitrary number of channels, establishing a scalable route to collective, sequence-controlled routing of topological states in integrated photonic circuits.

</details>


### [206] [Nonlinear Interaction and Topological Selection Rules in two Skyrmion Merging: Emergence of Skyrmionium Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07807)
*M. Al Busaidi,T. Hakioglu,R. Sbiaa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究结合解析建模与基于 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 动力学的微磁模拟，揭示两个 Néel 斯格明子合并的拓扑选择规则：仅"同极性、相反绕数"在中度重叠下形成稳定斯格明子环（skyrmionium），其余构型弛豫为单一斯格明子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧密相邻磁斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion）之间的非线性相互作用尚不清楚，尤其缺乏对其合并为单一斯格明子或稳定斯格明子环（skyrmionium）条件的理解。

Method: 采用解析建模与基于 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 动力学的微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulation），通过改变初始间距、极性（polarity）、绕数（winding number）与斯格明子尺寸，考察交换能、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用、各向异性与静磁能的影响。

Result: 发现拓扑选择规则：同极性且相反绕数的斯格明子在中等重叠时形成稳定斯格明子环，其他构型均弛豫为单一斯格明子；并确定存在临界半径，低于该半径斯格明子环塌缩，说明其形成需同时满足合适拓扑与最小尺寸。

Conclusion: 能量分析表明转变涉及交换阻挫的协同弛豫及 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 能与静磁能的重新分布，形成双畴壁（double-domain-wall）结构；所得斯格明子环在平衡半径附近持续呼吸振荡（breathing oscillation），确证其作为亚稳态（metastable state）的动力学稳定性，为控制斯格明子合并与设计手性磁纳米结构中的斯格明子环织构提供预测框架与设计准则。

Abstract: The nonlinear interaction of closely spaced magnetic skyrmions remains poorly understood, particularly the conditions governing their merger into a single skyrmion or a stable skyrmionium. Here, we investigate the merging dynamics of two Néel skyrmions using analytical modelling and micromagnetic simulations based on Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert dynamics. We examine the effects of exchange, Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, anisotropy, and magnetostatic energies by varying the initial separation, polarity, winding number, and skyrmion size. Our results reveal a topological selection rule: skyrmions with identical polarity and opposite winding numbers form a stable skyrmionium at intermediate overlap, while all other configurations relax into a single skyrmion. We also identify a critical radius below which the skyrmionium collapses, showing that its formation requires both the appropriate topology and a minimum size. Energy analysis reveals that the transition involves cooperative relaxation of exchange frustration and redistribution of Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya and magnetostatic energies, leading to a double-domain-wall structure. The resulting skyrmionium exhibits persistent breathing oscillations around an equilibrium radius, confirming its dynamical stability as a metastable state. These findings establish a predictive framework for controlling skyrmion merging and provide design guidelines for engineering skyrmionium textures in chiral magnetic nanostructures.

</details>


### [207] [Signature of chiral anomaly in a graphene-based heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07841)
*Ivan S. Sokolov,Dmitry V. Averyanov,Oleg E. Parfenov,Sergey P. Lebedev,Alexander A. Lebedev,Olesya O. Kapitanova,Alexey N. Mihalyuk,Oleg A. Kondratev,Andrey M. Tokmachev,Vyacheslav G. Storchak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在SiC衬底上合成具有二维与三维（类体相）Sr插层的Gr/Sr/SiC纳米结构，发现三维结构在平行电磁场中呈现手性异常（chiral anomaly）特征——二次负磁阻（quadratic negative magnetoresistance），为石墨烯体系中手性异常的观测提供了实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性异常（chiral anomaly）是手征不平衡导致的拓扑物质量子现象，可提供无耗散输运机制，但高度依赖系统维度且在二维极限下消失。此前虽提出多种在石墨烯基体系中观测手性异常的方案，却一直缺乏实验证据。

Method: 通过控制石墨烯下方Sr的插层量，合成两类Gr/Sr/SiC纳米结构：一类具有二维Sr层，另一类具有三维类体相（bulk-like）Sr层，并对二者进行电磁输运测量比较。

Result: 与二维样品不同，三维Gr/Sr/SiC在平行磁场与电场下表现出二次负磁阻（quadratic negative magnetoresistance），即手性异常的标志性信号；其磁阻温度依赖的函数形式亦与手性异常相符。此外，三维异质结构还表现出电导抑制与石墨烯中的量子振荡（quantum oscillations），可能与该石墨烯层嵌入复杂三维异质结构有关。

Conclusion: 在嵌入式石墨烯中发现的手性异常信号有望推动拓扑纳米材料（topological nanomaterials）中该现象的研究。

Abstract: Chiral anomaly is a fundamental manifestation of topological matter caused by chirality imbalance. It can be of great practical value by providing a mechanism for dissipationless transport. The phenomenon is highly sensitive to the system dimensionality, disappearing in the 2D limit. Different schemes have been proposed to observe the chiral anomaly in graphene-based systems but experimental evidence has been lacking. Here, we synthesized two types of the Gr/Sr/SiC nanostructure - with 2D and 3D, bulk-like, layers of Sr - the difference being the amount of Sr intercalated under graphene. In contrast to the 2D material, 3D Gr/Sr/SiC exhibits a signature of the chiral anomaly - quadratic negative magnetoresistance in parallel magnetic and electric fields. Also, the functional form of the temperature dependence of the magnetoresistance agrees with the chiral anomaly. In addition, the 3D heterostructure exhibits suppressed conductivity and quantum oscillations in graphene. These effects may be associated with the graphene layer incorporated into a complex 3D heterostructure. Hopefully, the discovered signature of chiral anomaly in the built-in graphene would spur research on the phenomenon in topological nanomaterials.

</details>


### [208] [Anisotropic g-tensor and strong spin-orbit interaction of holes in hexagonal germanium nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07846)
*Anirban Das,Baksa Kolok,Dániel Varjas,András Pályi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文用十带 $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ 模型研究六方锗（hexagonal germanium）纳米线中受限空穴态对磁场与电场的响应，揭示强各向异性 $g$ 张量、电场可调带隙及由二阶对称性选择规则介导的 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合（Rashba spin-orbit interaction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方锗兼具直接带隙光学活性与 IV 族材料低核自旋环境，是自旋量子比特（spin qubit）与自旋-光子接口（spin-photon interface）的候选平台，但其受限空穴态在外场下的行为尚需系统理论刻画。

Method: 采用十带 $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$（ten-band $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$）模型，计算六方锗纳米线中受限空穴态（confined hole states）在磁场与电场下的能带与 $g$ 张量（$g$ tensor）响应。

Result: 体材料价带顶（valence band）在垂直于 $c$ 轴平面内的 $g$ 因子为零，纳米线中受限仅引入很小的横向 $g$ 张量分量，故 $g$ 张量保持强各向异性；横向电场显著降低纳米线带隙（band gap），形成栅极可调的光学特征并限制可用场范围，同时诱导 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合，其对垂直于 $c$ 轴的电场更强且随场强非线性变化，强场下窄线中自旋轨道长度（spin-orbit length）可达数十纳米，与立方硅空穴器件相当。

Conclusion: Rashba 耦合的微观起源被确定为经由少数具有不同对称性选择规则（symmetry selection rules）的中间子带（intermediate subbands）所介导的二阶过程；结果可为六方锗纳米线中电控空穴自旋量子比特的设计提供指导。

Abstract: Hexagonal germanium combines direct-gap optical activity with the low-nuclear-spin environment of a group-IV material, making it a candidate platform for spin qubits and spin-photon interfaces. Using a ten-band $\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}$ model, we study the response of confined hole states in hexagonal germanium nanowires to magnetic and electric fields. We show that, by symmetry, the top valence band of the bulk crystal has a vanishing $g$ factor in the plane perpendicular to the $c$ axis, in analogy with the in-plane $g$ factor of heavy holes in two-dimensional hole gases. In nanowires, confinement induces only small transverse $g$-tensor components, so the $g$ tensor remains strongly anisotropic. A transverse electric field strongly reduces the nanowire band gap, providing a gate-tunable optical signature and limiting the usable field range. The same field induces a Rashba spin-orbit interaction that is markedly stronger for fields perpendicular to the $c$ axis and depends nonlinearly on the field strength. In narrow wires at strong fields, the spin-orbit length reaches a few tens of nanometers, comparable to hole devices in cubic silicon. We identify the microscopic origin of the Rashba coupling as a second-order process mediated by a few intermediate subbands with distinct symmetry selection rules. Our results provide guidance for the design of electrically controlled hole-spin qubits in hexagonal germanium nanowires.

</details>


### [209] [When Molecular Dimerization Induces Magnetic Bi-Stability at the Metal-Organic Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08049)
*Rouzhaji Tuerhong,Franck Ngassam,Mebarek Alouani,Jean-Pierre Bucher*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用低温扫描隧道显微镜（STM）与密度泛函理论（DFT），在Ag(111)表面构建MnPc二聚体，并通过小构象变化在反铁磁（AFM）与铁磁（FM）态间可逆切换，且以近藤态识别两种构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在表面实现二聚体磁性构型可控切换与读取的途径，为自旋电子学（spintronics）、逻辑与计算提供二聚体基磁性材料。

Method: 采用金属衬底上的表面组装（on-surface assembly）结合低温STM/STS实现二聚体制备与操控；通过密度泛函理论（DFT）计算判定两种构象的磁耦合性质。

Result: (MnPc)2/Ag(111)存在两种稳定构型：直接配位的Mn原子导致反铁磁耦合，间接（错位）配位导致铁磁耦合；二者可由近藤态（Kondo states）加以区分。

Conclusion: 该体系实现罕见的FM-AFM-FM转变，并提供“写”（FM与AFM态切换）与“读”（近藤态识别）双通道，指向面向自旋电子学与信息器件的二聚体基材料实验路线。

Abstract: We present a method to create metal-organic dimer complexes by on-surface assembly on a metal substrate using low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) and spectroscopy (STS). We demonstrate that a dimer of Mn-Phthalocyanine (MnPc)2 on a Ag(111) surface can be switched between two stable configurations upon a small conformational change controlled by STM manipulation. By means of density-functional theory calculations, it is found that the two conformations correspond to an antiferromagnetic (AFM) and a ferromagnetic (FM) state respectively. Directly coordinated Mn atoms of the dimer lead to an AFM-coupling whereas indirectly coordinated (shifted) Mn atoms lead to a FM-coupling. Rarely seen in a molecular- dimers with transition-metal atoms, the FM-AFM-FM transition is thus readily on-surface accessible. Furthermore, the two configurations of the switch are easily identified by their Kondo states, opening interesting routes in terms of both, writing (FM versus AFM states) and reading. These results pave the experimental route toward dimer-based materials with complex magnetic structures of potential interest for application in spintronics, logics and computing.

</details>


### [210] [Dynamical Keldysh model for colored noise and finite relaxation rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08280)
*F. Z. Bendjeddou,D. V. Efremov,M. N. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文求解了具有任意关联时间（correlation time）1/γ 的有色噪声（coloured noise）下的 Keldysh 模型。有限记忆（finite memory）导致赝能隙（pseudogap）被填充、峰合并与谱线变窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 Keldysh 模型描述受无限关联时间高斯噪声（Gaussian noise）作用的量子点（quantum dots）；当多分量噪声通过非对易矩阵（non-commuting matrices）耦合时，谱中出现赝能隙，但无限关联时间假设的限制性有待突破。

Method: 将 Keldysh 模型推广至任意关联时间 1/γ 的有色噪声情形，以考察有限噪声记忆时间对谱结构的影响。

Result: 有限记忆使赝能隙被填充（pseudogap being filled），其峰值合并（peak merging），同时谱线变窄（line narrowing）。

Conclusion: 噪声关联时间有限化是 Keldysh 模型中赝能隙结构演化的关键控制因素，其效应可通过填充、峰合并与线宽变窄刻画。

Abstract: The Keldysh model describes quantum dots subjected to Gaussian noise with infinite correlation time. For multi-component noise coupled through non-commuting matrices, the spectrum exhibits a pseudogap. We solve the model for coloured noise with any correlation time, $1/ γ$. Finite memory leads to the pseudogap being filled, its peaks merging and the line narrowing.

</details>


### [211] [Anisotropy lifts topological protection of transport in pumped spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08385)
*Mainak Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究二聚化自旋-1/2各向异性XY链中的拓扑自旋泵浦与输运，指出动量空间陈数（Chern number）虽预言量子化Thouless泵浦，但在各向异性下实空间演化显示输运通常不量子化，仅在各向同性极限下保持量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨动量空间拓扑不变量（topological invariant）能否保证各向异性自旋链中的量子化自旋输运，并检验传统Thouless泵浦框架在被输运量不守恒时的适用性。

Method: 通过Jordan-Wigner变换将系统映射为扩展的Rice-Mele型费米子模型，分析其动量空间能带结构与陈数；同时采用开边界条件下的实空间时间演化进行诊断。

Result: 动量空间分析预言部分陈数区域存在量子化Thouless型自旋泵浦，但实空间演化表明各向异性下输运一般不量子化：输运子空间内本征态在驱动周期中杂化，输运依赖驱动方案细节。

Conclusion: 量子化泵浦与输运仅在各向同性极限（哈密顿量退化为解耦Rice-Mele扇区、自旋分量守恒）下存活；动量空间拓扑不变量本身不足以保证量子化自旋输运，强调实空间诊断在拓扑输运现象中的重要性。

Abstract: We investigate the prospect of topological spin pumping and transport in a dimerized spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ anisotropic XY chain with tunable in-plane anisotropy. Through a Jordan-Wigner transformation, the system maps onto an extended Rice-Mele-type fermionic model whose momentum-space band structure exhibits gapped regions characterized by nonzero Chern numbers ($\pm 1$) as well as trivial and gapless regimes. Conventional momentum-space analysis therefore predicts quantized Thouless-type spin pumping in parts of the Chern number diagram. However, real-space time evolution under open boundary conditions reveals that the spin transport is generally not quantized away from the isotropic limit. In the anisotropic regime, eigen-energy states within the transporting subspace hybridize during the driving cycle, leading to a transport that depends on details of the driving protocol. Quantized pumping and transport survive only in the isotropic limit, where the Hamiltonian reduces to decoupled Rice-Mele sectors and the transported spin component is conserved. Our results demonstrate that momentum-space topological invariants alone do not guarantee quantized spin transport in anisotropic spin chains. More broadly, they show that the conventional Thouless-pumping framework does not straightforwardly extend to situations where the transported quantity is not conserved, highlighting the importance of real-space diagnostics in topological transport phenomena.

</details>


### [212] [Synaptic Multi-Weight Wafer-Scale Domain Wall Magnetic Tunnel Junctions Driven by Spin Orbit Torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08405)
*Nicholas Zogbi,Domenico Giuliano,Vaishnavi Kateel,Van Dai Nguyen,Jyotirmoy Chatterjee,Alvaro Palomino,Robert Carpenter,Siddharth Rao,Jean Anne C. Incorvia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文在300 mm晶圆级自旋轨道矩驱动的磁隧道结人工突触中实现并调控多达八个电阻态，并评估其神经网络推理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于磁随机存储器（MRAM）的自旋电子突触器件是模拟神经形态架构中快速发展的类别，但在尺寸缩小条件下获得多权重（multi-weight）或模拟行为以提升神经网络推理精度仍是挑战。

Method: 通过改变施加的面内磁场（in-plane field），借助畴壁成核与钉扎（domain wall nucleation and pinning）产生多权重电阻态；采用迭代编程算法（iterative programming algorithm）寻址特定电阻；并结合电磁仿真（electromagnetic simulations）分析机理。

Result: 多权重态呈随机出现；电磁仿真表明多态源于面内磁场与施加电压介导的畴壁成核。若所有器件提供相同数量的多权重态，MNIST数据集推理精度达96.1%；计入实验器件间差异（device-to-device variation）后精度为48.6%。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了面向多权重人工突触MRAM的技术路径，为基于自旋电子学（spintronics）的可扩展神经形态系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Spintronic synaptic devices based on magnetic random access memory (MRAM) are a rapidly evolving class within the analog neuromorphic architecture. However, obtaining the multi-weight or analog behavior desired for improved neural network inference accuracy in scaled MRAM remains a challenge. In this study, we demonstrate and tune up to eight distinct resistance levels in 300 mm wafer-scale magnetic tunnel junction artificial synapses driven by spin orbit torque. By varying the applied in-plane field, multi-weight resistance states emerge through domain wall nucleation and pinning. These multi-weight states appear stochastically and can be addressed with an iterative programming algorithm to program a specific resistance. Electromagnetic simulations indicate the multiple states are due to domain wall nucleation mediated by in-plane field and applied voltage. Implementation of the measured multi-weight devices in a neural network yields a 96.1% inference accuracy using the MNIST dataset if all devices provide the same number of multi-weight states. When experimental device-to-device variation is included, the accuracy is 48.6%. Overall, this work demonstrates a route towards multi-weight artificial synapse MRAM, establishing a path towards scalable neuromorphic systems based on spintronics.

</details>
