### [213] [Nonequilibrium Spin-Glass Order in a Long-Range Quantum Ising Glass and Its Sensitivity to Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07115)
*Giuseppe D'Auria,Alessandro Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究长程量子伊辛玻璃（long-range quantum Ising glass）在横场淬火（transverse-field quench）后的自旋玻璃序，发现对角系综的 Edwards-Anderson 序参量在无淬火与零场两个端点处重合，中间区域则出现冷壳层过冲、热壳层欠冲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画横场淬火后非平衡自旋玻璃序的行为，并厘清对角系综 Edwards-Anderson 序参量在淬火强度变化下的非单调结构来源。

Method: 采用长程量子伊辛玻璃模型进行横场淬火，考察对角系综 Edwards-Anderson 序参量随淬火强度的变化，并在相关空间（correlation space）中分析本征态刚度与配对模式扩展之间的竞争；同时引入弱纵向退相干（longitudinal dephasing）。

Result: Edwards-Anderson 序参量在无淬火与零场两端点数值一致，但中间区域冷壳层（cold shells）过冲、热壳层（hot shells）欠冲；该现象源于占据本征态的刚度（rigidity）与其配对模式（pair patterns）扩展之间的竞争。弱纵向退相干下，零场端点被精确钉住，而有限淬火的特征响应逐渐消退。

Conclusion: 结果给出一种自参考噪声探针（self-referenced noise probe），可用于量子自旋模拟器（quantum spin simulators）中淬火响应的探测；零场端点的钉扎特性为标定提供内禀参考。

Abstract: We study spin-glass order after a transverse-field quench in a long-range quantum Ising glass. The diagonal-ensemble Edwards-Anderson order coincides at the no-quench and zero-field endpoints, yet in between cold shells overshoot it and hot shells undershoot it. This stems from a competition in correlation space between the rigidity of populated eigenstates and the spread of their pair patterns. Under weak longitudinal dephasing the zero-field endpoint is exactly pinned while the characteristic finite-quench response fades, giving a self-referenced noise probe for quantum spin simulators.

</details>


### [214] [On the critical value of void percolation in high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07196)
*Patrick Charbonneau,Marcus Michelen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文证明高维随机 Lorentz 气体（random Lorentz gas）的空隙渗流阈值（void percolation threshold）满足 Φ_p(d)=Θ(d)，并给出 1/2·d ≤ Φ_p(d) ≤ 5·d 的显式界，从而确定其维度标度并修正平均场图像下激活窗口的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机 Lorentz 气体中，当空隙空间在 Φ_p(d) 处停止渗流时示踪粒子被局域化，但该阈值的维度标度长期未定：已有界（含猜想）从 Ω(1) 到 O(d ln d)，连标度本身都未确定。该文旨在确定 Φ_p(d) 的渐近标度。

Method: 证明 Φ_p(d)=Θ(d)。上界通过对称为树嵌入（tree embeddings）的层级逃逸路标族计数得到；下界通过在一个显式的弹道逃逸路径（ballistic escape routes）集合上做加权二阶矩估计（weighted second-moment estimate）得到。

Result: 对充分大的 d 证明 1/2·d ≤ Φ_p(d) ≤ 5·d，即 Φ_p(d)=Θ(d)；线性标度排除了与随机一阶相变（random first-order transition）普适类中其他系统类比所暗示的 d ln d 增长，并与数值估计 Φ_p/d → 3.40(5) 一致。

Conclusion: 由于平均场动力学转变（mean-field dynamical transition）同样是线性的，Φ_d ≃ 2.40·d，故该结果将激活窗口（activated window）[Φ_d, Φ_p] 确定为对平均场动力学阻塞的有界修正，而非随维度发散的修正。

Abstract: In the random Lorentz gas, a point tracer moves through spherical obstacles of (reduced) density $Φ$ centered on a Poisson point process in $d$-dimensional space, $\mathbb{R}^d$, and becomes localized once the vacant space stops percolating, at $Φ_{\rm p}(d)$. The dimensional scaling of this void percolation threshold has remained elusive. Prior bounds, conjectural ones included, ranged from $Ω(1)$ to $O(d\ln d)$, leaving even the scaling undetermined. We here prove that $Φ_{\rm p}(d)=Θ(d)$, and in particular that $\tfrac12\,d \le Φ_{\rm p}(d) \le 5\,d$ for all sufficiently large $d$. The upper bound counts hierarchical families of escape waypoints called tree embeddings; the lower bound is via a weighted second-moment estimate on an explicit ensemble of ballistic escape routes. A linear scaling of $Φ_{\rm p}(d)$ excludes the $d\ln d$ growth suggested by analogy with other systems in the random first-order transition universality class and is consistent with the numerical estimate $Φ_{\rm p}/d \to 3.40(5)$. Because the mean-field dynamical transition is likewise linear, $Φ_{\rm d}\simeq 2.40\,d$, our result fixes the activated window $[Φ_{\rm d},Φ_{\rm p}]$ as a bounded---rather than diverging---correction to mean-field dynamical arrest in sufficiently high $d$.

</details>


### [215] [Thermodynamics of Forgetting in a Bounded Hopfield Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08258)
*Andrea Lepre*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文给出 Hopfield-Parisi 模型的热力学描述：该网络等价于一个按年龄加权的 Hopfield 模型叠加在 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick 自旋玻璃上，并受突触强度上界约束。作者用 Guerra 插值法在复本对称层面重新推导方程并求解有限温度情形，得到相图与记忆时域（memory horizon），结果与蒙特卡洛模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Hopfield 网络（Hopfield network）在存储模式（pattern）超过临界数量后会陷入完全混乱、无法检索任何模式。Parisi 于 1986 年提出限制突触强度（synaptic strength）可治愈该病理，但该模型的热力学性质此前仅在零温下求解过，缺乏有限温度下的完整描述。

Method: 从学习规则出发，将网络映射为按模式年龄加权的 Hopfield 模型与 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick 自旋玻璃（spin glass）的叠加，两者均受突触上界支配。借助 Guerra 插值法（Guerra's interpolation）在复本对称（replica symmetric）层面重新推导原有启发式复本方法（heuristic replica method）给出的方程，并在有限温度下求解。

Result: 求得模型的相图（phase diagram）及记忆时域（memory horizon，即模式仍可被检索的最大年龄）。结果显示遗忘仅作用于信号，噪声对各年龄的模式相同；记忆时域在某一中间上界处取得最大值，且该最优上界基本不随温度变化。对采用原始学习规则训练的网络所做蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）支持上述预测。

Conclusion: Hopfield-Parisi 模型可被完整地以热力学方式刻画：突触上界越大记忆越持久但噪声也越大，存在使记忆时域最大化的最优上界且对温度不敏感；遗忘机制只削弱信号而不改变噪声结构。

Abstract: A memory that keeps learning must also forget. The Hopfield network does not: beyond a critical number of stored patterns it falls into a state of total confusion and retrieves none of them. Parisi showed in 1986 that bounding the synaptic strength cures this pathology, since the clipping of the couplings gradually erases the oldest memories and leaves room for the new ones. In this paper we provide a thermodynamic description of the resulting network, known as the Hopfield-Parisi model. Starting from the learning rule, we show that the network is equivalent to a Hopfield model whose patterns are weighted according to their age, superimposed on a Sherrington-Kirkpatrick spin glass, both governed by the synaptic bound: a larger bound makes the memory longer, but also noisier. The theory of nonlinear networks developed in the eighties already provides, through the heuristic replica method, the equations that govern this model at any temperature, but to the best of our knowledge they have been solved only at zero temperature. We rederive them at the replica symmetric level through Guerra's interpolation and solve them at finite temperature, obtaining the phase diagram of the model together with its memory horizon, namely the largest age at which a pattern can still be retrieved. The solution shows that forgetting acts on the signal alone, the noise being the same for patterns of every age, and that the horizon is maximal at an intermediate bound which is essentially insensitive to temperature. Monte Carlo simulations of networks trained with the original learning rule support these predictions.

</details>
